### [1] [Natural Chlorine Enhances Antineutrino Detection in the Deep Ocean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30754)
*Ruike Cao,Iwan Morton-Blake,Donglian Xu,Qichao Chang,Shuhua Hao,Jingtao Huang,Weilun Huang,Yifan Jiang,Shasha Liu,Hualin Mei,Hengbin Shao,Yingwei Wang,Peilian Wu,Xin Xiang,Fuyudi Zhang,Haoyan Zhang,Zhaozhi Zhang,Tailin Zhu,Yiwei Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文提出利用海水中天然丰度较高的氯（chlorine）作为中子俘获靶，通过逆β衰变（inverse beta decay）实现对电子反中微子（electron antineutrino）的高效延迟符合标记，并据此将大体积中微子望远镜的核坍缩超新星（core-collapse supernova）5σ发现距离从约50 kpc拓展至100 kpc以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IceCube、KM3NeT等大体积中微子望远镜（neutrino telescope）以天然冰或水为探测介质，但仪器布设稀疏，难以逐个识别MeV量级的中微子相互作用，制约了低能中微子天文观测能力。

Method: 依据氯俘获中子产生约8.6 MeV γ级联且时间更短、区别于氢俘获2.2 MeV γ射线的特性构建延迟符合信号；结合TRIDENT站址实测海水性质与探测器级模拟进行评估，并在中国锦屏地下实验室（China Jinping Underground Laboratory）以加盐（salted）水实验在压低宇宙μ子本底下验证探测原理。

Result: 模拟表明氯辅助标记可将核坍缩超新星5σ发现距离由约50 kpc提升至100 kpc以上；锦屏实验在抑制宇宙μ子本底条件下观测到相关的中子俘获信号特征，验证了基本原理。

Conclusion: 天然海水可将深海转化为本征增强的反中微子探测介质，为MeV中微子天文以及反应堆与地球物理反中微子的分布式测量开辟新途径。

Abstract: Large-volume neutrino telescopes such as IceCube and KM3NeT exploit natural ice or water as detection media, but their sparse instrumentation makes MeV neutrino interactions difficult to identify individually. We show that chlorine naturally abundant in seawater provides an intrinsic neutron-capture target that enables efficient tagging of electron antineutrinos through inverse beta decay. In contrast to the 2.2 MeV $γ$-ray from neutron capture on hydrogen, capture on chlorine produces an $\sim$8.6 MeV $γ$-ray cascade on a shorter time scale, yielding a distinctive delayed-coincidence signature. Using measured seawater properties at the TRIDENT site and a detector-level simulation, we find that chlorine-assisted tagging can extend the core-collapse supernova 5$σ$ discovery reach from $\sim$50 to over 100 kpc. We validate the underlying detection principle with a salted-water experiment at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory, observing correlated neutron-capture signatures under suppressed cosmic muon background conditions. Natural seawater thus transforms the deep ocean into an intrinsically enhanced antineutrino detection medium, opening new opportunities for MeV neutrino astronomy and distributed measurements of reactor and geophysical antineutrinos.

</details>


### [2] [CURTAIN: Towards a Foundation Model for Neutrino Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30894)
*Severin Magel,Felix J. Yu,Carlos A. Argüelles*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CURTAIN 是首个面向中微子望远镜（neutrino telescope）的自监督学习（self-supervised learning, SSL）任务，采用时间掩码（temporal masking）方案，同时满足预训练集增大时性能提升、可迁移至不同探测器几何、并优于无预训练基线三项条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子望远镜每年记录超过一万亿次光子探测，但现有 SSL 任务均无法同时满足三个条件：随预训练集增长而提升、迁移到不同探测器、以及超越最佳无预训练模型。该工作即针对此空白提出新 SSL 任务。

Method: 提出 CURTAIN——一种时间掩码方案，构建于当前 NuBench 基准方向重建任务领先模型 DeepIce 的编码器（encoder）之上，通过掩码式自监督预训练进行表征学习，并检验其缩放规律（scaling law）与跨探测器迁移能力。

Result: 与从头训练（training from scratch）相比，CURTAIN 预训练使中位角误差降低 45%、中位能量误差降低 31%；在预训练中未见过的探测器几何上，中位角误差仍降低 36%。在相同容量与预训练数据下，其在两项任务及所有预训练集规模上均优于现有 SSL 任务预训练的编码器；下游性能随预训练事件数单调提升。

Conclusion: CURTAIN 是首个同时实现随数据规模提升、跨探测器迁移与超越无预训练基线的中微子望远镜 SSL 任务，验证了缩放规律在该领域的适用性，并为探测器无关的通用表征预训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Neutrino telescopes record more than a trillion photon detections per year, yet there does not exist a self-supervised learning (SSL) task which fully satisfies all three conditions: improving as the pretraining set grows, transferring to a different detector, and outperforming the best model trained without pretraining at all. We introduce CURTAIN, a temporal masking scheme and the first SSL task for neutrino telescopes that achieves all of these goals. Downstream performance monotonically improves with the number of pretraining events, as predicted by scaling laws. Our encoder builds on DeepIce, currently the leading model on the NuBench benchmarks direction reconstruction task. Pretraining it with CURTAIN reduces its median angular error by 45% and its median energy error by 31% compared to training from scratch. It also transfers to a detector geometry never seen in pretraining, reducing the median angular error by 36% compared to training from scratch. At matched capacity and pretraining data, CURTAIN-pretrained encoders beat those pretrained by existing SSL tasks on both tasks and at every pretraining set size.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII在3.80–4.95 GeV的e+e-数据，通过标记D0、D+或Ds+介子测量开粲介子的单举产生截面σ(e+e-→D0/D+/Ds++X)，为强子产生理解、R值检验及奇特态研究提供参考与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开粲产生在阈值以上主导总强子产额，因此精确测量其截面对于理解强子产生机制和检验全局R值结果至关重要，同时可为研究较重粲偶素及与开粲末态耦合的奇特态提供关键输入。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在质心系能量3.80至4.95 GeV采集的e+e-对撞数据，通过标记D0、D+或Ds+介子，测量开粲介子的单举产生截面（inclusive production cross section），其中X表示任意伴随粒子。

Result: 给出了σ(e+e-→D0/D+/Ds++X)的单举产生截面测量结果，该结果可作为与各 Exclusive 道（exclusive channels）测量之和比较的基准，从而直接评估现有exclusive数据集的完备性。

Conclusion: 该测量有助于厘清开粲区域的强子化机制（hadronization mechanism），并检验现有exclusive数据的完备性，为相关物理研究提供关键实验依据。

Abstract: Using $e^+e^-$ collision data collected by the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies from $3.80$ to $4.95~\text{GeV}$, the inclusive production cross sections of open-charm mesons, $σ(e^{+}e^{-}\to D^{0}/D^{+}/D_{s}^{+}+X)$, are measured by tagging a $D^0$, $D^+$, or $D_s^+$ meson, where $X$ denotes any accompanying particles. Open-charm production dominates the total hadronic yield above threshold. Therefore these measurements provide a vital reference for understanding hadron production and for validating global $R$ value results. The results offer crucial inputs for investigating heavier charmonia and exotic states coupled to open-charm final states, while serving as a benchmark for comparison with the sum of measured exclusive channels. This comparison directly evaluates the completeness of current exclusive datasets and clarifies the underlying hadronization mechanisms in the open-charm region.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用Belle与Belle II数据测量了Λc+→ΛK+π0与Λc+→ΛK+η的分支比，其中后者为首次观测（显著性超过10个标准差），且其分支比显著大于SU(3)味对称性（SU(3) flavor symmetry）预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单Cabibbo压低（singly Cabibbo-suppressed）三体衰变Λc+→ΛK+π0与Λc+→ΛK+η的实验信息不足，尤其是Λc+→ΛK+η此前未被观测，且其分支比可用于检验SU(3)味对称性预言。

Method: 使用KEKB与SuperKEKB e+e-对撞机上Belle和Belle II探测器采集的数据，积分亮度分别为980 fb^-1与570 fb^-1；以Cabibbo允许衰变Λc+→Λπ+π0为参考，测量两衰变道的分支比比值。

Result: 测得B(Λc+→ΛK+π0)/B(Λc+→Λπ+π0)=(1.82±0.07±0.07)%，精度较此前测量显著提高；B(Λc+→ΛK+η)/B(Λc+→Λπ+π0)=(8.73±0.35±0.43)×10^-3。结合世界平均值得到B(Λc+→ΛK+π0)=(1.29±0.05±0.05±0.06)×10^-3与B(Λc+→ΛK+η)=(6.20±0.25±0.31±0.30)×10^-4。

Conclusion: Λc+→ΛK+η为首个观测量，统计显著性超过10个标准差；其分支比显著高于基于SU(3)味对称性的理论预言，提示该类对称性描述在此衰变中存在偏差。

Abstract: We present a study of the three-body, singly Cabibbo-suppressed decays $Λ_c^+ \to ΛK^+ π^0$ and $Λ_c^+ \to ΛK^+ η$ using data recorded with the Belle and Belle II detectors at the KEKB and SuperKEKB $e^+ e^-$ colliders. The data samples correspond to integrated luminosities of 980$\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ and 570$\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, respectively. We measure their branching-fraction ratios relative to the Cabibbo-favored decay $Λ_c^+ \to Λπ^+ π^0$ as $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+ \to ΛK^+ π^0)/\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+ \to Λπ^+ π^0)=(1.82 \pm 0.07 \pm 0.07)\%$, with significantly improved precision compared to the previous measurement, and $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+ \to ΛK^+ η)/\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+ \to Λπ^+ π^0)=(8.73 \pm 0.35 \pm 0.43)\times10^{-3}$, corresponding to the first observation of the $Λ_c^+ \to ΛK^+ η$ decay with a statistical significance exceeding 10 standard deviations. The first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. Using the world-average value of the branching fraction for $Λ_c^+ \to Λπ^+ π^0$, we obtain the branching fractions $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+ \to ΛK^+ π^0)=(1.29\pm 0.05\pm 0.05\pm 0.06)\times10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+ \to ΛK^+ η)=(6.20\pm 0.25\pm 0.31\pm 0.30)\times10^{-4}$, where the third uncertainty arises from the uncertainty in the world-average value of $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+ \to Λπ^+ π^0)$. The measured $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+\toΛK^+η)$ is significantly larger than predictions based on $SU(3)$ flavor symmetry.

</details>


### [5] [A superconducting LC resonator in a multi-tesla magnetic field for tabletop kHz gravitational-wave detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31430)
*D. Ahn,C. Braggio,G. Carugno,D. D'Agostino,R. Di Vora,P. Falferi,A. Ortolan,G. Ruoso,M. L. Ruggiero*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种置于磁场中的超导电感—电容（LC）谐振器方案，用于kHz频段引力波探测，并基于耦合电路模型估算应变噪声谱，预测灵敏度可达10^-21 Hz^-1/2量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有干涉式引力波探测在中高频（kHz）频段覆盖有限，需发展紧凑、可调谐的补充型探测器。论文借助静态磁场中引力波引起的磁场扰动效应，探索高频频段探测可能。

Method: 在长波极限下推导引力波与静态磁场耦合产生的振荡磁场扰动，并用超导拾取电路（superconducting pickup circuit）将其转换为由SQUID或量子极限放大器链读取的磁通信号。借鉴共振质量探测器（resonant-mass detector）思路，建立三回路耦合电路模型（three-loop coupled-circuit model），推导包含热噪声、SQUID反作用（backaction）与测量不精确度的等效应变噪声谱。

Result: 原型测量获得品质因数Q≃5×10^5，未来目标为Q=10^7。在给定磁通耦合与理想化量子极限SQUID噪声模型下，优化电路在1–20 kHz范围内于100 Hz带宽上投影应变灵敏度约为10^-21 Hz^-1/2。

Conclusion: 增大高场有效体积并改善量子极限读出可进一步提升灵敏度；该紧凑、可调谐的探测概念有望补充干涉式引力波搜索，并推动高频天体物理源研究。

Abstract: We present a concept for a superconducting inductance--capacitance (LC) resonator immersed in a magnetic field for gravitational-wave detection in the kHz band. In the long-wavelength limit, a gravitational wave in a static magnetic field induces an oscillating magnetic-field perturbation proportional to the static magnetic field applied to the resonator and to the gravitational-wave strain. We show that a superconducting pickup circuit can convert this perturbation into a flux signal read out by a superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID)-based or quantum-limited amplifier chain. Inspired by resonant-mass detectors, we develop a three-loop coupled-circuit model and derive the equivalent strain-noise spectrum, including thermal noise, SQUID backaction, and measurement imprecision. Prototype measurements demonstrate a quality factor of $Q\simeq 5\times 10^5$, while $Q=10^7$ is considered as a future development target. Under the assumed flux coupling and an idealized quantum-limited SQUID noise model, optimized circuits yield projected strain sensitivities of order $10^{-21}\,\mathrm{Hz}^{-1/2}$ over $100\,\mathrm{Hz}$ bands in the $1$--$20\,\mathrm{kHz}$ range. Increasing the active high-field volume and improving quantum-limited readout could enable further sensitivity gains. This compact, tunable detector concept could complement interferometric gravitational-wave searches and motivate investigations of high-frequency astrophysical sources.

</details>

### [6] [Lenses make many points: Pure point spectrum disproves Simon's $L^2$ conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31637)
*Simon Becker,Mengxuan Yang*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明 Barry Simon 提出的 $L^2$ 猜想（$L^2$ conjecture）不成立：对任意 $d\ge2$ 与 $0\le\sigma<1/3$，构造出范数可任意小的光滑有界非负透镜型位势（lens-type potential）$V$，使 $-\\Delta+V$ 在 $(0,\\infty)$ 上仅有纯点谱（pure point spectrum）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验 Simon 在“Schrödinger operators in the twenty-first century”中提出的第 8 号问题（$L^2$ 猜想）：即小位势的 $L^2$ 型弱局部条件是否足以保证连续谱（continuous spectrum）的存在。摘要表明该猜想被否定，从而修正了对位势衰减条件与谱类型关系的既有认识。

Method: 方法是构造性的：对每个 $d\ge2$ 和 $0\le\sigma<1/3$，借助 LLM 辅助（LLM-assistance）构造光滑、有界、非负的透镜型位势（lens-type potential）$V$，其 $\\|V\\|_\\infty+\\int_{\\mathbb{R}^d}|x|^{1-d+\\sigma}|V(x)|^2 dx$ 可任意小，并考察对应 Schrödinger 算子（Schrödinger operator）$-\\Delta+V$ 的谱结构。

Result: 所构造的位势满足任意小的范数条件，而 $-\\Delta+V$ 在 $(0,\\infty)$ 上只有纯点谱（pure point spectrum），即不存在绝对连续谱（absolutely continuous spectrum）。由此给出 $L^2$ 猜想的反例，说明该猜想为假。

Conclusion: 结论是 Simon 的 $L^2$ 猜想（其文集中的 Problem 8）不成立；即便位势满足小范数与 $L^2$ 型加权可积条件，算子仍可在正半轴呈现纯点谱。该结果对衰减条件与谱绝对连续性之间的传统判据构成反例。

Abstract: For every $d\ge2$ and $0\leσ<1/3$, we construct with LLM-assistance a smooth bounded nonnegative lens-type potential $V$ with arbitrarily small $\|V\|_\infty+\int_{\mathbb{R}^d}|x|^{1-d+σ}|V(x)|^2 dx$, such that $-Δ+V$ has only pure point spectrum in $(0,\infty)$. Thus, we show that Barry Simon's $L^2$ conjecture, Problem 8 in "Schrödinger operators in the twenty-first century", is false.

</details>

### [7] [Macroscopic Nuclear Resonance Energy Transfer Mediated by Coherent Magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31507)
*Ryan Weed*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 该文将广义核Dicke模型（generalized nuclear Dicke model）推广至铁磁共振磁振子（FMR magnons），提出磁振子介导的核共振能量转移（RET）机制，以规避金属氘化物中质量稀释、晶格断裂与自旋抵消的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属氘化物中的声子介导核共振能量转移受惯性静止质量稀释（inertial rest-mass dilution）、晶格断裂极限及热核自旋抵消三重制约，需寻找替代的耦合通道。

Method: 把广义核Dicke模型扩展至铁磁共振磁振子：借助超精细放大M1耦合消除质量稀释，并通过Overhauser自旋极化（Overhauser spin polarization）开启相对论性Dirac M2耦合；由此推导Suhl钳制（Suhl-clamped）FMR标度律。

Result: 标度律预测Pd-106的14.2 MeV与Ni-58的11.4 MeV断裂中子（breakup neutrons）、19.9 MeV α粒子，并调和了亚keV量级的聚变异常（sub-keV fusion anomalies）。

Conclusion: 磁振子介导的M1/M2耦合通道在理论上可绕开声子路径的固有限制，为金属氘化物中的核能量转移与异常现象提供统一解释框架。

Abstract: Phonon-mediated nuclear Resonance Energy Transfer (RET) in metal deuterides is severely constrained by inertial rest-mass dilution, lattice fracture limits, and thermal nuclear spin cancellation. Extending the generalized nuclear Dicke model to Ferromagnetic Resonance (FMR) magnons eliminates mass dilution via hyperfine-amplified M1 coupling while unlocking relativistic Dirac M2 coupling via Overhauser spin polarization. We derive a Suhl-clamped FMR scaling law predicting 14.2 MeV (Pd-106) and 11.4 MeV (Ni-58) breakup neutrons, 19.9 MeV alpha particles, and reconciled sub-keV fusion anomalies.

</details>

### [8] [Finite time implosions of the multidimensional compressible Euler equations with a finite far-field cutoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30331)
*Jack Luong,Reed Spitzer,Scott D. Ramsey,Andrea L. Bertozzi,Roy Baty*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文通过径向对称自适应网格高分辨率数值模拟（Lagrangian leapfrog 格式）结合相似解渐近分析，研究多维等熵可压缩 Euler 方程在 Kidder 自相似内爆初值下的有限时间奇点（finite time singularity）结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受一维问题已有工作的启发，需厘清多维径向对称情形下 Kidder 内爆在常数密度截断初值下的后续动力学，尤其是膨胀波与内爆解的相互作用及爆破机制。

Method: 采用径向对称自适应网格加密（adaptive mesh refinement）的高分辨率数值模拟，使用 Lagrangian leapfrog 格式以避免 Eulerian 方法的数值扩散，并在爆破附近递归加密网格；同时结合渐近相似解分析。

Result: 初值截断点处产生膨胀波，其两端按双曲守恒律系统的“内爆”与“外爆”特征速度传播，结构比一维精确非中心稀疏波更复杂；当时间趋近奇点时刻，Kidder 解被膨胀波吞没，且爆破发生在膨胀波“内爆”侧与 Kidder 解相接处，呈 Guderley 型自相似结构的渐近形式。

Conclusion: 与已有严格结果一致，该爆破不涉及质量集中（mass concentration），并存在与空间维数相关的密度集中临界 L^p 空间；模拟在多个数量级上分辨了自相似爆破过程。

Abstract: We study the problem of finite time singularities in the isentropic compressible Euler equations in multiple dimensions with radial symmetry. Our initial data consists of the radially symmetric Kidder self-similar implosion with a continuous constant density cutoff. Motivated by our recent work on the one-dimensional problem, we present high-resolution radially symmetric adaptive mesh refinement simulations that when combined with asymptotic similarity solution analysis present a full picture of the ensuing dynamics of the implosion. From the cutoff point in the initial data, an expanding wave emerges. The endpoints of the wave follow the "imploding" and "exploding" characteristic speeds associated with the system of hyperbolic conservation laws. This wave has much more complex structure than the exact non-centered rarefaction wave that emerges in the one-dimensional problem. As in the one-dimensional case, the inner Kidder solution is swallowed up by the expanding wave as time approaches the singularity time. However, unlike the one-dimensional case, a blowup occurs along the point where the `imploding' side of the expanding wave attaches to the Kidder solution. This composite blowup has an asymptotic self-similar form that has an interpretation in terms of the Gurderley-type self-similarity structure. The numerical method for the PDE necessitates a Lagrangian scheme to avoid the numerical diffusion inherent in Eulerian methods. We use a leapfrog method coupled to recursive refinement of the mesh near the blowup, which allows for very high resolution simulations, resolving many orders of magnitude of the self-similar blowup. Consistent with prior rigorous results for these equations, the blowup does not involve mass concentration and exhibits a critical $L^p$ space for the concentration of the density, depending on the dimension of space.

</details>


### [9] [Initial-boundary value problems for linear lattice differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31057)
*Andreas Chatziafratis,Spyridon Kamvissis,Antonín Slavík*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文系统研究半直线（half-line）上线性格微分方程（linear lattice differential equations）的初边值问题（initial-boundary value problems），给出基本解、卷积表示及适定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对半直线上格微分方程的初边值问题，现有研究缺乏对一般有限程相互作用（finite-range interactions）基本解的系统刻画，以及多种表示方法之间互补关系的统一处理。

Method: 先系统给出一般有限程相互作用方程的基本解，再基于反射法（reflection）、拉普拉斯变换（Laplace transform）与统一变换方法（unified transform method）发展互补途径，得到轮廓积分（contour integral）与贝塞尔函数（Bessel-function）表示。

Result: 对半离散扩散方程（semidiscrete diffusion equation），在连续边界数据与多项式增长初值下严格验证上述表示，建立相应增长类中的唯一性（uniqueness），并导出空间估计与长时间性质，包括渐近周期性（asymptotic periodicity）。

Conclusion: 研究进一步涵盖非齐次方程（nonhomogeneous equations）、Robin 边界条件、非对称系数（nonsymmetric coefficients）及一类时间依赖系数（time-dependent coefficients）；高阶例子表明统一变换方法可推广至二阶以上方程。

Abstract: We study initial-boundary value problems for linear lattice differential equations on the half-line. We first give a systematic account of fundamental solutions for equations with general finite-range interactions. We then develop complementary approaches based on reflection, the Laplace transform, and the unified transform method, obtaining contour integral and Bessel-function representations of solutions. For the semidiscrete diffusion equation, we justify these representations for continuous boundary data and initial data of polynomial growth, establish uniqueness in the corresponding growth class, and derive spatial estimates and long-time properties, including asymptotic periodicity. We also treat nonhomogeneous equations, Robin boundary conditions, nonsymmetric coefficients, and a class of time-dependent coefficients. Finally, higher-order examples illustrate how the unified transform approach extends beyond second-order equations.

</details>

### [10] [Discrete time liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30266)
*Zuo Wang,Paul M. Schindler,Marin Bukov,Marios H. Michael*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出并刻画了“离散时间液体（discrete time liquid）”这一新形式的时空序：由时间孤子（time-solitons）导致仅具短程时间相干性的周期倍增动力学，是时间晶体（time crystal）在有限时间孤子密度下的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间晶体自发破缺周期驱动的离散时间平移对称性，但其存在引发了对其他类型时间多体序（temporal many-body order）能否存在的追问，本文即针对此问题展开。

Method: 研究一个与有限温度腔（finite-temperature cavity）耦合的二维自旋系统，腔的涨落提供具有限时间相干性的含噪、近似周期驱动；并通过一个最小模型（minimal model）复现多体系统的时间关联分布。

Result: 尽管驱动仅有有限时间相干性，自旋仍在较宽参数区间形成具有稳健周期倍增动力学的亚稳态（metastable state）；与时间晶体不同，其时间相干性有限，单条轨迹会在两个对称相关的时间构型间随机切换。

Conclusion: 这些切换被识别为时间孤子——破坏长程时间相干性但保留局域周期倍增的时间序拓扑缺陷，从而产生时间液体；由于孤子可在任意时刻出现，系综平均（ensemble averaging）恢复连续时间平移对称性，暗示离散时间液体可能在热平衡（thermal equilibrium）中存在。

Abstract: Discrete time crystals exhibit robust subharmonic oscillations, spontaneously breaking the discrete time-translation symmetry of a periodic drive. Since their emergence, the question of whether other types of temporal many-body order can exist has attracted considerable attention. Here we identify discrete time liquids -- a new form of spatiotemporal order characterized by short-range temporal coherence as a result of time-solitons. We study a two-dimensional spin system coupled to a finite-temperature cavity, whose fluctuations provide a noisy, approximately periodic drive with only finite temporal coherence. Remarkably, the spins nevertheless form a metastable state with robust period-doubled dynamics over an extended parameter regime. Unlike a time crystal, this state has only finite temporal coherence: individual trajectories stochastically switch between the two symmetry-related temporal configurations of the period-doubled response. We identify these switches as time-solitons: topological defects in temporal order that preserve local period doubling while destroying long-range temporal coherence, thereby producing the time liquid state. We propose a minimal model which reproduces the distribution of temporal correlations of the many-body system, suggesting a general description of the time liquid as a time crystal with a finite density of time-solitons. Because these solitons can occur at arbitrary times, ensemble averaging restores continuous time-translation symmetry, raising the intriguing possibility that discrete time liquids can exist in thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [11] [Charge Density Waves and In-Gap States in Coupled Chains and Moiré Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30281)
*Muhammad Akram,Onur Erten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究耦合的 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 链（包括晶格匹配的梯形结构与晶格失配的莫尔超晶格），发现多种电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）相及对称性保护的束缚态；电子-声子耦合与链间隧穿的竞争决定 CDW 序列，晶格失配则将其重构为长周期超莫尔 CDW 与缺陷阵列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷密度波源于电子与晶格畸变的相互作用，是低维体系的标志性现象；研究意在厘清链间隧穿与晶格失配如何调控一维链中的 CDW 序与束缚态。

Method: 以耦合的 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 链为模型体系，分别考察晶格匹配的梯形结构（ladder）与晶格失配的莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice），分析电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）与链间隧穿（interchain tunneling）的竞争。

Result: 在晶格匹配链中，耦合竞争驱动出交错二聚体（staggered dimers）、三聚体（trimers）及不可公度态（incommensurate states）等序列化 CDW 相；这些相通常带隙，其束缚态由晶体对称性（crystalline symmetry）而非手性对称性（chiral symmetry）保护，故不钉扎于零能。晶格失配进一步将上述相重构为长周期超莫尔 CDW，并形成按莫尔长度尺度钉扎的有序缺陷阵列，其中存在局域电子态。

Conclusion: 链间隧穿与晶格失配可作为一维链中 CDW 序与束缚态的天然调控参数。

Abstract: Charge density waves (CDWs) arise from the interplay between electrons and lattice distortions and are a hallmark of low-dimensional systems. Here we study coupled Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains, both lattice-matched ladders and lattice-mismatched moiré superlattices, and find a rich set of CDW phases together with symmetry-protected bound states. In lattice-matched chains, the competition between electron-phonon coupling and interchain tunneling drives a sequence of CDW phases, including staggered dimers, trimers, and incommensurate states. These phases are generally gapped and host bound states protected by crystalline symmetries rather than chiral symmetry, and are therefore not pinned to zero energy. Lattice mismatch further reconstructs these phases into long-period supermoiré CDWs, with an ordered array of defects, pinned to the moiré length scale, that host localized electronic states. Interchain tunneling and lattice mismatch thus emerge as natural control parameters for CDW order and bound states in one-dimensional chains.

</details>


### [12] [$π$-Flux Tuning of Quantum Paramagnetism in the Triangular-Lattice Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30290)
*Zhaochen Liu,Andreas Feuerpfeil,Ronny Thomale*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在三角晶格哈伯德模型（Hubbard model）半填充下，比较零通量模型与交错π通量模型；两者的有效环交换（ring exchange）符号不同，进而在中强耦合区诱导出手性（chiral）与非手性两种量子顺磁（quantum paramagnet）基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两模型在强耦合极限领头阶均约化为相同海森堡模型（Heisenberg model），仅凭自旋自由度无法区分其量子顺磁行为，因此需要考察电荷涨落（charge fluctuation）层面的差异。

Method: 采用强耦合展开（strong-coupling expansion）结合无限密度矩阵重整化群（infinite density matrix renormalization group, iDMRG）数值计算，对两类模型在中间耦合区做对比分析。

Result: 零通量模型具有正环交换，促进自发手性（spontaneous chirality），与其手性自旋液体（chiral spin liquid）图像一致；交错π通量模型的无相互作用极限为狄拉克半金属（Dirac semimetal），其环交换反号、抑制手性序，未探测到可分辨的标量手性（scalar chirality）。

Conclusion: 通量调节的环交换（flux-tuned ring exchange）可作为有限耦合下区分手性与非手性量子顺磁体的高效判别依据。

Abstract: We compare two time-reversal-symmetric triangular-lattice Hubbard models at half filling: the zero-flux model and a staggered $π$-flux model with zero and $π$ flux through oppositely oriented triangles. Although both reduce to the same Heisenberg model at leading order in the strong-coupling limit, their distinct charge fluctuation profiles yield different quantum paramagnetic regimes. Combining a strong-coupling expansion with infinite density matrix renormalization group (iDMRG) calculations, we find that the positive ring-exchange of the zero-flux model promotes spontaneous chirality, consistent with its notion of a chiral spin liquid at intermediate coupling. In the staggered-$π$-flux model, whose noninteracting limit is a Dirac semimetal, the ring exchange changes sign and disfavors chiral order and we find no resolvable scalar chirality. This renders flux-tuned ring-exchange an efficient discriminator between chiral and non-chiral quantum paramagnets at finite coupling.

</details>


### [13] [Extreme competition between stripes and superconductivity in the hole-doped Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30321)
*Ao Chen,Johnnie Gray,Si-Jing Du,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作借助大规模 GPU 计算，结合神经张量网络（neural tensor network）与神经回传流态（neural backflow state）模拟，在最高 1024 格点的晶格上取得前所未有的变分精度，揭示 1/8 掺杂下均匀 d 波态、超导条纹与非超导条纹态在毫-t 能量尺度上近简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维 Hubbard 模型原被用作铜氧化物高温超导体中均匀 d 波超导（d-wave superconductivity）的最小模型，但近期在现实掺杂区间的计算常发现其他基态，且不同计算方法存在内在偏差，导致结果不确定性。该文旨在通过更高精度的计算方法消除这一不确定性。

Method: 采用最先进的神经张量网络（neural tensor network）与神经回传流态（neural backflow state）模拟，依托大规模 GPU 计算，在多达 1024 个格点的晶格上达到空前变分精度。研究考察纯 Hubbard 模型在 1/8 掺杂下、以及与空穴掺杂铜氧化物相关的次近邻跃迁（next-nearest-neighbor hopping）情形。

Result: 在 1/8 掺杂下，无论是纯 Hubbard 模型还是包含次近邻跃迁的情形，均发现均匀 d 波超导态、超导条纹（superconducting stripes）与非超导条纹态之间在毫-t（milli-t）能量尺度上接近简并。

Conclusion: 该研究将“Hubbard 模型在相关参数区间支持单一主导有序态”的叙事，重新调整为条纹与超导性之间存在极端竞争（extreme competition）的图景。

Abstract: The two-dimensional Hubbard model was originally proposed as the minimal model of uniform $d$-wave superconductivity in the cuprate high-temperature superconductors, but recent calculations with relevant parameters in a realistic doping regime have often found other ground states. Further, uncertainty remains over such results due to the intrinsic bias of different computational methods. Here we address this uncertainty through state-of-the-art neural tensor network and neural backflow state simulations, enabled by large-scale GPU computation, that together reach an unprecedented variational accuracy on lattices with up to 1024 sites. At $1/8$ doping in the pure Hubbard model, as well as with next-nearest-neighbor hopping relevant to the hole-doped cuprates, we reveal a near degeneracy among a low-energy uniform $d$-wave state, superconducting stripes, and non-superconducting stripe states on the milli-$t$ energy scale. Our work rebalances the narrative that the Hubbard model supports a single dominant order in relevant parameter regimes towards a picture of extreme competition between stripes and superconductivity.

</details>


### [14] [Emergent altermagnetism in (LaVO$_3$)$_n$/(LaNiO$_3$)$_m$ superlattices from interfacial charge transfer and octahedral tilts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30371)
*Gustavo H. Cassemiro,Walber H. Brito*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算表明，(LaVO3)n/(LaNiO3)m 超晶格中可通过界面电荷转移与八面体倾转的协同作用诱导出交错磁（altermagnetism），产生无需自旋轨道耦合的 d 波自旋劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在氧化物异质结中实现交错磁（altermagnetism）的可控设计途径，特别是界面电荷转移作为调控机制的潜力。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）结合 DFT+U，研究 (n,m)=(1,1)、(2,2)、(2,4) 三种 (LaVO3)n/(LaNiO3)m 超晶格的磁有序、八面体倾转与自旋劈裂。

Result: 电荷转移使体系趋向 V4+ 与 Ni2+ 构型，使原本顺磁的镍酸盐层成为磁性亚晶格；C 型反铁磁序（C-type antiferromagnetic order）与 a-b+c- 或 a-b-c- 八面体倾转结合，仅通过非对称操作（nonsymmorphic operations）连接相反自旋亚晶格，产生无自旋轨道耦合的 d 波自旋劈裂。绝缘的 (LVO)1/(LNO)1 超晶格中劈裂达 118 meV，约为块体 LaVO3 计算值的两倍，并随镍酸盐层增厚而减小；劈裂由 V 与 Ni 亚晶格共同承载，近一半权重位于氧 2p 态上。

Conclusion: 界面电荷转移可作为在氧化物异质结构中工程化交错磁的设计原则（design principle）。

Abstract: Using first-principles calculations, we show that altermagnetism emerges in (LaVO$_3$)$_n$/(LaNiO$_3$)$_m$ superlattices, with $(n,m) = (1,1), (2,2)$ and $(2,4)$, from the interplay of interfacial charge transfer and octahedral tilts. Charge transfer drives the system towards the V$^{4+}$ and Ni$^{2+}$ configuration, turning the otherwise paramagnetic nickelate layers into a magnetically active sublattice. The resulting $C$-type antiferromagnetic order, combined with $a^-b^+c^-$ or $a^-b^-c^-$ octahedral tilts, links opposite-spin sublattices only through nonsymmorphic operations, yielding a $d$-wave spin splitting without spin-orbit coupling. Within DFT+U, the splitting reaches 118 meV in the insulating (LVO)$_1$/(LNO)$_1$ superlattice, about twice that calculated for bulk LaVO$_3$, and decreases as the nickelate layers become thicker. Our findings indicate that this splitting is hosted by both the V and Ni sublattices, with nearly half of its weight on oxygen $2p$ states through $p$-$d$ covalency. Interfacial charge transfer thus emerges as a \textit{design} principle for engineering altermagnetism in oxide heterostructures.

</details>


### [15] [Unrestricted Binary Tree State for the Fermionic Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30399)
*Mahroo Mirabdolhagh Hazaveh,Armin Khamoshi,Fei Gao,Thomas M. Henderson,Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文将二元树态（binary tree state, BTS）推广至半填充费米子哈伯德模型（Hubbard model），采用限制型与非限制型轨道优化，证明非限制 BTS 能有效描述大 U/t 排斥区的主导单占据构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元树态此前作为紧凑的关联电子对波函数（correlated pair wave functions），在反对称双生幂（antisymmetrized geminal power, AGP）基础上增加变分灵活性；本文意在检验其能否刻画排斥哈伯德模型中强关联的单占据物理。

Method: 在半填充费米子哈伯德模型中引入限制型与非限制型轨道（restricted/unrestricted orbitals）的 BTS 拟设；虽在优化配对基下为 seniority zero，但在原始格点基展开后可含非零格点 seniority 成分，非限制轨道通过自旋上下独立优化提供额外灵活性。

Conclusion: BTS 配合非限制轨道优化可为配对型拟设提供描述强排斥哈伯德模型单占据构型的机制，是紧凑且具竞争力的关联波函数方案。

Abstract: Binary tree states have been introduced as compact correlated pair wave functions that improve upon the antisymmetrized geminal power by adding variational flexibility. Here, we extend the binary tree state to the fermionic half-filled Hubbard model using restricted and unrestricted orbitals. Although the ansatz is seniority zero in its optimized pairing basis, its expansion in the original Hubbard site basis can contain nonzero site- seniority character, while unrestricted orbital optimization provides additional flexibility through independent spin-up and spin-down orbitals. This provides a mechanism for a paired ansatz to describe the singly occupied configurations that dominate the repulsive Hubbard model at large U/t. We find that unrestricted BTS closely approaches optimized DOCI in the repulsive regime, substantially improves upon AGP, and reproduces DOCI density correlations more accurately than pCCD

</details>


### [16] [Nonlinear Canonical Transformations for Studying Spin-1/2 Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30403)
*Swarnamoy Ghosh,Thomas M. Henderson,Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种非线性“自旋-费米子-自旋（spin-fermion-spin, SFS）”变换，将强关联自旋哈密顿量映射为在平均场层次更易描述的新自旋哈密顿量，并识别出对应的Clifford线路；在XXZ与J1-J2模型上，变换后自旋平均场与基于自旋-BCS的耦合簇（coupled-cluster）计算精度均较未变换方法提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫自旋晶格等强关联量子系统基态具强多参考（multireference）特征并伴随对称性破缺，常规耦合簇方法难以胜任；多参考与对称性投影耦合簇尚非黑箱方法，需针对体系专门开发。同时，基于自旋-BCS的耦合簇在强关联区或需很高激发阶数才能还原精确基态物理，故需更好的算符表示作为预处理手段。

Method: 引入将强关联自旋哈密顿量映射为新自旋哈密顿量的非线性SFS变换，使新表示下的基态在平均场层次即可较准确描述；针对一个代表性SFS变换，构造紧凑Clifford线路（Clifford circuit），将变换后的直积态映射为原表示下由线路修饰、一般纠缠的态；随后以破缺对称性平均场理论（broken-symmetry mean-field theory）及平均场后方法处理变换后的哈密顿量，并在XXZ与J1-J2模型上做基准测试。

Result: 在XXZ与J1-J2哈密顿量基准上，变换表示下的自旋平均场较常规自旋-BCS平均场降低了能量误差；对一维XXZ模型，基于自旋-BCS的耦合簇计算相较未变换方法精度显著提升。二维情形下，变换后平均场解的精度依赖于格点排序与边界条件，原因是非局域弦（nonlocal strings）可能出现。

Conclusion: 算符表示的精确变换可作为多体预条件器（many-body preconditioner），用于强关联自旋体系的平均场与低秩耦合簇近似，从而改善其在强关联区的表现。

Abstract: Strongly correlated quantum systems, including frustrated spin lattices, remain challenging for conventional coupled-cluster methods because their ground states often exhibit strong multireference character and symmetry breaking. Although multireference and symmetry-projected coupled-cluster theories are promising, they are not yet black-box methods and require system-specific development. The XXZ and $J_1$-$J_2$ spin models provide demanding benchmarks. Spin-BCS-based coupled-cluster methods may require high excitation ranks to recover the exact ground-state physics in strongly correlated regimes. We introduce a nonlinear spin-fermion-spin (SFS) transformation designed to map a strongly correlated spin Hamiltonian into a new spin Hamiltonian whose ground state is more accurately described at the mean-field level. For a representative SFS transformation, we identify a compact Clifford circuit that maps transformed product states to circuit-dressed, generally entangled states in the original representation. The transformed Hamiltonian can then be treated using broken-symmetry mean-field theory and post-mean-field methods. Benchmarks for the XXZ and $J_1$-$J_2$ Hamiltonians show that spin mean-field theory in the transformed representation reduces the energy error relative to conventional spin-BCS mean field. The spin-BCS-based coupled-cluster calculations for the one-dimensional XXZ model provide substantially improved accuracy relative to the corresponding untransformed methods. In 2D, the accuracy of the transformed mean-field solutions depends on the site ordering and boundary conditions, as nonlocal strings can appear. These results show that exact transformations of operator representations can serve as many-body preconditioners for mean-field and low-rank coupled-cluster approximations to strongly correlated spin systems.

</details>


### [17] [Anisotropic thermoelectric transport in the Sr2RuO4-Ru eutectic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30547)
*Takeru Ito,Shogo Yoshida,Hiroshi Yaguchi,Ryuji Okazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究测量了Sr2RuO4-Ru共晶体系正常态的面内热电势（thermopower），发现其相对Ru片层取向存在显著各向异性；结合电子结构输运计算与有限元应变建模，表明该各向异性主要可由Sr2RuO4/Ru热膨胀失配引起的单轴压缩对费米面（Fermi surface）的修饰所解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sr2RuO4-Ru共晶体系因Ru金属包裹体嵌入Sr2RuO4基体而呈现约3 K的增强超导起始温度（3-K相）。为理解该体系正常态输运性质及其与界面效应的关联，论文考察其面内热电势响应及各向异性来源。

Result: 热电势在平行与垂直于Ru片层（Ru lamellae）的热梯度下表现出明显各向异性。计算表明Sr2RuO4的单轴压缩（uniaxial compression）通过改变费米面产生可观的热电势各向异性；引入Ru片层周围理想化的空间非均匀应变场后，所观测各向异性的符号与量级均较无应变两组分模型得到更贴近的再现。

Conclusion: 结果与界面附近应变诱导的Sr2RuO4电子结构变化有实质性贡献相一致，但界面相混合（interfacial phase mixing）与三维输运连通性（three-dimensional transport connectivity）的贡献尚不能排除。

Abstract: The Sr2RuO4-Ru eutectic system, in which metallic Ru inclusions are embedded in a Sr2RuO4 matrix, exhibits an enhanced superconducting onset temperature of about 3 K, known as the 3-K phase. Here we investigate the in-plane thermoelectric response of this eutectic system in the normal state and find a pronounced anisotropy in the thermopower for thermal gradients applied parallel and perpendicular to the Ru lamellae. To clarify its origin, we combine thermopower measurements with electronic-structure-based transport calculations and finite-element modeling of the local strain distribution generated by the thermal-expansion mismatch between Sr2RuO4 and Ru. The calculations show that uniaxial compression of Sr2RuO4 produces a sizable thermopower anisotropy through modification of the Fermi surfaces. When an idealized spatially inhomogeneous strain field around the Ru lamellae is included, the observed sign and qualitative magnitude of the anisotropy are reproduced more closely than in a simple unstrained two-component model. These results are consistent with a substantial contribution from strain-induced changes in the Sr2RuO4 electronic structure near the interfaces, although contributions from interfacial phase mixing and three-dimensional transport connectivity cannot be excluded.

</details>


### [18] [Sign-problem-free Bosonic Auxiliary Field Quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31541)
*Yin-Kai Yu,Zhong-Chao Wei,Zi-Xiang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出玻色子辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（BAFQMC）方法，并证明时间反演对称性与反射正定性各自构成无符号问题的充分条件，使该方法在阻挫晶格上仍可精确模拟相互作用玻色子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号问题（sign problem）严重阻碍数值精确的量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）模拟，限制了对量子多体物理的理解；几何阻挫（geometrical frustration）使相互作用玻色子面临的该障碍尤为突出。

Method: 发展玻色子辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（bosonic auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo, BAFQMC）方法，并严格证明时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）与反射正定性（reflection positivity）均为无符号问题模拟的一般充分条件。

Result: 在任一判据下，BAFQMC 即使在传统世界线（world-line）与随机级数展开（stochastic-series-expansion）方法失效的阻挫晶格上也能规避符号问题；在三角晶格双味玻色-哈伯德模型（two-flavor Bose-Hubbard model）上与有限温精确对角化（exact diagonalization）定量吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了解决 BAFQMC 中符号问题的统一原理，显著拓展了无偏、数值精确模拟方法在阻挫玻色物质研究中的适用范围。

Abstract: The sign problem poses a fundamental barrier to numerically exact quantum Monte Carlo simulations, severely hindering our understanding of quantum many-body physics across disciplines ranging from condensed matter to particle and nuclear physics. Geometrical frustration makes this barrier especially acute for interacting bosons. Here, we develop a bosonic auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (BAFQMC) method and rigorously prove that time-reversal symmetry and reflection positivity each provide a general sufficient condition for sign-problem-free simulations. Crucially, under either criterion, BAFQMC evades the sign problem even on frustrated lattices where conventional world-line and stochastic-series-expansion methods fail. Benchmarking our approach on a two-flavor Bose-Hubbard model on the triangular lattice yields excellent quantitative agreement with finite-temperature exact diagonalization. By establishing unifying principles to solve the sign problem in BAFQMC, our work substantially expands the reach of unbiased, numerically exact simulations for frustrated bosonic matter.

</details>


### [19] [String Order in Quench Dynamics as a Probe of Quantum Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30647)
*H. P. Zhang,Z. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过引入全局弦序参量（string order parameter）与量子淬火（quench）动力学，揭示各向异性横场 XY 链（anisotropic XY chain in a transverse field）存在隐藏的非局域序（nonlocal order），并将可测自旋布居（spin populations）与平衡及动力学拓扑结构相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子相变（quantum phase transitions）框架下的序参量多为局域型，难以刻画该典型模型中的隐藏序；同时平衡态与淬火后动力学响应之间的对应关系尚待厘清，故需引入非局域序参量加以统一描述。

Method: 定义整条链上局域投影自旋算符（local projected spin operators）之积构成的弦算符（string operator）；以基态及其场导数、以及从完全极化初态（fully polarized initial state）淬火后每格点弦算符期望值对数的长时间平均作为非平衡序参量，并解析推导其在量子临界线（quantum critical lines）处的非解析行为。

Result: 基态情形下，序参量的场导数在 Ising 临界线上呈奇异性；淬火后，稳态响应同样记录场边界（field boundaries）与各向异性转变（anisotropy transition），并给出临界线处非解析行为的解析结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明该常规量子相变范式中存在隐藏的非局域序，且可测自旋布居可同时反映平衡态与动力学拓扑结构，为用弦序参量刻画相变提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study the anisotropic XY chain in a transverse field by introducing a global string order parameter and examining its equilibrium behavior and its quench dynamics from a fully polarized initial state. The corresponding string operator is defined as the product of local projected spin operators over the entire chain. The nonequilibrium order parameter is the long-time average of the logarithm of the string-operator expectation value per site after the quench.For the ground state, its field derivative is singular on the Ising critical lines; after the quench, the steady-state response also records the field boundaries and the anisotropy transition. We derive analytically the nonanalytic behavior of the order parameter at the quantum critical lines. Our results reveal a hidden nonlocal order underlying this paradigmatic model of conventional quantum phase transitions. They also connect measurable spin populations with equilibrium and dynamical topological structure.

</details>


### [20] [Impact of localized $4f$ magnetism on Berry curvature and the intrinsic anomalous Hall effect in CeRu$_2$Si$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30684)
*Takehiro Kobayashi,Shunsuke Hirai,Ibuki Terada,Michi-To Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以第一性原理 LDA+U 结合 Wannier 插值方法研究 CeRu₂Si₂，发现即使 Ce-4f 态局域且不直接贡献费米面，本征反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity, AHC）仍为有限值，且其大小依赖于所占据的具体 Ce-4f 态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect, AHE）源于贝里曲率（Berry curvature），已在包括 Ce 基化合物在内的 f 电子体系中广泛研究，但局域 f 电子磁性如何影响本征 AHE 仍不清楚。

Method: 采用第一性原理 LDA+U 计算结合 Wannier 插值方法（Wannier interpolation），针对多个不同的 Ce-4f 占据态计算 CeRu₂Si₂ 的本征反常霍尔电导。

Result: 本征 AHC 在 Ce-4f 态局域、不直接参与费米面的情况下依然有限，其数值随占据的 Ce-4f 态不同而变化；机理为局域 4f 电子磁性在导带中引入交换劈裂（exchange splitting），在费米面附近特定区域产生贝里曲率。

Conclusion: 该机制使源于非磁性轨道、构成费米面的导带也能对本征 AHE 产生贡献，为在 f 电子材料中实现大本征反常霍尔效应提供了可能途径。

Abstract: The intrinsic anomalous Hall effect (AHE), originating from Berry curvature, has been extensively studied in $f$-electron systems, including Ce-based compounds. However, how the magnetism of localized $f$ electrons affect the intrinsic AHE remains unclear. Here, we investigate this issue in CeRu$_2$Si$_2$ by calculating the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) for several occupied Ce-$4f$ states using first-principles LDA+$U$ calculations combined with Wannier interpolation method. We find that the intrinsic AHC remains finite even when the Ce-$4f$ states are localized and do not contribute directly to the Fermi surface, and that its magnitude depends on which Ce-$4f$ state is occupied. We show that localized $4f$-electron magnetism induces exchange splitting in the conduction bands, generating Berry curvature in specific regions near the Fermi surface. This mechanism enables conduction bands that are derived from nonmagnetic orbitals and form the Fermi surface to contribute to the intrinsic AHE, suggesting a route to realizing large intrinsic AHE in $f$-electron materials.

</details>


### [21] [Fit-error corrected auxiliary master equation approach for low-temperature nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30846)
*Sebastian Alois Fuchs,Enrico Arrigoni,Tommaso Maria Mazzocchi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种拟合误差修正方案，用以消除辅助主方程方法（AMEA）中因拟合辅助杂化函数（auxiliary hybridization function）而导致的温度高估（虚假加热）问题，并在自旋极化杂质模型与Hubbard层中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMEA作为非微扰杂质求解器（impurity solver），其精度受限于对物理杂化函数的拟合质量：费米边（Fermi edge）在低温下变锐，拟合易高估温度，弱库耦合时尤为明显。

Method: 借助适用于任意杂化函数的近似求解器，估计物理杂化与辅助杂化各自自能（self-energy）之比，据此把AMEA的自能从辅助情形延拓到物理情形；以推广至自旋极化解的非平衡Kajueter-Kotliar迭代微扰理论（iterated perturbation theory, IPT）作为近似求解器进行基准测试。

Result: 应用于自旋极化浴的Anderson杂质模型（Anderson impurity model）及弱耦合于两个金属库的Hubbard层DMFT计算时，修正大幅消除了来自AMEA拟合与库热化不足的虚假加热，同时保留了对Mott绝缘体（Mott insulator）的正确描述。

Conclusion: 该修正方案在拟合或近似求解器任一精确时即为精确，可与NCA等其他近似求解器配合使用，从而在保持AMEA非微扰优势的同时提升其定量可靠性。

Abstract: The auxiliary master equation approach (AMEA) is a nonperturbative impurity solver for steady-state nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). It replaces the physical hybridization function by that of a small auxiliary open quantum system, whose parameters are fitted to reproduce it, and then solves the resulting many-body problem essentially exactly. AMEA's accuracy is thus limited by the quality of this fit: since the fastest-varying part of the hybridization is set by the distribution function, whose Fermi edges sharpen at low temperature, the fit tends to overestimate the temperature, especially for weak reservoir coupling. For the auxiliary hybridization itself, however, AMEA's self-energy is essentially exact. We correct for the fit error by estimating, via an approximate solver applicable to arbitrary hybridization functions, the ratio between the self-energies of the physical and auxiliary hybridizations, and using it to extend AMEA's self-energy from the auxiliary to the physical case. This fit-error correction becomes exact whenever either the fit or the approximate solver is exact. We benchmark the scheme using the nonequilibrium Kajueter-Kotliar iterated perturbation theory (IPT), generalized here to spin-polarized solutions, as the approximate solver; other approximate solvers, such as the noncrossing approximation, can be used as well. Applied to an Anderson impurity model with a spin-polarized bath, and within DMFT to a Hubbard layer weakly coupled to two metallic reservoirs, the correction largely removes the spurious heating from the AMEA fit and from insufficient thermalization by the reservoirs, while preserving the correct description of the Mott insulator, which IPT alone cannot fully recover.

</details>


### [22] [Interaction-enhanced antiferromagnetic correlations in a Kondo ladder with a gapped band structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31142)
*Tatsuya Kaneko,Ritsuki Hirabayashi,Yutaka Akagi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）研究两腿 Kondo-Hubbard 梯子，发现在无费米面（Fermi surface）的窄间接带隙区间内，适度的在位库仑相互作用（on-site Coulomb interaction）可增强局域自旋间的反铁磁关联（antiferromagnetic correlations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注的参数区间中，非相互作用电子体系具有窄间接带隙、不存在费米面，因此基于费米面的传统 RKKY（Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida）机制无法直接适用，局域自旋间磁关联随带隙增大而减弱，需要考察电子-电子相互作用在此情形下的作用。

Method: 使用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法对两腿 Kondo-Hubbard 梯子（two-leg Kondo-Hubbard ladder）的磁关联进行计算，并借助边缘钉扎场（pinning field）诱导的交错磁化（staggered magnetization）作为辅助观测量，同时考察铁磁与反铁磁 Kondo 耦合（Kondo coupling）两种情形。

Result: 计算结果显示，适度的在位库仑相互作用增强了局域自旋间的反铁磁关联；该增强在边缘钉扎场诱导的交错磁化中同样出现，且在铁磁与反铁磁 Kondo 耦合下均成立。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使底层非相互作用电子结构存在带隙，电子-电子相互作用仍可增强局域自旋间的磁关联，提示窄带隙 Kondo 晶格（Kondo-lattice）体系中存在一条由相互作用驱动的磁失稳（magnetic instability）路径。

Abstract: We investigate magnetic correlations in the two-leg Kondo-Hubbard ladder using the density-matrix renormalization group method. We focus on a parameter regime in which the noninteracting electronic system has a narrow indirect band gap. In this regime, there is no Fermi surface, and the conventional Fermi-surface-based Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida picture is not directly applicable; magnetic correlations between localized spins become weaker as the noninteracting band gap increases. We find that a moderate on-site Coulomb interaction enhances the antiferromagnetic correlations between localized spins. This enhancement, which is also seen in the staggered magnetization induced by a pinning field at the edges, occurs for both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Kondo couplings. Our results show that electron-electron interactions can enhance magnetic correlations between localized spins even when the underlying noninteracting electronic structure is gapped, suggesting an interaction-driven route to magnetic instabilities in narrow-gap Kondo-lattice systems.

</details>


### [23] [Graph-Recurrent Learning of Emergent Collective Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31350)
*Ali Rayat,Acacia Lee,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出图循环神经网络（GRNN）框架，无需微观运动方程即可从轨迹中学习关联电子系统的集体有序演化，并在半经典 Holstein 模型的电荷密度波（CDW）畴粗化动力学中成功再现时空关联与动力学标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体有序的可观测织构（texture）演化往往不具备封闭、无记忆（memoryless）的描述，且微观自由度未被解析；因此需要仅从轨迹数据出发、可扩展地刻画含空间相互作用与时间记忆的动力学表示。

Method: 将门控循环记忆（gated recurrent memory）与图消息传递（graph message passing）相结合：局部隐状态（hidden state）编码超越瞬时可观测量的信息，空间耦合使该信息在格点间传播；可观测量场与隐场通过共享的局部规则联合演化。

Result: 在半经典 Holstein 模型的电荷密度波（charge-density-wave）序涌现动力学上验证，成功复现了畴粗化（domain coarsening）过程中的时间与空间关联以及动力学标度（dynamical scaling）。

Conclusion: 直接学习有限时间演化（finite-time evolution）的 GRNN 为微观自由度未解析的集体动力学提供了一条高效、数据驱动的描述途径。

Abstract: Collective ordering in correlated electron systems emerges from microscopic dynamics, yet the evolution of observable textures need not admit a closed, memoryless description. We introduce a graph-recurrent neural network (GRNN) framework that learns such emergent dynamics directly from trajectories without specifying microscopic equations of motion. The framework integrates gated recurrent memory with graph message passing: local hidden states encode information beyond the instantaneous observables, while spatial coupling allows this information to propagate between sites. The observable and hidden fields evolve jointly through a shared local rule, providing a scalable representation of dynamics with spatial interactions and temporal memory. We demonstrate this framework on the emergent dynamics of charge-density-wave order in the semiclassical Holstein model, successfully reproducing temporal and spatial correlations and dynamical scaling during domain coarsening. By learning finite-time evolution directly, the GRNN offers a route to efficient, data-driven descriptions of collective dynamics with unresolved microscopic degrees of freedom.

</details>

### [24] [Quantum Phases and Structural Transitions of Bosons in Bubble-Shaped Confinements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30381)
*Fabio Cinti,Gabriele Costa,Giuseppe Pellicane,Santi Prestipino,Matteo Ciardi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用连续空间路径积分蒙特卡罗（Path Integral Monte Carlo）结合虫算法（worm algorithm）研究球形气泡阱中大粒子数软核玻色子（soft-core bosons），发现随粒子数增加出现分层转变并在多层区仍保持约10%的超流分数（superfluid fraction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨粒子数如何驱动曲面流形上的分层形成，并明确大粒子数下软核玻色子体系是否仍维持超流性，为气泡阱玻色子实验提供依据。

Method: 在连续空间中对球形气泡阱内软核玻色子（N ≤ 1800）进行路径积分蒙特卡罗（Path Integral Monte Carlo）模拟，采用虫算法（worm algorithm）抽样，并分析置换循环（permutation cycles）的分布以提取超流分数。

Result: 随N增大出现一系列分层转变（layering transitions）：在已有的双层超固（bilayer supersolid）之外，于N ≈ 1800时出现第三层同心团簇层；在此多层区域中超流分数仍为有限值，约 f_s ≈ 10%。

Conclusion: 粒子数是驱动曲面流形上分层形成的关键因素，且多层结构并不破坏超流性；结果为未来气泡阱玻色子实验提供了具体指导。

Abstract: We investigate the structural properties and superfluid behavior of soft-core bosons confined in a spherical bubble trap at large particle numbers ($N \le 1800$). Using Path Integral Monte Carlo simulations with worm algorithm sampling in continuous space, we show that increasing $N$ drives a sequence of layering transitions: beyond the established bilayer supersolid, a third concentric layer of clusters emerges at $N \approx 1800$. By analyzing the distribution of permutation cycles distribution, we demonstrate that a finite superfluid fraction ($f_s \approx 10\%$) persists even in this multi-layer regime. These findings clarify the role of particle number in driving layer formation on curved manifolds, offering a concrete guide for future experiments with bubble-trapped bosons.

</details>


### [25] [Realization of a Superleak in a Quantum Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31185)
*Wan-Ling Yang,Shao-Bo Liu,Xiang Luo,Chao Yang,Hai-Lin Li,Yu-Zhou Yang,Yong-Kui Wang,Bing Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 基于光学超晶格（optical superlattice）实现超漏（superleak），利用动力学选择性（dynamical selectivity）使超流组分经相干约瑟夫森输运（Josephson transport）通过，而正常组分响应缓慢，流量对比度达500(49)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷量子气体（ultracold quantum gases）为研究超流性提供了微观可控平台，但能够选择性输运超流、抑制正常流动的超漏长期未能实现。

Method: 采用光学超晶格构建超漏结构，借助超流组分的相干约瑟夫森输运与正常组分热力学受限的慢响应之间的时间尺度差异，实现动力学选择性。

Result: 获得约500（误差49）的流量对比度；据此实现正常组分的重复过滤与超流的动力学纯化（dynamical purification），并维持相干受驱振荡达100个循环。

Conclusion: 研究确立了可控的原子超漏，为量子流体中输运与热力学的操控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Ultracold quantum gases offer a controllable and microscopically accessible platform for understanding and engineering superfluidity. Yet, realizing a superleak that selectively transports the superfluid while suppressing normal flow has remained elusive. Here, we harness an optical superlattice to realize such a superleak through dynamical selectivity. The superfluid undergoes coherent Josephson transport, whereas the normal component exhibits slow, thermodynamically limited response, resulting in a flow-rate contrast of 500(49). Exploiting this contrast, we demonstrate repeated filtering of the normal component and dynamical purification of the superfluid, while sustaining coherent driven oscillations for up to 100 cycles. Our results establish a controllable atomic superleak and open possibilities for manipulating transport and thermodynamics in quantum fluids.

</details>


### [26] [Breathing and quadrupole modes in squeezed Bose-Bose liquid drops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31581)
*Marko Korda,Leandra Vranješ Markić,Jordi Boronat*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作利用基于第一性原理量子蒙特卡罗（quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）构建的密度泛函，研究一维谐振约束下二元钾-39（$^{39}$K）超稀薄液滴（ultradilute droplets）的最低激发模式及其从三维到二维的跨维演化，并对比扩展Gross-Pitaevskii理论的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维谐波约束可降低维持自束缚态（self-bound state）所需的临界粒子数，而约束下液滴的低能激发模式及其维度交叉（crossover）行为尚需系统刻画。

Method: 采用由第一性原理QMC计算构建、并包含有限程相互作用（finite range interactions）效应的密度泛函（density functional），计算约束液滴的最低激发模式，考察其随挤压强度从三维向二维的演化，并与扩展Gross-Pitaevskii理论结果对照。

Result: 弱挤压下，球对称性破缺导致呼吸模式（breathing mode）与四极模式（quadrupole mode）耦合；挤压增强时，对应三维呼吸模式的高频模式变得不稳定。在接近实验强度的挤压条件下，分析了多种相互作用下激发模式对液滴尺寸的依赖，并显示维持稳定振荡所需粒子数减少；QMC泛函与扩展Gross-Pitaevskii理论的差异较此前球形液滴情形有所增大。

Conclusion: 谐波约束下的二元超稀薄液滴呈现由维度交叉控制的模式耦合与失稳行为，且有限程效应对激发谱的影响在非球形几何中更为显著，需超越扩展Gross-Pitaevskii描述。

Abstract: We investigate the lowest excitation modes of binary $^{39}$K ultradilute droplets confined harmonically in one direction, which reduces the critical number of particles needed to sustain a self-bound state. Using the density functional built from first-principles quantum Monte Carlo calculations (QMC), which incorporates the effects of finite range interactions, we study the crossover of the oscillation modes from three to two dimensions. For weak squeezing, we observe the coupling between breathing and quadrupole modes, resulting from the breaking of spherical symmetry. Increasing the squeezing, the higher-frequency mode corresponding to three-dimensional breathing mode becomes unstable. For squeezings stronger and comparable to experiments, we analyze the dependence of the excitation modes on the droplet size for several interactions and show the reduction of the number of particles needed to sustain stable oscillations. The differences between the results obtained with the QMC functional and the extended Gross-Pitaevskii theory are increased with respect to the case of previously studied spherical droplets.

</details>

### [27] [Vacancy-driven topological transition of a Chern insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30241)
*Peize Ding,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文以空位束缚的带内环态为微观自由度，构建能量依赖杂化哈密顿量，阐明强无序如何破坏陈绝缘体的陈相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）中的强无序相变（strong-disorder transition）提供微观层面的描述，弥补以往对强无序破坏拓扑相机制缺乏微观图像的问题。

Method: 采用格林函数（Green's function）形式构建描述环态杂化的能量依赖哈密顿量；对公度周期空位晶格分析零能空位核（vacancy kernel）的狄拉克反演（Dirac inversion）；对弱位置无序使用自洽玻恩近似（self-consistent Born approximation）与数值无序平均；对独立分布空位采用转移矩阵（transfer-matrix）与多重分形标度（multifractal scaling）分析；并用局域陈标记（local Chern marker）与电流纹理（current textures）追踪实空间相变。

Result: 在公度周期空位晶格中，零能空位核的狄拉克反演解释了全系统占据态陈数的变化；局域陈标记与电流纹理解析了相变在实空间中的演化。

Conclusion: 非微扰环态（nonperturbative ring states）作为微观自由度，构成强无序破坏陈相的关键通道。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic description of the strong-disorder transition in a Chern insulator using the in-gap ring states bound to individual vacancies. Using Green's function formalism, we construct an energy-dependent Hamiltonian describing their hybridization. In a commensurate periodic vacancy lattice, a Dirac inversion of the zero-energy vacancy kernel accounts for the change in the occupied-state Chern number of the full system. We treat weak positional disorder using the self-consistent Born approximation and numerical disorder averaging, and study independently distributed vacancies using transfer-matrix and multifractal scaling. The local Chern marker and current textures track the transition in real space. Together, these results highlight the role that nonperturbative ring states have as microscopic degrees of freedom through which strong disorder destroys the Chern phase.

</details>


### [28] [Charge-monitored Luttinger liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30271)
*Victor Gurarie,Leo Radzihovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文研究无粒子数守恒的相互作用费米型 Luttinger 液体（Luttinger liquids）在弱连续粒子密度测量下的相图，指出测量诱导的面积极限相（area-law phase）与隧穿诱导的体积极限相（volume-law phase）之间的竞争决定体系相态，且相边界由 Luttinger 参数对测量速率的普适依赖关系给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于刻画与超导体近邻等无粒子数守恒的 Luttinger 液体在弱连续测量下会呈现何种相，并区分此类玻色化（bosonization）框架内的普适行为与一般高激发一维体系中的非普适行为。

Method: 方法上采用玻色化（bosonization）方法处理相互作用的费米型 Luttinger 液体，在弱连续测量粒子密度的情形下分析各相及其相互竞争。

Result: 结果显示体系相态由两类效应竞争控制：测量倾向于面积极限相（area-law phase），而粒子流入、流出 Luttinger 液体的隧穿倾向于体积极限相（volume-law phase）；该体积极限相始终为电荷模糊（charge fuzzy）的，且两相边界表现为 Luttinger 参数对测量速率的普适依赖关系。

Conclusion: 结论指出该普适相边界可与更一般的高激发一维体系中不再由 Luttinger 液体框架描述、且相边界非普适的行为形成对照。

Abstract: We discuss phases of interacting fermionic Luttinger liquids without particle number conservation, such as those in proximity to a superconductor, subjected to weak continuous measurements of their particle density, in the regime where the bosonization approach is applicable. We show that the phases of these systems are controlled by the competition between measurements, which favor the area-law phase and particle tunneling in and out of the Luttinger liquid, which favors the volume-law phase. The volume-law phase in this system is always charge fuzzy. We show that within this framework, the phase boundary between the two phases is given by a universal dependence of the Luttinger parameter on the measurement rate. This behavior can be contrasted with the non-universal phase boundaries in more generic highly excited one dimensional systems whose behavior is no longer captured by the Luttinger liquid framework.

</details>


### [29] [Non-adiabatic Optimisation via Localisation-Delocalisation Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30301)
*Ryan Smith,Sabrina Yue Wang,Filippo Maria Gambetta,Raul A. Santos*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用连续反向量子退火（continuous reverse quantum annealing）跨越多体局域化转变（many-body localisation transition），并以Landau-Zener交叉密度与有效Fokker-Planck方程解释其加速机制，进而给出数字化算法FRIO，在自旋玻璃（spin-glass）问题上优于QAOA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优化问题常可映射为自旋玻璃基态近似问题，而自旋玻璃又是研究多体局域化的范例模型；因此作者希望借用量子统计力学（quantum statistical mechanics）的直觉来指导新型量子优化算法的构造。

Method: 将淬火（quench）过程中Landau-Zener交叉的密度作为分析对象，用有效Fokker-Planck方程对算法成功概率给出解释；对连续动力学进行Trotter化并给出确定含时Hamiltonian参数的定量策略，形成数字化算法FRIO，并与QAOA在SK模型等自旋玻璃问题上进行数值比较。

Result: 该解释表明，对于能量密度非零的参考态，所需退火时间随系统尺寸N的标度由O(poly(N))改善至O(1)；Trotter化所需步数随系统规模增长仍保持适度，适合近期器件；数值结果显示FRIO几乎总是优于使用预优化角度的常规QAOA。

Conclusion: 连续反向量子退火跨越多体局域化转变可被显著加速，且其数字化实现FRIO在参数可由热化动力学（thermalizing dynamics）高效确定时，对自旋玻璃基态求解具有相对QAOA的性能优势。

Abstract: The promise of efficiently solving classical optimisation problems on quantum computers is tantalizing. As many such problems can be mapped to approximating the ground state of spin-glasses, paradigmatic models for studying many-body localisation, it is natural to ask if some of the intuition coming from quantum statistical mechanics can help guide the construction of novel quantum optimisation algorithms. In this work, we discuss how continuous reverse quantum annealing crossing a many-body localisation transition can be accelerated by considering the density of Landau-Zener crossings as the system is quenched. We provide an interpretation of this behaviour in terms of an effective Fokker-Planck equation for the success probability of the algorithm. This interpretation suggests an improvement in the required annealing time as a function of the system size $N$, which goes from $\mathcal{O}({\rm poly}(N))$ to $\mathcal{O}(1)$ for reference states with non-vanishing energy density. We show that the continuous dynamics can be Trotterised with a number of steps that remains modest as system size increases, making it appealing for near-term devices. For the resulting circuit implementation, we provide a quantitative strategy for determining the parameters of the time-dependent Hamiltonian. We compare the performance of our digitised algorithm, which we call FRIO, against that of the QAOA for finding the ground state of spin-glass problems, in particular the SK model. The numerical results suggest that with suitably selected circuit parameters which can be efficiently found using thermalizing dynamics, FRIO almost always outperforms the conventional QAOA ansatz with pre-optimized angles.

</details>


### [30] [Symmetry-Preserving Local Representation for Short-Range Interaction in Complex Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30726)
*Hao Cheng,Tong Yang,Hao Wu,Haokai Hong,Zhongrui Wang,Shu Ping Lau,Wanyu Lin,Kay Chen Tan,Ming Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出一种保持对称性的局域表征框架（symmetry-preserving local representation framework），通过边界钝化（boundary passivation）严格满足拓扑连续性与平移不变性，在高熵合金（high-entropy alloys）中自主揭示调控局域反应性的非线性配体效应，并准确捕捉覆盖度依赖的多体相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度无序材料中的短程相互作用（short-range interactions）建模受限于巨大的构型空间（configuration space），且截断的理论模型会破坏局域对称性（local symmetry）。

Method: 基于边界钝化（boundary passivation）构建保持对称性的局域表征框架，严格强制拓扑连续性（topological continuity）与平移不变性（translational invariance），无需显式周期扩展（explicit periodic expansion）。

Result: 在高熵合金（high-entropy alloys）应用中，该框架自主解析出调制局域反应性的非线性配体效应（nonlinear ligand effects），并准确捕捉覆盖度依赖的多体相互作用（coverage-dependent many-body interactions），同时将计算复杂度（computational complexity）降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该框架化解了局域化学环境（localized chemical environments）与扩展周期边界（extended periodic boundaries）之间的冲突，为解析复杂凝聚态系统（condensed matter systems）中的短程有序（short-range order）提供了物理上透明的范式。

Abstract: Modeling short-range interactions in highly disordered materials remains challenging due to the immense configuration space and the disruption of local symmetry in truncated theoretical models. Here, we propose a symmetry-preserving local representation framework that strictly enforces topological continuity and translational invariance via boundary passivation. Applied to high-entropy alloys, this approach autonomously unravels the nonlinear ligand effects modulating local reactivity and accurately captures coverage-dependent many-body interactions without explicit periodic expansion. By resolving the conflict between localized chemical environments and extended periodic boundaries, this framework not only reduces computational complexity by an order of magnitude but also provides a physically transparent paradigm for deciphering short-range order in complex condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [31] [Localization Contrast As a Fundamental Limit of Radiative Quantum Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30741)
*Arsineh Apelian,Tanay Tak,Xuance Jiang,Tsung-Yin Tsai,James S. Speck,Vojtech Vlcek*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究揭示无序（disorder）对价带与导带局域化程度的差异化影响（localization contrast）如何改变载流子复合通道：在InGaN随机合金中，局域化对比抑制辐射复合而极大增强俄歇-迈特纳复合（Auger-Meitner recombination）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序可使价带态与导带态产生不同程度的局域化，但这种局域化对比（localization contrast）是否具有可观测后果尚不清楚，尤其对相互竞争的载流子复合通道的影响缺乏微观认识。

Method: 以随机In_xGa_{1-x}N合金为模型体系，将In含量从x=13.5%增至22%，考察价带顶（valence-band maximum）与导带底（conduction-band minimum）态局域化程度的演化，并分析其对辐射复合与俄歇-迈特纳复合耦合强度的影响。

Result: 随In含量增加，价带顶态逐渐局域化而导带底态相对保持离域，此对比降低了辐射复合的带间光学动量矩阵元（optical momentum matrix element）；同时，原子尺度无序破坏原胞平移对称性，改变态的动量空间与相位结构，产生额外低-Q分量，使俄歇耦合相对纯净GaN增强超过五个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究为辐射复合与俄歇复合的相反趋势提供了微观解释，并将局域化对比确立为无序调控半导体光学与载流子-载流子相互作用的普适机制。

Abstract: Disorder can localize valence- and conduction-band states to different degrees, yet whether such localization contrast has observable consequences remains poorly understood. Here, we show that localization contrast strongly impacts competing carrier-recombination channels. We illustrate this behavior in random In$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$N alloys. Increasing the In content from $x=13.5\%$ to $x=22\%$ progressively localizes the valence-band maximum state while leaving the conduction-band minimum state comparatively delocalized. This contrasting localization decreases the interband optical momentum matrix element underlying radiative recombination. For Auger-Meitner recombination, however, localization alone does not determine the coupling; atomic-scale disorder also breaks primitive-cell translational symmetry and modifies the momentum-space and phase structure of the states. This generates additional low-$Q$ components and enhances the Auger coupling by more than five orders of magnitude relative to pristine GaN. Thus, we provide a microscopic explanation for the competing trends in radiative and Auger recombination. More broadly, our results establish localization contrast as a mechanism through which disorder can control optical and carrier-carrier interactions in semiconductors.

</details>

### [32] [Flexible Proposal Distributions for Gravitational-Wave Population Inference with Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30187)
*Simon M. C. Yeung,Ignacio Magaña Hernandez,Jolien Creighton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出以高斯混合模型（Gaussian mixture models, GMM）拟合事件后验与探测注入，并通过重要性采样（importance sampling）增加样本量，从而在固定事件后验样本的情况下降低引力波族群分析中蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）似然估计的方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波源族群分析依赖分层贝叶斯推断（hierarchical Bayesian inference）并需校正选择效应（selection effects），其似然通常由有限事件后验样本与模拟注入的蒙特卡洛积分估计。随着星表增大需更多样本控制方差，但事件样本受参数估计固定，且族群尖锐特征可能位于后验采样不足区域。

Method: 对事件后验拟合GMM，并采用两个提议分布进行重要性采样：事件后验的集合，以及该集合与一个基准族群（fiducial population）的混合；同时对探测到的注入以GMM表示，从而为事件积分与选择积分额外抽取样本。

Result: 在模拟星表中，基于GMM的估计量与后验样本估计量相比未检出额外偏差，其校准（calibration）在测试限制内相当；应用于GWTC-3并采用不同质量模型时，各估计量结果一致。

Conclusion: 该方法将数值采样预算与原始样本数解耦，但需显式检验密度拟合（density-fit）不确定性与近似误差；增加样本可降低蒙特卡洛方差，而有限训练集拟合密度带来的不确定性仍然存在。

Abstract: Population analyses of gravitational-wave (GW) sources use hierarchical Bayesian inference to constrain astrophysical models while accounting for selection effects. The likelihood is usually estimated by Monte Carlo integration over finite sets of event posterior samples and simulated injections. As catalogs grow, more samples are needed to control the Monte Carlo variance, but the available event samples are fixed by parameter estimation. Sharp population features can also lie in regions poorly sampled by the event posteriors. We fit Gaussian mixture models (GMMs) to the event posteriors and evaluate the likelihood by importance sampling with two proposals: an ensemble of event posteriors and a mixture of that ensemble with a fiducial population. Together with a GMM representation of detected injections, this allows additional samples to be drawn for both event and selection integrals. Increasing the sample count reduces Monte Carlo variance but leaves uncertainty from fitting the densities to finite training sets. In simulated catalogs, the GMM-based estimators show no detectable additional bias relative to the posterior-sample estimator; their calibration is comparable within the limitations of the tests. An application to GWTC-3 with a different mass model gives consistent results across the estimators. The method separates the numerical sampling budget from the original sample count, while requiring explicit checks of density-fit uncertainty and approximation error.

</details>


### [33] [Partial geometrization of dark matter in metric $f(R)$ gravity: Exact reconstruction and growth constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30201)
*Davide Campanella Galanti,Oliver F. Piattella*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在要求膨胀历史与ΛCDM完全一致的前提下，稳定度规f(R)理论的几何项最多只能解释可忽略份额的暗物质，因为稳定性、局域引力检验与结构增长观测共同将几何化份额限制在1-ξ≲10⁻³乃至10⁻¹⁰量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文追问：稳定度规f(R)理论的几何自由度能够承担多大比例的暗物质，同时保持膨胀历史严格等于ΛCDM模型。该问题将"几何暗物质"方案置于观测约束下检验。

Method: 设暗物质中仅份额ξ以尘埃形式成团，其余与Λ均由f(R)几何提供；背景重构化为关于F≡f_R的线性方程，其解为F-1=(1-ξ)Θ(a)且Θ与ξ无关。作者在物质+Λ时代与辐射+物质时代求得闭式解（一个初等特解加两个超几何模），并分析稳定性、高曲率行为及增长因子、σ₈、DESI DR1与SDSS DR16增长率和Cassini约束。

Result: 稳定性要求F随时间单调下降，故F不能在早晚时期均取广义相对论值F=1，有效引力耦合G/F必然跑动：从辐射时代到de Sitter吸引子，F至少变化0.837(1-ξ)。除一个例外，所有稳定重构在高曲率下均有F-1∝R^{1/3}，被太阳等致密环境的局域引力检验排除；唯一幸存者在早期F→1并具有最小跑动。将k=0.1 h Mpc⁻¹处线性增长因子限制在ΛCDM值10%以内要求1-ξ≲10⁻³，σ₈限制在3%以内要求1-ξ≲5×10⁻⁵；DESI DR1与SDSS DR16增长率数据给出1-ξ≲10⁻³–10⁻²，Cassini给出1-ξ≲10⁻¹⁰。

Conclusion: 平滑演化的度规f(R)背景至多只能将极小份额的暗物质几何化，因而该方案无法作为暗物质的显著替代解释；唯一可行重构仍受结构增长与局域引力检验的严格压制。

Abstract: We ask what fraction of dark matter can be accounted for by the geometry of a stable metric $f(R)$ theory, requiring the expansion history to be exactly equal to that of the $Λ$CDM model. Only a fraction $ξ$ of dark matter clusters as dust, whereas $f(R)$ supplies the rest, as well as $Λ$. We show that the background reconstruction reduces to a linear equation for $F\equiv f_R$, whose solution is $F-1=(1-ξ)\,Θ(a)$, with $Θ$ independent of $ξ$: the departure from general relativity is exactly proportional to the geometrized fraction. We solve this equation in closed form in the matter$+Λ$ and radiation$+$matter eras: in both, the solution is an elementary particular solution plus two hypergeometric modes. Stability requires $F$ to decrease monotonically in time, so $F$ cannot take its general-relativity value $F=1$ both at early and at late times. The effective gravitational coupling $G/F$ therefore necessarily runs: between the radiation era and the de Sitter attractor, $F$ changes by at least $0.837\,(1-ξ)$. In all stable reconstructions but one, $F-1$ grows as $R^{1/3}$ at high curvature, i.e., in dense environments such as the Sun, and local tests of gravity exclude them. The surviving member has $F\to1$ at early times: it recovers general relativity at high curvature and attains the minimal running of $F$. Keeping the linear growth factor at $k=0.1\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ within $10\%$ of its $Λ$CDM value requires $1-ξ\lesssim10^{-3}$, the weakest bound among all stable reconstructions; keeping $σ_8$ within $3\%$ requires $1-ξ\lesssim5\times10^{-5}$. Growth-rate data from DESI DR1 and SDSS DR16 give $1-ξ\lesssim10^{-3}$--$10^{-2}$, and the Cassini bound gives $1-ξ\lesssim10^{-10}$. A smoothly evolving metric $f(R)$ background can therefore geometrize only a negligible fraction of dark matter.

</details>


### [34] [Wormhole geometries and energy conditions in linear and non-linear $f(Q,L_m)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30224)
*Sara Rastgoo,Foad Parsaei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在扩展的对称远平行引力框架下研究静态球对称虫洞解，考察线性与非线性 $f(Q,L_m)$ 模型，发现线性模型可无需极端能量条件违背而支持物理上可实现的虫洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中可穿越虫洞需违背零能量条件（null energy condition, NEC），因此论文旨在检验扩展的对称远平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）能否缓解这一要求。

Method: 采用 $f(Q,L_m)=\alpha(-Q)+\beta L_m$ 与 $f(Q,L_m)=-Q+L_m^{\alpha}$ 两种形式（$Q$ 为非度规标量 non-metricity scalar，$L_m$ 为物质拉格朗日量 matter Lagrangian），对度规变分得到场方程，并在线性与渐近线性状态方程（equation of state）下求解虫洞构型。

Result: 线性模型可支持物理上现实的虫洞，且不需极端能量条件违背；在广义相对论中同时违背径向与横向 NEC 的时空解，在 $f(Q,L_m)$ 引力中仍可满足 NEC；非线性模型中得到了非奇异、渐近平直（asymptotically flat）的虫洞解。

Conclusion: 结果表明修正引力模型可与奇异时空的可观测天体物理特征相联系，为后续研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This study explores static and spherically symmetric wormhole configurations in an extended formulation of symmetric teleparallel gravity governed by $f(Q,L_m)=α(-Q)+βL_m $ and $f(Q,L_m)= -Q+L_m^α $ (where $Q$ is the non-metricity scalar, $L_m$ is the matter Lagrangian, and $α, β$ are constants). By varying the action with respect to the metric, we obtain the corresponding field equations. We conclude that the linear $f(Q,L_m)=α(-Q)+βL_m$ framework successfully supports physically realistic wormholes without requiring extreme energy condition violations. It has been demonstrated that spacetime solutions violating both the radial and lateral null energy conditions in general relativity can nevertheless satisfy the NEC in $f(Q, L_m)$ gravity. Within non-linear $f(Q,L_m)$ gravity, non-exotic asymptotically flat wormhole solutions are derived using linear and asymptotically linear equations of state. Our results open new avenues to link modified gravity models with the observable astrophysical traits of exotic spacetimes.

</details>


### [35] [Ricci-flat Seeds, Trombone Rescaling and Magnetizing Kerr Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30256)
*Liang Ma,H. Lu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一族新的 Ricci 平坦旋转度规（Ricci-flat rotating metrics），称为 Solution II，由对 genuine Kerr–Bertotti–Robinson（G-KBR）黑洞实施“去磁化（demagnetization）”得到；它与此前由 KBR 黑洞去磁化得到的 Solution I 共同构成两族以参数 B 标记的 Kerr 黑洞的 Ricci 平坦形变。

Abstract: We identify the general finite-$\ell$ neutral Alekseev--Astorino Schwarzschild--Bertotti--Robinson family with the subcritical branch of the independently obtained accelerating Schwarzschild--Bertotti--Robinson solution. The exact scale-free dictionary is \begin{equation*}
  B^2M^2=\frac{m^2}{b^2+\ell^2},\qquad
  AM=\frac{m\ell}{b^2+\ell^2},\qquad
  \frac{A}{B}=\frac{\ell}{\sqrt{b^2+\ell^2}}, \end{equation*} up to the north--south orientation convention. Thus finite real $\ell$ covers precisely the acceleration interval $|A|<|B|$: for $\ell\ne0$ the corresponding OP acceleration parameter is nonzero and the two axial openings are unequal, although no acceleration horizon is present. We establish the identification by canonical Weyl-rod matching and horizon-shape comparison, an independent conicity check, and an exact off-horizon reconstruction of the metric and Maxwell field. The centered limit $\ell=0$ is the nonaccelerating Schwarzschild--BR solution, whereas $|\ell|\to\infty$ approaches the critical boundary $|A/B|=1$ from below. The Astorino parameter $\ell$ therefore parametrizes subcritical acceleration rather than an additional independent neutral black-hole degree of freedom.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是检验带电 Simpson–Visser–AdS 时空（含电荷参数 $Q$、正则化尺度 $b$、AdS 半径 $l$）能否描述黑洞候选体的双峰 HF-QPO 观测，并厘清静态度规中额外参数的可测性。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we study circular geodesics and twin-peak high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (HF-QPOs) in the charged Simpson--Visser--anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime, which depends on a charge parameter $Q$, a regularization scale $b$ and an AdS radius $l$. Written in terms of the areal radius $R=\sqrt{r^{2}+b^{2}}$, the specific energy, angular momentum, Keplerian frequency and innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) of test particles coincide with those of the Reissner--Nordström--AdS black hole, so $b$ enters the epicyclic spectrum only through a factor $1-b^{2}/R^{2}$ in the radial frequency. We give this frequency and the ISCO condition in closed form. We then fit the relativistic-precession model to the HF-QPO pairs of GRO~J1655$-$40 and XTE~J1550$-$564, with their dynamical masses included as Gaussian priors. The Schwarzschild limit gives $χ^{2}=32.9$ for two degrees of freedom. One additional deformation removes this tension: $Q/M=0.814^{+0.059}_{-0.074}$ at fixed $l=100M$, or $l=40.6^{+6.2}_{-4.8}\,M$ at $Q=0$, both with $χ^{2}\simeq0.13$. These two solutions lie on a single degeneracy valley in the $(Q,l)$ plane, and, at $l=100M$, $b$ is left unconstrained up to $b\simeq3.9M$ (68\%), a limit set by the charge prior rather than by the data. A Kerr black hole with spin $a\simeq0.3$--$0.4$ reproduces the same pairs, so the inferred deformation most likely absorbs the spin neglected by the static metric. Our results therefore describe degeneracies among orbital-frequency deformations; they are not a measurement of an electric charge or of a cosmological constant.

</details>

We also investigate universal massive-field tails and the breakdown of the standard eikonal correspondence between quasinormal modes and null geodesics. Particular attention is devoted to veiled singularities, horizonless configurations that satisfy a form of weak cosmic censorship and can mimic black hole shadows. We analyze its perturbative stability and quasinormal mode spectrum, finding characteristic deviations from Schwarzschild black holes. We explore the phenomenological implications of quantum corrections for shadows and Hawking radiation. While shadow remains compatible with current constraints, the modified evaporation process features lower temperatures and suppressed emission rates, with implications for primordial black holes and their possible contribution to dark matter. Overall, this work demonstrates how the perturbative dynamics of compact objects can connect quantum gravity models to potentially observable astrophysical phenomena.

For the highly charged binaries with equal-magnitude opposite charges, however, the dominant gravitational strain exhibits visible differences between the EMD cases with $a\neq0$ and the charged Einstein-Maxwell reference at $a=0$. At $a=1$, the coordinate-time interval from the same separation to the waveform-based merger is approximately $62\%$ shorter than for the neutral binary. In this high-charge sequence at fixed input charges, increasing $a$ strengthens the scalar dressing while leaving the individual dilaton-weighted horizon charges nearly unchanged. Within the same sequence, increasing $a$ across the sampled values decreases the dipolar electromagnetic-flux peak, increases the scalar-flux peak, and modestly lengthens the merger interval. The sampled like-charged binaries have corresponding merger intervals approximately $15\%$-$20\%$ longer than the neutral baseline and emit predominantly quadrupolar electromagnetic radiation. At $a=1$ and $a=\sqrt{3}$, the like-charged mergers leave charged remnants with scalar fields consistent with secondary dilaton hair. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PQ 方案是强 CP 问题（strong CP problem）的主流解释，但显式 PQ 破缺或额外 CP 破坏相互作用会使其不再“完美”，从而留下可观测的物理效应；需要寻找能灵敏检验这些偏离的探针。

Conclusion: $^{229}$Th 核钟为检验 PQ 解的偏离提供了新途径，可同时作为轴子暗物质探测与第五种力搜寻的平台。

Abstract: We show that $^{229}$Th nuclear clocks can probe departures from the Peccei-Quinn (PQ) solution to the strong CP problem. Explicit PQ breaking or additional CP-violating interactions can shift the minimum of the axion potential and generate CP-violating couplings to matter, inducing a clock response linear in the axion field. We investigate explicit PQ breaking and quark chromo-electric dipole operators as representative realizations of these \textit{imperfect axion} scenarios, consistent with electric dipole moment constraints. For axion dark matter, the linear signal oscillates at the Compton frequency and can dominate the quadratic response. Even without axion dark matter, the Earth can source a static axion field, enabling fifth-force searches with nuclear clocks.

Using a parton-level example with an exact reference likelihood and reconstruction-level analyses of $A\to ZH\to \ell\ell b\bar b$ and $Y\to HH\to (t\bar t)(t\bar t)$, we demonstrate that sequential training produces significantly more accurate and better calibrated likelihoods than single round training with the same amount of training events.

With it, we compute the exact axion-induced number distribution for the first time, demonstrating that it is not Poisson. We argue that virialized axion dark matter is expected to be in a Gaussian quantum state, and show that number or quadrature measurements can distinguish this from the predictions of a coherent state or even a Bose-Einstein condensate. Abstract: We study Neutrino Transition Polarizability, where a light pseudoscalar $φ$ couples active neutrinos to heavy neutral leptons $N$ and interacts with two photons. For $m_N>m_φ$, the decay $N\toνφ$ can greatly shorten the heavy neutrino lifetime, while $φ\toγγ$ produces a displaced photon pair. This changes the relevant search strategies, favoring production and decay inside detectors. We discuss existing constraints and estimate the sensitivity of displaced photon searches at DUNE, Belle II, LHCb and FCC-ee, highlighting the complementarity of neutrino scattering and meson and electroweak boson decays.

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于结构函数参数化的数据驱动截面建模框架，使其覆盖多轻子味、中微子与反中微子束流模式，并在类DUNE的电子出现道分析中同时确定θ23、Δm²31与δCP，结果表明该模型可无显著偏倚地恢复注入的振荡参数。

Method: 将原有框架扩展至多轻子味及中微子/反中微子（neutrino and antineutrino）两种束流模式，纳入电子出现道（electron appearance channel），实现sin²θ23、Δm²31与CP破坏相位（CP-violating phase）δCP的联合测定；并将通量 nuisance 参数（flux nuisance parameters）纳入学习问题。
