Abstract: Neutrino-nucleus cross sections are essential inputs for long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiments, yet remain challenging to model with high precision. Recent work demonstrated that fully black-box data-driven cross-section models cannot accurately generalize between near- and far-detector fluxes, but imposing Standard-Model symmetries via structure-function parameterizations provides a well-posed learning problem in a simplified DUNE-inspired muon-neutrino disappearance analysis. Here, we extend this framework to multiple lepton flavors and both neutrino and antineutrino beam modes, incorporating the electron appearance channel and enabling a simultaneous determination of $\sin^2θ_{23}$, $Δm^2_{31}$, and the CP-violating phase $δ_{\rm CP}$. We find that the learned cross section model recovers the injected oscillation parameters without significant bias, with constraints comparable to those obtained using the exact underlying cross section. Flux uncertainties have more significant effects in the multi-channel case, and incorporating flux nuisance parameters into the learning problem is required for accurate results. Our results demonstrate that this data-driven approach can be extended to the multi-channel measurements needed for long-baseline neutrino oscillation analyses.

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the possibility that Dark Matter contains particles that interact with known matter only through gravity, the least interacting particles (LIPs). Breaking this symmetry introduces kinetic mixing. Here, we study two realization mechanisms, namely kinetic mixing with the electromagnetic field after electroweak symmetry breaking (Scenario A) and kinetic mixing with the weak hypercharge gauge field (Scenario B). We also consider generalized kinetic terms that violate parity and time-reversal symmetries.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Conventional estimates of $J/ψ$ weak decays, based on independent single-charm decays, suggest a branching fraction of order $10^{-8}$. The heavy-quark expansion identifies the leading pair-annihilation structure and motivates vacuum saturation for the leading color-singlet component. With tree-level hard kernels and leading-logarithmic Wilson coefficients at $μ_0=M_{J/ψ}$, we obtain $B_{\rm weak}=(7.96^{+3.43}_{-0.43}\pm1.24)\times10^{-8}$. The contribution mediated by the $W$ boson alone, i.e. single-charm decays and $W$-exchange annihilation without the $W$-$Z$ interference, has a branching fraction of $(2.37^{+1.64}_{-0.42}\pm0.13)\times10^{-8}$. For the flavor sectors $(ΔC,|ΔS|)=(0,1)$ and $|ΔC|=1$, the branching fractions are $(4.97^{+16.44}_{-4.97}\pm0.99)\times10^{-10}$ and $(1.86^{+0.20}_{-0.06}\pm0.02)\times10^{-8}$, respectively, including charge-conjugate channels. The first uncertainty estimates missing perturbative corrections through scale variation over $1~\mathrm{GeV}\leqμ\leq3M_{J/ψ}$. The second is a hadronic uncertainty, obtained by jointly varying each vacuum-saturation and kinetic/chromomagnetic input within relative ranges of $Λ_{\rm QCD}/m_c\simeq20\%$. Method: 综述并更新铯原子宇称不守恒（atomic parity violation, APV）测定的现状并与其他探针比较；随后一致性地重新分析低能中微子散射数据，合并相干弹性散射（CEνNS）与中微子-电子散射的结果，并考虑电弱辐射修正（electroweak radiative corrections）及相关理论不确定度。

Abstract: The weak mixing angle, $\sin^2θ_W$, is a fundamental parameter of the Standard Model and a sensitive probe of electroweak physics across various energy scales. At low momentum transfer, the Standard Model prediction approaches a well-defined low-energy plateau, providing a target for precision tests of the electroweak theory. Here, we review and update the status of this determination and compare it with other available probes. We then consistently reanalyze low-energy neutrino scattering data and combine the resulting determinations from coherent elastic scattering (CE$ν$NS) and neutrino-electron scattering.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: To match the experimental precision targeted by future collider programs, theoretical predictions for Drell-Yan production must incorporate higher-order mixed Quantum Chromodynamics and electroweak corrections. By systematically evaluating the missing singlet contributions, we obtain the complete set of ${\mathcal{O}}(α_s^2α)$ quark form factors, which we use to construct the soft-virtual cross-section by exploiting renormalization group invariance, the K+G evolution equation, and universal soft-collinear factorization. Method: 系统考察辐射时代中若干新物理时期——包括额外相对论自由度（extra relativistic degrees of freedom）、早期物质主导（early matter domination）、中间真空主导（intermediate vacuum domination）与运动时期（kination）——对宇宙弦网络演化和环产生过程的修正，并分析晚期动力学暗能量对弦GW谱的作用。

Abstract: In the standard cosmological paradigm, the gravitational wave (GW) spectrum from a cosmic string network is well defined. We find that a kination era enhances the stochastic GW signal, whereas the other scenarios suppress the signal to varying degrees. The mechanism applies to thermal and nonthermal relics that reach internal equilibrium after production, and permits mass variation over a broad range of timescales.

Motivation: 守恒荷关联（conserved-charge correlations）被视为探测强相互作用物质中磁场的候选探针，但需要能正确描述介质中有效相互作用磁场依赖的理论框架；固定耦合模型难以再现相关响应。

Method: 采用受格点约束的三味 Polyakov–Nambu–Jona-Lasinio 模型，利用高斯过程回归（Gaussian process regression）从格点 QCD 的平均轻夸克凝聚数据推断热磁标量耦合；并计算至六阶的压力泰勒系数（Taylor coefficients），重构非零重子化学势下的压力与重子密度。

Result: 在未拟合守恒荷可观测量的情况下，该框架较固定耦合模型显著改善了守恒荷关联的磁场依赖性与交叉（crossover）行为，再现了重子–电荷关联及相关磁化率比值（susceptibility ratios）的明显磁增强，支持其作为 QCD 磁强计（QCD magnetometers）的用途；同时给出六阶以内的压力泰勒系数及非零重子化学势下的热力学量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，有效相互作用的介质依赖性是模型内描述该磁响应的关键；来自单一手征可观测量的格点信息可约束较广泛的热力学响应，将逆磁催化、守恒荷关联与状态方程（equation of state）纳入统一框架，为研究 QCD 物质的磁场特征与有限密度热力学提供格点约束基础。

Abstract: Conserved-charge correlations offer a promising probe of magnetic fields in strongly interacting matter. We investigate their magnetic response and the finite-density equation of state within a lattice-constrained three-flavor Polyakov--Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model. A thermomagnetic scalar coupling, inferred by Gaussian process regression from lattice QCD results for the average light-quark condensate, incorporates inverse magnetic catalysis into the effective interaction. Without fitting conserved-charge observables, the resulting framework substantially improves their magnetic-field dependence and crossover behavior compared with the fixed-coupling model. In particular, it captures the pronounced magnetic enhancement of the baryon--electric-charge correlation and related susceptibility ratios, supporting their use as QCD magnetometers. The comparison demonstrates that the medium dependence of the effective interaction is essential to describe this response within the model. We further calculate pressure Taylor coefficients through sixth order and reconstruct the pressure and baryon density at nonzero baryon chemical potential. Our results show that lattice information from a single chiral observable constrains a broad thermodynamic response, connecting inverse magnetic catalysis, conserved-charge correlations, and the equation of state in a common framework. This provides a lattice-constrained basis for investigating magnetic-field signatures and finite-density thermodynamics in QCD matter.

</details>


### [67] [Localized Neutrinos in Two Dark Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30526)
*Waqas Ahmed,George K. Leontaris*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究六维（six-dimensional）两暗维紧致化框架下的中微子质量（neutrino masses）。结果表明，将惰性中微子（sterile neutrino）波函数局域化于标准模型膜（Standard-Model brane）附近，可用微扰的O(1)汤川耦合（Yukawa coupling）获得现实的狄拉克中微子质量（Dirac neutrino masses），并同时缓解对KK塔（Kaluza–Klein tower）的振荡约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在常规平坦体（flat-bulk）实现中，惰性中微子零模（zero mode）受到强烈体体积压低，基本标度约10 TeV时需很大的高维汤川耦合才能重现观测到的中微子质量，且伴随的二维KK塔受振荡数据严格限制。论文旨在寻找可同时缓解这两个问题的机制。

Method: 采用高斯局域化（Gaussian localizer）将惰性中微子波函数局域在标准模型膜附近，推导膜上可见惰性激发形成的壳层谱（shell spectrum）M_s^2=8μ^2 s 及按1/s衰减的活性–惰性混合平方，并给出三味（three-flavour）结构。

Result: 局域化使现实狄拉克中微子质量可由微扰的O(1)汤川耦合获得；惰性激发呈壳层谱，活性–惰性混合平方随1/s减小，局域化标度可将实验相关的惰性中微子谱与引力紧致化标度分离。论文进一步给出对反应堆振荡（reactor oscillations）与氚β衰变（tritium beta decay）的唯象后果，并与现有平坦LED及KATRIN约束进行比较。

Conclusion: 膜附近波函数局域化是高维中微子质量模型中一种有效机制，能同时缓解大汤川耦合需求与KK塔振荡约束，并通过局域化标度实现谱的分离。

Abstract: We study neutrino masses in a six-dimensional framework with two compact dark dimensions. In the conventional flat-bulk realization, the sterile-neutrino zero mode is strongly volume suppressed, so a fundamental scale near $10~\mathrm{TeV}$ requires a large higher-dimensional Yukawa coupling to reproduce the observed neutrino masses, while the accompanying two-dimensional Kaluza--Klein tower is strongly constrained by oscillation data. We show that localizing the sterile-neutrino wave function near the Standard-Model brane substantially alleviates both effects. Using a Gaussian localizer, realistic Dirac neutrino masses are obtained with perturbative, order-one Yukawa couplings, while the brane-visible sterile excitations form a shell spectrum with $M_s^2=8μ^2 s$, and squared active--sterile mixings that decrease as $1/s$. The localization scale therefore separates the experimentally relevant sterile-neutrino spectrum from the gravitational compactification scale. We derive the resulting three-flavour structure and study its phenomenological consequences for reactor oscillations and tritium beta decay, including comparisons with current flat-LED and KATRIN constraints.

</details>

*Tatsuo Kobayashi,Tomoyasu Kori,Shuhei Miyamoto,Riku Nakano,Haruki Uchida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究由带磁通量与 Scherk-Schwarz 相位的磁化 $T^2/\mathbf{Z}_2$、$T^2/\mathbf{Z}_4$ 轨道流形（orbifold）模型导出的 Yukawa 纹理（Yukawa textures）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统刻画磁化轨道流形模型中汤川纹理（Yukawa textures）的可能形式，并检验哪些纹理模式在统计上更受青睐、选择定则（selection rules）是否可由群论对称性解释。

Method: 在 $T^2/\mathbf{Z}_2$ 与 $T^2/\mathbf{Z}_4$ 磁化轨道流形模型中，变化磁通量（magnetic fluxes）与 Scherk-Schwarz 相位，系统推导对应的 Yukawa 纹理，并进行统计分析。

Conclusion: 磁化轨道流形模型可自然给出具有统计偏好的 Yukawa 纹理，且 $\mathbf{Z}_4$ 情形的选择定则超出群论对称性所能刻画的纹理范围，显示该类模型的纹理结构具有独立于群论来源的额外限制。

Abstract: We study systematically Yukawa textures, which can be derived from magnetized $T^2/\mathbf{Z}_2$ and $T^2/\mathbf{Z}_4$ orbifold models with various magnetic fluxes and Scherk-Schwarz phases. Remarkably, the nearest neighbor interaction pattern as well as the diagonal Yukawa matrix is statistically favorable in $T^2/\mathbf{Z}_2$ orbifold models. Also, in $T^2/\mathbf{Z}_4$ orbifold models, selection rules are nontrivial and lead to textures, which cannot be derived from group-theoretical symmetries.

</details>


### [69] [Charmed baryon-to-meson ratios inside jets as a discriminator of hadronization mechanisms: from color reconnection in p+p to coalescence in Pb+Pb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31316)
*Zi-Xuan Xu,Wei Dai,Ben-Wei Zhang,Enke Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过研究喷注内粲重子与介子比值 $\Lambda_c^+/D^0$ 在 p+p 与 Pb+Pb 碰撞中的表现，利用其在喷注内修饰的符号来区分色重联（color reconnection）与并合（coalescence）两种强子化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注可分离硬散射产物与底层事件，有助于厘清从小系统到大碰撞系统中重子与介子比值增强的来源；本文旨在辨别导致粲强子产额改变的候选强子化机制。

Method: 在 $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5.02$ TeV 下，用 PYTHIA8（含色重联 CR-BLC Mode 2，作为超越领头色情形的代表）模拟 p+p 碰撞，用含并合加碎裂混合强子化的 SHELL 模型描述 Pb+Pb 碰撞，比较喷注内与包含性的 $\Lambda_c^+/D^0$ 比值、平行喷注动量份额 $z^{ch}_{||}$ 谱以及相对喷注轴的径向分布。

Result: p+p 中喷注内比值在低至中等 $p_T$ 相对包含性情形增强（与轻味 $\Lambda/K^0_S$ 相反），因色重联导致的比值超出仍与喷注相关；Pb+Pb 中喷注内比值在中等 $p_T$ 反而被适度压低，因由软热部分子并合形成的强子与喷注方向无关联而被喷注选择剔除。两种机制均使粲强子向小 $z^{ch}_{||}$ 移动，且对 $\Lambda_c^+$ 影响更强；喷注内比值的径向分布随离轴距离上升，色重联情形最低 $p_T$ 区间偏移最远，并合情形为中等 $p_T$ 区间。

Conclusion: 喷注内修饰的符号可作为判别两种强子化机制的观测量，$z^{ch}_{||}$ 谱可独立于部分子能量损失探测非碎裂强子化；结果为未来两种碰撞系统中喷注标记粲强子测量提供了基线（baseline）与预言（predictions）。

Abstract: Jets separate the products of hard scatterings from the underlying event and thus help to clarify the origin of the enhanced baryon-to-meson ratios observed from small to large collision systems. We investigate the charmed baryon-to-meson ratio $Λ_c^+/D^0$ within jets in p+p and Pb+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5.02$~TeV to discriminate between candidate hadronization mechanisms. In p+p collisions, simulated with PYTHIA8 using color reconnection (CR-BLC Mode 2) as a representative beyond-leading-color scenario, the in-jet ratio is enhanced at low-to-intermediate $p_T$ relative to the inclusive one---opposite to the light-flavor $Λ/K^0_S$ case---because the color-reconnection-induced excess of the ratio remains correlated with the jet and survives the jet requirement, while the fragmentation baseline shows no such enhancement. In Pb+Pb collisions, described by the SHELL model with a coalescence plus fragmentation hybrid hadronization, the in-jet ratio is instead suppressed moderately at intermediate $p_T$, because coalescence hadrons assembled from soft thermal partons carry no correlation with the jet direction and are preferentially discarded by the jet selection. The sign of the in-jet modification thus discriminates between the two mechanisms. Both mechanisms shift charmed hadrons toward small values of the parallel jet momentum fraction $z^{ch}_{||}$, with a stronger effect on $Λ_c^+$, so that the $z^{ch}_{||}$ spectrum probes non-fragmentation hadronization independently of parton energy loss. The radial profile of the in-jet ratio rises with the distance from the jet axis in both systems, with the lowest-$p_T$ interval displaced farthest for color reconnection and the intermediate-$p_T$ interval for coalescence. These results provide the baseline and predictions needed for future jet-tagged charmed-hadron measurements in both collision systems.

</details>


### [70] [$N \to Δ$ Transition Form Factors in the Thermal AdS/QCD Soft-Wall Model with Modified Propagators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31395)
*Narmin Nasibova,Xerxes D. Arsiwalla,Shakir Nagiyev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在热 AdS/QCD 软墙模型（soft-wall model）中，轴矢传播子因双基本标量凝聚而渐近发散，作者引入动量依赖截断的正则化方案，并据此估算核子-Δ 跃迁的电磁与轴矢形状因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量传播子有良好解析解，但横向轴矢运动方程含双基本标量（bifundamental scalar）真空期望值（手征对称性破缺来源），导致轴矢传播子渐近发散，无法直接计算软墙模型中的可观测量的困难。

Method: 对轴矢传播子施加动量依赖截断（momentum-dependent cut-off）的正则化，并以内部约束方法（interior constraint approach）求解边值问题以确定截断点；在紫外（UV）极限附近验证一致性，并将该修正传播子及其参数形变版本用于 N→Δ 跃迁形状因子的估算。

Result: 正则化后的传播子给出的轴矢形状因子与实验观测、格点 QCD（lattice QCD）及其他理论模型的结果具有中等程度可比性；该方案使热软墙模型可用于考察 N→Δ 跃迁中电磁与轴矢两个部分的温度相关特征。

Conclusion: 正则化使热软墙模型成为研究 QCD 形状因子热学特性的可用工具，并在电磁与轴矢两个扇区展示了其适用性。

Abstract: We investigate electromagnetic (EM) and axial vector (AV) form factors of the nucleon-Delta baryon transition within the thermal AdS/QCD soft-wall model. While the vector bulk-to-boundary propagator admits a well-behaved analytical solution, the transverse axial vector equation of motion involves the vacuum expectation value of the bifundamental scalar (generating chiral symmetry breaking). Due to this, the axial propagator diverges asymptotically, rendering it unsuitable for computing observables in the soft-wall model. To address this problem, we implement a regularization for the axial propagator in the form of a momentum-dependent cut-off, that identifies its correct physical regime. This is achieved via an interior constraint approach to the boundary-value problem, that determines the cut-off point. The consistency of this procedure is verified near the UV limit. As a proof of concept, we test this modified propagator (and also a parametrically deformed version of it) to estimate axial vector form factors of the $N \to Δ$ transition, and find results moderately comparable with form factor profiles from experimental observations, lattice QCD, and other theoretical models. Equipped with this regularization, the thermal soft-wall model becomes a useful tool to probe thermal characteristics of QCD form factors. We demonstrate this for both, the EM and AV sectors of the $N \to Δ$ transition.

</details>

### [71] [Stochastic Diversity Stabilization in Nonlinear Competitive Dynamics: A Statistical Mechanics Approach to Fluctuation Suppression and Finite-Size Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30169)
*Ruocun Ou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在 N 维概率单纯形上的非线性随机复制子动力学中，维持多样性所需的最小噪声呈幂律衰减 σ_c ~ N^(-α)，指数 α 为模型依赖量而非普适指数；(γ, σ) 平面扫描显示平滑动力学交叉而非二级相变，涨落抑制源于多路径收敛而非弛豫加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机强迫可防止竞争性动力系统中的多样性崩塌，但其定量条件与动力学机制仍缺乏刻画，论文旨在同时回答这两个问题。

Method: 在 N 维概率单纯形（probability simplex）上研究一类非线性随机复制子动力学（stochastic replicator dynamics），以非线性指数 γ 与加性高斯噪声强度 σ 为参数；通过扫描 (γ, σ) 平面、线性稳定性分析、匹配的有限尺度分析（finite-size analysis）以及熵弛豫时间 τ_relax 的测量进行考察。

Result: 基线相互作用下 σ_c ~ N^(-α)；改为超线性变体后 α 由约 1.1 移至约 2.0，与线性稳定性预测 α = (γ+2)/2 一致。涨落磁化率 χ_H = N Var(H) 在 γ ~ 0.7 处达峰（χ_H ~ 0.6），高 γ 下受抑制，γ ~ 1.4 附近无发散，有限尺度分析未发现峰值随 N 的系统性发散。τ_relax(γ) 在全程近似恒定（约 2.9 个时间单位），而 χ_H 下降六倍。

Conclusion: 弛豫时间尺度与涨落幅度的解耦表明，抑制来自多条路径收敛至相似终点的路径多重性（pathway multiplicity），而非单一通道上的加速弛豫；该机制区别于噪声诱导跃迁（noise-induced transitions）与平衡临界性（equilibrium criticality），是本类非平衡随机系统中起作用的稳定化模式。

Abstract: Stochastic forcing can prevent diversity collapse in competitive dynamical systems, yet the quantitative conditions and the dynamical mechanism responsible remain poorly characterized. We address both questions in a class of nonlinear stochastic replicator dynamics on the N-dimensional probability simplex, parameterized by a nonlinearity exponent gamma and additive Gaussian noise of intensity sigma. Under the baseline interaction, the minimum noise required to sustain diversity decays as a power law, sigma_c ~ N^(-alpha); replacing the baseline interaction with a superlinear variant shifts alpha from about 1.1 to about 2.0, in agreement with a linear-stability prediction alpha = (gamma+2)/2, identifying alpha as a model-specific quantity rather than a universal index. Scanning the (gamma, sigma) plane reveals a smooth dynamical crossover: the fluctuation susceptibility chi_H = N Var(H) peaks at gamma ~ 0.7 (chi_H ~ 0.6) and is suppressed at high gamma, with no divergence near gamma ~ 1.4; matched finite-size analysis finds no systematic divergence of the peak with N, ruling out a genuine second-order phase transition. The entropy relaxation time tau_relax(gamma) remains approximately constant (about 2.9 time units) across the full range of gamma, even as chi_H decreases sixfold; this decoupling between relaxation timescale and fluctuation amplitude implies that suppression arises from convergence of diverse pathways to similar endpoints rather than from accelerated relaxation along a single channel. These findings identify nonlinear fluctuation suppression via pathway multiplicity -- distinct from both noise-induced transitions and equilibrium criticality -- as the operative stabilization pattern in this class of nonequilibrium stochastic systems.

</details>


### [72] [Resonant-pair energy return beyond the early Green-Kubo plateau in a quartic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30172)
*Julian Olszewski,Zijian Liu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在弱耦合四元振子链（quartic oscillator chain）中提出一种定量机制，解释早期格林–库比（Green–Kubo）平台如何被共振邻近振子的能量相干返回所改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由有限时长模拟推断热导率时，需区分快速的局域能量交换与持续输运；早期格林–库比平台可能并非稳态输运信号，而源于相干能量返回。

Method: 将成对热流积分映射为经典单摆俘获响应（pendulum trapping response）；利用四元齐次性给出与能量无关的参考周期 T_lib=7.77ε^{-1/2}；并以孤立对模拟及与混沌单胞、能量守恒噪声的对照检验该参考。

Result: 无拟合参数下，主导响应预言在0.952T_lib处出现负的一阶极小，深度为独立单胞交换系数的-0.74倍；孤立对模拟验证该参考并解析有限耦合修正，整链在同一时间尺度达到首个极小但更深浅、并伴随小幅后期残余。

Conclusion: 这些有限时间结果为区分早期交换平台与后续关联动力学提供定量参照；在混沌单胞与能量守恒噪声情形下，相干返回在测量窗口内缺失或被打断。

Abstract: Distinguishing rapid local energy exchange from sustained transport matters when thermal conductivity is inferred from finite simulations. In a weakly coupled chain of separable quartic oscillators, we identify a quantitative mechanism that changes an early Green--Kubo plateau: resonant neighbors exchange energy and coherently return it. The pair heat-current integral maps to the classical pendulum trapping response. Quartic homogeneity gives an energy-independent reference period $T_{\rm lib}=7.77ε^{-1/2}$, where $ε$ is the coupling. Without fitted parameters, the leading response predicts a negative first minimum at $0.952T_{\rm lib}$ with depth $-0.74$ times the independent-cell exchange coefficient. Isolated-pair simulations test this reference and resolve finite-coupling corrections. The chain reaches its first minimum on the same time scale, with a shallower minimum and a small later residual. Comparisons with chaotic cells and energy-conserving noise show regimes in which coherent return is absent or interrupted over the measured windows. These finite-time results provide a quantitative reference for separating the early exchange plateau from subsequent correlated dynamics.

</details>


### [73] [Foresight-dependent entropy production in stochastic two-agent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30189)
*Arpan Dey*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究在个体协调偏好为互惠（reciprocal）但状态调整规则由前瞻性优化（foresight）决定时，是否仍会产生非零稳态概率流。通过精确可解的随机线性二次博弈，发现互惠协调下的不可逆性以 τ^8 的高阶形式消失，而顺从—逆反耦合下则存在非零极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互惠相互作用（nonreciprocal interactions）可通过维持稳态概率流（stationary probability currents）破坏细致平衡（detailed balance）；本文追问当个体即时协调偏好本身互惠、而状态调整源于前瞻性优化（foresight）时，此类概率流是否依然涌现。

Method: 构建一个最小随机线性二次博弈（stochastic linear-quadratic game），两名主体控制含噪声的标量状态，在全信息反馈纳什均衡（feedback Nash equilibrium）下最小化各自折扣成本。所得最优控制对应 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程，其稳态概率流与熵产生率（entropy-production rate）可精确计算，并从耦合的纳什 Riccati 方程推导抵消机制。

Result: 互惠协调且自锚定强度（self-anchoring strengths）不等时，有效漂移不对称性首次出现在规划时间尺度 τ 的 5 阶，归一化熵产生在 τ→0 时按 τ^8 标度；顺从—逆反耦合（conformist-contrarian coupling）下，归一化熵产生趋于非零的短前瞻极限，并随 τ^2 从该极限初始下降；另给出等自锚定强度下的长前瞻极限。全纳什方程数值解验证了以上渐近律。

Conclusion: 前瞻性优化（forward-looking optimization）既能产生也能降低归一化稳态不可逆性，具体取决于底层相互作用类型；表明非互惠有效动力学可由互惠偏好加前瞻优化机制生成。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions can break detailed balance by sustaining stationary probability currents. Here we ask whether such currents can also emerge when the agents' immediate coordination preferences are reciprocal, but their state-adjustment rules are determined by forward-looking optimization (foresight). We study a minimal stochastic linear-quadratic game in which two agents control noisy scalar states and minimize individual discounted costs in a full-information feedback Nash equilibrium. The optimized controls yield an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process whose stationary probability current and entropy-production rate, measuring statistical time-irreversibility, can be calculated exactly. For reciprocal coordination with unequal self-anchoring strengths, the effective drift asymmetry first appears at order $τ^5$, where $τ$ is the planning timescale, and the normalized entropy production consequently scales as $τ^8$ in the limit $τ\to0$. We derive this cancellation from the coupled Nash Riccati equations. For conformist-contrarian coupling, normalized entropy production approaches a nonzero short-foresight limit and initially decreases from that limit by an amount proportional to $τ^2$. We also determine the long-foresight limit for a conformist-contrarian pair with equal self-anchoring strengths. Numerical solutions of the full Nash equations verify the asymptotic laws and reveal the effect of foresight. Thus, forward-looking optimization can generate or reduce normalized stationary irreversibility, depending on the underlying interaction.

</details>


### [74] [Einstein's theory of Brownian motion: The non-Fickian normal diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30194)
*A. Bhattacharyay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文指出，在爱因斯坦（Einstein）扩散理论中，空间依赖扩散系数（space-dependent diffusivity）的情形可自然导出“正态非菲克扩散”（normal non-Fickian diffusion），因此非菲克扩散亦可属于正态扩散范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上正态扩散（normal diffusion）与菲克扩散（Fickian diffusion）被等同视之，而非菲克扩散（non-Fickian diffusion）常被视为反常扩散。作者意在澄清这一混淆：爱因斯坦原始推导中已隐含将理论推广至空间依赖扩散系数的可能性。

Method: 采用理论分析与推导性讨论的方法，重新审视爱因斯坦扩散（diffusion）原始推导中的假设与结构，考查在扩散系数随空间变化时该推导所允许的推广形式。

Result: 分析表明，在扩散系数依赖空间的情况下，非菲克扩散可具有正态扩散的特征；即非菲克扩散能够驱动浓度不均匀性（concentration inhomogeneities），同时仍属于正态扩散。

Conclusion: 论文论证“正态非菲克扩散”源于爱因斯坦扩散理论本身，从而拓展了正态扩散的概念边界，并提示不应将菲克性与正态性简单等同。

Abstract: Einstein's theory of diffusion defines what is considered {\it normal diffusion}. Historically, Einstein considered homogeneous diffusion characterised by a diffusion constant in his original calculations, resulting in what is known as {\it Fickian} diffusion, which thermally relaxes concentration inhomogeneities. However, in the same derivation of Einstein, the seed of generalisation of the theory to {\it non-Fickian} diffusion in the presence of space-dependent diffusivity is hidden. This indicates that non-Fickian diffusion, which can drive concentration inhomogeneities, can also be normal diffusion. The present article discusses the origin of the {\it normal} non-Fickian diffusion in Einstein's theory of diffusion.

</details>


### [75] [Learning Cole-Hopf Coordinates for Stochastic Growth Dynamics: the KPZ class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30919)
*Lorenzo Mensi,Simone Cuonzo,Maria Chiara Angelini,Sergio Chibbaro,Cyril Furtlehner,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动框架，通过神经网络学习变量变换，将非线性随机动力学映射为线性隐空间演化，并以短时矩匹配目标训练，从而从轨迹数据中推断可解释的随机方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非线性随机过程的观测中推断有效方程十分困难，因为单条轨迹只是含噪实现，而物理信息编码于系综统计中；因此需要一种能绕过轨迹噪声、直接从统计信息学习动力学的方法。

Method: 模型由神经表示变换与线性随机隐变量演化组成，训练采用短时矩匹配（short-time moment-matching）目标，在优化过程中无需轨迹模拟（simulation）；所得生成模型中变换与隐空间演化算符均可直接检视。

Result: 在一维Kardar–Parisi–Zhang（KPZ）方程上验证：仅从模拟的高度轨迹中，模型恢复了局域指数型Cole–Hopf映射及相应线性随机算符；生成轨迹重现该普适类（universality class）的主要统计特征，包括β=1/3的增长律与平直初条件下的GOE Tracy–Widom高度分布。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过学习使动力学以简单形式闭合的表示，可从数据中推断可解释的随机方程；物理信息学习的引入亦有助于获取关于动力学过程物理机制的洞见。

Abstract: Inferring effective equations from observations of nonlinear stochastic processes is challenging because individual trajectories are noisy realizations, while the relevant physical information is encoded in ensemble statistics. We introduce a data-driven framework that learns a change of variables in which nonlinear stochastic dynamics become linear. The model combines a neural change of representation with a linear stochastic latent evolution and is trained through a short-time moment-matching objective, thereby avoiding trajectory \rev{simulation} during optimization. This construction yields an interpretable generative model: both the learned transformation and the latent evolution operators can be directly inspected.
  We validate the method on the one-dimensional Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) equation, a paradigmatic model of stochastic growth for which the Cole--Hopf transformation maps the nonlinear dynamics to the stochastic heat equation. From simulated height trajectories alone, the model recovers the local exponential Cole--Hopf map and the associated linear stochastic operator. Generated trajectories reproduce the main statistical signatures of the KPZ universality class, including the growth law $\mathrm{Var}(h^{t})\sim t^{2β}$ with $β=1/3$ and the GOE Tracy--Widom height distribution for flat initial conditions. These results show that interpretable stochastic equations can be inferred from data by learning representations in which the dynamics closes in a simple form. Furthermore the development of the physical informed learning permits to get insights on the physics of the dynamical process.

</details>


### [76] [Active phase separation from concentration-dependent diffusion in chemical networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30958)
*Giulia Pisegna,Ramin Golestanian*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从两种相互作用的化学物种的随机质量守恒反应-扩散动力学（reaction-diffusion dynamics）出发，通过系统性慢模约简（slow-mode reduction），完整推导出Active Model B+场论，并将全部唯象系数（phenomenological coefficients）用微观参数表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活系统中的活性相分离（active phase separation）虽普遍存在，但活性场论（active field theories）中非平衡项（non-equilibrium terms）的微观起源仍基本未知，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用自下而上的方法：以两物种相互作用的随机质量守恒反应-扩散动力学为起点，进行系统性慢模约简，推导得到完整的Active Model B+场论，并用随机数值模拟（stochastic numerical simulations）进行验证。

Result: 识别出浓度依赖扩散系数（concentration-dependent diffusivities）与非对称种间趋化耦合（asymmetric inter-species chemotactic couplings）是产生真正非平衡系数的最小微观要素；该系数对应微相分离（microphase separation）、团簇相（cluster phases）与粗化停滞（arrested coarsening）。框架自洽地恢复平衡极限（equilibrium limit）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，化学反应网络（chemical reaction networks）的结构与其涌现的大尺度动力学普适类（universality class）之间存在直接的自下而上联系。

Abstract: Active phase separation in living systems is ubiquitous, yet the microscopic origins of the non-equilibrium terms in active field theories remain largely unknown. Here, starting from stochastic mass-conserving reaction-diffusion dynamics of two interacting chemical species, we perform a systematic slow-mode reduction and derive the complete Active Model B+ field theory, with all phenomenological coefficients expressed in terms of microscopic parameters. We identify concentration-dependent diffusivities and asymmetric inter-species chemotactic couplings as the minimal microscopic ingredients generating the genuinely non-equilibrium coefficient responsible for microphase separation, cluster phases, and arrested coarsening. Our framework recovers the equilibrium limit consistently and is validated by stochastic numerical simulations. These results establish a direct, bottom-up link between the architecture of chemical reaction networks and the universality class of their emergent large-scale dynamics.

</details>


### [77] [Site and bond percolation thresholds of all $k$-uniform tilings with $k\le 10$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31190)
*Maksim Elisov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文用蒙特卡洛模拟系统测定了平面上全部23,396种k≤10的正多边形均匀镶嵌（uniform tilings）的座点与键渗流阈值（site and bond percolation thresholds），并借助临界多项式判据（critical-polynomial criterion）的蒙特卡洛形式规避对大尺度各向异性的先验知识，进而用顶点类型分数等局部描述符解释阈值差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的渗流阈值测定多局限于高对称的Archimedean晶格，绝大多数具有显著各向异性的非Archimedean镶嵌缺乏阈值数据，且常规方法需预先知道大尺度各向异性。

Method: 在环面（tori）上进行蒙特卡洛模拟，采用"无缠绕（no wrapping）与交叉缠绕（cross-wrapping）集团等概率"的临界多项式判据的蒙特卡洛形式，按已知枚举对全部23,396种k≤10的均匀镶嵌逐一定位阈值。

Result: 486个k≤4的阈值精度达第六位小数数个单位的量级，重现22个Archimedean参考值（rms偏差0.71个标准误）及20个双均匀晶格（two-uniform lattices）的未发表键阈值（χ²=19.5）；其余阈值中位统计不确定度为2×10⁻⁵（k≤7）至7×10⁻⁵（k≥8），未检出有限尺寸偏倚。<br>仅用顶点类型分数即可把k≥5的阈值确定到约2×10⁻³，而成分相同的镶嵌间阈值差异可达6×10⁻³（座点）和2×10⁻²（键）；半径一（radius one）描述符可解释其中过半差异，相同条带堆垛（stackings of identical strips）的阈值对各类边界份额呈线性。

Conclusion: k≤4阈值的统计检验与独立参考值高度一致，验证了方法的可靠性；阈值主要由顶点类型分数等半径一局部描述符决定，而以h行方格间隔的条带仅靠配准（registry）不同时，键阈值差异每行衰减6–8倍，与该方形条带转移矩阵（transfer matrix）动量π的主导本征值量级相当。

Abstract: We determine the site and bond percolation thresholds of all 23,396 $k$-uniform tilings of the plane by regular polygons with $k\le 10$, the complete set according to the known enumeration. We use Monte Carlo simulation on tori and the condition that no wrapping and a cross-wrapping cluster are equally probable, the Monte Carlo form of the critical-polynomial criterion, which needs no knowledge of the large-scale anisotropy that most of these lattices have. The 486 thresholds with $k\le4$ have uncertainties of a few units in the sixth decimal and reproduce the 22 Archimedean reference values (rms deviation 0.71 standard errors) and unpublished bond thresholds of the 20 two-uniform lattices ($χ^2=19.5$ for 20 values); the others have median statistical uncertainties of $2\times10^{-5}$ ($k\le7$) to $7\times10^{-5}$ ($k\ge8$) and no detectable finite-size bias. The vertex-type fractions alone fix the thresholds for $k\ge5$ to about $2\times10^{-3}$ (linear rule fitted on $k\le4$), while tilings with equal composition differ by up to $6\times10^{-3}$ (site) and $2\times10^{-2}$ (bond). In the range of domain sizes covered, descriptors of radius one account for more than half of this difference, and stackings of identical strips have thresholds linear in the fraction of each boundary type. For tilings that differ only by the registry of strips separated by $h$ rows of squares, the bond threshold difference decreases by a factor of 6-8 per row, comparable to the leading momentum-$π$ eigenvalue of the square-band transfer matrix.

</details>


### [78] [Optimal Search Strategies under Finite Resource Constraints: The Role of Environmental Memory and Directional Information in Informed and Blind Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31244)
*Hugues Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文研究在有限资源预算下如何分配资源以最大化随机搜索（stochastic search）效率，比较三种资源模型在信息可利用与盲策略（blind strategy）下的最优分配方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的搜索过程常受有限资源预算约束（如环境记忆（environmental memory）中搜索者沿路径留下持久线索），而非资源无限；因此需要回答如何分配有限预算以最大化搜索效率。

Method: 构建一维扩散搜索者（one-dimensional diffusing agent）模型，考察三种资源模型：外部控制器施加的拉力、搜索者沿途放置的定向旗帜（directional flags）、以及自生成化学排斥线索（self-generated chemorepulsive cues）作为力场；并区分利用当前位置与历史信息的策略与遵循预设时间协议的盲策略。

Result: 当具备搜索者当前位置与历史信息时，资源应集中于狭窄的空间区域；而在盲策略下，资源应以稳定速率使用，以确保在到达目标前尽可能用尽其容量。此外，在具环境记忆的非马尔可夫过程（non-Markovian process）中，由于已沉积线索位置的固有涨落，盲策略效率显著降低。

Conclusion: 有限预算下的最优资源分配依赖于信息可得性：信息策略宜空间集中，盲策略宜匀速使用；环境记忆带来的位置涨落会显著削弱盲策略的效率。

Abstract: Improving the efficiency of a stochastic search often requires to provide the searching agent with resources that modify its dynamics and help it explore space more quickly. Although such resources are often assumed to be unlimited and treated as a cost to mitigate, many realistic search processes operate under a finite resource budget, e.g. in processes with environmental memory in which the searcher deposits long-lasting cues along its path that it can interact with at later times to guide its further search. Here, we address the question of how to allocate a limited budget to maximize search efficiency by investigating a one-dimensional diffusing agent whose access to resources is constrained. We consider three resource models, namely an external force applied by an external controller to pull the searcher towards the target, directional flags deposited by the searcher along its path that redirect its motion towards the target, and self-generated chemorepulsive cues that act as a force field. For all these models, we find that resource usage should be concentrated in narrow regions of space when information about the searcher's current and past positions is available. In contrast, in blind strategies where a predefined time protocol is followed, resources should be used at a steady rate to ensure it to be used at most of its capacity by the time it reaches the target. We further show that blind strategies in non-Markovian processes with environmental memory are significantly less efficient due to the inherent fluctuations in the locations of deposited cues.

</details>

### [79] [Electronic Screening as a Design Lever in La-Doped BaTiO$_3$: Coupled Polarization, Dielectric Response, and Optical Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30210)
*Sikandar Azam,Muhammad Fareed,Qaiser Rafiq,Jan Minar,Abed Alataway*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用密度泛函理论等方法研究12.5% La掺杂BaTiO3，揭示施主掺杂通过增强电子屏蔽、硬化极性声子模与降低异常玻恩有效电荷，削弱四方铁电性并抑制光学各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施主替代可通过调控晶格畸变与电子屏蔽来调节钙钛矿氧化物的铁电、介电、电子与光学性质，但其微观耦合机制尚需系统阐明，故以La掺杂BaTiO3为对象展开研究。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory）、密度泛函微扰理论（density-functional perturbation theory）及极化（polarization）的Berry相位形式（Berry-phase formalism），构建Ba位12.5% La替代模型进行计算。

Result: La引入导致各向异性晶格收缩，降低四方度（tetragonality）与Ti偏心位移，削弱四方铁电相的能量稳定性；极化降低与电子屏蔽增强及异常玻恩有效电荷（Born effective charges）减小相关。声子计算表明掺杂结构动力学稳定，低频极性模硬化与铁电性减弱一致；高频介电响应适度增强而静态介电响应下降。电子结构显示费米能级（Fermi level）向 conduction band 移动且无带隙内缺陷态；光学谱呈现符合Burstein-Moss型机制的吸收边移动、近边透明度增强及双折射（birefringence）减弱。

Conclusion: 结果提示施主诱导屏蔽、极性模硬化、动态电荷减小、极化抑制与光学各向异性降低之间存在微观联系；结论仅适用于所研究的12.5%组分，需更多浓度计算以确立定量掺杂趋势。

Abstract: Donor substitution can tune the coupled ferroelectric, dielectric, electronic, and optical properties of perovskite oxides by modifying lattice distortions and electronic screening. We investigate these effects in La-doped BaTiO3 using density functional theory, density-functional perturbation theory, and the Berry-phase formalism for polarization. For a model with 12.5% La substitution at the Ba site, La incorporation induces anisotropic lattice contraction, reduces tetragonality and Ti off-centering, and weakens the energetic stabilization of the tetragonal ferroelectric phase. The accompanying reduction in polarization is associated with enhanced electronic screening and reduced anomalous Born effective charges. Phonon calculations indicate that the relaxed doped structure is dynamically stable, while hardening of a low-frequency polar mode is consistent with weakened ferroelectricity. The high-frequency dielectric response increases moderately, whereas the static dielectric response decreases as the lattice contribution is suppressed. Electronic-structure calculations show a Fermi-level shift toward the conduction band without mid-gap defect states. The optical spectra exhibit an absorption-edge shift consistent with a Burstein-Moss-type mechanism, enhanced near-edge transparency, and reduced birefringence. Together, these results suggest a microscopic connection between donor-induced screening, polar-mode hardening, reduced dynamical charges, polarization suppression, and decreased optical anisotropy. The conclusions apply to the investigated 12.5% composition; calculations at additional concentrations are needed to establish quantitative doping trends.

</details>


### [80] [Domain walls in $R3c$-structured NaNbO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30318)
*Jack G. Nedell,Craig J. Fennie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究基于第一性原理系统分析了钙钛矿氧化物NaNbO₃在低温菱方R3c相中的结构畴壁，通过群论分类得到八类畴壁，并发现一类能量极低的109°畴壁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温R3c相钙钛矿氧化物中结构畴壁（domain walls）的性质尚缺乏系统认识，本文旨在对NaNbO₃的R3c相畴壁进行系统性的分类与能量评估。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）结合群论分类（group-theoretic classification）方法，识别出八类独特的畴壁，并对每类代表畴壁进行结构弛豫（structural relaxation）；其中四类的取向由弹性相容性（elastic compatibility）与电中性（charge neutrality）条件唯一确定。

Result: 一类109°畴壁能量显著偏低，为9 mJ m⁻²，与BaTiO₃中的低能铁电畴壁（ferroelectric walls）相当；该类畴壁与实验中R3c相观察到的晶体孪晶（crystal twinning）相容。

Conclusion: 提出了一种R3c↔Pbcm相变（phase transition）的原子尺度机制，并确立低能109°畴壁在R3c相结构中的重要性。

Abstract: In this work, we undertake a systematic first-principles study of structural domain walls in the low-temperature rhombohedral $R3c$ phase of perovskite oxide NaNbO$_3$. Employing a group-theoretic classification, we introduce eight unique classes of domain walls in the $R3c$ structure, four of which have a domain wall orientation determined uniquely by the conditions of elastic compatibility and charge neutrality, and perform structural relaxations for each representative domain wall class. We find that one class of 109° domain walls has a notably low energy of 9 mJ m$^{-2}$, comparable to the low-energy ferroelectric walls in BaTiO$_3$. Finally, we find that this class of 109° walls is compatible with the crystal twinning observed in experiments on the $R3c$ phase, and propose an atomistic mechanism for the $R3c \leftrightarrow Pbcm$ phase transition.

</details>


### [81] [Peridynamic Thermo-Mechanical Modeling of Cracking in Tungsten under Fusion-Relevant Heat Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30356)
*Rinkle Juneja,Pablo Seleson,Samuel Temple Reeve,Justin Weinmeister,Gary Staebler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向钨（tungsten）等离子体面向材料（plasma-facing materials, PFMs）的热-力损伤建模仿真方法，利用近场动力学（peridynamics）代码 CabanaPD，结合 JUDITH 电子束（e-beam）实验数据，预测循环热载荷下的裂纹与损伤演化，并识别不同热流密度与基温组合下的开裂阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFMs 在聚变堆中承受极高热流、高能粒子与强中子辐照，产生热-力应力与辐照损伤；其中极端热流引起的热-力损伤是限制 PFMs 寿命的关键因素，因此需要能预测裂纹与损伤的建模手段。

Method: 采用基于近场动力学断裂力学的组件尺度热-力仿真（CabanaPD），以 JUDITH 电子束装置上再结晶纯钨的实验数据作为输入与校验，研究不同基温与热流密度组合下循环电子束暴露引起的损伤。

Result: 仿真结果与实验分类吻合良好，并可在一定热流密度与基温范围内识别开裂阈值（cracking thresholds）；同时可给出裂纹与损伤的空间分布及时间累积细节。

Conclusion: 该建模仿真路径可为候选 PFMs 的筛选及其在聚变相关条件下的寿命量化提供依据。

Abstract: Plasma-facing materials (PFMs) are the primary armor materials in fusion reactors, protecting reactor wall components from extreme heat fluxes, energetic particles, and intense neutron irradiation. These harsh conditions induce thermo-mechanical stresses, irradiation damage, and overall degradation in PFMs. Among these, thermo-mechanical damage induced by extreme heat flux is one of the key factors limiting the lifetime of PFMs. We present a modeling and simulation approach for predicting thermo-mechanical cracking and damage in tungsten, a leading PFM candidate, under fusion-reactor-relevant heat fluxes. Specifically, we study damage induced by cyclic electron beam (e-beam) exposure under different combinations of base temperature and heat flux. We carry out component-scale thermo-mechanical simulations using CabanaPD, a peridynamics fracture mechanics code, informed by experimental data for recrystallized pure tungsten from the JUDITH e-beam facility. The simulations compare well with the experimental classification and enable identification of cracking thresholds across a range of heat fluxes and base temperatures. These predictions can provide detailed insights into the spatial distribution and temporal accumulation of cracks and damage, while offering a pathway for screening candidate PFMs and quantifying their lifespan under fusion-relevant conditions.

</details>


### [82] [Suppression of Grain Boundary Nitrogen Degradation by Boron in Superalloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30451)
*C. Crabb,G. Topore,J. O. Douglas,M. C. Hardy,M. S. Conroy,D. Dye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在一种Ni/Co基γ/γ′强化高温合金（superalloy）中，硼（boron）以内核—外壳偏聚（core-shell segregation）方式富集于内部氮化物—基体界面；与无硼合金相比，含硼合金在800 °C、100 h氧化后形成的Ti/Nb富集晶界氮化物更小、更浅、更不伸长，表明微量硼合金化可抑制有害内部反应产物的析出与长大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温合金服役中环境辅助退化（environmentally assisted degradation）限制寿命：氮穿过表面保护性氧化层向内部渗透，在晶界形成致脆的氮化物（nitrides）。硼常以微量添加以强化晶界（grain boundary），但其在氧化相关退化中的作用尚不明确。

Method: 对比无硼与含硼的Ni/Co基γ/γ′强化高温合金，在800 °C、100 h条件下进行氧化实验，并采用关联式四维扫描/透射电子显微镜（4D S/TEM）与原子探针层析（atom probe tomography）对亚表面氮化物颗粒的成分与界面结构进行表征。

Result: 两类合金均形成分层的铬氧化物/铝氧化物（chromia/alumina）氧化皮；但无硼合金生成更大、更深且更伸长的Ti/Nb富集亚表面晶界氮化物。表征确认该颗粒为化学复杂的B1结构氮化物，并观察到界面处的硼偏聚。

Conclusion: 微量合金化（trace alloying）可作为合金设计策略，通过界面核心—外壳偏聚控制有害内部反应产物的析出与生长，从而影响高温合金抗氧化相关退化行为。

Abstract: Superalloys enable the hottest sections of jet engines to operate at high temperatures, but environmentally assisted degradation limits their lifetime. In polycrystalline alloys, nitrogen penetrates beneath the protective surface oxides, to produce embrittling grain boundary nitrides. Boron is added in trace amounts to improve grain boundary strength, yet its role, if any, in oxidation-related degradation remains unclear. Here we show that boron segregates to an internal nitride--matrix interface in a Ni/Co-base $γ$/$γ^{\prime}$ strengthened superalloy. Both B-free and B-containing alloys develop stratified chromia/alumina scales at 800~$^{\circ}$C in \SI{100}{\hour}, but the B-free variant produces larger, deeper and more elongated Ti/Nb-rich subsurface grain boundary nitrides. Correlative 4D S/TEM and atom probe tomography identify the particle as a chemically complex B1 nitride and reveal interfacial boron segregation. This shows that trace alloying as an alloy strategy can control damaging internal reaction product precipitation and growth by core-shell segregation.

</details>


### [83] [How good are universal MLIPs for alloys? A benchmark is only as trustworthy as its controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30641)
*Gus L. W. Hart,Jacob N. Pursley*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以独立DFT数据库（AFLOW）作为参照，对14个通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）在145万种有序金属间化合物（ordered intermetallics）上进行基准测试，指出数据库间分歧（disagreement）构成任何模型可测误差的下限，重新参考（re-referenced）后最佳模型的误差逼近该下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有uMLIPs以DFT数据库的生成能误差排名，默认该数据库为真值（ground truth），但独立DFT数据库彼此不一致，导致排名与误差评估可能存在系统性偏差，需量化这种分歧对评测的影响。

Method: 在145万种有序金属间化合物上，用未参与训练的AFLOW数据库对14个uMLIPs进行基准测试，并将每个能量与稳定性误差同AFLOW、Alexandria、OQMD三库之间的分歧逐一比较；同时筛查元素参考态（elemental references）错误并做相应控制分析。

Result: 半数结构的数据库间分歧小于6–8 meV/atom，少数结构分歧极大，使平均值升至16–30 meV/atom；重新参考后最佳uMLIP与AFLOW的一致性达到Alexandria的水平，其后四个模型位于下限之上2–7 meV/atom；筛查元素参考态错误后，能量与稳定性排名几乎处处一致。

Conclusion: 数据库间分歧为模型可测误差设定了下限，uMLIPs的实际性能差异被高估；在恰当控制下，三项表面上的模型失效（剪切模量相关性差、数千条“坏”DFT参考、领先者被夸大的优势）显著缩小或消失。

Abstract: Universal machine-learned interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) are ranked by their formation-energy error against a DFT database, as if that database were ground truth, but independent DFT databases disagree. We benchmark fourteen uMLIPs on 1.45 million ordered intermetallics against AFLOW, which none trained on, and compare every energy and stability error with that disagreement. For half the structures, AFLOW, Alexandria, and OQMD differ by less than 6-8 meV/atom, but a minority differ by far more, raising the average to 16-30. That disagreement sets a floor on any model's measurable error. Re-referenced, the best uMLIP agrees with AFLOW as closely as Alexandria does, and the next four sit 2-7 meV/atom above the floor. Once broken elemental references are screened, the energy and stability rankings agree almost everywhere. Three apparent model failures shrink or vanish under the right control: poorly correlated shear moduli, thousands of "bad" DFT references, and the leader's inflated lead.

</details>


### [84] [From phase analysis to phase tracking: a robust approach to atomic-scale strain mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30665)
*Ying Han,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出几何相位追踪分析（geometric phase tracking analysis, GPTA），使傅里叶孔径（Fourier aperture）在实空间中随每个像素的布拉格反射（Bragg reflection）移动，在13,795幅模拟与实验图像上将几何相位分析（geometric phase analysis, GPA）的失败率从27%降至11.5%，精度最高提升335倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子尺度应变（atomic-scale strain）决定材料的电子、铁性与力学性质，原子分辨显微术可最直接测量应变；但标准工具GPA在强应变或转动使布拉格反射移出固定傅里叶孔径时，或取向失配晶粒中会失效。

Method: GPTA使傅里叶孔径在实空间逐像素追踪布拉格反射，从而可用窄孔径抑制噪声而不丢失位移后的反射；在13,795幅涵盖多种成像条件的模拟与实验原子分辨图像上进行评估。

Result: GPA失败率由27%降至11.5%，精度最高提升335倍；GPTA还可给出跨晶粒的应变与位错分布图、对动态散射消光（dynamical-scattering extinction）的抵抗能力以及置信度图（confidence map），这些为GPA所不具备。

Conclusion: 孔径追踪将倒空间（reciprocal space）的剂量效率与实空间的空间分辨率结合起来，把原子尺度应变计量（strain metrology）拓展至束敏感材料与苛刻成像条件。

Abstract: Atomic-scale strain governs the electronic, ferroic and mechanical properties of materials, and atomic-resolution microscopy measures it most directly. Geometric phase analysis (GPA) is the standard tool, but it fails when a Bragg reflection moves outside its fixed Fourier aperture under large strain or rotation, or in misoriented grains. Here we introduce geometric phase tracking analysis (GPTA), whose Fourier aperture tracks the Bragg reflection at each pixel in real space. A narrow aperture can therefore reject noise without losing a displaced reflection. On 13,795 simulated and experimental atomic-resolution images under various imaging conditions, GPTA reduces GPA's failure rate from 27% to 11.5% with up to 335 times better accuracy. It also gives strain and dislocation maps across grains, resistance to dynamical-scattering extinction, and a confidence map, capabilities that GPA lacks. Tracking the aperture combines reciprocal-space dose efficiency with real-space spatial resolution and extends atomic-scale strain metrology to beam-sensitive materials and challenging imaging conditions.

</details>


### [85] [Universal Synthesis of Large-Area Hybrid Perovskite Monolayers Enabling Lithography-Free, Low-Static-Power Optoelectronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30764)
*Shankeerthan Kalyanasundaram,Swarit Dwivedi,Sanje Mahasivam,Christopher R. McNeill,Sudha Mokkapati*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出空间位阻工程三元溶剂生长策略，抑制面外堆叠，合成横向尺寸超过1000 μm2的单层及少层A2MX4杂化钙钛矿，并实现超低暗电流平面光电器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶液相合成单层杂化有机-无机钙钛矿（hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites）受限于快速面外晶体生长（out-of-plane crystal growth）、不可控成核与强层间聚集，所得薄片横向尺寸仅数微米，限制其器件应用。

Method: 采用空间位阻工程（steric-engineering）三元溶剂生长策略：醇类共溶剂（cosolvents）与胺类添加剂联用以抑制面外堆叠；结合分子动力学（molecular dynamics）与量子化学（quantum-chemical）计算解析溶剂化重组与边缘介导前驱体传输机制，并验证该策略在多种有机间隔阳离子、金属中心与卤素上的普适性。

Result: 以(PEA)2CuCl4为模型体系获得横向面积超过1000 μm2的单层；长链醇重组长程溶剂化，溶剂挥发性在退火中延长边缘介导传输。基于孤立超薄(PEA)2PbI4沟道制备的平面器件暗电流低至10^-15–10^-13 A，5 V下静态功耗约0.2 pW，较滴涂薄膜降低250倍，光暗电流比达4.3×10^2。

Conclusion: 精确的空间位阻与溶剂化调控可作为实现可规模化、组分多样、低静态功耗的单层二维钙钛矿光电子学的范式；大横向尺寸支持免溶剂物理掩模接触制备，规避传统光刻溶剂导致的化学降解。

Abstract: Solution-phase synthesis of monolayer hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites is constrained by rapid out-of-plane crystal growth, uncontrolled nucleation, and strong interlayer aggregation, yielding flakes of only a few μm. We introduce a steric-engineering ternary-solvent growth strategy to synthesize monolayer and few-layer A2MX4 hybrid perovskites with exceptionally large surface areas. By combining alcohol cosolvents with an amine additive, out-of-plane stacking is suppressed. Using (PEA)2CuCl4 as a model system, monolayers with lateral areas exceeding 1000 μm2 are achieved, representing a massive dimensional increase over conventional methods. Molecular dynamics and quantum-chemical calculations show that longer-chain alcohols reorganize precursor solvation, while solvent volatility prolongs edge-mediated precursor transport during annealing. This strategy demonstrates universal applicability across diverse organic spacer cations (PEA+, F-PEA+, PentA+), metal centers (Cu2+, Mn2+, Pb2+), and halides (Cl-, Br-, I-). The large lateral dimensions enable direct, solvent-free electrical contact fabrication via physical shadow masking, bypassing chemical degradation from solvents used in conventional lithography. Planar devices fabricated from isolated ultrathin (PEA)2PbI4 channels exhibit ultra-low dark currents down to the 10^-15 - 10^-13 A range, enabling femtowatt-to-picowatt static power consumption, ~ 0.2 pW at 5 V, a 250-fold reduction relative to drop-cast films, while maintaining a high light-to-dark current ratio of 4.3 x 102. This work establishes precise steric and solvation control as a paradigm for scalable, compositionally diverse, low-static-power monolayer 2D perovskite optoelectronics.

</details>


### [86] [Designing sustainable high-entropy alloys directly from mixed oxides: A thermodynamic perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30870)
*Albert Linda,Shiv Shankar,Dierk Raabe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文建立基于CALPHAD的热力学框架，界定CoCrFeMnNi基混合氧化物完全金属化所需的成分—温度—H₂/H₂O窗口，并揭示固溶体耦合平衡对还原难度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢基直接还原（hydrogen-based direct reduction, HyDR）可绕过传统冶金中分步提取、精炼与重熔流程，但多元氧化物完全金属化的热力学极限及最佳H₂/H₂O比尚未确立，原因是金属与氧化物固溶体改变了各组元化学活度，其耦合平衡无法由纯氧化物稳定性推得。

Method: 采用基于CALPHAD（计算相图）的热力学框架，针对CoCrFeMnNi基混合氧化物体系，计算并构建成分—温度—H₂/H₂O的完全金属化窗口，分析残余氧化物稳定性与H₂/H₂O比之间的关系。

Result: Mn与Cr总体上提高氧化物稳定性，Co、Fe、Ni则降低稳定性从而允许在更低H₂/H₂O比下还原；各元素的作用并非固定，取决于初始混合氧化物成分，并可在最后被还原的氧化物发生变化时反转。还原路径复杂度与最终脱氧难度不相关：中间转变步骤少的体系仍可能以高稳定残余氧化物终止，而广泛的部分还原未必改变最终残余氧化物的稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过将残余氧化物稳定性与H₂/H₂O比关联，该框架明确了完全金属化的热力学极限，并为直接由混合氧化物前驱体设计可持续多元合金提供指导准则。

Abstract: Hydrogen-based direct reduction (HyDR) of mixed oxide precursors offers an alternative route to produce sustainable alloys, bypassing the sequential extraction, refining, and remelting steps that dominate the carbon footprint of conventional metallurgy and individual metal production. However, the thermodynamic limits and most effective H$_2$/H$_2$O ratio needed for full metallization of multicomponent oxides are not yet established. This is because the formation of metallic and oxide solid solutions alters the chemical activity of both the reduced metallic phases and the unreduced oxides, coupling their equilibria in ways that cannot be deduced from the stability of the pure individual oxides. Herein, we develop a CALPHAD-based thermodynamic framework to establish the compoistion-temperature-H$_2$/H$_2$O window for complete metallization of CoCrFeMnNi-based oxide mixtures. Mn and Cr generally increase oxide stability, while Co, Fe, and Ni decrease it, allowing reduction at lower H$_2$/H$_2$O ratios. The effect of each element is not fixed; it depends on the starting mixed oxide composition and can reverse when the last oxide to reduce changes. Furthermore, we show that the reduction pathway complexity does not correlate with the difficulty of removing the final oxygen: a system with few intermediate transformation steps can still terminate as a highly stable residual oxide, whereas extensive partial reduction may leave the stability of the final residual oxide essentially unchanged. By linking residual-oxide stability to the H$_2$/H$_2$O ratio, the framework identifies the thermodynamic limits and provides guidelines for designing sustainable multicomponent alloys directly from mixed oxide precursors.

</details>


### [87] [Thermal expansion mechanisms, heat transport, and band-gap renormalization in strongly anharmonic Rb$_2$SO$_4$, Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ from machine-learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30918)
*Ekaterina Spirande,Rustam Arabov,Sofya Demina,Nikita Rybin,Alexander Shapeev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用主动学习矩张量势的分子动力学方法，研究了三种结构复杂硫酸盐/硼酸盐的晶格动力学，揭示其非谐性、热膨胀机制、热导与带隙重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 Rb$_2$SO$_4$、Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$、Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ 热膨胀的解释仅基于高温 X 射线衍射（x-ray diffraction）推测的多面体重排机制，缺乏晶格动力学层面的验证与定量分析。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（density-functional theory）数据，通过主动学习（active learning）训练矩张量势（moment tensor potentials），并开展分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟；计算非谐性度量 $σ^A$、有限温度声子稳定性、热膨胀、Green-Kubo 晶格热导率及声子诱导带隙重整化（band-gap renormalization）。

Result: $σ^A$ 在 $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$、Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$、Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ 中约为 0.3–0.5，在取向无序的 $α$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$ 中约 0.75；虚频谐波模式在有限温度下被稳定。模拟重现实测热膨胀并支持衍射提出的机制。$σ^A$ 相近的化合物热导率相差逾一个数量级；带隙重整化在 1250 K 分别达 2.7 与 1.7 eV，在 650 K 达 1.4 eV，零点重整化为 0.64、0.70、0.31 eV。

Conclusion: 晶格动力学模拟验证了基于衍射的热膨胀机理，并表明非谐性度量 $σ^A$ 单独不能决定热输运，同时量化了显著的有限温度带隙重整化。

Abstract: Rb$_2$SO$_4$, Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ are structurally complex materials whose thermal expansion has been interpreted, on the basis of high-temperature x-ray diffraction, in terms of specific polyhedral rearrangements. We study their lattice dynamics and its consequences for thermal and electronic properties using molecular dynamics with actively learned moment tensor potentials trained on density-functional theory data. The anharmonicity measure $σ^A$ (compares the anharmonic contribution to the interatomic forces with the total forces acting on atoms) reaches $\approx$0.3-0.5 for $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$, Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ and $\approx$0.75 for orientationally disordered $α$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$, and the imaginary harmonic modes of Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$ and $α$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$ are stabilized at finite temperature. The simulations reproduce the measured thermal expansion and support the mechanisms proposed from diffraction: expansion along $c$ in $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$ arises from both elongation and straightening of corrugated Rb chains, BO$_3$ reorientation accompanies the anisotropic expansion of Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$, and bending of polyhedral linkages limits expansion along the weakly expanding directions and in cubic Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$. Green-Kubo lattice thermal conductivities differ by more than an order of magnitude between compounds with comparable $σ^A$, showing that the anharmonicity measure alone does not determine heat transport. Phonon-induced band-gap renormalization, including lattice thermal expansion, reaches 2.7 and 1.7 eV at 1250 K in Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$ and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ and 1.4 eV at 650 K in $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$, with zero-point renormalizations of 0.64, 0.70, and 0.31 eV for $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$, Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$, respectively.

</details>


### [88] [Piezo-Kerr effect in altermagnets and noncollinear antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30929)
*Yuqi Liu,Qiu-Shi Huang,Xinfeng Chen,Yilin Han,Run-Wu Zhang,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将表征匹配原理（representation-matching principle）从序参量共轭场推广到响应张量，提出“压电克尔效应（piezo-Kerr effect）”：在无应变时克尔响应为零的磁点群中，特定应变组合可线性激活并反转克尔响应，并经第一性原理计算在MnTe₂、CoF₂及Cr₂Se₂O/Cr₂Te₂O单层中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性通常被视为对物理响应的约束，但亦可反过来用于设计控制场。作者希望找到一条通用途径，在对称性禁戒（symmetry-forbidden）的补偿磁体（compensated magnets）中产生大且可切换的克尔响应。

Method: 采用表征匹配原理：当控制场的某一分量与所选响应分量共享系统磁点群（magnetic point group, MPG）的同一不可约表示（irreducible representation）时，线性耦合被对称性允许。将该原理应用于磁光克尔效应（magneto-optical Kerr effect）以推导压电克尔效应，并对全部122个MPG进行枚举筛选。

Result: 枚举122个MPG，识别出16个群：无应变克尔响应被禁戒，而线性应变诱导贡献被允许；这些群可由交错磁体（altermagnets）与净磁化被对称性强制为零的非共线反铁磁体（noncollinear antiferromagnets）实现。第一性原理计算在块体MnTe₂、CoF₂及Cr₂Se₂O、Cr₂Te₂O单层中证实：无应变时克尔旋转为零，0.5–3%应变产生数十至数百毫度（millidegrees）的旋转，反转匹配的应变通道即反转克尔信号符号。

Conclusion: 研究确立了从补偿磁体的对称性禁戒态出发，获得大且可切换克尔响应的一般性路线；活性应变通道与一阶符号反转均可直接由对称性推得。

Abstract: Symmetry is usually viewed as a constraint on physical responses, but it can also be used to design the fields that control them. Here, we adopt the representation-matching principle used to identify conjugate fields of order parameters and apply it to response tensors. A linear coupling, the key ingredient for sign-switching control, is symmetry-allowed when a component (or a combination of components) of the control field and the selected response component share the same irreducible representation of the magnetic point group (MPG) of the system. Applied to the magneto-optical Kerr effect, this principle yields a piezo-Kerr effect, in which specific strain combinations activate a Kerr response forbidden in the unstrained state. Enumerating all 122 MPGs, we identify 16 groups in which the unstrained Kerr response is forbidden while a linear strain-induced contribution is allowed. These groups are realized by altermagnets and noncollinear antiferromagnets with symmetry-enforced vanishing net magnetization. The active strain channels and the associated first-order sign reversals follow directly from symmetry. First-principles calculations for bulk MnTe$_{2}$ and CoF$_{2}$, and Cr$_{2}$Se$_{2}$O and Cr$_{2}$Te$_{2}$O monolayers confirm these predictions. In these materials, the Kerr rotation vanishes without strain, whereas 0.5-3% strain produces rotations ranging from tens to hundreds of millidegrees, and reversing the matched strain channel reverses the Kerr sign. These results establish a general route to large and switchable Kerr responses emerging from symmetry-forbidden states in compensated magnets.

</details>


### [89] [Chiroptical Valleytronic in SnSe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30930)
*Yiming Pan,Sotirios Fragkos,Umberto Dellasette,Quentin Courtade,Dominique Descamps,Stéphane Petit,Fabio Caruso,Samuel Beaulieu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文结合时间/角分辨极紫外光电子能谱与从头算实时载流子动力学模拟，在SnSe中揭示了由量子几何张量支配的圆二色性谷选择定则，并表明其诱导的谷极化在亚皮秒尺度经电子-声子谷间散射衰减，理论上可产生螺旋度可反转的瞬态光电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布洛赫电子的量子几何性质（quantum geometry）在固体光-物质相互作用与输运中起核心作用，但量子几何如何约束手性光学选择定则、谷极化及其弛豫仍缺乏微观认识。

Method: 以IV族单硫族化合物SnSe为对象，结合时间分辨与角分辨极紫外光电子能谱（time- and angle-resolved extreme-ultraviolet photoemission spectroscopy）与从头算实时光激发载流子动力学模拟（ab initio real-time simulations）。

Result: 观测到圆偏振光打破时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）后在相反晶体动量处产生谷选择性激发；理论上证明谷圆二色性（valley circular dichroism）大小受晶体对称性通过量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor）的强约束，手性诱导谷极化经电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）介导的谷间散射在亚皮秒尺度衰减，且相反动量谷间布居失衡可产生螺旋度可反转的瞬态光电流（helicity-reversible transient photocurrent）。

Conclusion: 研究从微观层面阐明量子几何如何支配具有隐藏与瞬态对称性破缺（hidden and transient symmetry breaking）的范德华材料（van der Waals materials）中的谷选择性光-物质相互作用、谷极化与光电流产生。

Abstract: Quantum geometric properties of Bloch electrons play a central role in governing light-matter interactions and transport phenomena in solids. Here, we report quantum-geometric chiroptical selection rules that generate a helicity-dependent momentum-space population imbalance in the group-IV monochalcogenide SnSe. By combining time- and angle-resolved extreme-ultraviolet photoemission spectroscopy with ab initio real-time simulations of photoexcited carrier dynamics, we identify valley-selective excitation at opposite crystal momenta arising from the breaking of time-reversal symmetry under circularly polarized illumination. We theoretically demonstrate that the magnitude of valley circular dichroism is strongly constrained by crystal symmetry through its influence on the quantum geometric tensor, the key quantity underlying valley optical selection rules. The resulting chiroptically induced valley polarization decays on sub-picosecond timescales through intervalley scattering mediated by electron-phonon coupling. Furthermore, we show theoretically that the population imbalance between valleys at opposite momenta gives rise to a helicity-reversible transient photocurrent. These findings provide microscopic insight into how quantum geometry governs valley-selective light-matter interactions, valley polarization, and photocurrent generation in van der Waals materials exhibiting hidden and transient symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [90] [Intensity-driven Mott-like transition in a 2D Ruddlesden-Popper perovskite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30964)
*Koushik Mondal,Amit Haldar,Indrajit Mondal,Arpita Dutta,Himanshu Bhatt,Biswajit Mahata,Soumili Banerjee,Hirendra Nath Ghosh,Satyaprasad P Senanayak,Shovon Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以1030 nm红外飞秒脉冲研究二维Ruddlesden-Popper型卤化物钙钛矿(BA)₂PbI₄的非线性光学响应与超快载流子动力学，观测到随泵浦强度由可饱和吸收（saturable absorption）向反可饱和吸收（reverse saturable absorption）的转变，并归因于类莫特（Mott-like）相变，即由激子主导区向稠密电子-空穴等离子体区的转变；该强且可切换的非线性响应使其适用于红外波段全光操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Ruddlesden-Popper型卤化物钙钛矿因强激子效应（excitonic effects）与载流子密度依赖的光学响应，被视为有前景的前沿非线性光学材料；论文旨在探究(BA)₂PbI₄在红外激发下的非线性光学性质与超快载流子动力学。

Method: 采用1030 nm泵浦脉冲的非线性飞秒Z扫描（Z-scan）测量，激发条件与其激子跃迁的双光子能量共振；并在相同激发强度下进行强度依赖的瞬态吸收光谱（transient absorption spectroscopy）以解析载流子动力学。

Result: Z扫描显示非线性吸收系数（nonlinear absorption coefficient）与折射率（refractive index）呈显著的强度依赖演化；随泵浦强度增大，出现由可饱和吸收向反可饱和吸收的切换。瞬态吸收光谱表明，在估计的莫特密度（Mott density）之上存在长寿命载流子布居，反映由激子主导区向稠密电子-空穴等离子体（electron-hole plasma）区的明确相变。

Conclusion: 该强度驱动、强且可切换的非线性光学响应以及红外脉冲激发下的类莫特相变，使二维(BA)₂PbI₄钙钛矿适用于红外波段的全光操作（all-optical operation）。

Abstract: Two-dimensional Ruddlesden-Popper-type halide perovskites have emerged as promising cutting-edge nonlinear optical materials due to their strong excitonic effects and carrier-density-dependent optical responses. In this work, we investigate the nonlinear optical properties and ultrafast carrier dynamics of (BA)$_2$PbI$_4$ under infrared excitation, resonant with the two-photon energy of its excitonic transition. Using a nonlinear femtosecond Z-scan measurement with a 1030\,nm pump pulse, we observe a pronounced intensity-dependent evolution of the nonlinear absorption coefficient and the refractive index. With increasing pump intensity, a switch from saturable absorption to reverse saturable absorption emerges, suggesting an intensity-driven Mott-like transition. To elucidate the underlying carrier dynamics, intensity-dependent transient absorption spectroscopy was performed under the same excitation intensities. This reveals a long-lived charge carrier population above the estimated Mott density, reflecting a clear phase transition from an exciton-dominated regime to a dense electron-hole plasma regime. Moreover, such an intensity-driven, high, and switchable nonlinear optical response, along with a Mott-like transition under infrared pulse excitation, makes the 2D (BA)$_2$PbI$_4$ perovskites suitable for all-optical operation in the infrared regime.

</details>


### [91] [Donor-acceptor recombination in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30977)
*Konstantin Iakoubovskii,Andrey Katrusha,Weihua Peng,Jianguo Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文重新审视金刚石中此前被归因于施主-受主（donor-acceptor, D-A）复合的若干光学谱带（2.8 eV、2.3 eV 宽带及 2.0–2.7 eV、4.0–5.0 eV 锐线系），认为缺陷中心内部跃迁（internal transitions）比 D-A 模型更能一致地解释实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献将金刚石中多个光学特征归因于 D-A 复合，但该解释与极低杂质浓度下的物理图像存在矛盾，因此有必要重新检验这些归属的合理性。

Method: 对多个光学系统逐一进行定性再分析，将 D-A 复合模型与单一或多个缺陷中心内部跃迁（internal transitions at one or multiple defect centers）的解释进行对比；针对 2.3 eV 情形，以隧穿概率论证（tunneling probability）作为关键判据。

Result: 所有考察情形下，缺陷中心内部跃迁均给出比 D-A 模型更一致的实验解释；2.3 eV 情形处于临界状态，反对 D-A 复合的核心论据是在杂质浓度极低（约 0.1 ppm）的金刚石中，远距离施主与受主间隧穿概率低得不合理。

Conclusion: 2.3 eV 谱带及其相关磷光（phosphorescence）与热释光（thermoluminescence）现象的更现实模型是：电子与空穴分别被氮缺陷和硼缺陷俘获（trapping），随后在深中心（deep center）复合，该深中心暂定为最近邻 B-N 对（nearest neighbor B-N pair）。

Abstract: We re-examine several optical systems that have previously been attributed to donor-acceptor (D-A) recombination in diamond, including the broad 2.8 eV and 2.3 eV bands, and the sharp-lined series in the ranges 2.0-2.7 eV and 4.0-5.0 eV. In all cases, internal transitions at one or multiple defect centers provide a more consistent explanation of the experimental observations than the D-A model. The 2.3 eV case is marginal, where the key argument against the D-A recombination is the improbably low probability of tunneling between remote donors and acceptors in diamonds with very low concentrations of impurities (~0.1 ppm). A more realistic model for the 2.3 eV band and the associated phosphorescence and thermoluminescence phenomena is trapping of electrons and holes at nitrogen and boron defects, respectively, followed by recombination at a deep center, which is tentatively assigned to the nearest neighbor B-N pair.

</details>


### [92] [Role of Chalcogen-p States in the Optical Response of Bulk PtSe2 and PtTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30978)
*Ji Eun Lee,R. Mathew Roy,Premakumar Yanda,Chandra Shekhar,Claudia Felser,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文通过变温红外反射率测量结合能带分辨的密度泛函计算，揭示同构同价的PtSe₂与PtTe₂电子性质差异源于硫族元素p带能级位置不同，而非狄拉克锥间跃迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PtSe₂与PtTe₂为同构同价的层状II型狄拉克半金属（type-II Dirac semimetal），却表现出显著不同的电子性质，其微观起源尚待澄清。

Method: 在10–300 K温区测量体单晶的面内红外反射率（in-plane infrared reflectivity），提取光电导的带间贡献并与能带分辨的密度泛函（density-functional）计算对比。

Result: 低温下PtTe₂的Drude谱重（Drude spectral weight）比PtSe₂高一个数量级；PtTe₂呈现宽的中红外吸收连续谱（mid-infrared absorption continuum），而PtSe₂吸收较弱，计算将其归因于硫族p带向Pt-d带的跃迁。

Conclusion: PtTe₂中硫族p带能量更高，降低该类跃迁的起始能量并增大半金属能带重叠，与更大的Drude谱重一致；II型狄拉克点深低于费米能级，故形成狄拉克锥的能带间跃迁对带间电导贡献甚微。

Abstract: The type-II Dirac semimetals PtSe$_2$ and PtTe$_2$ are isostructural and isovalent layered compounds but exhibit distinct electronic properties. We measure the in-plane infrared reflectivity of bulk crystals between 10 and 300 K and compare the interband contribution to the optical conductivity with band-resolved density-functional calculations. At low temperature, the Drude spectral weight of PtTe$_2$ is an order of magnitude larger than that of PtSe$_2$. PtTe$_2$ also shows a broad mid-infrared absorption continuum, whereas the absorption of PtSe$_2$ remains weak. The calculations attribute this continuum to transitions from a predominantly chalcogen-$p$ band into a Pt-$d$ band. In PtTe$_2$, the chalcogen-$p$ band lies higher in energy, which lowers the onset of these transitions and increases the semimetallic band overlap, in line with the larger Drude spectral weight. The type-II Dirac points lie far below the Fermi level, so that transitions between the bands forming the Dirac cones do not contribute appreciably to the interband conductivity.

</details>


### [93] [Nitrogen vacancy cluster-mediated ferroelectric domain growth in wurtzite AlN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31084)
*Zijian Zhou,Kan-Hao Xue,Ziqi Pang,Heng Yu,Shanzhong Xie,Hang Liu,Haiming Qin,Yi Tong,Bowen Su,Yi Yang,Tian-Ling Ren,Xiangshui Miao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合密度泛函理论、深度势能分子动力学与实验表征，揭示氮空位调控AlN基铁电体畴生长与矫顽场（E_c）的多尺度机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlN基铁电体因高剩余极化、热稳定等优势适用于高密度非易失存储器，但高矫顽场及极化翻转机制不清限制了应用；且理论与实验对反向畴生长方向存在分歧（沿极性轴 vs 近各向同性或垂直极性轴）。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）、深度势能分子动力学（Deep Potential molecular dynamics）与实验表征相结合的多尺度方法，考察氮空位（V_N）对畴生长动力学和E_c的调控。

Result: 理想晶体中沿极性轴的强晶格耦合导致该方向优先畴生长；V_N局域削弱该耦合并促进极化形核，V_N的空间分布与空位诱导翻转的时间序列共同决定反向畴的方向与形貌；Sc掺杂促进V_N形成，增强缺陷介导形核并降低E_c。

Conclusion: 研究建立了支配薄膜畴生长动力学与宏观E_c的多尺度机制，弥合了实验观测与理想晶体模拟的分歧，并为低压、可靠、可扩展的铁电器件提供缺陷工程原则。

Abstract: Aluminum nitride-based ferroelectrics combine high remanent polarization, thermal stability, weak size effects, a wide bandgap and back-end process compatibility, making them promising for high density non-volatile memory. However, their high coercive field ($E_\mathrm{c}$) and unclear polarization switching mechanism limit further applications. Theoretical predictions indicate preferential reversed domain growth along the polar axis, whereas experiments show nearly isotropic growth or preferential growth perpendicular to this axis. Here, we combine density functional theory, Deep Potential molecular dynamics and experimental characterization to determine how nitrogen vacancies ($V_\mathrm{N}$) regulate domain growth and $E_\mathrm{c}$. In ideal crystal, strong lattice coupling along the polar axis drives preferential domain growth in this direction. $V_\mathrm{N}$ locally weakens this coupling and promotes polarization nucleation. The spatial distribution of $V_\mathrm{N}$ and the temporal sequence of vacancy induced switching jointly determine the direction and morphology of reversed domain growth. We further show that scandium doping promotes $V_\mathrm{N}$ formation, thereby enhancing defect mediated nucleation and reducing $E_\mathrm{c}$. These findings establish a multiscale mechanism governing thin-film domain-growth kinetics and macroscopic $E_\mathrm{c}$, reconciling experimental observations with ideal crystal simulations and providing defect engineering principles for low-voltage, reliable and scalable ferroelectric devices.

</details>


### [94] [The often overlooked role of sound in liquid thermal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31127)
*Kamran Behnia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文回顾百年前提出的布里奇曼液体导热公式（Bridgman formula），指出其将声速与热导率联系并暗示液体中声子（phonon）为热载流子，但该经验规律的概念意义长期被忽视，且未被主流凝聚态教材收录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布里奇曼公式的经验成功未获充分重视，其概念含义被忽略，且未见于知名凝聚态物理教科书；作者认为有必要重新审视其理论地位与物理内涵。

Method: 采用综述性分析，梳理该公式的起源，考察其与流体动力学欧拉方程（Euler equations）的关联，并讨论其与抛物线型热方程（parabolic heat equation）所涉基本悖论的关系，以及其在量子—经典交叉（quantum-classical crossover）中的演变。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果，仅指出该公式将声速与热导率视为成正比，从而把声子确立为液体中的热载体，并揭示其与欧拉方程及热传导悖论存在联系。

Conclusion: 布里奇曼公式具有被低估的理论价值，应被视为理解液体热输运（thermal transport）与量子—经典过渡的重要线索，值得在凝聚态物理框架内重新评价。

Abstract: Formulated a hundred years ago, the Bridgman formula for thermal conductivity of liquids postulates a proportionality between sound velocity and thermal conductivity. This designates phonons as heat carriers in a liquid. Its empirical success has been under-appreciated and its conceptual implications overlooked. It is not mentioned in well-known condensed-matter textbook. Here, we review its origin, its links to hydrodynamic Euler equations and to the fundamental paradox surrounding the parabolic heat equation and its fate across the quantum-classical crossover.

</details>


### [95] [Tracking the Emergence and Persistence of Abnormal Grain Growth in an Aluminum Alloy using Diffraction-Contrast Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31176)
*Helmuth-André Schulz-Harder,Karolína Gutbrod,Madlen Atzen,Markus Ziehmer,Thomas Wilhelm,Orkun Furat,Yubin Zhang,Jette Oddershede,Haixing Fang,Pierre-Olivier Autran,Wolfgang Ludwig,Marc Bernacki,Volker Schmidt,Carl E. Krill,Jules M. Dake*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究结合同步辐射衍射衬度断层成像（diffraction-contrast tomography）与相位衬度断层成像（phase-contrast tomography），对AA5252铝合金中异常晶粒长大（abnormal grain growth）进行三维追踪，发现第二相粒子密度与异常晶粒的出现和快速长大无显著关联，而晶界取向差与邻接晶粒尺寸是更强的预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常晶粒长大本质上是三维过程，需对晶粒演化进行三维追踪方能揭示其内在机制；同时，第二相粒子（second-phase particles）对基体晶粒长大的抑制作用与异常长大之间的关系尚待厘清。

Method: 采用同步辐射衍射衬度断层成像与相位衬度断层成像表征AA5252铝合金的三维晶粒结构，并量化第二相粒子的尺寸与空间分布；通过中断退火（interrupted annealing）方案，持续追踪异常晶粒的长大以及一个原属基体晶粒转为异常长大的过程。

Result: 第二相粒子虽有效抑制基体晶粒长大，但局部粒子密度差异与异常晶粒的出现或后续快速长大无明显相关性；晶体学取向差（crystallographic misorientation）尤其是邻接晶粒尺寸（neighboring grain size）是异常长大速率的更强预测因子。当基体晶粒较小且与长大中的异常晶粒共享大角度晶界（high-angle grain boundaries）时，其被消耗得更快；发生正常到异常转变的晶粒在转变前即被具有纯大角度晶界的小尺寸基体晶粒包围，而其局部粒子密度略高于平均水平。

Conclusion: 相同的微观结构特征（小尺寸邻接晶粒、大角度晶界）似乎同时促进异常晶粒长大的出现与持续；但触发向快速长大转变的机制仍未解决。

Abstract: Abnormal grain growth is inherently three-dimensional, making 3D tracking of grain evolution essential for understanding its underlying mechanisms. This study combines synchrotron-based diffraction-contrast tomography and phase-contrast tomography to characterize the three-dimensional grain structure and quantify the size and spatial distribution of second-phase particles in the aluminum alloy AA5252. Using an interrupted annealing protocol, we tracked the persistent growth of abnormal grains and the emergence of abnormal growth in a grain initially belonging to the matrix. Although second-phase particles effectively suppressed grain growth in the matrix, variations in local particle density showed no apparent correlation with either the emergence or subsequent rapid growth of abnormal grains. Instead, crystallographic misorientation and, more notably, neighboring grain size were identified as stronger predictors of abnormal growth rate. Matrix grains were consumed more rapidly when they were relatively small and shared high-angle grain boundaries with the growing abnormal grain. The same trends were observed for the grain that underwent a transition from normal to abnormal growth. Remarkably, even before the onset of abnormal growth, this grain was surrounded by smaller matrix grains with exclusively high-angle grain boundaries, while the local particle density was slightly higher than average. These findings suggest that the same microstructural characteristics facilitate both the emergence and persistence of abnormal grain growth, although the mechanism triggering the transition to rapid growth remains unresolved.

</details>


### [96] [Anomalous polar chains in halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31184)
*Erik Fransson,Apinya Ngoipala,Priyanka Deswal,Ethan Berger,Nicholas C. Bristowe,Paul Erhart,Julia Wiktor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）结合第一性原理训练（first-principles-trained）的原子间势，在卤化物钙钛矿（halide perovskite）CsGeBr₃中发现长程一维极性链（one-dimensional polar chains）与异常缓慢的动力学，并揭示了一种"逐链反转"的一维极化翻转机制（one-dimensional switching mechanism）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电钙钛矿（ferroelectric perovskites）中的局域极性畸变（local polar distortions）、其集体有序化及场致反转虽已被长期研究，但作者关注是否存在不同于传统三氧化合物铁电体的新行为，尤其是名义立方相（nominally cubic phase）中的局域极性与动力学特征。

Method: 采用大规模分子动力学模拟，配合由第一性原理训练得到的原子间势（interatomic potentials），对铁电卤化物钙钛矿 CsGeBr₃ 进行系统研究，并与 BaTiO₃、PbTiO₃ 及居中的 CsSnBr₃ 进行对比。

Result: 在名义立方相中，Ge 的偏心（Ge off-centering）形成延伸至少数十纳米、弛豫时间达纳秒量级的一维极性链（polar chains），其动力学比 BaTiO₃和 PbTiO₃慢四个数量级；立方相是动态无序态（dynamically disordered state），反向极化的链在整个晶体中不断翻转，长寿命一维局域极化与零净极化（zero net polarization）共存。在铁电相中，同样的链构成一维翻转机制，极化逐链（chain by chain）反转。

Conclusion: 一个简单的三维钙钛矿即可承载长程一维极性关联（one-dimensional polar correlations）及极其缓慢的动力学，表明卤化物钙钛矿中可能存在有别于传统铁电体的极化组织与翻转模式。

Abstract: Local polar distortions, their collective ordering, and their field-driven reversal have been studied extensively in ferroelectric perovskites for decades. Here we identify an unusual behavior in the ferroelectric halide perovskite CsGeBr3 using large-scale molecular dynamics with first-principles-trained interatomic potentials. In its nominally cubic phase the Ge off-centering forms one-dimensional polar chains that extend over at least tens of nanometers and relax on nanosecond timescales, four orders of magnitude slower than in BaTiO3 and PbTiO3, with CsSnBr3 in between. The cubic CsGeBr3 phase is a dynamically disordered state in which oppositely polarized chains flip throughout the crystal, and a long-lived one-dimensional local polarization coexists with zero net polarization. A simple three-dimensional perovskite thus hosts long-ranged one-dimensional polar correlations with remarkably slow dynamics. In the ferroelectric phase the same chains set a one-dimensional switching mechanism, in which the polarization reverses chain by chain.

</details>


### [97] [Mn-deficient Ni-Mn-Ga alloys: phase transitions, magnetic properties, and disappearance of martensitic transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31206)
*Valentina Moskvina,Jaroslav Čapek,Michal Rameš,Denys Musiienko,Petr Veřtát,Alexej Perevertov,Ladislav Straka,Oleg Heczko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用定向凝固形成的成分梯度（composition gradient）Ni-Mn-Ga 寡晶（oligocrystal），在相同生长与热处理条件下系统研究成分—结构—磁性关系；发现 Mn 含量与电子浓度（e/a）存在约 7.40–7.46 的突变阈值，并给出饱和磁化强度（saturation magnetization）随 Mn 含量的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向凝固过程中的成分梯度通常被视为 Ni-Mn-Ga 磁性形状记忆合金（magnetic shape-memory alloy）的不利缺陷，但该现象可使多组成分在同一生长与热处理历史下被比较，从而为系统研究成分—性能关系（composition-property relationships）提供有效平台。

Method: 采用光学区域重熔（optical zone remelting）生长一个贫 Mn 与两个近化学计量比 Ni-Mn-Ga 寡晶，并借助 X 射线荧光面扫描（X-ray fluorescence mapping）、光学显微、X 射线衍射（XRD）、低场磁测量与振动样品磁强计（vibrating-sample magnetometry）进行表征。

Result: 铸锭呈拉长晶粒与轴向成分梯度，主要由 Mn 与 Ga 的再分配造成，Ni 分布相对均匀；马氏体与预马氏体相变（martensitic and premartensitic transformation）温度总体随 Mn 含量升高。Mn 为 20.5–22.0 at.%（e/a < 7.40）的样品在 10–400 K 未检测到相变，而 Mn ≥ 22.1 at.%（e/a ≥ 7.46）的样品均出现两种相变，提示存在电子浓度阈值；饱和磁化强度随 Mn 含量增至 25.8 at.% 而升高，10 K 达 96.9 A m²/kg、300 K 达 71.2 A m²/kg。生长后退火（post-growth annealing）提高转变温度并改变低场磁响应。

Conclusion: 成分梯度寡晶可作为研究同一加工历史下 Ni-Mn-Ga 合金成分、结构与磁性能关系的有效平台，并将通常被视为缺陷的凝固偏析转化为成分筛选手段。

Abstract: Composition gradients formed during directional solidification are generally considered undesirable in Ni-Mn-Ga magnetic shape-memory alloys. However, these gradients offer a highly effective platform for systematically investigating composition-property relationships under identical growth and heat-treatment conditions. To exploit this approach, one Mn-deficient and two near-stoichiometric Ni-Mn-Ga oligocrystals were grown by optical zone remelting and characterized by X-ray fluorescence mapping, optical microscopy, X-ray diffraction, low-field magnetic measurements, and vibrating-sample magnetometry. The ingots contained elongated grains and axial compositional gradients, primarily associated with the redistribution of Mn and Ga, whereas Ni remained relatively uniformly distributed. The martensitic and premartensitic transformation temperatures generally increased with increasing Mn content. Neither transformation was detected between 10 and 400 K in specimens containing 20.5-22.0 at.% Mn, all of which had $e/a < 7.40$. In contrast, both transformations were observed in the investigated specimens containing at least 22.1 at.% ($e/a \geq 7.46$). This abrupt change may indicate a possible electron-concentration threshold for the occurrence of these transformations. Saturation magnetization increased with Mn content up to 25.8 at.%, reaching 96.9 A m$^2$/kg at 10 K and 71.2 A m$^2$/kg at 300 K. Post-growth annealing increased the transition temperatures and changed the low-field magnetic response. The results indicate that composition-gradient oligocrystals serve as an effective platform for investigating the relationships between composition, structure, and magnetic properties in Ni-Mn-Ga alloys with the same processing history.

</details>
