### [98] [Orbital-coherence memory in electrical transport and its self-consistent Maxwell response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31390)
*Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了一个约化描述，说明内部电子相干（electronic coherence）只有在反馈到物理电流时才改变电输运。基于反宇称双轨道模型与显式闭合/碰撞假设，得到具有指数衰减振荡记忆的三态无源响应，并给出可检验的预测与实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内电子相干是否以及如何影响电输运需要明确界定，尤其要区分反馈的严格结论与微观实现所需的额外假设，避免过度解读电导率数据。

Method: 构建电流耦合的约化理论：用反宇称两轨道哈密顿量（opposite-parity two-orbital Hamiltonian）识别电流耦合的轨道力，在明确陈述的矩闭合（moment closure）与碰撞拟设（collision ansatz）下导出三态响应，并作电荷守恒的空间延拓以耦合麦克斯韦方程（Maxwell equations）。

Result: 响应呈无源三态形式，带指数阻尼的振荡记忆；电导率重新分配固定光学权重（optical weight），并在固定其余参数时预测直流电导对相干阻尼的非单调依赖。轨道约化通过共享的裸能级劈裂（bare splitting）约束耦合与内频率，提供超越无约束谱拟合的检验；空间延拓以正能量平衡分离本构响应与纵向集体模、横向电磁模。

Conclusion: 模型给出轨道动力学、输运记忆与电磁可观测量之间的条件性联系；仅凭电导率无法确立唯一量子机制，仍需材料特定的碰撞计算与闭合误差评估。

Abstract: An internal electronic coherence changes electrical transport only when it feeds back on the physical current. We develop a reduced description of this feedback and distinguish its exact consequences from the assumptions required for a microscopic realization. An opposite-parity two-orbital Hamiltonian identifies a current-coupled orbital force. Under an explicitly stated moment closure and collision ansatz, it yields a passive three-state response with an exponentially damped oscillatory memory. The resulting conductivity redistributes a fixed optical weight and predicts a nonmonotonic dependence of dc conduction on coherence damping at fixed remaining parameters. The orbital reduction constrains the coupling and internal frequency through a shared bare splitting, providing a test beyond unconstrained spectral fitting. A charge-conserving spatial extension couples to Maxwell equations through a positive energy balance and separates the constitutive response from longitudinal collective and transverse electromagnetic modes. We formulate complex-conductivity and transmission tests, including the limits of parameter identification over a finite bandwidth. The model supplies a conditional connection between orbital dynamics, transport memory, and electromagnetic observables; conductivity alone does not establish a uniquely quantum mechanism, and a material-specific collision calculation and closure-error assessment remain necessary.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

### [99] [Twisted scattering from Chern-Simons theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30232)
*Guillermo Arias-Tamargo,Philip Boyle Smith,Rishi Mouland,Max Velásquez Cotini Hutt*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维狄拉克费米子（Dirac fermions）保持非反常连续对称性的边界条件，将其等价为保持对称性的外尔费米子（Weyl fermions）拓扑缺陷，并利用陈-西蒙斯理论（Chern-Simons theory）给出一般构造，计算了扭结线（twist lines）、边界中心荷（boundary central charge）与 mod-2 SPT 类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是刻画左右手移动子（left- and right-movers）上作用不同的连续对称性所允许的二维狄拉克费米子边界条件，并通过与保持对称性的外尔费米子拓扑缺陷的等价性来系统研究此类边界。

Method: 对共形嵌入（conformally-embedded）对称性，使用陈-西蒙斯理论（Chern-Simons theory）给出这类缺陷的一般构造，并将其实现为某有限范畴对称性（finite categorical symmetry）的半空间规范缺陷（half-space gauging defects）；据此计算费米子散射穿过缺陷时附着的扭结线、对应折叠边界态（folded boundary state）的边界中心荷及其 mod-2 值 SPT 类。

Result: 此类缺陷总是存在且可构造；在阿贝尔（abelian）对称性情形下结果与文献已知结果一致。在新研究的保持 SU(2)×SU(4)×U(1)^2 对称性的例子中，扭结线本质上是非可逆的（non-invertible），并根据散射向左或向右分别属于 TY(Z_3×Z_3) 或 Rep(S_3)×Z_3 对称性。

Conclusion: 保持左右手作用不同连续对称性的狄拉克费米子边界可统一由陈-西蒙斯理论构造的拓扑缺陷描述，且非阿贝尔（non-abelian）情形下扭结线呈现本质非可逆性，与非阿贝尔对称性情形形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: We consider boundary conditions of two-dimensional Dirac fermions which preserve a non-anomalous continuous symmetry which acts differently on the left- and right-movers. Such boundaries are equivalent to topological defects of Weyl fermions preserving the symmetry. We give a general construction of such defects for conformally-embedded symmetries using Chern-Simons theory. They always exist and can be realised as half-space gauging defects for some finite categorical symmetry. Using this technology, we compute the twist lines to which fermions become attached upon scattering through the defect, the boundary central charge of the corresponding folded boundary state, and its mod-2 valued SPT class. As applications, we first consider abelian symmetries and find agreement with known results in the literature. We then study a novel example where an $SU(2) \times SU(4) \times U(1)^2$ symmetry is preserved. In contrast to the abelian case, the twist lines are intrinsically non-invertible and belong to either a $\text{TY}(\mathbb{Z}_3 \times \mathbb{Z}_3)$ or $\text{Rep}(\mathbb{S}_3) \times \mathbb{Z}_3$ symmetry depending on whether scattering is to the left or the right.

</details>


### [100] [Finding CFTs from spatial positivity of partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30234)
*Léo Buisine,Horacio Casini,Leandro Martinek*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出配分函数（partition function）的一个正性（positivity）性质，将其视为模不变性（modular invariance）的推广，并猜想其适用于一般二维共形场论（2D CFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模不变性虽是二维共形场论的核心约束，但可能并非最普适的性质；本文试图寻找一个更一般、以正性为核心的原则来刻画允许的二维共形场论。

Method: 通过研究该正性猜想与Rényi熵（Rényi entropy）正性性质的关系，并从拓扑缺陷线（topological defect line）的角度阐释其含义；进而将该猜想应用于构造局部酉（local unitary）共形场论模型。

Result: 建立了配分函数正性与Rényi熵正性之间的联系，并在拓扑缺陷线框架下给出该猜想的解释；利用该猜想得到了不必满足模不变性的自洽局部酉共形场论模型。

Conclusion: 该正性性质可作为模不变性的推广，为筛选和构造更一般的二维共形场论提供判据，并允许存在非模不变的局部酉模型。

Abstract: We discuss a positivity property for partition functions that generalizes modular invariance and which is here conjectured to hold for general 2D CFTs. We show its relation to positivity properties of Rényi entropies and its meaning in terms of topological defect lines. We apply the conjecture to find consistent local unitary CFT models which are not necessarily modular invariant.

</details>


### [101] [Collinear Gluons from Gravity: Poincaré-Birkhoff-Witt Decomposition and Twisted Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30235)
*Jin Dong,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文给出一种从爱因斯坦–杨–米尔斯理论（Einstein–Yang–Mills theory）及其弦修正中的单引力子振幅（one-graviton amplitudes）显式重构次领头共线杨–米尔斯振幅（subleading collinear Yang–Mills amplitudes）的方法，并借助 PBW 定理与扭交数（twisted intersection numbers）刻画重构系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共线极限下的杨–米尔斯振幅（collinear Yang–Mills amplitudes）与引力子振幅之间存在混合开–闭弦关系（mixed open-closed string relation），但次领头层次的重构机制及其系数结构尚待显式阐明。

Method: 将混合开–闭弦关系中的单值核（monodromy kernel）实现为胶子标号词上的加权李代数递推（weighted Lie-algebraic recursion）；利用 Poincaré–Birkhoff–Witt（PBW）定理把胶子排序张成的空间分解为维数由第一类无符号斯特林数（unsigned Stirling numbers of the first kind）给出的扇区，并用单引力子振幅的适当洗牌和（shuffle sums）提取相应次数扇区。

Result: 在 α'^r 阶，递推输出至多含 r 个李因子（Lie factors）；所提取的次数-r 扇区中弦周期矩阵（string-period matrix）消去，重构系数分解为线性运动学因子（linear kinematic factors）与二部划分因子（bipartition factors）之积的比值形式；重构系数可表示为 Parke–Taylor 形式与由对数四点微分（logarithmic four-point differentials）递推构造的显式森林余循环（forest cocycles）之间的扭交数。

Conclusion: 次领头共线杨–米尔斯振幅可由单引力子振幅经李代数递推与洗牌求和系统重构，其系数具有运动学因子化结构并可用扭交数（twisted intersection numbers）几何化表述。

Abstract: We give an explicit reconstruction of subleading collinear Yang--Mills amplitudes from one-graviton amplitudes in Einstein--Yang--Mills theory and its string corrections. We realize the monodromy kernel in the mixed open-closed string relation as a weighted Lie-algebraic recursion on words of gluon labels. At order $α'^r$, its output contains at most $r$ Lie factors. The Poincaré--Birkhoff--Witt (PBW) theorem decomposes the space spanned by gluon orderings into sectors whose dimensions are given by unsigned Stirling numbers of the first kind. Appropriate shuffle sums of one-graviton amplitudes extract the degree-$r$ sector, for which the string-period matrix drops out. Accordingly, we show that the reconstruction coefficients factor into linear kinematic factors over the product of bipartition factors. Finally, we express the reconstruction coefficients as twisted intersection numbers between Parke--Taylor forms and explicit forest cocycles constructed recursively from logarithmic four-point differentials.

</details>


### [102] [Reentrant $d=2+1$ Superfluidity at High Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30236)
*Subham Dutta Chowdhury,Joan Elias Miro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在 \(d=2+1\) 维具有整体 \(U(1)\) 对称性的紫外完备（UV-complete）模型，该模型在任意高温下仍保持 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）有序相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是构造一个紫外完备的 \(2+1\) 维 \(U(1)\) 模型，用以考察 BKT 有序相能否在任意高温下持续存在。

Method: 方法包括对尺度（scales）进行细致分离，导出二维欧氏有效场论（two-dimensional Euclidean effective field theory），其构造在软模（soft modes）强耦合时仍受微扰控制；并通过与该理论同已有格点蒙特卡洛（lattice Monte Carlo）结果的仔细匹配确定 BKT 相边界。

Result: 确定了 BKT 相边界；高温相支持无隙静模（gapless static modes），且当 \(T\to\infty\) 时关联仍呈幂律衰减（algebraically decaying correlations）。

Conclusion: 结论是存在一个紫外完备模型，其在任意高温下均展现 BKT 有序相，且高温相保持无隙静模与代数衰减关联。

Abstract: We present a UV-complete model with a global $U(1)$ symmetry in $d=2+1$ dimensions that exhibits a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) ordered phase at arbitrarily high temperatures. A careful separation of scales yields a two-dimensional Euclidean effective field theory whose construction remains perturbatively controlled even when its soft modes become strongly coupled. We determine the BKT phase boundary by carefully matching this theory to existing lattice Monte Carlo results. The high-temperature phase supports gapless static modes and algebraically decaying correlations even as $T\to\infty$.

</details>


### [103] [Defect flows from functional relations in the asymptotic semiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30242)
*Federico Ambrosino,Anatoly Konechny,Ingo Runkel,Gérard M. T. Watts*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究二维共形场论中拓扑缺陷（topological defects）受相关全纯缺陷场（holomorphic defect field）微扰后的重整化群流向，提出平移不变缺陷构成幺半范畴（monoidal category），并论证重整化群流定义了一个从平移不变缺陷半环到由拓扑缺陷构造的“渐近半环（asymptotic semiring）”的半环同态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缺陷的微扰及其在红外（infrared）极限下的归宿是理解缺陷重整化群流的关键问题；需要一个代数框架来约束缺陷流的端点。

Method: 以相关全纯缺陷场微扰拓扑缺陷，得到平移不变缺陷（translation invariant defects）；证明其构成幺半范畴，并论证重整化群流（renormalisation group flow）给出平移不变缺陷半环到渐近半环的半环同态（semiring homomorphism）。

Result: 该半环同态保持函数关系（functional relations）；在 Virasoro 极小模型（Virasoro minimal models）的例子中，渐近半环内由此产生的关系可约束缺陷流的端点。

Conclusion: 平移不变缺陷的代数结构与重整化群流的半环同态性质为缺陷流提供约束；伴随论文[1]对若干极小模型中的此类缺陷流进行了数值与解析的详细研究。

Abstract: We study perturbations of topological defects by a relevant holomorphic defect field in two-dimensional conformal field theory. This produces translation invariant defects and these necessarily flow to topological defects in the infrared. Translation invariant defects form a monoidal category which extends the fusion category of topological defects. We argue that the renormalisation group flow defines a semiring homomorphism from the semiring of translation invariant defects to an "asymptotic semiring" which we construct from topological defects. The semiring homomorphism preserves functional relations, and we illustrate in the example of Virasoro minimal models that the resulting relations in the asymptotic semiring constrain the defect flow endpoints. The companion paper [1] contains a detailed study of such defect flows in several minimal models, both numerically and analytically.

</details>


### [104] [From bulk flows to boundary generators and costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30246)
*Rafael Carrasco,Carlos Pérez-Pardavila*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出一种局部处方（local prescription），将特定体观测量（bulk observables）与由欧氏源（Euclidean sources）制备的态族上的二次代价几何（quadratic cost geometries）相联系，并以此考察全息复杂度（holographic complexity）与边界态制备代价之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息复杂度包含一大类体观测量，但其与边界态制备代价（boundary state-preparation costs）的对应关系尚不明确，因此需要建立体观测量选择与边界制备代价赋值之间的显式联系。

Method: 作者利用体哈密顿流（bulk Hamiltonian flows）向欧氏制备源的“全息输运（holographic transport）”，在静止参考态处取输运生成元的黑塞矩阵（Hessian）作为候选代价度规（cost metric）；当该黑塞矩阵正定时，最小化制备能量可在二次阶重现体观测量的变化。随后将制备惩罚（preparation penalties）与 Fubini-Study 度规（Fubini-Study metric）对比，并在 AdS₃ 标量扰动（scalar perturbations）中对最大体积（maximal volume）进行计算。

Result: 在大共形维数（large conformal dimension）极限下，复现代价度规与 Fubini-Study 度规的已知正比关系；在有限维数下，对位移体场及其共轭动量（conjugate momentum）的源变分得到正但一般不相等的惩罚，即使对单一标量算符（scalar operator）也破坏了该正比性。进一步研究大维数区域中由二次标量与梯度项（quadratic scalar and gradient terms）对体积的形变，确定耦合如何修改惩罚并约束正定性：这些附加项选择性改变场位移代价，其中梯度项引入模式依赖（mode dependence）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，体观测量的选择与边界制备惩罚的赋值之间存在显式的局部联系，为全息复杂度类观测量的边界代价解释提供了具体框架。

Abstract: Holographic complexity admits a broad family of bulk observables, yet their relation to boundary state-preparation costs remains unclear. We propose a local prescription that associates suitable bulk observables with quadratic cost geometries on families of states prepared by Euclidean sources. Building on the holographic transport of bulk Hamiltonian flows to Euclidean preparation sources, we use the Hessian of the transported generator at a stationary reference state to define a candidate cost metric. When this Hessian is positive definite, the minimised preparation energy reproduces the change in the bulk observable to quadratic order. We characterise the resulting preparation penalties relative to the Fubini-Study metric and compute them for the maximal volume in scalar perturbations of AdS$_3$. At large-conformal dimension, we recover the known proportionality between cost and Fubini-Study metrics. At finite dimension, we obtain positive but generally unequal penalties for source variations that displace the bulk field and its conjugate momentum, breaking this proportionality even for a single scalar operator. We further study a deformation of volume by quadratic scalar and gradient terms in the large-dimension regime, determining how the couplings modify the penalties and constrain positivity. These additions selectively change field-displacement costs, with the gradient term introducing mode dependence. Our results provide an explicit local connection between the choice of bulk observables and the assignment of boundary preparation penalties.

</details>


### [105] [3D de Sitter from complex Virasoro TQFT: Observers and knots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30247)
*Jeevan Chandra,Diego Liska,Boris Post*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出在一类球锥流形（spherical cone manifolds）上固定拓扑的精确引力配分函数（gravitational partition functions），通过对两个 Virasoro TQFT 配分函数乘积做解析延拓（analytic continuation）构造，并用半经典球体积、世界线关联函数、纽结世界线与 Seifert 流形等多重检验支持该提案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正宇宙学常数下的三维爱因斯坦引力（3D Einstein gravity）的欧氏鞍点为球面三流形，加入重观测者后变为带锥奇点的球锥流形；需要在这些流形上给出精确配分函数，并理解其从双曲到球面的相变（hyperbolic-to-spherical transitions）。

Method: 通过对两个 Virasoro TQFT 配分函数乘积的仔细解析延拓，构造固定拓扑下球锥流形的精确引力配分函数，并将其与引力在壳作用量（on-shell action）比较以作检验；进一步把 theta 图与四面体图振幅（tetrahedron graph amplitudes）同重观测者世界线上的两点函数与乱序四点函数（out-of-time-ordered four-point function）联系起来，并分析纽结世界线、Seifert 流形及环面纽结与链环的 Dehn 手术（Dehn surgery）。

Result: 精确配分函数在半经典极限下指数化为球体积（spherical volume），与引力在壳作用量一致，由此提出关于延拓 Virasoro TQFT 振幅乘积的球体积猜想（spherical volume conjecture）；在适当极限下可重现带有限观测者反作用的静态片区标量两点函数（static patch scalar two-point function）以及观测者反冲（recoil）的半经典效应；纽结世界线与 Seifert 流形的球体积在半经典层面得到复现与吻合。

Conclusion: 该构造为 dS$_3$ 中带观测者反作用的精确配分函数提供了自洽框架，并对偶地给出 Virasoro TQFT 振幅的球体积猜想；借助环面纽结与链环上的 Dehn 手术可获得光滑球面三流形的配分函数。

Abstract: The Euclidean saddles of 3D Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant are spherical three-manifolds. When one adds a heavy observer, the backreacted saddles are spherical cone manifolds, with a conical singularity along the observer worldline. We propose exact gravitational partition functions at fixed topology on a class of spherical cone manifolds that undergo hyperbolic-to-spherical transitions as the cone angles increase. Our construction relies on a careful analytic continuation of the product of two Virasoro TQFT partition functions. We test the proposal by showing that the exact partition functions exponentiate in the semiclassical limit to the spherical volume, in agreement with the gravitational on-shell action. This leads to a spherical volume conjecture for the product of continued Virasoro TQFT amplitudes. As further evidence, we relate the theta and tetrahedron graph amplitudes to the two-point function and out-of-time-ordered four-point function of operators on a massive observer's worldline. In appropriate limits, we recover the static patch scalar two-point function with finite observer backreaction and the semiclassical effects of observer recoil. In addition, we analyze examples with knotted worldlines and reproduce their spherical volumes. We also apply our prescription to Seifert manifolds, which are off-shell in AdS$_3$ gravity but admit dS$_3$ saddles, and find agreement with their spherical volumes semiclassically. Finally, using Dehn surgery on torus knots and links, we obtain partition functions on smooth spherical three-manifolds.

</details>


### [106] [Big Crunching Your Big Bang: Doubly Warped Time-Dependent Type II Flux Backgrounds and Scale Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30248)
*Kazi Fahim Reza Arko,Walid Hasan,M. Fahim Hoque,Ahmed Rakin Kamal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了三个解析的无质量 IIA 型超引力双重翘曲宇宙学解，分别带有 H₃、F̃₂、F̃₄ 通量；三者均从大爆炸膨胀到最大尺度后收缩至大挤压，并在特定短时段内表现出小的爱因斯坦标架哈勃尺度与 KK 质量比，且全部破坏 IIA 型所有形式的超对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注的核心问题是：在没有定域源（localised sources）的情况下，能否在光滑紧致空间上实现非平凡的翘曲（warping），并据此构建可解析研究尺度分离（scale separation）与宇宙学演化的背景。

Method: 在无质量 IIA 型超引力（massless type IIA supergravity）框架下，沿内部方向分别引入 H₃、F̃₂、F̃₄ 三种通量选择，解析构造三个双重翘曲（doubly warped）宇宙学解，并推导其十一维提升（eleven-dimensional uplift）与四维爱因斯坦标架宇宙学。

Result: 三个解均描述一个经历减速膨胀（decelerating expansion）、达到最大尺度、随后收缩至大挤压（Big Crunch）的宇宙；尺度分离分析显示 |H_E|/m_KK ≪ 1，但仅在爱因斯坦标架中尺度因子极大值附近的短暂时期成立；超对称分析表明三个解均破坏 IIA 型所有形式的超对称（supersymmetry）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需定域源即可在光滑紧致空间上获得非常数翘曲，并给出同时具备解析可控性、尺度分离（尽管短暂）与完全破缺超对称的宇宙学背景；其中 H₃ 解亦属于 IIB 型共同的 NS–NS 扇区（NS–NS sector）。

Abstract: We construct three analytic, doubly warped cosmological solutions of massless type IIA supergravity with different choices of fluxes, namely, $H_3$, $\tilde F_2$ and $\tilde F_4$ along internal directions. The $H_3$ solution also belongs to the common NS--NS sector of type IIB. Our solutions show that, without localised sources, nonconstant warping on a smooth compact space is possible. We derive their eleven-dimensional uplifts and four-dimensional Einstein-frame cosmology. Each universe undergoes (decelerating) expansion from a Big Bang, reaches a maximum size, and undergoes contraction towards a Big Crunch. We also analyse the scale separation of these backgrounds and find that they exhibit a small Einstein frame Hubble scale to KK mass ratio $\frac{|H_E|}{m_{\mathrm{KK}}}\ll1,$ although for a brief period of time, around the maxima of the scale factor in the Einstein frame. Finally, we analyse the supersymmetry of these backgrounds, which demonstrates that all three solutions break all forms of type IIA supersymmetry.

</details>


### [107] [Pressure in Wilsonian effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30249)
*Vinícius Bernardes,Atakan Hilmi Fırat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在Wilson有效场论中研究指数型滚动静止快子（rolling tachyon）解，作为开弦场论中滚动快子的模型，发现其压强仅在一段有限时间内与裸理论一致，晚期行为由另一类解主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开弦场论（open string field theory）中的滚动静止快子解难以直接处理，故借助Wilsonian有效场论（Wilsonian effective field theory）中的指数型解作为可计算的类比模型，以理解其晚期物理。

Method: 分析Wilsonian有效场论中指数型滚动静止快子解，并将其压强演化与底层裸理论（bare theory）进行对比，同时考察其时间方向的渐近行为。

Result: 该解的压强与裸理论一致，但仅在有限时间内成立；此后压强演化由另一解主导，该解朝过去方向呈振荡（oscillatory towards the past）、在晚期则保持正则（regular at late times）。

Conclusion: 据此推测，开弦场论中滚动静止快子的晚期物理可能由不同于标准滚动快子解（standard rolling tachyon solutions）的解所描述。

Abstract: We study the exponential rolling tachyon solution in Wilsonian effective field theory as a model for rolling tachyons in open string field theory. Although this solution exhibits runaway oscillations towards the future similar to its counterparts in string field theory, its pressure is shown to be consistent with the underlying bare theory, but only up to a finite time. The rest of the evolution of the pressure is instead governed by a solution that is oscillatory towards the past and regular at late times. We conjecture that the late time physics of rolling tachyons in open string field theory may be captured by the solutions that are different from the standard rolling tachyon solutions based on this analysis.

</details>


### [108] [Fluids on null hypersurfaces and Carrollian hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30254)
*Emil Have*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文研究零超曲面（null hypersurface）上的内禀理想流体（intrinsic ideal fluid）及其由环境时空中因果流体（causal fluid）实现的物理，建立了对应的 Carrollian 结构、子流形与变分学，并按 Carroll 助推（Carroll boosts）与时间 Weyl 对称性的实现方式对流体相进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解零超曲面上内禀流体的性质，以及它如何由环境时空中的流体实现；通过对称性（Carroll 助推与时间 Weyl 对称性）的实现方式来刻画不同的 Carrollian 流体相，并探讨环境流体被限制在零超曲面上的条件。

Method: 在嵌入的零超曲面上引入带有源自零生成元（null generator）重标度的时间 Weyl 对称性的诱导 Carrollian 结构（Carrollian structure），发展零子流形与变分学（variational calculus）；按 Carroll 助推与时间 Weyl 对称性的实现方式对流体相分类。对环境流体要求被约束于零超曲面时，其速度须为类光（null）；一般类时（timelike）或类光环境流体则会穿过零超曲面，形成开放 Carrollian 系统，其在零叶层（null foliation）上的源项对应零叶间的输运。进一步构造类时环境流体的解耦极限（decoupling limit）。

Result: 被约束的环境流体的类光速度实现了零温、Carroll 助推未破缺的内禀 Carrollian 相；类时或类光环境流体一般导致开放 Carrollian 系统。存在一类时类环境流体的解耦极限，给出 Carroll 助推自发破缺且无源项的自治 Carrollian 流体；该极限对应保持焓（enthalpy）有限非零的无限助推，环境能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）发散，但诱导的 Carrollian 响应不敏感于发散项而保持有限，并且独立的 Carroll 助推 Goldstone 模（Goldstone）从环境流体速度的次领头分量中出现。

Conclusion: 论文建立了零超曲面上内禀理想流体的 Carrollian 描述框架，并表明环境因果流体可通过不同方式实现相应的 Carrollian 相：类光约束给出未破缺相，而无限助推解耦极限给出自发破缺助推的自治相，其中 Goldstone 模源自速度的次领头分量，同时环境发散不影响诱导响应。

Abstract: We study intrinsic ideal fluids on null hypersurfaces and their realisations by causal fluids living in the ambient spacetime. An embedded null hypersurface is equipped with an induced Carrollian structure with a temporal Weyl symmetry inherited from rescalings of the null generator, and we develop the corresponding null submanifold and variational calculus. The intrinsic fluid phases are classified according to their realisation of Carroll boosts and temporal Weyl symmetry. If we demand that an ambient fluid be confined to a null hypersurface, the ambient fluid velocity must be null, which realises an intrinsic zero-temperature Carrollian phase with unbroken Carroll boosts. In general, timelike or null ambient fluids will cross a null hypersurface and lead to an open Carrollian system. On a null foliation, the sources correspond to transport between null leaves. We then show that there exists a decoupling limit of a timelike ambient fluid which gives rise to an autonomous Carrollian fluid with spontaneously broken Carroll boosts and no source terms. This decoupling limit corresponds to an infinite boost while keeping the enthalpy finite and nonzero, which leads to a divergent ambient energy-momentum tensor. Nevertheless, the induced Carrollian responses do not see the divergent term and remain finite, and an independent Carroll boost Goldstone emerges from a subleading component of the ambient fluid velocity in this limit.

</details>


### [109] [Maxwell-Chern-Simons-Matter in AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30260)
*Dean Carmi,Riccardo Ciccone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维反德西特（AdS$_3$）空间中Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论耦合$N$个复标量或Dirac费米子（含六次项与Gross-Neveu相互作用），在大$N$极限下导出了体光子传播子与边界自旋一谱结构，并给出了相图与相关BCFT数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对AdS$_3$中Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论耦合物质的研究，在传播子结构、Dirichlet边界条件对质量扇区与平坦扇区的处理以及大$N$下相图与边界算符谱的完整刻画方面仍不清晰，本文旨在系统厘清这些问题。

Method: 先推导体Maxwell-Chern-Simons传播子，阐明Dirichlet边界条件如何固定质量扇区与平坦扇区的贡献；再耦合物质场，在固定$\kappa=k/N$与$\alpha=Ne^2$的大$N$极限下重求和物质插入，得到领头阶、对$\alpha$与$\kappa$精确的光子传播子。

Result: 光子传播子的极点给出被Chern-Simons相互作用分裂为两个螺旋度扇区的边界自旋一谱；Dirichlet边界条件下，未Higgs化的标量相包含受保护的手征流（由Higgs化抬升），费米子相中该流在非零有效Chern-Simons能级下持续存在，螺旋度由诱导能级移动决定；在体共形点处提取了临界与三临界标量理论及正则费米子与Gross-Neveu理论的大$N$ BCFT数据。

Conclusion: Chern-Simons项通过分裂螺旋度扇区并诱导能级移动主导边界自旋一谱结构，Dirichlet边界条件与Higgs化决定了手征流是否受保护；该框架可在体共形点处系统提取大$N$下的BCFT数据。

Abstract: We study Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory on AdS$_3$ coupled to $N$ complex scalars or Dirac fermions, including sextic and Gross-Neveu interactions, respectively. First, we derive the bulk Maxwell-Chern-Simons propagator, clarifying how Dirichlet boundary conditions fix the contributions of the massive and flat sectors. Upon coupling to the matter sector, we work at large $N$ with $κ=k/N$ and $α=Ne^2$ fixed, where $k$ is the Chern-Simons level. We determine the corresponding phase diagrams, and we resum matter insertions to obtain the photon propagator at leading order in large $N$, exactly in $α$ and $κ$. Its poles determine the spin-one boundary spectrum, split into two helicity sectors by the Chern-Simons interaction. With Dirichlet boundary conditions, the unHiggsed scalar phase contains a protected chiral current, which is lifted by Higgsing. In the fermionic phases, the current persists for nonzero effective Chern-Simons level, with helicity determined by the induced level shift. At bulk conformal points, we extract large-$N$ BCFT data for the critical and tricritical scalar theories, as well as for the regular fermion and Gross-Neveu theories.

</details>


### [110] [A tale of three points: mixed and tensor bispectra beyond leading order in Horndeski gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30269)
*Y. A. Ageeva,M. A. Stepanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在一阶慢变近似下推导了广义Horndeski理论中原始双谱（bispectrum）⟨ζζh⟩、⟨ζhh⟩、⟨hhh⟩的完整动量与螺旋度依赖，并验证了相应的一致性关系（consistency relations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宇宙学扰动计算多聚焦标量或张量单一场情形；混合标量-张量双谱在广义Horndeski框架下缺乏完整的一阶慢变处理，且有限压缩（finite squeezing）下软极限行为尚待澄清。

Method: 通过一阶慢变展开（first order in slow variation），保留立方项系数的时间演化、尺度因子修正、一阶标量与张量模式函数以及功率谱归一化修正，计算三类双谱，并构造相应的非线性参数（nonlinearity parameters）。

Result: 在同等阶数上验证了张量软与标量软的Maldacena一致性关系；发现当标量声速与张量声速不等（c_S≠c_T）时，相关软变量为比值c_Tq/(c_Sk)，由此产生几何压缩（geometric squeezing）无法描述的声学交叉（acoustic crossover）现象。

Conclusion: 将一般结果应用于非奇异收缩模型（nonsingular contracting model）表明，混合双谱通过标量传播速度保留对收缩参数的敏感性，为区分早期宇宙模型提供可能观测信号。

Abstract: We derive the primordial $\langleζζh\rangle$, $\langleζhh\rangle$, and $\langle hhh\rangle$ bispectra in general Horndeski theory through first order in slow variation, retaining their complete momentum and helicity dependence. The calculation includes the time evolution of the cubic coefficients, the correction to the scale factor, the first-order scalar and tensor mode functions, and the corrected power-spectrum normalization. We verify the tensor-soft and scalar-soft Maldacena consistency relations at the same order. We also study finite squeezing and show that, when $c_S\neq c_T$, the relevant soft variable is the ratio $c_Tq/(c_Sk)$, leading to an acoustic crossover not captured by geometric squeezing alone. We construct the corresponding nonlinearity parameters and apply the general results to a nonsingular contracting model, in which the mixed bispectra retain sensitivity to the contraction parameter through the scalar propagation speed.

</details>


### [111] [Data analysis of millions of Type IIB flux vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30273)
*Yuta Hamada,Yuma Uematsu,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究对IIB型弦论在特定卡拉比-丘流形上的通量紧致化真空结构进行大规模扫描，结合约束优化与机器学习分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解通量紧致化（flux compactification）中真空的数量、分布与物理量相关性，并探索以机器学习高效识别和预测真空性质的可能性。

Method: 在\mathbb{CP}^4_{1,1,1,6,9}中的18次超曲面、含两个有效复结构模的情形下，扫描30,770,178个满足tadpole约束的通量（flux）选择，采用基于SLSQP算法的梯度约束极小化（constrained minimization）寻找标量势局部极小候选；以二次通量不变量（quadratic flux invariants）为输入特征训练分类器与回归模型。

Result: 获得数百万候选通量真空（flux vacua），从中筛选1,296,633个可靠解分析物理量分布与相关性；分类器在随机测试集上选取排名前5%可召回47.2%满足SUSY判据|V|<10^{-4.5}的解；回归中二次通量不变量对势值与复结构坐标预测更优，而原始六个通量参数在测试设置下对axio-dilaton坐标预测更好。

Conclusion: 大规模通量扫描结合机器学习可有效探索弦论真空景观（vacua landscape），特征选择对不同物理量的预测效果存在显著差异。

Abstract: We study the vacuum structure of flux compactifications of type IIB string theory on the degree 18 hypersurface in $\mathbb{CP}^4_{1,1,1,6,9}$ with two effective complex structure moduli. We scan $30{,}770{,}178$ flux choices compatible with the tadpole bound, and use a gradient-based constrained minimization based on the SLSQP algorithm to identify candidates for local minima of the scalar potential, yielding a dataset of millions of candidate flux vacua. Using the $1{,}296{,}633$ reliable solutions selected from this dataset, we analyze distributions and correlations of various physical quantities such as vacuum expectation values of moduli and vacuum energy. Using quadratic flux invariants as input features, we train a classifier to identify solutions satisfying the numerical SUSY criterion $|V|<10^{-4.5}$. On a randomly selected test set, selecting the top-ranked $5\%$ recovers $47.2\%$ of the SUSY test solutions. We also use supervised regression to predict the potential and moduli field values. In the regression comparison, quadratic flux invariant inputs improve prediction of potential value and the complex-structure coordinates, while the vanilla six flux parameters give better predictions for the axio-dilaton coordinates under the tested settings.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum field theory and four-manifolds: Perspectives from physics and geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30288)
*Niklas Garner,Martijn Kool,Jan Manschot,Yuuji Tanaka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本讲义汇集作者在2025年马萨诸塞大学阿默斯特分校“量子场论物理数学”暑期学校的授课内容，聚焦超对称量子场论扭变所产生的代数结构、拓扑扭变超对称规范理论的关联函数，及其作为四维流形或复射影曲面上瞬子、半稳定层与Higgs层模空间的交叠论不变量的数学对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讲义旨在沟通物理与数学两个视角：一方面说明超对称量子场论（supersymmetric quantum field theory）扭变（twist）所导出的代数结构，另一方面建立拓扑扭变规范理论关联函数（correlation functions）与模空间（moduli space）交叠论不变量（intersection-theoretic invariants）之间的对应，为“量子场论物理数学”提供系统教材。

Method: 方法为讲义式综述：以暑期学校系列讲座为基础，围绕超对称场论的扭变操作组织内容，讨论由扭变产生的代数结构，并将拓扑扭变超对称规范理论的关联函数与瞬子（instanton）、半稳定层（semistable sheaf）、Higgs层（Higgs sheaf）在四维流形（four-manifold）及复射影曲面（complex projective surface）上的模空间所对应的交叠论不变量相对照。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定理或计算结论；所述内容框架为：扭变超对称场论中的代数结构，以及拓扑扭变规范理论关联函数与瞬子、半稳定层、Higgs层模空间的交叠论不变量之间的数学对应关系。

Conclusion: 这些讲义将超对称量子场论的扭变、拓扑扭变规范理论的关联函数与模空间交叠论不变量统一在同一框架下，构成连接物理与数学（几何表示论与枚举几何方向）的教学性论述；具体结论与证明细节需参见讲义正文。

Abstract: These lecture notes are based on lecture series by the authors for the Summer School 2025 on ``Physical mathematics of quantum field theory" at the University of Massachusetts Amherst organized by Owen Gwilliam, Chris Elliott, and Matteo Lotito. The lectures centred around algebraic structures arising from twists of supersymmetric quantum field theories, correlation functions of topologically twisted supersymmetric gauge theories, and their mathematical counterparts as intersection-theoretic invariants associated to moduli spaces of instantons, semistable sheaves, or Higgs sheaves on four-manifolds or complex projective surfaces.

</details>


### [113] [Amplified EFT corrections near exceptional QNMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30311)
*William L. Boyce,Jorge E. Santos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出，有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）修正对单个准正规模（quasinormal mode, QNM）频率的常规展开在例外点（exceptional point）处失效，并在接近例外点时被增强；作者以等自旋 Myers–Perry–AdS₅ 黑洞的 α'³ 修正为例进行数值验证，并讨论对全响应及其他黑洞物理领域的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于揭示通常用于计算 EFT 对个别 QNM 频率修正的微扰展开，在例外 QNM 处存在的结构性失效问题，并说明近例外情形下修正被增强的普遍性。

Method: 先给出该现象为何普遍发生的简要论证，再通过数值计算等自旋 Myers–Perry–AdS₅ 黑洞 QNM 的 α'³ 修正来展示该行为；该计算对应边界共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）中推迟格林函数（retarded Green's function）极点的 λ^{-3/2} 修正（λ 为 't Hooft 耦合）。对合并成对模式统一处理，计算未修正例外点处的主导 λ^{-3/4} 劈裂，以及例外点在温度与角速度上的 λ^{-3/2} 位移。

Result: 结果表明，单个频率的非简并展开失效；但背景导数展开（derivative expansion）仍可保持有效，而例外点处的劈裂（splitting）与位移（displacement）分别以 λ^{-3/4} 和 λ^{-3/2} 标度出现。

Conclusion: 结论是：背景导数展开的有效性与个体频率非简并展开的失效可以并存；作者进一步讨论了该现象对全响应（full response）以及其他黑洞物理研究方向的含义。

Abstract: We show that the ordinary expansion of EFT corrections to individual QNM frequencies breaks down at an exceptional QNM, and that these corrections become enhanced for near-exceptional QNMs. We give a brief argument for why this should generally happen, before numerically demonstrating this behaviour by calculating the $α'^3$ corrections to QNMs of equal-spin Myers--Perry-AdS$_5$ black holes. This calculation corresponds to $λ^{-3/2}$ corrections to the poles of the retarded Green's function in the boundary CFT, where $λ$ is the 't Hooft coupling. Treating the coalescing pair together, we calculate the leading $λ^{-3/4}$ splitting at the uncorrected exceptional point and the $λ^{-3/2}$ displacement of the exceptional point in temperature and angular velocity. The background derivative expansion can remain valid while the nondegenerate expansion of individual frequencies fails. We discuss the implications for the full response and for other areas of black hole physics.

</details>


### [114] [The Stringy Photon Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30426)
*Ahmed Sheta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究渐近平坦史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）上作为无穷远未来（\(\mathcal{I}\)）位置函数的S矩阵，推导由体（bulk）零测地线（null geodesic）连接两点时产生的光锥奇点（lightcone singularities），并表明有限弦长（string length \(\ell_s\)）的世界面（worldsheet）修正会将奇点移入复时间平面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以S矩阵在\(\mathcal{I}\)上随位置变化的视角刻画黑洞散射，关注光锥奇点这一结构，并考察弦论修正如何在其中起作用。

Method: 计算渐近平坦史瓦西背景下S矩阵的光锥奇点，包含长时间绕行光子球（photon sphere）附近的测地线运动学；引入有限弦长\(\ell_s\)并计入世界面修正，分析其对奇点位置的影响以及绕行光子球的弦的运动学。

Result: 当\(\mathcal{I}\)上两点由体零测地线相连时S矩阵出现光锥奇点；世界面修正使这些奇点在复时间平面上发生移动，从而在实时间处消除奇点，并给出光子球奇点的领头阶弦论修正（stringy resolution）。同时计算了史瓦西eikonal准正规模（quasinormal mode）谱的弦论修正。

Conclusion: 有限弦长效应将实时间光锥奇点解析延拓至复时间平面，并系统性地修正光子球相关奇点与eikonal准正规模谱，为黑洞S矩阵的弦论修正提供了定量结果。

Abstract: The S matrix on an asymptotically-flat Schwarzschild black hole is studied as a function of position on $\mathcal{I}$. We derive the lightcone singularities that arise in the S matrix whenever the two points on $\mathcal{I}$ are connected by a bulk null geodesic. This includes geodesics orbiting near the photon sphere for a long time, whose kinematics determine the associated lightcone singularities. By turning on a finite string length $\ell_s$, worldsheet corrections are shown to shift the lightcone singularities into the complex time plane, resolving the singularities at real times. In particular, we derive the leading stringy resolution of the photon sphere singularities. We also compute stringy corrections to the eikonal quasinormal mode spectrum of Schwarzschild, and discuss the kinematics of strings orbiting the photon sphere.

</details>


### [115] [Normal Matrix Model and Hyperelliptic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30475)
*Valdemar Melin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出一个双矩阵模型（two-matrix model）：其正规矩阵（normal matrix）的本征值被限制在上半平面的若干围道（contours）上，并与一个厄米矩阵（Hermitian matrix）耦合，是 Hoppe 单条水平线模型的推广；通过求解鞍点方程（saddle point equations）得到以大 N 自由能（large-N free energy）表示的超椭圆积分（hyperelliptic integrals）形式，并将该模型解释为存在无限导体边界（conducting boundary）的库仑气体（Coulomb gas）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hoppe 先前提出的矩阵模型只对应单条水平线这一特殊情形，其适用范围有限。本文意在将本征值支撑集从单条水平线推广到上半平面中的多条围道，以刻画更一般的构型。

Method: 构造由本征值限制于上半平面围道上的正规矩阵与厄米矩阵耦合而成的双矩阵模型；针对多条线的情形求解鞍点方程，并借助超椭圆积分处理大 N 极限下的自由能。

Result: 多条线情形下的鞍点方程被成功求解，大 N 自由能可由超椭圆积分表达；模型被证明等价于存在无限导体边界的库仑气体描述。

Conclusion: 该工作把 Hoppe 的单线模型推广为多围道双矩阵模型，给出了以超椭圆积分表示的大 N 自由能，并建立了其与带无限导体边界库仑气体的对应关系。

Abstract: We present a two-matrix model with a normal matrix with eigenvalues confined to contours in the upper half plane coupled to a Hermitian matrix. This generalizes the matrix model proposed by Jens Hoppe which corresponds to a single horizontal line. We solve the saddle point equations for several lines and show that the large-N free energy can be expressed in terms of hyperelliptic integrals. We show that the model describes a Coulomb gas in the presence of an infinite conducting boundary.

</details>


### [116] [Higher-Loop Vacuum Integrals on $S^D$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30489)
*Varun Lochab,Frederik Denef*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出一套在球面 \(S^D\) 上计算标量场（scalar fields）高圈积分（higher-loop integrals）的新框架，通过将全导数恒等式（total-derivative identities）与边收缩关系（edge-contraction relations）耦合成线性系统来求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面上高圈标量积分的系统化评估缺乏统一且可操作的计算框架，需要发展能够处理一般圈图的方法。

Method: 从球面上一般高圈积分的 Schwinger 参数化形式（Schwinger-parametric form）出发，结合全导数恒等式与边收缩关系，构成一个耦合线性系统（coupled linear system）以求解积分。

Result: 作为示例，求解了直至两圈（two loops）的全部真空图（vacuum graphs），其中包括两圈三瓜图（two-loop three-melon）：给出任意维数 \(D\) 的解析解，并获得所有整数维数下的显式维数正规化（dimensional regularization）结果。

Conclusion: 所提框架可有效处理球面上标量场高圈积分，并在两圈真空图层面得到完整的解析与维数正规化结果，为更高圈计算提供方法基础。

Abstract: We develop a new framework for evaluating higher-loop integrals for scalar fields on $S^D$. Starting from a Schwinger-parametric form for general higher-loop integrals on the sphere, it combines total-derivative identities with edge-contraction relations into a coupled linear system. To illustrate our method, we solve for all vacuum graphs through two loops. This includes, in particular, the two-loop three-melon, for which we construct the analytic solution for general $D$ and obtain explicit dimensionally regularized results at all integer dimensions.

</details>


### [117] [Inverse KLT kernel of AdS string-amplitude building blocks from the intersection matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30862)
*Yi-xiao Tao,Shijie Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文为 n 点 AdS 弦振幅构造块（disk 积分与 sphere 积分）的 Kawai–Lewellen–Tye（KLT）关系提供拓扑与组合解释，借助非交换环上的扭曲 de Rham 理论（twisted de Rham theory）与 associahedral 胞腔，将交叠矩阵等同于逆 KLT 核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KLT 关系通常以代数恒等式形式出现，缺乏对其拓扑与组合起源的系统刻画；本文旨在为含多变量多重多对数插入的 AdS 弦振幅构造块建立此类解释。

Method: 在非交换环上的扭曲 de Rham 理论框架下，用携带 Knizhnik–Zamolodchikov 方程解的模空间 associahedral 胞腔（associahedral chambers）重构问题；构造正则化扭曲闭链（regularized twisted cycles），通过局部单值（local monodromies）与 Drinfeld associator 表达其交叠配对，并按公共边界面组织交叠贡献得到交叠矩阵。

Result: 由扭曲周期关系（twisted period relation），所得交叠矩阵被等同于逆 KLT 核（inverse KLT kernel）；同时给出逆 KLT 核的场论极限（field-theory limit）。

Conclusion: KLT 关系可被理解为扭曲周期框架下交叠配对的组合-拓扑产物，Drinfeld associator 控制融合树归一化之间的传输；该表述亦覆盖场论极限情形。

Abstract: We develop a topological and combinatorial interpretation of the Kawai--Lewellen--Tye (KLT) relations for $n$-point AdS string-amplitude building blocks, which are disk integrals with multivariable multiple polylogarithm insertions and sphere integrals with their single-valued counterparts. Building on the framework of twisted de Rham theory over a noncommutative ring, we reformulate the problem in terms of associahedral chambers of the moduli space loaded with solutions of the Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov equation. We construct regularized twisted cycles and express their intersection pairings through local monodromies and Drinfeld associators, which control transport between fusion-tree normalizations. Organizing the intersection contributions by common boundary faces yields the intersection matrix, which is identified with the inverse KLT kernel by the twisted period relation. The field-theory limit of the inverse KLT kernel is also given.

</details>


### [118] [Phases of flux vacua and their quantum symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30865)
*Dan Israel,Ilarion V. Melnikov,Yann Proto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文构造了一个具有(0,2)超对称的扭曲线性sigma模型，其中大半径相（large radius phase）实现五维异形通量真空（heterotic flux vacuum），并将该几何与由Gepner模型与圆的不对称轨道折叠（asymmetric orbifold）构成的弦论区域相连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为异形通量真空提供可在弦论微扰区域可控分析的描述，弥合几何大半径图像与精确可解的弦论构造（Gepner模型轨道折叠）之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用(0,2)超对称扭曲线性sigma模型（torsional linear sigma model）作为工具，利用其相结构连接大半径几何相与不对称轨道折叠相；过程中发展了一套适用于更一般(0,2)规范线性sigma模型（gauged linear sigma model）构造异形真空的技术工具。

Result: 模型的大半径相给出五维异形通量真空；该几何被成功关联到Gepner模型与圆之不对称轨道折叠所实现的弦论区域，并附带若干可推广至一般(0,2) GLSM构造的工具。

Conclusion: (0,2)扭曲线性sigma模型可作为桥梁，将异形通量真空的几何描述与其弦论精确描述相统一，且所发展的方法对更一般的异形真空构造具有普适价值。

Abstract: We present a torsional linear sigma model with (0,2) supersymmetry that realizes, in its large radius phase, a five-dimensional heterotic flux vacuum, and we use the linear sigma model to connect this geometry to a stringy regime realized as an asymmetric orbifold of a Gepner model and a circle. Along the way we develop a number of tools relevant for more general (0,2) gauged linear sigma model constructions of heterotic vacua.

</details>


### [119] [Quasinormal modes, complex null geodesics and singularity resolution in regular AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30915)
*Heng-Yu Chen,Yasuaki Hikida,Yasutaka Koga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文借助全息对偶（holographic duality），以正则（regular）AdS黑洞作为正则化几何（regularized geometry），研究黑洞奇点（black hole singularity）的消解如何编码在边界共形场论（conformal field theory）的两点函数中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞奇点被视界遮蔽，难以判断其是否被量子引力（quantum gravity）或弦效应（stringy effects）消解；而对AdS-Schwarzschild黑洞，对偶CFT两点函数中存在与"从奇点反弹的零测地线（null geodesic）"相关的奇异性，可作为探针。

Method: 采用WKB分析（WKB analysis）计算正则AdS黑洞的准正规模（quasinormal modes），并由这些模式读出对偶两点函数；同时结合两点函数算符乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）的结构进行分析。

Result: 奇异黑洞中对应反弹零测地线的奇异性，被一个与复（而非实）零测地线相关的平滑隆起（smooth bump）所取代；该复测地线奇点的位置得到OPE结构分析的支撑。

Conclusion: 结果给出了黑洞奇点消解的全息印记（holographic signature），并阐明了正则内部（regular interior）信息如何编码在边界关联函数（boundary correlation functions）之中。

Abstract: The black hole singularity is hidden behind its horizon, and it is difficult to determine whether it is resolved by quantum gravity or stringy effects. For an AdS-Schwarzschild black hole, it is known that the two-point function of the dual conformal field theory has a singularity associated with a null geodesic that bounces off the black hole singularity. In this paper, we investigate how the resolution of the black hole singularity is encoded in the dual conformal field theory by considering regular AdS black holes as regularized geometries. Using WKB analysis, we compute the quasinormal modes of regular AdS black holes and read off the dual two-point functions from the modes. We find that the singularity corresponding to the bouncing null geodesic for singular black holes is replaced by a smooth bump associated with a complex, rather than real, null geodesic. The location of the complex geodesic singularity is supported by analyzing the structure of the operator product expansion for the two-point function. Our results provide a holographic signature of black hole singularity resolution and clarify how information about the regular interior is encoded in boundary correlation functions.

</details>


### [120] [Timelike Supersymmetric Geometries in Seven-Dimensional Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31191)
*Michèle Levi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文系统构建了七维超引力（seven-dimensional supergravity）中最大 Cartan 规范理论的规范不变高形式通量表述及其双线性体系，并在等电荷极限下分析了超对称约束的代数与微分结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 七维超引力涉及拓扑质量高形式规范动力学、特殊 Clifford 代数性质与丰富几何结构，构成独特的超对称难题；现有理论缺乏以规范不变高形式通量为核心的统一表述与系统的解构造工具。

Method: 以规范不变高形式通量（gauge-invariant higher-form flux）建立最大 Cartan 规范理论的玻色作用量与超对称变换，并通过等电荷极限下与最小 Cartan 规范理论的一致截断（consistent truncation）加以确定；进而构造类时 Killing 向量（timelike Killing vector）的完整双线性体系，将代数约束分解为 SU(3)-同型分量（SU(3)-isotypic components）并分析微分双线性关系；同时构造与自对偶条件相容的 Killing 规范。

Result: 证明在含规范场与活跃 Cartan 通量时复标量双线性恒消失；在含规范但无活跃 Cartan 通量时，Killing 向量同时作为规范 R-对称（R-symmetry）生成元，底流形结构由 SU(3) 扩展为 U(3)，BPS 流解可实现该情形；并在四类划分下为两种理论隔离出 3-形式方程的单一独立分量。

Conclusion: 该分类方法应用于两种理论的已知与候选解，揭示出尚未探索的扇区，并为构造、检验解以及发展新的几何与全息（holographic）应用提供了易用的工具箱。

Abstract: Seven-dimensional supergravity poses a uniquely intricate supersymmetry problem, combining topologically massive higher-form gauge dynamics, distinct Clifford algebra properties, and rich geometric structures. We first establish the complete formulation of the maximal Cartan-gauged theory in terms of the gauge-invariant higher-form flux, including both the bosonic action and supersymmetry variations. This formulation, which also makes the smooth ungauged limit manifest, is achieved by requiring a consistent truncation to the minimal Cartan-gauged theory in the equal-charge limit. We construct the complete bilinear system for a timelike Killing vector, where additional internal orientation of the symplectic-Majorana spinor arises already in the scalar sector, uniquely among pure minimal ungauged theories. In the equal-charge sector, we decouple the algebraic constraints into SU$(3)$-isotypic components, and analyse the differential bilinear relations, determining the permitted dilaton gradient, fluxes, fibration twist and intrinsic torsion of the base. We prove that with gauging and active Cartan flux, the complex scalar bilinear vanishes identically. We also construct a Killing gauge for the higher-form potential compatible with the self-duality condition. We show that with gauging but no active Cartan flux, the Killing vector also acts as a generator of the gauged R-symmetry, and the base structure is enlarged from SU$(3)$ to U$(3)$. We find that BPS-flow solutions realize this setting. With supersymmetry, we isolate a single independent component of the 3-form equation across an exhaustive four-class partition for both theories within this setup. Applied to known and proposed solutions in both theories, this classification exposes unexplored sectors and provides an accessible toolbox for constructing and testing solutions and developing new geometric and holographic applications.

</details>


### [121] [Unitarity methods for Magnus amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31345)
*Jung-Wook Kim,Sungsoo Kim,Jian-Rong Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将现代幺正性方法（unitarity methods）推广至 Magnus 振幅（Magnus amplitudes，即 S-矩阵对数的矩阵元）的计算，提出一套可避开直接使用费曼图（Feynman diagrams）的切可构造部分计算流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Magnus 振幅由 S-矩阵（S-matrix）的对数矩阵元定义，而现有幺正性方法主要针对散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）本身；因此需要一个将幺正性方法适配到 Magnus 振幅计算的方案。

Method: 所提方法改造幺正性方法（unitarity methods），使其适用于 Magnus 振幅中切可构造部分（cut-constructible parts）的构造，而不直接依赖费曼图（Feynman diagrams）。

Result: 摘要仅陈述方法的提出，未给出具体计算结果或数值验证；可确认的是该流程可高效获得散射振幅的圈被积函数（loop integrands）这一幺正性方法的既有优势被延伸至 Magnus 振幅。

Conclusion: 幺正性方法可被适配用于 Magnus 振幅的计算，从而将该类方法的适用范围从散射振幅拓展到 S-矩阵对数的矩阵元。

Abstract: Magnus amplitudes refer to the matrix elements of the logarithm of the $S$-matrix. Modern unitarity methods construct cut-constructible parts of scattering amplitudes without directly invoking Feynman diagrams, offering an efficient way of obtaining loop integrands for scattering amplitudes. A procedure is presented that adapts unitarity methods to the computation of Magnus amplitudes.

</details>


### [122] [The classical two-loop amplitude at leading logarithmic accuracy for graviton emission in multi-Regge kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31378)
*Federico Buccioni,Vittorio Del Duca,Emanuele Rosi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用引力冲击波形式（gravitational shockwave formalism），计算了两个无质量标量（massless scalars）碰撞中发射一个引力子（graviton）的五点振幅的经典部分，阶数为 $\mathcal{O}(G^{7/2})$，并在领头对数精度（leading logarithmic accuracy）下给出结果，情境为多雷吉运动学（multi-Regge kinematics, MRK）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画经典引力（classical gravity）中领头对数振幅的结构，并检验其在无质量极限下的普适性（universality）猜想，从而为五点辐射振幅的经典部分提供独立计算途径。

Method: 采用引力冲击波形式计算 $\mathcal{O}(G^{7/2})$ 五点振幅的经典部分；结果分解为双粒子可约项（two-particle reducible term）与三粒子可约项（three-particle reducible term），后者对应 $H$-图（$H$-diagram）在 MRK 极限下取辐射的对应物。同一振幅亦可通过光性定理（optical theorem），由相应有质量振幅（massive amplitude）的超相对论极限（ultrarelativistic limit）等价求得。

Result: 所得振幅可分解为双粒子可约项与三粒子可约项两部分，其中三粒子可约项是 $H$-图在 MRK 极限下发射引力子的辐射对应物；两种计算途径（冲击波形式与有质量振幅的超相对论极限加光性定理）给出等价结果。

Conclusion: 结果支持经典引力中领头对数振幅普适性的猜想，表明冲击波方法与基于光性定理的超相对论极限方法在 $\mathcal{O}(G^{7/2})$ 五点振幅层面相互一致。

Abstract: Using the gravitational shockwave formalism, we compute the classical part of the $\mathcal{O}(G^{7/2})$ five-point amplitude at leading logarithmic accuracy describing the emission of a graviton in the collision of two massless scalars in multi-Regge kinematics (MRK). The result splits into a two-particle reducible term and a three-particle reducible one, the latter being the radiative counterpart of the $H$-diagram with the emission taken in MRK limit. The same amplitude can be equivalently calculated as the ultrarelativistic limit of the corresponding massive amplitude by applying the optical theorem according to the conjectured universality of the leading logarithmic amplitudes in classical gravity.

</details>


### [123] [Krylov Complexity and Entropy of Composite Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31394)
*Jeff Murugan,Victor Van Aswegen,Hendrik J R Van Zyl*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究Krylov复杂度（Krylov complexity）随量子系统自由度数增加的行为，指出对于相互对易子系统的张量积，领先的超额复杂度由子系统Lanczos系数的加权方差控制，并证明Krylov熵可区分子系统组成信息与动力学失配信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Krylov复杂度与Krylov熵在系统自由度增多、由多子系统组合而成时的行为，需要定量刻画集体动力学（collective dynamics）的涌现及子系统间动力学差异。

Method: 以相互对易（commuting）子系统的张量积（tensor products）为对象，分析其超额复杂度（excess complexity）的领先项，引入子系统Lanczos系数（Lanczos coefficients）的加权方差作为度量，并考察Krylov熵（Krylov entropy）对信息的区分能力。

Result: 领先的超额复杂度由子系统Lanczos系数的加权方差控制，该量给出了动力学异质性（dynamical heterogeneity）的定量度量及同步演化（synchronous evolution）的精确刻画；Krylov熵能够区分子系统组成所致信息与动力学失配（dynamical mismatch）所致信息，且同一Lanczos方差同时控制复杂度与熵中领先的失配贡献。

Conclusion: 超额复杂度与Krylov熵构成互补的探针（complementary probes），可用于刻画大量子系统中集体动力学的涌现。

Abstract: We investigate the behaviour of Krylov complexity as the number of degrees of freedom of a quantum system increases. Specifically, we show that for tensor products of commuting subsystems, the leading excess complexity is controlled by a weighted variance of the subsystem Lanczos coefficients. This provides a quantitative measure of dynamical heterogeneity and a precise characterization of synchronous evolution. We also show that Krylov entropy is able to disambiguate the information associated with subsystems composition from that generated by dynamical mismatch, and that the same Lanczos variance controls the leading mismatch contribution to both complexity and the entropy. Our results establish excess complexity and Krylov entropy as complementary probes of the emergence of collective dynamics in large quantum systems.

</details>


### [124] [The Spectral Edge at Large Central Charge and Black Hole Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31413)
*Eric Perlmutter*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出在 AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ 中处理高能谱的新方法，借助二维 CFT 的 $L$-函数解析理论，将谱统计纳入模域 bootstrap（modular bootstrap），并由此给出更精细的能隙（gap）上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模域 bootstrap 长期难以纳入谱统计信息，如何在其中刻画高能态的谱涨落是一个未解难题；该文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用附着于二维 CFT 的 $L$-函数（$L$-functions）的解析理论，并借由高能态之间的模约束（modular constraints）推导出一个和规则（sum rule），将谱边缘（spectral edge）与体能谱涨落（bulk spectral fluctuations）联系起来。

Result: 设 $Δ_1$ 为中心荷 $c$ 的幺正 CFT 中最轻激发 Virasoro 原初态的共形维数，证明 $Δ_1 - (c-1)/12 \leq o(c)$，且在每个足够大的 $c$ 处至多存在一个例外标量能隙取值；在实现该例外能隙的 $c$ 序列上，其余能隙满足更强的 $Δ_1 - (c-1)/12 \leq O(1)$。

Conclusion: 所得结果适用于具有谱计数涨落对数刚性（logarithmic rigidity）的理论，如随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）所示情形，为高能谱统计与模域 bootstrap 的结合提供了新框架。

Abstract: We introduce a new approach to high-energy spectra in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$, addressing the open challenge of incorporating spectral statistics into the modular bootstrap. Using the analytic theory of $L$-functions attached to two-dimensional CFTs, we leverage modular constraints among high-energy states to derive a sum rule relating the spectral edge to bulk spectral fluctuations. As an application, we establish a refined modular bootstrap bound: letting $Δ_1$ be the conformal dimension of the lightest excited Virasoro primary of a unitary CFT of central charge $c$, we show that $Δ_1 - {c-1\over 12} \leq o(c)$, with at most one exceptional value of the scalar gap at each sufficiently large $c$; moreover, along any sequence in $c$ for which such exceptional gaps are realized, the remaining gaps obey the stronger bound $Δ_1 - {c-1\over 12} \leq O(1)$. The result applies to theories with logarithmic rigidity of spectral counting fluctuations, as in random matrix theory.

</details>


### [125] [Ricci flow of Integrable Sigma Models, Hitchin Systems and Quantum Seiberg-Witten theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31672)
*G. Bonelli,S. Lacroix,T. Pedroni,A. Tanzini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过4维Chern-Simons理论构造一族可积2维sigma模型，并证明其经典点粒子约化对应Hitchin系统；Hitchin系统的可积量子化给出单圈约化动力学的候选Hamilton量，且在所有考察情形下与Perelman算子一致，从而可用4d N=2规范场论与2d共形场论方法研究谱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为可积2维sigma模型的约化动力学寻找自然的Hamilton量结构，并借助Hitchin系统、规范场论与共形场论的成熟工具处理谱问题。

Method: 方法上，先由4d Chern-Simons理论构造可积2维sigma模型族，考察其经典点粒子约化到Hitchin系统的对应，再利用Hitchin系统的可积量子化（integrable quantisation）构造单圈约化动力学的候选Hamilton量，并借助4d N=2规范场论（gauge theory）与2d共形场论（conformal field theory）方法求解谱问题。

Result: 所得候选Hamilton量在所有考察情形下均与Perelman算子（Perelman operator）一致；对sausage模型给出了精确量子化条件（exact quantisation conditions），并计算了与数值有限体积（finite-volume）结果相符所需的指数小修正（exponentially small corrections）。

Conclusion: 结论表明，Hitchin系统量子化为这类可积sigma模型的单圈约化动力学提供了自然的Hamilton量描述，并与Perelman算子相吻合；结合4d N=2与2d CFT方法可有效处理谱问题，且在sausage模型中精确量子化条件与指数小修正足以复现有限体积数值结果。

Abstract: We construct a family of integrable 2d sigma models through 4d Chern-Simons theory and show that their classical point-particle reduction corresponds to a Hitchin system. The integrable Hitchin quantisation then provides a natural candidate Hamiltonian for the one-loop reduced dynamics of the sigma models, which coincides with the Perelman operator in all cases examined. This allows us to study the corresponding spectral problems using 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ gauge theory and 2d conformal field theory methods. For the sausage model, we derive exact quantisation conditions and compute exponentially small corrections essential for the agreement with numerical finite-volume results.

</details>


### [126] [Five-loop static two-body potential in massless scalar-tensor gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30972)
*Gabriel Luz Almeida,Zhengwen Liu,Jia Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在无质量标量-张量理论中，利用后牛顿有效场论（post-Newtonian effective field theory）计算了到五阶后牛顿（5PN）的保守两体势静场贡献，并证明无需求解全新的五圈主积分（master integrals）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波观测需要超越广义相对论（general relativity）的高精度理论预言，因此需将保守两体势的标量-张量修正推进到更高后牛顿阶，以用于引力波检验。

Method: 将紧致天体的强场标量响应（scalar responses）编码为标量依赖质量，在后牛顿有效场论框架下用一至五圈无质量两点费曼积分（Feynman integrals）计算1PN–5PN静场项；借助标量扇区的反射对称性（reflection symmetries）推广奇PN因子化机制。

Result: 结果复现已知的2PN静相互作用，独立给出3PN的EFT确定，并将显式势扩展到4PN与5PN；验证了广义相对论极限与粒子交换对称性（body-exchange symmetry），且完整结果中正规化极点与残余超越项相消，仅剩有理系数。

Conclusion: 标量-张量理论的静态主拓扑与广义相对论情形一致，奇PN因子化机制仍成立，故无需新的五圈主积分；所提高精度结果可为超越广义相对论的引力波检验提供输入。

Abstract: We compute the static contribution to the conservative two-body potential in massless scalar-tensor theories through fifth post-Newtonian (5PN) order, with the strong-field scalar responses of the compact objects encoded in scalar-dependent masses. Within post-Newtonian effective field theory (EFT), evaluating the 1PN-5PN static terms requires one- through five-loop massless two-point Feynman integrals. Although the additional scalar modifies both source couplings and integrand numerators, the required scalar master topologies remain those of the corresponding static problem in general relativity. Moreover, independent reflection symmetries of the two static scalar sectors extend the odd-PN factorization mechanism to the present theory, so that no genuinely new five-loop master integrals are required. Our results reproduce the known static interaction through 2PN, provide an independent EFT determination at 3PN, and extend the explicit potential to 4PN and 5PN. We verify the general-relativity limit and body-exchange symmetry; regulator poles and residual transcendental terms cancel in the complete result, leaving rational coefficients. These high-precision results provide ingredients for increasingly precise gravitational-wave tests of gravity beyond general relativity.

</details>


### [127] [Exact Two-Loop Effective Potentials in de Sitter Space via Finite-Difference Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31527)
*Bruce Bassett,Sergio Luigi Cacciatori,Ugo Moschella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次在四维德西特（de Sitter）空间中对 O(N) 对称标量理论（O(N)-symmetric scalar theory）完成精确的两圈有效势（two-loop effective potential）计算，核心是两圈西瓜图真空积分（two-loop watermelon vacuum integral）有限部分的提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前虽已有西瓜图真空积分的闭式表示，但在四维附近提取其有限部分极为困难，导致四维德西特空间中缺乏精确的两圈有效势结果。

Method: 利用传播子谱标号（propagator spectral labels）中的精确有限差分方程（finite-difference equations），将紫外极点（ultraviolet poles）隔离于 Gamma 函数因子中，使剩余级数在四维附近正则；证明选取物理解的边界条件，并结合蝌蚪图重构（tadpole reconstruction）得到势。

Result: 完成了次发散减除（subtraction of subdivergences）与物质部门重整化群函数（renormalisation-group functions）的推导；证明在森林减除（forest subtraction）后，因保持场依赖质量与标量曲率固定而引入的谱标号调节子依赖（regulator dependence）的导数项相互抵消。

Conclusion: 所得势在质量-曲率比（mass-to-curvature ratios）上于两圈阶精确，适用于传播子极点之外且受微扰论（perturbation theory）通常限制；这是四维德西特空间中有效势的首个精确两圈确定结果。

Abstract: We compute the renormalised two-loop effective potential of the $O(N)$-symmetric scalar theory on four-dimensional de Sitter space. The main technical ingredient is the two-loop watermelon vacuum integral. Although a closed-form representation of this integral was previously available, extracting its finite part near four dimensions remained exceedingly difficult. We use exact finite-difference equations in the propagator spectral labels to obtain a representation in which the ultraviolet poles are isolated in Gamma-function factors and the remaining series are regular near four dimensions. We prove the boundary condition selecting the physical solution, extract the finite part, and combine the result with the tadpole reconstruction of the potential. The calculation includes the subtraction of subdivergences and the derivation of the matter-sector renormalisation-group functions. Holding the field-dependent masses and scalar curvature fixed makes the spectral labels regulator-dependent. We track this dependence explicitly and show that its derivative terms cancel after the forest subtraction. The resulting potential is exact in the mass-to-curvature ratios at two-loop order, away from propagator poles and subject to the usual limitations of perturbation theory. This is the first exact two-loop determination of the effective potential in four-dimensional de Sitter space.

</details>


### [128] [Large $N$ hadron scattering bootstrap: massive pions and glueballs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31578)
*Sebastiano Bocchia,Andrea Guerrieri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作将大N玻ootstrap方法从手征极限推广到非零π介子质量，并首次应用于胶球散射，对平面振幅的泰勒系数与在壳耦合给出了不依赖截断手征展开的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有大N bootstrap仅在手征极限下处理π介子散射，依赖截断的手征微扰展开，无法随夸克质量变化，也无法用于缺乏低能有效理论的胶球系统。

Method: 将大N bootstrap（large N bootstrap）推广至非零π介子质量，直接对精确平面振幅（planar amplitude）的泰勒系数（Taylor coefficients）与在壳耦合（on-shell couplings）施加约束，不依赖截断的手征展开；胶球部分以大N格谱（lattice spectrum）作为输入。

Result: 对π介子给出了O(p^4)系数与ππf_2耦合的界；ππf_2耦合的界呈现拐点（kink），该处提取的谱与现实世界介子谱高度接近。对胶球给出了最轻标量胶球与更重高自旋胶球耦合的界，并发现具有线性Regge轨迹（linear Regge trajectories）的极值解。

Conclusion: 结果表明大N bootstrap可随夸克质量连续追踪约束，并可扩展至无低能有效理论的胶球散射，极值解自然再现线性Regge轨迹等强子谱特征。

Abstract: We extend the large $N$ bootstrap of pion scattering to nonzero pion mass, and apply the same formalism to glueball scattering. Our bounds apply to Taylor coefficients and on-shell couplings of the exact planar amplitude. They do not rely on a truncated chiral expansion, so we can follow them as the quark mass varies, and apply them to glueballs, for which no low-energy effective theory exists. For pions, we bound the $O(p^4)$ coefficients and the $ππf_2$ coupling. As in the chiral limit, the bound on the $ππf_2$ coupling features a kink, where the extracted spectrum closely resembles the real-world meson spectrum. For glueballs, we bound the couplings of the lightest scalar glueball to heavier glueballs of higher spin, using the large $N$ lattice spectrum as input, and find extremal solutions with linear Regge trajectories.

</details>


### [129] [Small black hole in de Sitter as an observer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31027)
*Guanda Lin,Haifeng Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了四维德西特空间中磁性带电“温凉黑洞（lukewarm black hole）”周围的欧几里得路径积分，数值求得引力子与光子涨落的完整单圈谱，发现配分函数相位与“纯引力加世界线观察者”的情形一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是检验德西特空间中带电磁荷黑洞的量子修正是否与纯引力加观察者图像相符，并厘清观察者世界线作用量能否由涨落谱中重构。

Method: 采用欧几里得路径积分（Euclidean path integral）框架，对耦合的引力子（graviton）与光子（photon）涨落做数值单圈（one-loop）谱分析，并在小质量极限下考察局域本征模。

Result: 配分函数（partition function）的相位与空德西特加世界线观察者情形下的纯引力结果一致；小质量极限下部分局域本征模可视为描述观察者运动且本征值符合预期，从而可重构世界线作用量（worldline action）。

Conclusion: 这些模式可等同于渐近平坦时空中极端带电黑洞的平移零模（translational zero modes），并由德西特曲率（de Sitter curvature）抬升。

Abstract: We study the Euclidean path integral around magnetically charged lukewarm black holes in four-dimensional de Sitter space. We numerically determine the full one-loop spectrum of coupled graviton and photon fluctuations and find that the phase of the partition function agrees with that of pure gravity in empty de Sitter plus a worldline observer. In the small-mass limit, certain localized eigenmodes can be effectively viewed as describing the observer's motion and have the expected eigenvalues, allowing us to reconstruct the worldline action. We further identify these modes with translational zero modes of an extremal charged black hole in asymptotically flat spacetime, lifted by de Sitter curvature.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum gravity on the bulk cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31610)
*Ahmed Almheiri,Matthew Dodelson,Zohar Komargodski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证在有限牛顿常数（$G_N$）下，共形场论（CFT）不能具有体光锥奇点（bulk-cone singularities），无论对单个微观态还是热态均成立，其根源是量子引力中体解的模糊性（fuzziness）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体光锥奇点（bulk-cone singularities）编码了沿体零测地线（null geodesics）的传播，典型例子是AdS黑洞中入射零测地线绕光子球（photon sphere）回返边界并在有限体积热两点函数中留下实时奇点；作者意在检验此类奇点在有限$G_N$的全息对偶中是否依然存在。

Method: 主要论证手段是对温度做解析延拓（analytic continuation in the temperature）；同时构造一个含两个碰撞激波（colliding shocks）的AdS$_3$几何作为微观示例，并在CFT中显式计算以检验该奇点。

Result: 论证表明有限$G_N$下的引力对偶CFT不可能出现此类奇点，单个微观态与热态均被排除；在AdS$_3$碰撞激波例子中，CFT显式计算显示该体光锥奇点在有限$G_N$下被抹平（smoothed out）。

Conclusion: 奇点的消除定性上是量子引力中体解模糊性的结果，即体光锥奇点并非有限$G_N$下CFT的精确特征。

Abstract: Bulk-cone singularities in holographic correlators encode propagation along bulk null geodesics. The prototypical example is an AdS black hole, where incoming null geodesics can wind around the photon sphere and return to the boundary. These singularities appear in real time in the thermal two-point function at finite volume. We show that conformal field theories dual to gravitational theories at finite $G_N$ cannot have such singularities. We rule out these singularities both for individual microstates and for the thermal state. The main thrust of the argument is to invoke an analytic continuation in the temperature. As a microscopic example, we construct an AdS$_3$ geometry with two colliding shocks which exhibits a bulk cone singularity. We show by an explicit calculation in the CFT that this singularity is smoothed out at finite $G_N$. Qualitatively, the singularity resolution is a consequence of the fuzziness of the bulk solution in quantum gravity.

</details>


### [131] [On Born-Infeld-type higher-spin interactions and supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31652)
*Nikita Zaigraev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维平空间构造了无质量高自旋场（higher-spin fields）的 Born–Infeld 型立方相互作用（cubic interactions），基于单一手征高自旋 Weyl 张量（Weyl tensors）组成的守恒流（conserved current），并推广至 N=1、N=2 超对称（supersymmetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有著名的 Berends–Burgers–van Dam 流不能直接给出 Born–Infeld 型高自旋立方顶角，需寻找与之不同的、由单一手征 Weyl 张量构成的守恒流构造，并系统推广到超对称情形。

Method: 采用 Lorentz 协变（Lorentz-covariant）构造，从一般拟设（ansatz）出发推导递推关系（recurrence relations）以固定流系数，剔除平庸（fake）顶角后确定唯一的非平庸守恒流；通过高阶自旋 Weyl 张量给出规范不变表示，并借助离壳超场（off-shell superfield）形式与调和超空间（harmonic superspace）推广至 N=1、N=2。

Result: 对任意自旋 s ≥ s₁ ≥ s₂，流形如 J ∼ ∂C∂C 且含 s+s₁+s₂ 个导数；在 s ≤ s₁+s₂ 范围内顶角具有以 Weyl 张量表示的显式规范不变形式。N=1 情形得到一般守恒整数超自旋（superspin）超流，当 Y₁=Y₂ 且 s 为奇数时为零，与 Gates–Koutrolikos 结果一致；N=2 情形由守恒方程唯一确定主高自旋超流，且相同超场下顶角不消失，与 N=0,1 不同。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Born–Infeld 型高自旋相互作用及其超对称推广提供了系统框架，并揭示了 N=2 情形在相同超场下顶角非零这一区别于 N=0,1 的特性。

Abstract: We develop a simple Lorentz-covariant construction of the Born--Infeld-type cubic interactions $(s, s_1, s_2)$ of massless higher-spin fields with $s + s_1 + s_2$ derivatives in four-dimensiona flat space. For arbitrary spins $s \ge s_1 \ge s_2$, the vertex is formulated in terms of a conserved higher-spin current and its trace. In contrast to the famous Berends--Burgers--van Dam currents, these currents are constructed from higher-spin Weyl tensors of a single chirality, schematically $J \sim \partial C \partial C$. Starting with a general ansatz, we derive recurrence relations fixing the current coefficients, identify the unique non-trivial conserved current after eliminating trivial (fake) vertices, and show that in the range $s \le s_1 + s_2$ the interaction admits a manifestly gauge-invariant representation in terms of higher-spin Weyl tensors. The construction is then extended to $\mathcal{N}=1$ and $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetry using the corresponding off-shell superfield formalisms. In the $\mathcal{N}=1$ case, we obtain the general conserved integer superspin $s$ supercurrent with manifest supersymmetry, which is bilinear in the Weyl supertensors of superspins $Y_1$ and $Y_2$. For identical superfields, $Y_1 = Y_2$, this supercurrent vanishes for odd $s$ and is in agreement with earlier results by Gates and Koutrolikos. In the $\mathcal{N}=2$ case, using harmonic superspace, we construct the principal higher-spin supercurrent whose coefficients are uniquely fixed by the conservation equations. In contrast to the $\mathcal{N}=0,1$ cases, for identical $\mathcal{N}=2$ superfields, the corresponding vertex does not vanish. The results provide a systematic framework for Born--Infeld-type higher-spin interactions and their supersymmetric extensions.

</details>


### [132] [Onsager Algebras and Lattice Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31653)
*Ioanna Kourkoulou,David Tong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作研究自由格点费米子（free lattice fermions）在交错（staggered）、Wilson 与重叠（overlap）三种实现下的精确 Onsager 代数（exact Onsager algebra），并证明其蕴含低能理论的全部手征对称性（chiral symmetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清格点费米子实现中精确格点代数与连续极限手征对称性之间的对应关系，理解不同格点形式（formulation）在对称性生成元量子化与局域性上的差异。

Method: 在四维时空（four spacetime dimensions）同时采用哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）与欧几里得（Euclidean）表述，对交错、Wilson、重叠三种自由格点费米子构造并进行精确 Onsager 代数的分析。

Result: 每种情形下，精确格点代数均导出低能理论的完整手征对称性：若连续理论含 N 个无质量狄拉克费米子（massless Dirac fermions），Onsager 代数约化为 U(2N) 手征对称性；不同表述间量子化（quantised）或超局域（ultra-local）的生成元细节不同。

Conclusion: 生成元的局域性（locality）与 Onsager 代数最大紧致子群（maximum compact subgroup）之间存在权衡（trade-off），为格点费米子对称性实现提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We study the exact Onsager algebra of free lattice fermions in staggered, Wilson, and overlap incarnations, working in both Hamiltonian and Euclidean formulations in four spacetime dimensions. In each case, we show that the exact lattice algebra emanates the full chiral symmetry of the low-energy theory: if there are $N$ massless Dirac fermions in the continuum, the Onsager algebra reduces to a $U(2N)$ chiral symmetry. The details of which generators are quantised or ultra-local differ between formulations. There is also a trade-off between the locality of generators and the maximum compact subgroup of the Onsager algebra.

</details>


### [133] [Half-Completeness from Standard Gravitational Dispersion Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31665)
*Lukas W. Lindwasser,Laurentiu Rodina,Justinas Rumbutis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在不额外假设高能上界的前提下，仅用两次减除色散关系（twice-subtracted dispersion relations）、正定性（positivity）与交叉对称性（crossing symmetry），证明了外部无质量 SU(2) 同位旋 J 情形下的“半完备性”，并说明高能界为独立假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于引力完备性假设（completeness hypothesis）的 S-矩阵（S-matrix）论证依赖引力子极点（graviton pole）及对交叉奇振幅（crossing-odd amplitudes）附加的高能界；本文旨在考察去掉该高能假设后标准框架本身能推出什么。

Method: 采用标准两次减除色散关系、正定性与交叉对称性进行分析；对 J=1 构造显式振幅，对 J≥2 在一类弦启发的 Euler 振幅（string-inspired Euler amplitudes）中检验低自旋谱，并推广至任意紧致内部对称群（compact internal symmetry group）下的标量物质。

Result: 对无质量 SU(2) 同位旋 J 的外部态证明了“半完备性”（half-completeness）：有质量交换要么包含所有 j≤J，要么至少需要一个 j>J。J=1 时给出仅含 j=0,1 交换且满足条件但违反交叉奇上界的显式振幅，表明该上界不由标准框架导出。J≥2 时在该类 Euler 振幅中未能实现低自旋谱。此外，对任意紧致内部对称群下的标量物质，证明必须交换洛伦兹自旋（Lorentz spin）无界的有质量单态。

Conclusion: 标准色散关系、正定性与交叉对称性不足以给出完整完备性结论，需区分具体自旋情形：J=1 处附属高能假设可被违反，J≥2 的低自旋谱可实现性仍为开放问题，而一般内部对称群下要求存在无界洛伦兹自旋的有质量单态。

Abstract: The completeness hypothesis states that quantum gravity realizes every representation allowed by its gauge group. Previous S-matrix arguments used the graviton pole to constrain exchanged representations, including a proof of completeness by imposing an additional high-energy bound on crossing-odd amplitudes. We investigate what follows without this assumption, using standard twice-subtracted dispersion relations, positivity, and crossing symmetry. For external massless SU(2) isospin $J$, we prove ``half-completeness'': either massive exchanges include every isospin $j\le J$, or at least one $j>J$ is required. At $J=1$, we construct an explicit amplitude with only $j=0,1$ exchanges that satisfies these conditions but violates the crossing-odd bound. This establishes that the additional high-energy assumption does not follow from the standard framework. For $J\ge2$, however, we show that lower-only spectra cannot be realized within a class of string-inspired Euler amplitudes, leaving their existence an open question. More generally, for scalar matter transforming under any compact internal symmetry group, we prove that massive singlets of unbounded Lorentz spin must be exchanged.

</details>


### [134] [High precision black hole scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31238)
*Benjamin Sauer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在五阶后闵可夫斯基（Post-Minkowskian, PM）展开下计算经典黑洞散射的冲量（impulse），采用世界线量子场论（worldline quantum field theory, WQFT）图解求解，并发现Calabi–Yau流形周期（Calabi–Yau manifold periods）在经典物理观测量中出现，给出了该类散射可观测量的首个解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画黑洞散射动力学需要在牛顿常数（Newton's constant）G的弱场展开中不断提高阶数，而五阶后闵可夫斯基（5PM）阶此前缺乏解析结果，尤其是其可能涉及的非平凡数学结构尚未被揭示。

Method: 将黑洞视为有效场论（effective field theory）框架下的点粒子并通过引力耦合，使用世界线量子场论（WQFT）形式将满足因果边界条件的经典运动方程解编码为树阶费曼图（tree-level Feynman diagrams），再借助积分表约化（integration-by-parts, IBP）技术与微分方程方法（method of differential equations）约化并求解费曼积分。

Result: 在五阶后闵可夫斯基（5PM）阶得到经典冲量的解析结果，即该类散射观测量的首个解析计算；同时发现源自弦理论（string theory）的Calabi–Yau流形周期出现在积分解中并贡献于经典冲量，构成Calabi–Yau三重周期（Calabi–Yau threefold periods）在经典物理观测量中的首次出现。

Conclusion: 结果表明高阶后闵可夫斯基引力散射会引入超越以往认知的数学结构（Calabi–Yau周期），凸显WQFT与先进积分约化技术在经典引力精确计算中的有效性，并为后续更高阶及更复杂观测量研究提供基础。

Abstract: In this thesis we compute the impulse of a classical black hole scattering encounter in the Post-Minkowskian (PM) expansion - a weak-field expansion in Newton's constant $G$ - up to the fifth order. We describe the black holes as point particles within an effective field theory framework and couple them through gravity. The classical equations of motion are solved diagrammatically using the worldline quantum field theory (WQFT) formalism, in which the solution with causal boundary conditions is encoded in tree-level Feynman diagrams built from graviton and worldline fields. The Feynman integrals arising at each order are reduced by advanced IBP reduction techniques and evaluated with the method of differential equations. We find that, in the solution of these integrals, periods over Calabi--Yau manifolds - familiar from string theory - appear and contribute to the classical impulse at the fifth Post-Minkowskian order. The results of this work are the first analytic results for the scattering observables at fifth Post-Minkowskian order, together with the appearance of Calabi--Yau threefold periods in a classical physics observable.

</details>


### [135] [Exact stress-tensor generators distinguish integrable continuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31297)
*H. Babaei-Aghbolagh,Song He,Zahra Pezhman*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作表明，即便在因果且经典可积的二维 sigma 模型中，相同的领头 $T\bar T$ 相互作用也能伴随截然不同的强场响应。作者通过重构精确的自治应力张量生成元，将重构问题化为单标量反演，并用所得到的 profile（即压力的能量密度函数）区分广义 Born–Infeld 与对数模型的饱和型与无界增长型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识中，领头阶匹配常被视为足以刻画相互作用，但论文关注同一领头 $T\bar T$ 耦合下强场行为是否存在不可消除的自由度。研究动机在于量化高阶重整化群（renormalization group）流所残留的非线性自由度，而非仅依赖首项匹配。

Method: 对给定单尺度 Courant–Hilbert 族，构造精确的自治应力张量生成元；将不变应力尺度与带符号的迹比（trace ratio）分离，使重构在每条可逆物理分支上归约为一次标量反演。所得 profile 同时确定形变律与局域能量密度-压力关系，并在辅助势表述中通过正则 Legendre 变换复现同一结果。

Result: 广义 Born–Infeld 与对数模型的领头 $T\bar T$ 相互作用相同，但前者压力饱和、生成元为二次型，后者压力无界增长、生成元为非多项式并涉及 Lambert $W_{-1}$。两类流在共同光滑分支上局部与 root-$T\bar T$ 对易，且均保留共同的经典 Lax 形式。

Conclusion: 精确重构表明，领头阶匹配并不能唯一固定理论，仍留下可量化的非线性自由度；这些自由度体现在强场压力响应与应力张量生成元的多项式结构上。

Abstract: The same leading $T\bar T$ interaction can coexist with sharply different strong-field responses, even within causal, classically integrable sigma models. We reconstruct exact autonomous stress-tensor generators for prescribed one-scale Courant--Hilbert families in two dimensions. Separating the invariant stress scale from a signed trace ratio reduces reconstruction to one scalar inversion on each invertible physical branch. The resulting profile determines both the deformation law and the relation between local energy density and pressure. For generalized Born--Infeld and logarithmic models, the leading interactions coincide, yet pressure saturates in the former and grows without bound in the latter. Their exact generators are respectively quadratic and nonpolynomial, the latter involving Lambert $W_{-1}$. A regular Legendre transformation gives the same profile in the auxiliary potential formulation. The flows commute locally with root-$T\bar T$ within a common smooth branch and retain a common classical Lax form. Exact reconstruction thus quantifies the nonlinear freedom left by leading-order matching.

</details>


### [136] [Monodromy Defects of 3D Wilson-Fisher Criticality on the Fuzzy Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31269)
*Yin Tang,W. Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过模糊球正则化中施加对称性选择性的 Aharonov-Bohm 磁通，实现单值（twist）线缺陷，并系统提取 3D Ising、O(2) 与 O(3) CFT 中单值缺陷的非微扰共形数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单值（monodromy/twist）线缺陷是拓扑对称面的边界，可在量子磁体磁涡旋、规范理论 Wilson/'t Hooft 线及量子信息复制 twist 算符中实现，但其相互作用动力学难以非微扰刻画。

Method: 在模糊球（fuzzy-sphere）正则化中，于球两极穿入对称性选择性的 Aharonov-Bohm 磁通，以实现单值边界条件；该方法应用于 3D Ising CFT 的 Z2 twist、3D O(2) CFT 的 π-twist，以及 O(3) 的三种不等价单值（中心 twist、z 轴 π-twist 与反射）。

Result: 对每个缺陷提取低能缺陷谱及体-缺陷（bulk-to-defect）与缺陷-缺陷（defect-defect）算符乘积展开系数；在所解析的低能缺陷原初算符中，未发现保持缺陷全部对称性的相关标量微扰。

Conclusion: 所识别的相关形变至少破坏一个保持对称性，表明这些单值缺陷对保持对称性的相关微扰稳定；成果建立了超越 Ising twist 的单值缺陷系统性非微扰共形数据，并将对称性磁通变为模糊球正则化的可调参数。

Abstract: Monodromy (twist) line defects arise as the boundaries of topological symmetry surfaces, which are among the simplest non-trivial conformal defects with direct realizations as magnetic vortex in quantum magnets, as Wilson and t Hooft lines of gauge theories, and as the replica twist operators in quantum information theory. However, their interacting dynamics remains difficult to characterize non-perturbatively. Here we design a way to realize the monodromy boundary conditions in the fuzzy-sphere regularization by threading a symmetry-selective Aharonov-Bohm flux through the two poles of the sphere. We apply this construction to the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ twist of the 3D Ising conformal field theory (CFT), $π$-twist of the 3D O(2) CFT and three inequivalent monodromies of O(3) (centre twist, z-axis $π$-twist and the reflection). For each defect, we extract the low-lying defect spectrum together with bulk-to-defect and defect-defect operator product expansion coefficients, which characterize the operators living on the defect line, their mutual interactions, and how they couple to the surrounding critical bulk. Among the low-lying defect primaries resolved in our spectra, we find no relevant scalar perturbation that preserves all the symmetries of the corresponding defect. The relevant deformations identified in our calculations break at least one preserved symmetry, providing evidence that these monodromy defects are stable against symmetry-preserving relevant perturbations. These results establish a systematic set of non-perturbative conformal data for monodromy defects beyond the Ising twist. Our construction therefore turns the symmetry flux into a tunable knob of the fuzzy-sphere regularization, opening a route to exploring the response of critical matter to distinct defects.

</details>
