### [137] [In situ performance of the Vertex Silicon Plane in the BM@N experiment during Run 9](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31077)
*D. Dementev,M. Shitenkov,A. Kolozhvari,V. Leontyev,A. Sheremetev,D. Andreev,R. Arteche Diaz,D. Baranov,C. Ceballos Sanchez,I. Gabdrakhmanov,I. Gorelikov,T. Lygdenova,I. Osokin,S. Reyes Peña,I. Rufanov,E. Tsapulina,A. Voronin,Yu. Murin*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 顶点硅平面（Vertex Silicon Plane, VSP）在NICA加速器复合体上BM@N实验第9次运行（Run 9）中首次作为最上游追踪站投入运行，其模块性能与追踪性能经测量均满足要求，并扩展了中心追踪系统的几何接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BM@N实验需要靠近靶区的额外追踪点，以扩展原有基于前向硅探测器（Forward Silicon Detector, FSD）与气体电子倍增器（Gas Electron Multiplier, GEM）的中心追踪系统的几何接受度，尤其是大极角覆盖。

Method: VSP由六个双面硅微条模块（double-sided silicon microstrip modules）组成，基于CBM-BM@N探测器技术，安装于SP-41分析磁体内靶下游约8 cm处；通过信噪比、时间分辨率与磁场中洛伦兹漂移（Lorentz drift）表征模块性能，并以径迹-击中残差（track-hit residual）和击中匹配效率评估追踪性能，采用FSD与GEM两套独立参考配置进行交叉检验。

Result: 最可几簇信噪比（cluster signal-to-noise ratio）为15.5至33.2，相对触发脉冲的时间分辨率约12 ns；基于FSD与GEM的两套参考配置给出一致约95%的击中匹配效率；并通过横向径迹碰撞参数（transverse track impact parameter）与短VSP-GEM径迹段（tracklet）重建进一步研究了VSP对BM@N追踪性能的贡献。

Conclusion: VSP凭借靠近靶区的位置与大极角覆盖，提供了额外的近靶追踪点，使几何接受度得以超越基于FSD与GEM的中心追踪系统，成功实现首次运行。

Abstract: The Vertex Silicon Plane (VSP) was commissioned and operated for the first time as the most upstream tracking station of the Baryonic Matter at Nuclotron (BM@N) experiment at the NICA accelerator complex during Run 9. The station consisting of six double-sided silicon microstrip modules based on the CBM-BM@N detector technology was installed about 8 cm downstream from the target inside the SP-41 analyzing magnet. The in situ module performance was characterized in terms of the signal-to-noise ratio, time resolution, and Lorentz drift in the magnetic field. The most probable cluster signal-to-noise ratio ranges from 15.5 to 33.2, while the time resolution relative to the trigger pulse is about 12 ns. The tracking performance was evaluated using track-hit residuals and hit-matching efficiency; two independent reference configurations based on the Forward Silicon Detector (FSD) and Gas Electron Multiplier (GEM) tracking systems give consistent efficiencies of about 95%. The contribution of the VSP to the BM@N tracking performance was further studied through the transverse track impact parameter and the reconstruction of short VSP-GEM tracklets. Owing to its position close to the target and its coverage at large polar angles, the VSP provides an additional near-target tracking point and makes it possible to extend the geometrical acceptance beyond that of the FSD- and GEM-based central tracking system.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Non-Destructive Detection of Early Drought Stress via Spectral Submanifold Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30287)
*Sunghwan Jung,Isabella Thiel,Jisoo Yuk,George Haller*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出基于谱子流形（Spectral Submanifold, SSM）降维的快速无损方法，通过分析大豆植株非线性振动动力学实现水分胁迫的早期检测，并首次实验证明SSM非线性模型降维可探测活体生物内部状态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业（precision agriculture）需要无损、早期的水分胁迫（water stress）检测手段，而现有方法多为劳动密集或具破坏性，且传统线性指标（如线性频率、叶片萎蔫）缺乏一致的统计信号。

Method: 采用数据驱动的SSM降维方法对完整大豆植株的非线性振动动力学建模，相较传统线性模态分析（linear modal analysis），在降维模型中提取非线性阻尼系数并追踪其在干旱进程中的变化。

Result: 发现植株茎部主导动力学随干旱进展出现明显转变：SSM降维模型中的非线性阻尼系数发生符号反转，即从富水加压导管的超线性（黏弹性）阻尼转变为干燥细胞固体的亚线性（摩擦）阻尼；该转变与成熟叶片SPAD值及比色指标（colorimetric metrics）显著下降所指示的生理胁迫起始密切相关。

Conclusion: 该转变为活体生物耗散机制（dissipative mechanics）的基本转变提供了证据，SSM非线性降维可作为基于物理的高通量植物表型分析（high-throughput plant phenotyping）指标。

Abstract: Non-destructive early detection of water stress is critical for precision agriculture, yet current detection methods are often labor-intensive or destructive. We propose a rapid, non-invasive method based on Spectral Submanifold (SSM) reduction to analyze the nonlinear vibration dynamics of soybean plants. Unlike traditional linear modal analysis, our data-driven approach reveals a distinct transition in the dominant dynamics of the plant stem as drought progresses. Specifically, we uncover a sign reversal in the nonlinear damping coefficient within SSM-reduced models of full plants. Our finding reveals a fundamental transition in the plant's dissipative mechanics from a pressurized, fluid-rich vessel exhibiting superlinear (viscoelastic) damping to a dry, cellular solid dominated by sublinear (frictional) damping. This transition is closely correlated with the onset of physiological stress indicated by a significant decrease in the values of mature-leaf soil plant analysis development (SPAD) and colorimetric metrics. In contrast, traditional metrics, such as linear frequency and leaf wilting, do not provide consistent statistical signals of water stress. This study provides the first experimental evidence that SSM-based nonlinear model reduction can detect the changing internal state of a living organism, offering a physics-based metric for high-throughput plant phenotyping.

</details>


### [139] [Shear-induced mechanical yielding controls cluster morphology and dynamics in aggregating non-Brownian suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30330)
*Jae Hwan Jeong,Donald L. Koch,Sarah Hormozi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 利用并行加速Stokesian动力学模拟，揭示剪切作用下吸引性非布朗悬浮液中团簇应力子随回转半径的三段标度规律（立方、平方、线性），建立了微观团簇形态动力学与宏观流变学的机理联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪切团聚悬浮液中，流动同时驱动颗粒团簇的形成与破碎，其动态演化影响输运与流变性质；但颗粒间作用力、流体动力学相互作用与剪切流的复杂耦合使相关机理尚不清晰。

Method: 采用并行加速Stokesian动力学（Accelerated Stokesian Dynamics, PASD）模拟，研究剪切、吸引性非布朗（non-Brownian）悬浮液中团簇的微观结构与旋转动力学，以及二者耦合如何产生悬浮液应力；并将团簇应力子（stresslet）与尺寸分别用其力学屈服尺度归一化后分析标度关系。

Result: 归一化团簇应力子塌缩到一条主曲线，呈现三个区制：立方区制中小而致密的团簇近似刚体，应力子随体积R_g^3标度；平方区制中低长径比团簇优先屈服，平均长径比随尺寸近似正比增长，高长径比团簇遵循Jeffery旋转动力学、更多时间沿流动方向排列，使时间平均应力子降为R_g^2标度；线性区制中团簇尺寸受颗粒间键强度上限约束，内部张力有界，应力子呈R_g依赖。

Conclusion: 研究确立了连接微观团簇形态与动力学同宏观流变学（rheology）的机理框架，为理解剪切团聚悬浮液的应力起源提供了统一标度描述。

Abstract: In sheared aggregating suspensions, flow drives both the formation and breakage of particle clusters, whose dynamic evolution influences transport and rheological properties. Understanding these mechanisms remains challenging due to the complex interplay between interparticle forces, hydrodynamic interactions, and shear flow. Using parallelized Accelerated Stokesian Dynamics (PASD) simulations, we investigate cluster microstructure and rotational dynamics, and how their coupling produces suspension stress in sheared, attractive non-Brownian suspensions. When interparticle attraction dominates over separating shear forces, clusters grow until mechanical loading balances maximum interparticle bond strength. We examine how the cluster stresslet depends on cluster size, with both quantities normalized by their mechanical yielding scales. Scaled cluster stresslets collapse onto a master curve exhibiting three distinct regimes: cubic, quadratic, and linear with respect to the scaled radius of gyration $R_g$. In the cubic regime, small, compact clusters behave as rigid bodies, whose hydrodynamic stresslet scales with cluster volume $R_g^3$. As clusters grow into the quadratic regime, low-aspect-ratio clusters preferentially yield under flow, driving a proportional increase in average aspect ratio with size. High-aspect-ratio clusters follow Jeffery rotational dynamics within the flow-gradient plane, spending longer durations aligned with the flow and thereby reducing their time-averaged hydrodynamic stresslet to scale as $R_g^2$. In the linear regime, clusters approach the maximum size sustainable by the interparticle bonds. The internal tension is bounded by the maximum bond strength, limiting the stresslet to an $R_g$ dependence. These findings establish a mechanistic framework connecting microscale cluster morphology and dynamics to macroscale rheology.

</details>


### [140] [Emergent Entropic Forces Drive Inverse and Reentrant Phase Transitions in Condensates of Intrinsically Disordered Proteins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30627)
*David A. Herrera-Martí,Marc Jamin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一个基于矩阵化Flory-Huggins自由能的唯象平均场模型，用以描述固有无序蛋白凝聚体的逆相变及盐浓度依赖的重入相行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固有无序蛋白（intrinsically disordered proteins）凝聚体存在逆相变（inverse phase transitions）与重入相行为（reentrant phase behavior），需要理论框架加以解释。

Method: 采用矩阵形式的Flory-Huggins自由能（Flory-Huggins free energy），将焓交互参数与蛋白价数（定义为有效“粘性斑块”数目）视为自由参数；通过将共溶质的高斯积分（Gaussian integration）与矩阵自由能的Schur补（Schur complement）相联系，刻画消除快运动组分后产生的有效熵力（effective entropic force）。

Result: 模型定性预测了随盐浓度变化的逆相变与重入相行为，并定性再现了关于狂犬病毒磷酸蛋白（rabies virus phosphoprotein）的近期实验观测。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个一般性热力学框架，说明增加可用微观状态数的焓交互必然诱导有效熵力。

Abstract: We present a phenomenological, mean-field model for condensates of intrinsically disordered proteins that describes inverse phase transitions and qualitatively predicts reentrant phase behavior as a function of salt concentration. The model is based on a matrix-based formulation of the Flory-Huggins free energy, in which the enthalpic interaction parameter and the protein valence, defined here as the number of effective "sticky patches", are treated as free parameters. Its central insight is that integrating out dynamics of fast-moving molecular species generates an effective entropic force that increases the free-energy barrier to mixing, even within a mean-field description. We account for this by linking Gaussian integration of co-solutes to the Schur complement of the matrix-based Flory-Huggins free energy. This provides a general thermodynamic framework illustrating how enthalpic interactions that increase the number of available microstates necessarily induce effective entropic forces. We demonstrate the expressiveness and predictive power of the model by qualitatively reproducing recent experimental observations on the rabies virus phosphoprotein.

</details>


### [141] [Strain Waves Generate Compression and Trigger Buckling in Active Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30749)
*Wen-de Tian,Kang Chen,Tianhui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出并验证了化学反应的活性收缩（active contraction）可在弯曲（buckling）阈值以下驱动半柔性聚合物（semiflexible polymer）产生有限波长行波应变波（traveling strain waves），并以张力依赖的化学转变（tension-dependent chemical transitions）与键弹性（bond elasticity）耦合为机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学能驱动聚合物运动通常需先产生压缩以实现屈曲，但并非所有活性体系都伴随足够的压缩，因此需要探索无需屈曲中间步骤的活性运动机制。

Method: 采用理论分析与模拟（theory and simulations），构建张力依赖化学转变与键弹性耦合的模型，考察固定轴向跨距（fixed axial span）的直链及平均载荷释放条件下的动力学行为。

Result: 在固定轴向跨距的直链中，行波降低平均活性收缩（mean active contraction）而平均弹性张力（mean elastic tension）不变；超过某一阈值时产生驱动弯曲的压缩。释放平均载荷后，纵向波持续存在而横向扰动（transverse perturbations）衰减。

Conclusion: 张力依赖的化学转变与键弹性耦合可产生有限波长的纵向行波，并通过降低平均活性收缩在阈值以上引发弯曲，揭示了一种区别于屈曲介导路径的活性聚合物运动模式。

Abstract: Chemical reactions can sustain polymer motion without necessarily generating the compression required for buckling. Theory and simulations show that tension-dependent chemical transitions coupled to bond elasticity generate finite-wavelength traveling strain waves in a semiflexible polymer. In the straight chain at fixed axial span, the waves reduce mean active contraction while leaving mean elastic tension unchanged, generating compression that drives bending above a threshold. Releasing the mean load allows longitudinal waves to persist while transverse perturbations decay.

</details>


### [142] [Surface morphology and shear effect on lubricated granular system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30831)
*Ravindra S. Ghodake,Ashish V. Orpe*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究考察细粉润滑剂（fine powder lubricant）在流动散料颗粒（bulk powder particles）中的润滑行为，揭示润滑剂浓度、剪切环境（shear environment）、受限程度（confinement）与颗粒表面形貌（surface morphology）如何耦合影响颗粒流动性（flowability）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善颗粒表面摩擦行为（particle surface frictional behavior）对调控粉体流动性（powder flowability）与输送过程具有重要工业价值，但受限条件、剪切环境与颗粒表面特性如何共同作用于润滑效果尚需厘清。

Method: 在两种剪切环境、两种受限水平下，采用三种表面形貌不同的散料颗粒开展剪切实验，并添加细粉润滑剂；润滑剂颗粒附着于主体颗粒表面从而改变其表面形貌与颗粒间摩擦（inter-particle friction）。

Result: 少量添加润滑剂通常降低颗粒间摩擦，过量添加则使其升高，从而改变流动性；延长剪切时间（extended shear duration）可拓宽改善流动性的润滑剂浓度范围，而流动受限则限制该浓度范围内的润滑程度，两种相反效应均依赖于颗粒表面形貌及其与润滑剂颗粒的表观亲和性（affinity）。

Conclusion: 所考察的参数类型与范围覆盖多数工业系统的实际条件，研究结果为含润滑剂-颗粒混合物（lubricant-particle mixtures）的工艺设计与应用提供依据。

Abstract: Improving the particle surface frictional behavior is of considerable importance in several industries so as to manipulate the powder flowability and handling. In this work, we have studied the lubrication phenomena in an assembly of flowing bulk powder particles in presence of a fine powder lubricant. Experiments were conducted in two different shear environments, for two different levels of confinement and using three different bulk powder particles having distinct surface morphology. The added lubricant particles tend to adhere to the surface of bulk particles thereby altering their surface morphology. This results in altered inter-particle friction, which typically reduces for small addition of lubricant, but increases on its excess addition, thereby altering the particle flowability. While this behavior is generally understood through previous studies, the current work explores how the effect of confinement, shear environment and particle surface characteristics combine in a unique manner to provide modified flow behavior for different lubricant concentrations. Extended shear duration seems to extend the range of lubricant concentrations for improved flowability. On the other hand flow confinement seems to limit the extent of lubrication for these range of concentrations. Both these opposing effects also exhibit a unique dependence on the particle surface morphology and its seeming affinity with the lubricant particle. The parameter types and ranges explored comprise most conditions encountered in industrial systems, the outcome thereby providing a basis for designing of applications handling lubricant-particle mixtures.

</details>


### [143] [Entropy-production cost limits hierarchical decision-making in homing pigeon flocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31126)
*Bibaswan Ghosh,Vipul Rai,Harsh Soni,Nitin Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以信鸽（homing pigeon）群体为模型，构建基于轨迹（trajectory-based）的框架来推断并量化导航群体中的等级结构（hierarchy），发现小群体等级显著、群体增大后等级逐渐削弱而趋于平等决策，并用热力学框架解释其物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体决策（collective decision-making）要求个体调和多样甚至冲突的意见，但群体内个体影响力并不对等，形成单向（non-reciprocal）沟通模式，表现为领导者—追随者（leader-follower）关系与社会等级（social hierarchy）。作者希望量化活体集体导航中的等级结构并给出其物理机制。

Method: 以信鸽群为模型系统，提出基于轨迹的框架来推断和量化群体内的等级；并引入热力学框架（thermodynamic framework），将个体间的非互惠沟通（non-reciprocal communication）与信息熵代价（information-entropy cost）联系起来进行计算。

Result: 小规模鸽群表现出显著的等级结构，随群体规模增大等级逐步减弱，群体趋向更平等的决策方式。计算表明，这种等级削弱是各规模下维持个体熵产生率（per-individual entropy production rate）恒定所导致的直接结果。

Conclusion: 对信息处理能力（information-processing capacity）的约束可能在活体生物的集体动力学（collective dynamics）中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Collective decision-making requires individuals to reconcile diverse, often conflicting opinions to reach a common course of action. However, within a group, they rarely exert equal influence, leading to non-reciprocal communication patterns manifested as leader-follower relationships and social hierarchies. Here, using homing pigeon flocks as a model system, we develop a trajectory-based framework to infer and quantify hierarchy within navigating living collectives. We find that while small flocks exhibit pronounced hierarchy, increasing flock size progressively weakens it, driving the collection toward more egalitarian decision-making. We provide a physical basis of this observation using a thermodynamic framework in which non-reciprocal communication between birds carries an information-entropy cost. Our calculations reveal that hierarchy weakening observed in larger flocks is a direct consequence of maintaining a constant per-individual entropy production rate across flock sizes. Together, these results suggest that constraints on information-processing capacity may play an important role in the collective dynamics of living organisms.

</details>


### [144] [Functional understanding of structural components in polypseudorotaxane gels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31157)
*Ella Sapsford,Andrea Bonato,Simon Weir,Nawar Zebari,Paul J. Lusby,Chenfeng Ke,Davide Michieletto*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过简单合成扰动分别调控准聚轮烷（pseudopolyrotaxane, pPR）溶液中的聚合物-环糊精（CD）与CD-CD相互作用，并结合粗粒化分子动力学（coarse-grained Molecular Dynamics）模拟，阐明滑环凝胶（slide-ring gels, SRGs）组分结构-功能关系，为增强型水凝胶与弹性体的理性设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学器件、可穿戴界面与软体机器人需要兼具柔顺性与可靠力学性能的软材料，但设计此类材料的分子原理尚不完全清楚；滑环凝胶（SRG）虽可显著提升韧性与拉伸性，因缺乏对各组分作用的机理理解，其理性设计仍具挑战。

Method: 在无端基封堵的准聚轮烷（pPR）溶液中实施简单合成扰动，分别靶向聚合物-CD相互作用与CD-CD相互作用，并测量黏弹性（viscoelastic）性质的相应变化；同时采用粗粒化分子动力学模拟对实验观察进行合理化解释。

Result: 削弱CD间分子间相互作用会抑制结晶并显著弱化凝胶；而使用偶氮苯修饰的PEG链（azobenzene-modified PEG chains）在热力学上促进穿线（threading），即使在极低CD化学计量比下也能形成稳定且长寿命的网络。

Conclusion: 该工作通过实验扰动与模拟相结合，澄清了构成此类材料的各组分的结构-功能关系，有助于指导增强型SRG水凝胶与弹性体的理性设计与开发。

Abstract: Biomedical devices, wearable interfaces, and soft robots require compliant soft materials with reliable mechanical performance, yet the molecular principles needed to design such materials are not always fully understood. Thus, there is an urgent need to understand how to design novel stretchable and soft hydrogels with enhanced toughness, i.e. resistance to breakage. Slide-ring gels (SRGs) are a class of unconventional soft materials formed by polyrotaxanes (PR) building blocks whereby sliding cyclodextrins (CD) replace traditional chemical and physical crosslinks and in turn enhance toughness and stretchability by several orders of magnitude. The rational design of SRGs re- mains challenging due to a lack of a mechanistic understanding of the role played by individual components. In this work, we implement simple synthetic perturbations that individually target polymer-CD and CD-CD interactions in unstoppered pseudopolyrotaxanes (pPR) solutions and measure the consequent changes in viscoelastic properties. Weakening the intermolecular interaction between CDs abolishes crystallisation and significantly weakens the gel. At the same time, thermodynamically favouring threading by using azobenzene-modified PEG chains favours the formation of stable and long-lived networks even at remarkably low CD stoichiometries. We rationalise these observations with coarse-grained Molecular Dynamics simulations which clarify the structure-function relationship of the components forming these materials. Our work contributes to guiding the rational design and development of enhanced SRG hydrogels and elastomers.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Entropy bounded compact stencil Runge-Kutta discontinuous Galerkin method for hyperbolic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30496)
*Deepak Kumar,Pratima Rai,Arvind Patel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保持最小熵原理（minimum entropy principle）的紧致模板龙格-库塔间断伽辽金方法（compact stencil Runge-Kutta discontinuous Galerkin, cRKDG），用于可压缩气体动力学欧拉方程（Euler equations）。方法借助cRKDG的保界框架（bound preserving framework）、熵界限制器（entropy bounded limiter）与经典正性保持限制器（positivity preserving limiter）实现该性质，并通过数值实验验证稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RKDG方法虽成熟，但模板较大；cRKDG具有更小模板尺寸与并行效率优势，然而其必须写成Butcher形式，且并非所有该形式的Runge-Kutta方法都是前向欧拉步的凸组合，故难以直接继承Tadmor针对欧拉方程熵解提出的最小熵原理。

Method: 将cRKDG方法表达为Butcher形式，并联合利用cRKDG格式的保界框架、熵界限制器以及经典正性保持限制器，以建立所需的凸组合性质，从而使格式保持最小熵原理。

Result: 摘要仅指出在最后一节给出数值实验以展示方法的稳健性（robustness），未报告具体数值指标或对比结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧致模板RKDG方法在保持最小熵原理的同时继承了cRKDG的小模板与并行效率优势，数值实验表明其具有稳健性。

Abstract: In this paper we introduce a compact stencil Runge-Kutta discontinuous Galerkin method for the Euler equations of compressible gas dynamics, which preserves a minimum entropy principle similar to that introduced by Tadmor for entropy solution of the Euler equations [4]. Compact stencil RKDG, introduced by Chen et al. in [15], is a variant of standard RKDG method which is known for its smaller stencil size and parallel efficiency. The main challenge is that the cRKDG method has to be expressed in Butcher form and not every Runge-Kutta method of this form is a convex combination of forward-Euler steps. Therefore, in order to establish this property we leverage the bound preserving framework of the cRKDG schemes [8], an entropy bounded limiter [6] and the classical positivity preserving limiter. In the last section numerical experiments are provided to demonstrate the robustness of the method.

</details>


### [146] [Robust topology optimization of isogeometric Kirchhoff--Love shells by the Speed Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30389)
*Philipp Eisenhardt,Fernando Hübner Scherer,Barbara Wohlmuth,Andrei Constantinescu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于等几何分析（isogeometric analysis）与非匹配网格（non-matching meshes）的 Kirchhoff-Love 多片壳体（multi-patch shells）鲁棒拓扑优化（robust topology optimization）框架，采用 NURBS 水平集方法（level set method）实现与 CAD 兼容的优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一种 CAD 兼容（CAD-compatible）的壳体拓扑优化方法，避免传统有限元（FEM）或细化等几何分析对局部加密、自适应积分与裁剪（trimming）的依赖，并处理载荷与材料属性不确定性下的鲁棒设计。

Method: 材料分布通过基于 NURBS 的水平集方法优化；形状导数（shape derivative）以速度法（speed method）计算，得到体积型（volume-based）公式，柔度与体积目标的下降方向由双调和方程（biharmonic equation）求得；并通过蒙特卡洛采样（Monte Carlo sampling）与高斯随机场（Gaussian Random Fields）扩展至不确定性下的鲁棒优化。

Result: 数值算例表明该方法在单片与多片壳体上均高效且鲁棒，以少于传统 FEM 或细化 IGA 方法的单元数再现文献结果；实现代码开源发布于 GitHub。

Conclusion: 所提方法与工具可直接扩展至基于应力（stress-based）的优化与风险规避指标（risk-averse metrics）问题。

Abstract: This paper presents a framework for robust topology optimization of Kirchhoff-Love (KL) multi-patch shells using isogeometric analysis and non-matching meshes and provides a CAD-compatible method. The material distribution is optimized via a NURBS-based level set method, enabling accurate boundary representation and seamless export to standard CAD/CAM workflows. The shape derivative is computed using the speed method, yielding a volume-based formulation that avoids boundary approximations and aligns with the isogeometric principle of coarse, high-order discretizations. This eliminates the need for local refinement, adaptive quadrature, or trimming as the descent direction for the compliance and volume objectives are obtained through a biharmonic equation. The framework is extended to robust optimization under uncertainty, accounting for stochastic loading and material properties via Monte Carlo sampling and Gaussian Random Fields. Numerical examples demonstrate the method's efficiency and robustness across single- and multi-patch shells, reproducing literature results with fewer elements than traditional FEM or refined IGA approaches. The developed methods and tools can be directly extended to problems involving stress-based optimization and risk-averse metrics. The numerical implementations are open-source and available on GitHub.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [General-purpose equations of state for hadron-quark matter: first application to accretion-induced phase transitions in neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30233)
*János Takátsy,Henrique Gieg,Tim Dietrich*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于新构建的含中微子吸收不透明度的强子—夸克物态方程，广义相对论辐射流体力学模拟显示，吸积诱导的一阶相变不会产生非束缚抛射物，因而难以成为可观测电磁暂现源的候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星（neutron star, NS）核心的强一阶相变（first-order phase transition）对天体物理观测量可能具有深远影响，但其是否存在仍是开放问题；多数中子星形成于双星系统，预期经历长期吸积并逐渐升高中心密度，从而可能触发相变并产生多信使信号。

Method: 构建一组新的强子—夸克物质物态方程（equations of state），涵盖不同强度的一阶相变，并纳入夸克相中的中微子吸收不透明度（neutrino absorption opacities）；随后对吸积诱导相变的中子星开展广义相对论辐射流体力学模拟（general-relativistic radiation hydrodynamics simulations）。

Result: 与先前研究结论相反，在所考察的模型中系统不产生非束缚抛射物（unbound ejecta），因此吸积诱导相变不太可能是可观测电磁暂现源（electromagnetic transients）的来源；但相变伴随强烈中微子发射，最强相变情形的峰值光度约达 10^52 erg/s。

Conclusion: 吸积诱导相变预计不产生电磁暂现信号，但可产生强中微子辐射；作者公开了所用物态方程族与中微子不透明度，以便后续系统研究相变强度所起作用。

Abstract: Strong first-order phase transitions in the cores of neutron stars (NSs) can have profound implications for astrophysical observables, however, their existence remains an open question. Since most NSs form in binary systems, they are expected to experience prolonged episodes of mass accretion that gradually increase their central density and can potentially trigger such a transition. This might, in turn, generate observable multi-messenger signals, as suggested by previous studies. Motivated by this prospect, we generate a new set of equations of state for hadron--quark matter with first-order phase transitions of various strengths, including neutrino absorption opacities in the quark phase as well. We then perform general-relativistic radiation hydrodynamics simulations of NSs undergoing accretion-induced phase transitions. In contrast to previous findings, our results indicate that these systems do not produce unbound ejecta for the models considered, making accretion-induced phase transitions unlikely sources of observable electromagnetic transients. However, we find that transitions are accompanied by intense neutrino emission, with peak luminosities reaching $\sim10^{52}~\mathrm{erg/s}$ for the strongest transition considered. To facilitate future numerical studies, we make the family of equations of state and neutrino opacities used in this work publicly available, enabling the systematic exploration of the role of the phase transition strength.

</details>


### [148] [Charged fluid tori with an approximate magnetic self-field around magnetized Schwarzschild black holes: A semi-analytic approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31577)
*Abylaikhan Tlemissov,Jiri Kovar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文研究浸没于极向磁场（poloidal magnetic field）中的Schwarzschild黑洞周围、由带电无自引力完美流体形成的环形结构（torus），在冷、静质量主导、均匀比荷与零电导率（纯对流电流）假设下构造解析与半解析解，并考察外场与近似自场的叠加对环形态与存在域的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多聚焦理想广义相对论磁流体力学（GRMHD）极限，而对纯对流电流（convective current）这一形式上的对立区域缺乏解析刻画；同时环形结构的磁自场（self-field）如何改变其形态与存在条件尚不清楚。

Method: 采用冷、静质量主导、均匀比荷、零电导率的完美流体模型；总磁场由规定的渐近均匀外场与环向电流的近似自场组成，后者以有效圆电流环场的正则化偶极近似（regularized dipole approximation）表示。忽略自场时用特征线法（method of characteristics）得到可积条件的隐式通解，并对径向依赖类积分Euler方程求焓势（enthalpy potential）。

Result: 径向依赖类给出赤道居中环的局域存在条件；分别考察无场极限、两种磁场分量及其叠加。外场在解析导出的参数界内支持闭合环，而孤立近似自场在数值探索域内未发现此类平衡。代表性组合场序列中，径向范围随相对自场强度非单调变化：弱自场时增大，自场与外场可比时减小。

Conclusion: 近似磁自场显著改变带电流体环的形态与存在域（domain of existence）；结果为非理想、纯对流电流 regime 下的解析建模提供了可用的解族与参数约束。

Abstract: We study toroidal structures formed by a charged, non-self-gravitating perfect fluid orbiting a Schwarzschild black hole immersed in a poloidal magnetic field. To obtain analytic and semi-analytic solutions, we assume a cold, rest-mass-dominated fluid with uniform specific charge and zero conductivity, so the current is purely convective. This regime is formally opposite to the ideal GRMHD limit. The total magnetic field comprises a prescribed asymptotically uniform external component and an approximate self-field associated with the azimuthal current in the torus. We represent the latter by a regularized dipole approximation to the field of an effective circular current loop. Neglecting the self-field, the method of characteristics yields a general implicit solution of the integrability condition for a function parametrizing the specific-angular-momentum profile. A separately obtained cylindrically dependent class recovers a solution from our earlier work. For the radially dependent class, we integrate the Euler equations, obtain the enthalpy potential, and derive local conditions for equatorially centered tori. We then examine the field-free limit, both magnetic components separately, and their superposition. The external field supports closed tori within analytically derived parameter bounds, whereas no such equilibrium is found for the isolated approximate self-field within the numerically explored domain. In a representative combined-field sequence, the radial extent varies nonmonotonically with relative self-field strength: it increases for weak self-fields and decreases as the self-field becomes comparable to the external field. The results show how an approximate magnetic self-field modifies the morphology and domain of existence of charged-fluid tori.

</details>


### [149] [The deepest X-ray view of a kiloparsec scale jet at z=6.1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30259)
*Fabio A. Meanti,Luca Ighina,Alberto Moretti,Kyra Bettwy,Alessandro Caccianiga,Thomas Connor,Fabrizio Tavecchio,Silvia Belladitta,Emmanuel K. Bempong-Manful,Cristiana Spingola*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用总曝光360 ks的钱德拉（Chandra）观测，将z=6.1类星体PSO J030947.49+271757.31的千秒差距尺度喷流延展X射线辐射解析为两个成分，并测得较陡的延展辐射光子指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千秒差距尺度喷流（kiloparsec-scale jets）的高能辐射机制在宇宙演化各时期仍不清楚；该z=6.1活动星系核（AGN）喷流是目前X射线波段可分辨的最高红移大尺度喷流，为研究此类结构提供独特样本。

Method: 结合新钱德拉观测与档案数据（总曝光360 ks），对延展X射线辐射进行空间分辨光谱分析，并以钱德拉图像为输入模拟类AXIS任务的探测能力。

Result: 延展辐射被解析为两个轻微可分辨成分，光子指数Gamma = 2.25 (+0.45, -0.44)，陡于核心的Gamma = 1.86 +/- 0.09；延展与核心光度比为18%；模拟显示类AXIS任务可将探测光子统计量提高约17倍。

Conclusion: 该源是当前已知最遥远且X射线最明亮的大尺度喷流之一；未来具备角秒级角分辨率的X射线任务有望更深入研究中心系统，并揭示钱德拉无法触及的更暗弱高红移喷流群体。

Abstract: The mechanism responsible for the high-energy emission observed in kiloparsec-scale jets across cosmic time remains poorly understood. The relativistic jet associated with the z=6.1 AGN PSO J030947.49+271757.31 is the highest-redshift large-scale jet currently resolved in X-rays, providing a unique opportunity to study these structures.
  We present new Chandra observations, combined with archival data, for a total exposure of 360 ks. The increased exposure allowed us to resolve the extended X-ray emission into two distinct, slightly resolved components and perform spatially resolved spectral analyses.
  The extended emission has a photon index of Gamma = 2.25 (+0.45, -0.44), steeper than that of the core (Gamma = 1.86 +/- 0.09), and an extended-to-core luminosity ratio of 18%. Simulations using the Chandra image as input show that an AXIS-like mission could increase the detected photon statistics by a factor of approximately 17.
  We resolve the kiloparsec-scale jet into two main components and constrain its spectral properties. This is the most distant and one of the most X-ray-luminous large-scale jets currently known. Future X-ray missions with arcsecond-scale angular resolution could enable more detailed studies of such systems and reveal a population of fainter high-redshift jets beyond Chandra's reach.

</details>

### [150] [Occupancy Driven Phase Transitions in Gapped Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31564)
*Ian Madden,Alex Kociubinski,Wouter Montfrooij*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出，具有基态与激发态之间能隙（energy gap）的系统中，热激发（thermal excitations）数目达到临界值时会使激发粒子数在热力学上失稳，引发两个状态之间的转变，其特征是比热（specific heat）发散，且不必然伴随序参量（order parameter）或能隙完全闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识是：能隙系统中温度升高会增多热激发，而激发间相互作用会像阻尼相关的谐振子那样改变能隙大小。作者希望说明激发数目本身即可驱动热力学不稳定性，并寻找无需序参量的相变实例。

Method: 以简单的二能级系统（two level system）和超流$^4$He为示例说明该热力学图像，并论证URu$_2$Si$_2$中的隐藏序转变（hidden order transition）属于此类由占据数驱动、无序参量的转变。

Result: 当激发数目达到临界值时，激发粒子数变为热力学不稳定，系统在两态间转变；该转变表现为比热发散，但不必伴随序参量出现，也不要求能隙完全闭合。

Conclusion: 存在一类由激发占据数驱动的转变，可由比热发散识别，且无需序参量或能隙完全闭合；URu$_2$Si$_2$的隐藏序转变被视为该类转变的候选实例。

Abstract: Systems characterized by an energy gap between the ground state and excited states support a growing number of thermal excitations upon raising the temperature. Provided these excitations interact with each other, their number affects the size of the energy gap akin to a harmonic oscillator where the oscillation frequency is damping dependent. Here we show that once a critical number of excitations is reached, the excitation population becomes thermodynamically unstable and the system transitions between two states. This transition is marked by a divergence in the specific heat, which does not have to be accompanied by an order parameter, nor by the full closure of the gap. We illustrate the thermodynamics using a simple two level system and superfluid $^4$He as examples, and we argue that the hidden order transition in URu$_2$Si$_2$ is an example of such an occupancy driven transition in the absence of an order parameter.

</details>

### [151] [Coulomb-Driven Electronic Textures in Lightly Hole-Doped Cuprates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30766)
*Wei-Wei Yang,Yang Shen,Yuxin Wang,Pengfei Li,Mingpu Qin,Kun Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文基于对 Ca$_2$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$ 的实验，研究轻空穴掺杂铜氧化物中掺杂剂与局域 Zhang-Rice 单态（Zhang-Rice singlet）的相互作用，提出库仑相互作用与隧穿矩阵元效应共同构成 $4a_0$ 电子结构的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 Ca$_2$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$ 实验观测到的 $4a_0$ 电子结构缺乏微观解释，本文旨在阐明轻掺杂铜氧化物中掺杂剂与局域 Zhang-Rice 单态之间的相互作用如何产生该现象。

Method: 利用屏蔽较弱的库仑势（Coulomb potential）分析掺杂剂与局域 Zhang-Rice 单态的耦合，指出其倾向于形成等效电"偶极"（dipole）的非对称电子构型；并采用基于矩阵乘积态（MPS）的 Lanczos 方法进行计算，同时计入隧穿矩阵元（tunneling matrix-element）效应。

Result: 局域空穴在电荷转移隙（charge-transfer gap）内形成隙内束缚态（in-gap bound states）；计入隧穿矩阵元效应后，束缚态呈现特征的 $4a_0 \times 2a_0$ 内部结构，与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 掺杂剂与局域 Zhang-Rice 单态间的库仑相互作用结合隧穿矩阵元效应，为所观测的 $4a_0$ 电子结构提供了微观机制。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experiments on Ca$_2$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$, we present a comprehensive study of the interplay between dopants and localized Zhang-Rice singlets in lightly hole-doped cuprates. Owing to the poorly screened Coulomb potential, the interactions between dopants and localized Zhang-Rice singlets favor asymmetric electronic configurations characterized by effective electric "dipoles". Using the MPS-based Lanczos method, we show that these localized holes give rise to in-gap bound states within the charge-transfer gap. When tunneling matrix-element effects are included, the bound states exhibit a characteristic $4a_0 \times 2a_0$ internal structure, consistent with experimental observations. Our results suggest that Coulomb interactions between dopants and localized Zhang-Rice singlets, together with tunneling matrix-element effects, provide a microscopic mechanism for the observed $4a_0$ electronic structures.

</details>


### [152] [Contact Renormalization of One-Mode Electron Correlation: Validation in Four- and Ten-Electron Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31031)
*Itai Panas*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一种在正则化Hartree–Fock框架下，用“源–返回（source–return）”的单一有效机制从稳态信息载体确定标量关联能的无参数构造，并在Be-like、十电子分子与Ne-like离子上验证其定量重现关联趋势的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态多电子本征态虽只有一个动力学相位，其内部响应却可能是强多模的；作者据此追问：稳态Hartree–Fock信息载体是否包含足够信息，通过一次有效返回即可确定标量关联能（correlation energy）。

Method: 在正则化Hartree–Fock（regularized Hartree–Fock）中，先用高斯原函数乘积（Gaussian primitive products）固定最小信息的相对坐标模，再进行单模约化；以维里完备化（virial completion）定义关联源，以精确接触（exact contact）度量相邻占据对带来的额外返回限制。

Result: Be-like $1s^22s^2$ 序列给出单价对边界：无相邻占据价对时，源通过普适下限 $D_0=\sqrt{1+\delta^2}$ 返回；十电子分子与Ne-like离子进一步检验有限对接触对该返回的修饰。同一无参数构造定量重现了既有分子与离子关联趋势。

Conclusion: 数值一致性被视为“源强度与返回限制相物理分离”这一图景的验证，而非研究目标本身。

Abstract: A stationary many-electron eigenstate has one dynamical phase although its internal response can be strongly multimode. We ask whether the stationary Hartree--Fock information carrier contains enough information to determine the scalar correlation energy through one effective return. In regularized Hartree--Fock, Gaussian primitive products fix a minimum-information relative-coordinate mode before any one-mode reduction; virial completion defines the correlation source, while exact contact measures the additional return restriction created by neighboring occupied pairs. The Be-like $1s^22s^2$ sequence establishes the one-valence-pair boundary: with no neighboring occupied valence pair, the source returns through the universal floor $D_0=\sqrt{1+δ^2}$. Ten-electron molecules and Ne-like ions then test how finite-pair contact dresses that return. The same parameter-free source--return construction reproduces the established molecular and ionic correlation trends with quantitative accuracy. The numerical agreement therefore serves as validation of a physical separation between source strength and return restriction, rather than as the objective by itself.

</details>

### [153] [Dynamics of random recurrent neural networks: colored noise and sublinear activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31575)
*German Mato,Facundo Rigatuso,Gonzalo Torroba*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究分析具有随机耦合、次线性奇激活函数（sublinear odd activation functions）与时间相关噪声（temporally correlated noise）的循环神经网络（recurrent neural networks）动力学，区分局部线性稳定性丧失与混沌（chaos）起始这两个阈值，并刻画二者之间的中间区域及噪声对相关时间的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究常将局部线性稳定性丧失与混沌起始混为一谈，而随机耦合、次线性激活与时间相关噪声共同作用下二者的关系尚不清晰；噪声相关时间（noise correlation time）与激活函数渐近增长如何影响这些阈值及内在相关时间的发散，需要系统刻画。

Method: 采用大N方法（large N methods）确定稳态相关（stationary correlations），以正的最大李雅普诺夫指数（largest Lyapunov exponent）判据识别混沌；并由四点相关函数（four-point correlation function）计算频率依赖的参与维度（participation dimension）。

Result: 局部不稳定与共同噪声历史下的渐近稳定可共存，形成中间区域，其两个阈值的分离取决于噪声相关时间与激活函数渐近增长。任意有限相关时间的内部噪声会截断混沌边缘处网络内在相关时间（intrinsic correlation time）的散度，给出临界标度 tau_{corr,max} ∝ 1/sqrt{D_0}；而静态随机输入下两阈值重合且相关时间发散。参与维度的零频值与网络相关时间四次方成反比，预示弱噪声混沌阈值处的平滑极小值；数值模拟支持大网络预测并显示明显的有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects）。

Conclusion: 噪声的时间相关性是分离局部稳定性阈值与混沌阈值的关键因素，并决定了相关时间在混沌边缘的临界行为；静态输入与动态噪声导致定性不同的阈值结构。有限尺寸效应提示大N预测在有限网络中需谨慎外推。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of recurrent neural networks with random couplings, sublinear odd activation functions, and temporally correlated noise. Using large N methods, we determine stationary correlations and distinguish the loss of local linear stability from the onset of chaos, characterized by a positive largest Lyapunov exponent. These thresholds delimit an intermediate regime in which local instability coexists with asymptotic stability under a common noise history. Their separation depends on both the noise correlation time and the asymptotic growth of the activation function. We show that internal noise with any finite correlation time cuts off the divergence of the network's intrinsic correlation time tau_corr at the edge of chaos; it exhibits a critical scaling tau_{corr,max} propto 1/sqrt{D_0}, where D_0 is the integrated noise strength. Static random input instead produces coincident thresholds and a divergent correlation time. We also calculate the frequency-dependent participation dimension from a four-point correlation function. Its zero-frequency value is inversely proportional to the fourth power of the network correlation time, predicting a smooth minimum at the weak-noise chaos threshold. Numerical simulations support the large-network predictions and reveal appreciable finite-size effects.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Supercoiling Drives DNA-Protein Co-Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30588)
*Sihan Liu,Hongbo Zhao,Andrej Košmrlj*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 通过将DNA弹性与蛋白浓度场耦合，该研究提出超螺旋（supercoiling）作为共凝聚（co-condensation）的拓扑催化剂：蛋白润湿促进扭转-缠绕转换，产生高缠绕DNA构型并诱发毛细塌缩，从而在无需超螺旋时被动力学困住的体系中实现自发共凝聚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA与蛋白共凝聚在异染色质形成与染色体分离等基因组压缩过程中至关重要，而转录与复制持续产生DNA超螺旋，但扭转应力（torsional stress）如何影响共凝聚尚不清楚。

Method: 建立将DNA弹性（DNA elasticity）与蛋白浓度场（protein concentration field）耦合的理论模型，考察超螺旋与蛋白润湿（protein wetting）之间的相互增强作用。

Result: 蛋白润湿促进扭转-缠绕转换（twist-to-writhe conversion），生成高度缠绕的DNA构型，进而促进毛细塌缩（capillary collapse）形成富蛋白液滴；无超螺旋时DNA在模型中动力学困于亚稳态伸展构型，足够强的超螺旋则消除成核势垒（nucleation barrier），实现自发共凝聚。

Conclusion: 拓扑（topology）被确立为生物分子凝聚（biomolecular condensation）的一个独立调控参数，并为活跃基因组过程如何动态调控大规模基因组组织提供了力学框架。

Abstract: The co-condensation of DNA and proteins plays an important role in genome compaction, including heterochromatin formation and chromosome segregation. Meanwhile, transcription and replication continuously generate DNA supercoiling, yet how the resulting torsional stress affects co-condensation remains poorly understood. Here, by coupling DNA elasticity to a protein concentration field, we show that supercoiling acts as a topological catalyst for co-condensation through a mutually reinforcing interplay with protein wetting. Specifically, protein wetting facilitates twist-to-writhe conversion, generating highly writhed DNA conformations that in turn promote capillary collapse into a protein-rich droplet. In the absence of supercoiling, DNA remains kinetically trapped in metastable extended states within our model, whereas sufficiently strong supercoiling removes the nucleation barrier and enables spontaneous co-condensation. These results establish topology as a distinct control parameter for biomolecular condensation and provide a mechanical framework for how active genomic processes can dynamically regulate large-scale genome organization.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Nature of the bosonic edge phase in a double quantum spin Hall insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30251)
*Andreas Feuerpfeil,Ronny Thomale,Trithep Devakul,Nicolas Regnault,Julian May-Mann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过数值模拟验证了双量子自旋霍尔绝缘体（double quantum spin Hall insulator, DQSHI）边缘在相互作用下可由费米子边缘模式转变为纯玻色子边缘模式，并确认了单费米子能隙与配对无能隙的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场论分析预言（field-theoretic analysis）DQSHI边缘可发生相互作用驱动的费米子—玻色子边缘相变，但缺乏数值验证，且该边缘存在电荷与S^z守恒间的混合反常（mixed anomaly），难以用常规开放边界方法直接嵌入体态。

Method: 采用无限二维圆柱上的分段密度矩阵重整化群（segment DMRG），将DQSHI体态建模为两份自旋向上最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level）及其时间反演对应物，从而显式嵌入边缘。

Result: 数值结果确认存在玻色子边缘相：相互作用打开单费米子能隙而费米子配对保持无能隙；单费米子能隙随相互作用强度的标度行为（scaling）与场论计算一致。

Conclusion: 该结果支持玻色子边缘相的存在，并讨论其与WSe$_2$、MoTe$_2$扭转同质双层（twisted homobilayers）的关联，以及分段几何方法向其他带边缘反常的拓扑相的推广。

Abstract: Field-theoretic analysis has predicted that the edge of a double quantum spin Hall insulator (DQSHI) can undergo an interaction-driven transition from a phase with fermionic edge modes into one with only bosonic modes. All single-fermion edge excitations are gapped in this bosonic edge phase, but fermion pairs remain gapless, leading to distinct experimental signatures. We numerically simulate the interacting DQSHI edge and verify the existence of the bosonic edge phase. This calculation requires an explicit bulk embedding of the DQSHI edge, due to its mixed anomaly between charge and $S^z$ conservation. We hence employ segment DMRG on an infinite two-dimensional cylinder, where the DQSHI bulk is modeled in terms of two copies of the spin-up lowest Landau level, combined with their time-reversed counterparts. We find that interactions can open a single-fermion gap at the DQSHI edge while fermion pairs remain gapless, matching the predictions for the bosonic edge phase. We find the scaling of the single-fermion gap with interaction strength to be consistent with field-theoretic calculations, and discuss the relevance of the bosonic edge phase to twisted homobilayers of WSe$_2$ and MoTe$_2$. We elaborate on the methodological extension of the segment geometry used here to other topological phases with edge anomalies.

</details>


### [156] [Kinetic-inductance Parametric Amplification in Superconducting Hybrid Magnonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30322)
*Pratap Kumar Pal,Moojune Song,Jinho Lim,Juliang Li,Serhii Kruhlov,Ralu Divan,Ulrich Welp,Clarence Chang,Kab-Jin Kim,Valentine Novosad,Axel Hoffmann,Yi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用非线性超导纳米线谐振器与钇铁石榴石球强耦合的杂化磁子体系，实现动力学电感介导的参量放大，并证明磁子—光子耦合可调谐放大谱位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参量放大（parametric amplification）能以极低附加噪声放大微弱信号，是灵敏微波探测与信号处理的关键；研究旨在探索杂化磁子体系（hybrid magnonic system）中可磁调谐的非线性微波放大与动力学行为，补充现有放大机制的可调性不足。

Method: 构建由非线性氮化铌（NbN）纳米线谐振器与钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）球强耦合组成的杂化体系，利用谐振器的四波混频（four wave mixing）与非线性频率牵引（nonlinear frequency pulling）实现参量放大，并在磁子—光子强耦合区内外分别测量放大响应及泵浦功率更高时的自振荡行为。

Result: 在磁子失谐条件下获得 7 dB 增益；与 YIG 磁子耦合使参量放大响应移动约 20 MHz，并在杂化模式中得到 3.4 dB 增益；高泵浦功率下系统转入具有梳状边带（comb-like sidebands）和探测激活非线性模式混合的自振荡区。

Conclusion: 磁子—光子耦合为磁调控动力学电感参量放大的谱位置提供了途径，杂化磁子学可作为可调谐非线性微波放大与非线性动力学的平台。

Abstract: Parametric amplification enables sensitive microwave detection and signal processing by amplifying weak signals with minimal added noise. Here, we demonstrate kinetic inductance mediated parametric amplification in a hybrid magnonic system comprising a nonlinear NbN nanowire resonator strongly coupled to a yttrium iron garnet sphere. The resonator exhibits four wave mixing and nonlinear frequency pulling, enabling parametric amplification both away from and within the magnon-photon strong coupling regime. In the magnon detuned regime, we observe 7 dB gain, while coupling to the YIG magnon shifts the parametric amplification response by ~20 MHz and yields 3.4 dB gain in the hybrid mode. Thus, magnon photon coupling provides a route to magnetically tune the spectral position of kinetic inductance parametric amplification. At higher pump powers, the system transitions to a self oscillatory regime featuring comb-like sidebands and probe activated nonlinear mode mixing. These results establish hybrid magnonics as a platform for tunable nonlinear microwave amplification and dynamics.

</details>


### [157] [Nonlinear and Chaotic Dynamics of Two Dipolar-Coupled Anisotropic Spins under Periodic Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30414)
*Manish Anand*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值与解析方法刻画两个相同各向异性宏观自旋在偶极耦合与周期横向驱动下的非线性磁化动力学，揭示偶极耦合、驱动幅度与单轴各向异性对长期动力学区域的非单调重组规律，并识别出超混沌（hyperchaotic）区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于建立一个最小相互作用自旋体系，用以理解偶极耦合（dipolar coupling）、各向异性（anisotropy）与周期激发（periodic excitation）在非线性磁化动力学中的相互作用机制。

Method: 对耦合的 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程（LLG equations）采用四阶 Runge-Kutta 方法数值求解，并结合分岔图（bifurcation diagrams）、局部极大值分析、Lyapunov 指数、二维稳定性图、对称与反对称坐标分析、傅里叶谱与三维轨迹进行刻画；同时辅以解析线性化与铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance）计算。

Result: 结果显示动力学区域呈非单调重组，不稳定性呈现增强与抑制交替的区间；两个最大 Lyapunov 指数同时为正的参数区域与超混沌（hyperchaotic）动力学一致。对称与反对称坐标分析区分了集体与相对自旋响应，解析计算则揭示集体模频率对耦合具有独特依赖，并说明线性化描述在共线参考态稳定性阈值之外存在局限。

Conclusion: 研究为理解最小相互作用自旋体系中偶极耦合、各向异性与周期激发的非线性动力学相互作用建立了统一框架。

Abstract: We investigate the non-linear magnetization dynamics of two identical anisotropic macrospins coupled by dipolar interaction and subjected to periodic transverse excitation. The coupled Landau- Lifshitz-Gilbert equations are solved numerically using a fourth-order Runge-Kutta method. By combining bifurcation diagrams, local-maxima analysis, Lyapunov exponents, and two-dimensional stability maps, we characterize how dipolar coupling, transverse driving amplitudes, and uniaxial anisotropy govern the long-time dynamics. The results reveal a non-monotonic reorganization of dynamical regimes, with alternating intervals of enhanced and suppressed instability. Analysis of symmetric and antisymmetric coordinates distinguishes the collective and relative spin responses, while Fourier spectra and three-dimensional trajectories provide complementary frequency-domain and geometrical characterizations. Simultaneous positivity of the two largest Lyapunov exponents identifies parameter regions consistent with hyperchaotic dynamics. Finally, analytical linearization and ferromagnetic-resonance calculations reveal the distinct coupling dependence of the collectivemode frequencies and clarify the limitations of the linearized description beyond the stability thresholds of the collinear reference state. These findings establish a unified framework for understanding the interplay of dipolar coupling, anisotropy, and periodic excitation in the non-linear dynamics of a minimal interacting-spin system.

</details>


### [158] [Spin polarization in a chiral ladder: elastic and inelastic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30419)
*D. T. Verrilli H.,Jorge Cardenas-Gamboa,Mayra Peralta,Claudia Felser,José H. Garcia,Solmar Varela,Nelson Bolívar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究传输窗口与环境再注入（reinjection）如何共同决定最小双链手性梯模型中的自旋极化，指出子晶格对称性使相干自旋透射核在能量上严格为奇函数；窗口居中时积分自旋流为零，窗口偏移则出现未被抵消的谱区间，且极化在两种对映体间反号，在解耦链、零自旋轨道耦合与等价支极限下消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清在解释手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity）相关最小模型中的退相干趋势时，必须控制哪些能量区间与储池（reservoir）设定，特别是传输窗口位置与环境再注入对净自旋信号的影响。

Method: 构建含时间反演不变螺旋自旋轨道耦合（helical spin–orbit coupling）、自旋无关链间跃迁及非磁接触（每电极两个轨道模式）的双链手性梯模型，计算相干自旋透射核；在同一有限偏压下比较均匀吸收、能量分辨弹性探针（elastic probes）与热化电压探针（thermalized voltage probes），并在存在探针时纳入再注入；另考察静态在位无序（onsite disorder）的系综效应。

Result: 居中窗口的积分自旋流为零，偏移窗口则给出未抵消贡献；极化在对映体间反号。电荷守恒本身并不禁止积分自旋信号增强：弹性探针响应可在有限偏压下增大，即使其线性响应（linear-response）对应量减小；热化探针还能解除弹性探针所保持的居中窗口零点。吸收与再注入同时改变透射电荷，故自旋流增强与归一化极化增强需区分。无序导致样品相关涨落，其带符号系综均值以不确定度报告，在偏移窗口下不被强制为零。

Conclusion: 结果明确了在解读最小模型中的退相干趋势时须控制的能量区间与储池设定，为手性诱导自旋选择性研究提供了判据性框架。

Abstract: We study how the transport window and environmental reinjection determine the spin polarization transmitted through a minimal two-leg chiral ladder. The model contains time-reversal-invariant helical spin--orbit coupling, spin-independent interchain hopping, and nonmagnetic contacts with two orbital modes per lead. Sublattice symmetry makes the coherent spin-transmission kernel exactly odd in energy. A window centered on the symmetry point therefore gives zero integrated spin current, whereas a displaced window selects an uncanceled spectral interval. The resulting polarization reverses between enantiomers and vanishes in the decoupled-chain, zero-spin--orbit, and equivalent-strand limits of this model. We compare uniform absorption, energy-resolved elastic probes, and thermalized voltage probes at the same finite bias, including reinjection whenever probes are present. Charge conservation alone does not prohibit enhancement of the integrated spin signal: the elastic-probe response can increase at finite bias even where its linear-response counterpart decreases. Thermalized probes can also lift the centered-window zero that elastic probes preserve. Absorption and reinjection also change the transmitted charge, so enhancement of spin current and enhancement of normalized polarization must be distinguished. Static onsite disorder produces sample-dependent fluctuations; its signed ensemble mean is reported with uncertainty and is not forced to vanish in a displaced window. These results identify the energy interval and reservoir prescription that must be controlled when interpreting dephasing trends in minimal models relevant to chirality-induced spin selectivity.

</details>


### [159] [Orientation-selective microwave heating of laser-graphitized diamond at 2.45 GHz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30453)
*Massimiliano Zamengo,Meguya Ryu,Haoran Mu,Hsin-Hui Huang,Nguyen Hoai An Le,Tomas Katkus,Darius Gailevičius,Junko Morikawa,Saulius Juodkazis*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 飞秒激光在N掺杂HPHT金刚石内部写入埋藏石墨相（graphitic, sp2）结构，可使原本几乎不吸收2.45 GHz场的晶体产生强微波吸收，且吸收可由结构相对腔电场的取向进行开关调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始金刚石（pristine diamond）与2.45 GHz微波场耦合极弱，缺乏可调的微波吸收机制；研究旨在通过内部微结构写入赋予透明宿主可控的微波吸收能力。

Method: 在4×4×2 mm³、N掺杂（100 ppm）HPHT金刚石中用飞秒激光写入亚波长石墨轨迹构成的同心光栅（concentric grating），并在不同取向下施加阶梯式微波功率；以亚波长轨迹的去极化因子（depolarisation factors）L 归并为屏蔽参数 S = Lσ/(ωε0εh) 进行建模。

Result: 光栅沿电场方向（E）、深度1.8 mm时，80 W下呈平滑阶梯升温至约500C，90 W时突跃至约900C并伴随表面粗糙化与台阶状氧化；将样品旋转使光栅直径沿E、或使用未改性晶体（任意取向），均无高于约40C本底（floor）的信号。

Conclusion: 去极化因子归并的屏蔽参数模型可将单元长度、直径与写入参数（writing regime）转化为透明宿主中微波吸收的设计变量，实现取向可切换的强微波吸收与加热。

Abstract: Pristine diamond couples negligibly to a 2.45 GHz field. We show that femtosecond-laser inscription of a buried graphitic (sp2) architecture makes a 4 x 4 x 2 mm^3 N-doped (100 ppm) high-pressure high- temperature (HPHT) crystal absorb strongly, and that the absorption is switched by the orientation of the inscribed structure relative to the cavity electric field. With the 1.8 mm depth of a concentric grating along E, stepped power gave a smooth staircase to an indicated ~500C at 80 W followed, at 90 W, by an abrupt excursion to an indicated ~900C with surface roughening and terraced oxidation. Rotating the same sample so that the grating diameter lay along E, and an unmodified crystal in either orientation, gave no signal above the ~40C floor. We account for this by the depolarisation factors L of the sub-wavelength tracks, collapsed into one screening parameter S = Lσ/(ωε0εh), here, σ conductivity, ω - cyclic frequency, εh,0 - permittivity of host and vacuum, respectively. The model turns element length, diameter and writing regime into design variables for microwave absorption in a transparent host.

</details>


### [160] [Chiral Cavity Vacua as Local Generators and Probes of Non-Abelian Kitaev Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30502)
*Yu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文展示回旋腔真空如何在 Kitaev 蜂窝模型中生成、探测并空间编程非阿贝尔拓扑，给出局域与体拓扑诱导的不同判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是建立腔真空与量子自旋液体拓扑之间的可控联系，厘清腔诱导拓扑在局域模式与扩展模式下的成立条件。

Method: 采用规范一致的虚光子消除（virtual-photon elimination），导出螺旋性奇（helicity-odd）相互作用 i[P_x,P_y] 与同阶偶修饰；借助精确 Majorana 解与实空间计算分析质量项与边缘态。

Result: 该对易子在主导键极化下恰为 Kitaev 三自旋质量；归一化模式给出热力学约束——单个扩展模式的质量随样品面积消失，局域模式写出有限拓扑岛，有限模式密度才产生体质量。圆二色性（circular dichroism）精确测量质量生成元，⟨i[P_x,P_y]⟩=−√3p²⟨O_3⟩；实空间出现非阿贝尔岛与含两支共传播 Majorana 模的螺旋性壁。

Conclusion: 均匀螺旋性反转为 Dirac 质量淬火（n_ex∝τ_Q^{-1}），移动壁则使能隙闭合局域化；平衡真空核含和频而非共振分母，由此确立局域与体腔诱导拓扑的独立判据。

Abstract: We show how a gyrotropic cavity vacuum generates, probes, and spatially programs non-Abelian topology in the Kitaev honeycomb model. Gauge-consistent virtual-photon elimination produces a universal helicity-odd interaction $\mathrm{i}[P_x,P_y]$ alongside same-order even dressing. For the leading bond polarization, this commutator is exactly the Kitaev three-spin mass. Mode normalization imposes a thermodynamic constraint: one extended normalized mode gives a local mass that vanishes with sample area, whereas a localized mode writes a finite topological island and a finite mode density enables a bulk mass. The exact Majorana solution yields helicity-reversed Chern gaps and edge propagation, while integrated circular dichroism exactly measures the mass generator, $\langle\mathrm{i}[P_x,P_y]\rangle=-\sqrt3p^2\langle O_3\rangle$. Real-space calculations reveal a non-Abelian island and a helicity wall with two co-propagating Majorana modes. Uniform helicity reversal is a Dirac-mass quench with $n_{\rm ex}\proptoτ_Q^{-1}$, whereas moving a wall localizes the gap closing. The equilibrium vacuum kernel contains sum-frequency rather than resonant denominators. These results establish distinct criteria for local and bulk cavity-induced topology.

</details>


### [161] [Finite-width altermagnetic quantum rings: angular-channel hybridization and Aharonov--Bohm physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30536)
*Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作以带二次 \(d\)-波交替磁性（altermagnetism）项的有限宽度二维量子环为对象，结合 Tan–Inkson 限制、垂直磁场与可独立调节的 AB 通量，解析并数值刻画了角动量通道混合、费米边重构与持续电流行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清有限宽度量子环中交替磁性耦合与几何限制、磁场、AB 通量共同作用下的能谱与磁响应，并区分体态量子振荡与有限环壳层效应。

Method: 采用含二次 \(d\)-波交替磁性项的圆对称模型，交替磁性项混合 \(m\) 与 \(m\pm2\) 通道但保持宇称；进行 \(N_e=1400\) 与 \(1800\) 两套独立收敛计算，并以解析常规参考分离径向子带逐出（dHvA 型）与壳层振荡；少电子热响应由固定粒子数的正则配分函数精确求得。

Result: 圆对称使晶体取向在谱上无关，两自旋块严格等谱（isospectral）；低填充下同宇称反交叉（anticrossing）被分辨，高占据环在费米能级附近出现多通道混合。计算确立了有限磁场窗口内重构的费米边、磁响应及全 AB 周期的持续电流。

Conclusion: 圆对称模型支持平衡电荷电流，但不支持持续自旋电流；研究表明需以已验证的磁场扫描为前提解释交替磁性信号，固定粒子数正则处理可避免固定平均粒子数近似的有限体系误差。

Abstract: We study a finite-width two-dimensional quantum ring with a quadratic $d$-wave altermagnetic term, Tan--Inkson confinement, a perpendicular magnetic field, and independently tunable Aharonov--Bohm (AB) flux. The altermagnetic interaction mixes $m$ and $m\pm2$ angular channels while preserving their parity. Circular symmetry renders the crystal orientation spectrally irrelevant and makes the two spin blocks exactly isospectral. The resulting same-parity anticrossings are resolved at low filling, and multichannel mixing is found near the Fermi level of highly occupied rings. Independent converged calculations for $N_e=1400$ and $1800$ establish the reconstructed Fermi edge and magnetic response in a finite field window and the persistent current over a full AB flux period. The analytic conventional reference separates radial-subband depopulation (dHvA-type cycles) from finer finite-ring shell oscillations; we do not assign a global Fourier spectrum to unverified altermagnetic field scans. The few-electron thermal response is obtained from an exact fixed-particle-number canonical partition function, avoiding finite-system errors of a fixed-mean-number approximation. The circular model supports an equilibrium charge current but no persistent spin current.

</details>


### [162] [Ferroelastic Reversal Magnon Thermal Hall Effect in a Fully Compensated Ferrimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30733)
*Yingxi Bai,Zihan Chen,Chengwang Niu,Ming Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出以铁弹翻转（ferroelastic switching）作为调控完全补偿亚铁磁体中磁子横向输运的可逆手段，两个正交变体的磁子热霍尔（magnon thermal Hall）响应符号相反，中间交替磁相中响应消失，并以单层V2SSe为候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁子（magnons）可承载自旋与热输运，但在零净磁化系统中，其热霍尔响应的可逆、非易失调控仍是基本难题。

Method: 构建最小海森堡-贾洛申斯基-莫里亚（Heisenberg-Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya）模型，分析铁弹翻转对晶格各向异性、手性分辨磁子能带与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）分布的重构；并结合第一性原理计算筛选材料。

Result: 由[C2||C4z]联系的两个正交完全补偿亚铁磁变体呈现符号相反的磁子热霍尔响应，中间交替磁（altermagnetic）相中响应消失；第一性原理计算识别单层V2SSe为实现该调控的候选材料，并伴随磁子-电子能带双重重构、各向异性轨道霍尔（orbital Hall）响应与磁光谱指纹。

Conclusion: 铁弹性被确认为零净磁化系统中实现非易失磁子调控与多通道状态识别的有效途径。

Abstract: Magnons offer a charge-neutral channel for spin and heat transport, yet reversible and nonvolatile control of their thermal Hall response poses a fundamental challenge in systems with zero net magnetization. Here, we establish ferroelastic switching as an effective route to manipulate magnonic transverse responses in fully compensated ferrimagnets. A minimal Heisenberg-Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya model reveals that ferroelastic switching can re-orientate the lattice anisotropy, which reconstructs the chirality-resolved magnon bands and Berry curvature distribution. Consequently, the two orthogonal fully compensated ferrimagnetic variants related by [C2||C4z] exhibit magnon thermal Hall responses with opposite signs, whereas the response vanishes in the intermediate altermagnetic phase. First-principles calculations further identify monolayer V2SSe as a promising material candidate for realizing the proposed ferroelastic control of transverse magnon transport. Remarkably, ferroelastic switching drives a dual magnonic-electronic band reconstruction, enabling nonvolatile reversal of the magnon thermal Hall effect while producing anisotropic orbital Hall responses and distinct magneto-optical spectra as complementary transport and optical fingerprints. Our findings establish ferroelasticity as an effective route to nonvolatile magnon control and multichannel state identification in zero net magnetization systems.

</details>


### [163] [Anyon Chern bands of opposite sign and reversed drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30743)
*Zhenyu Xiao,Minxuan Wang,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究从微观波函数出发，为分数量子霍尔态中的 Laughlin 准空穴（Laughlin quasihole）定义了由多体贝里曲率（many-body Berry curvature）决定的导心坐标对易子，并证明周期性调制可使其能带获得相互反号的陈数（Chern number），从而产生逆 $\mathbf{E}\times\mathbf{B}$ 漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态的基本带电激发是任意子（anyons），在缺乏连续平移对称性时它们会获得色散与更丰富的动力学。论文旨在刻画这种任意子能带自身的拓扑性质，并以二维材料中分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators）常用的周期调制磁场为模型。

Method: 在填充因子 $1/3$ 下，利用微观波函数推导 Laughlin 准空穴导心坐标的对易子，其中出现支配其波包运动的多体贝里曲率。随后采用调制强度的微扰论分析能带分裂，并用大规模蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）计算加以验证。

Result: 每单胞两个磁通量子的调制将准空穴能带分裂为陈数为 $6$ 与 $-3$ 的子带；只要弱调制打开任意子能带间的能隙，这种反号现象即具有普适性。在 $C=-3$ 的能带中，尽管磁场处处同号，准空穴仍逆着 $\mathbf{E}\times\mathbf{B}$ 方向漂移。微扰论解释了上述结果，并被大规模蒙特卡洛计算证实。

Conclusion: 任意子能带具有自身的拓扑结构，该拓扑控制任意子的运动方式，并可能塑造巡游任意子（itinerant anyons）的相图。这为在周期调制或晶格环境中研究任意子拓扑动力学提供了微观基础。

Abstract: The elementary charged excitations of fractional quantum Hall states are anyons. Without continuous translation symmetry, they acquire a dispersion and richer dynamics. We study the Laughlin quasihole at filling $1/3$ in a periodically modulated magnetic field, a common model of fractional Chern insulators in two-dimensional materials. From the microscopic wave functions, we derive the commutator of its guiding-center coordinates, set by a many-body Berry curvature that governs its wave packets. A modulation with two flux quanta per unit cell splits the quasihole band into bands with Chern numbers $6$ and $-3$; such opposite signs are generic as long as a weak modulation opens a gap between the anyon bands. In the $C=-3$ band, the quasihole drifts against $\mathbf{E}\times\mathbf{B}$, although the magnetic field has the same sign everywhere. Perturbation theory in the modulation explains these results, and large-scale Monte Carlo calculations confirm them. Anyon bands thus acquire their own topology, which controls anyon motion and may shape the phases of itinerant anyons.

</details>


### [164] [Broadband emitter localization and prefabrication two-photon interference from InAs/InP quantum dots monolithically grown on an InGaAsP/InP distributed Bragg reflector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30905)
*Paweł Mrowiński,Jan Śmigiel,Mikołaj Badura,Beata Ściana,Wojciech Kijaszek,Emilia Zięba-Ostój,Paweł Holewa,Marcin Syperek*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作展示了一种单片（monolithic）电信C波段单光子平台，基于低密度InAs/InP量子点（quantum dots, QDs）生长于InGaAsP/InP分布布拉格反射镜（distributed Bragg reflector, DBR）上，实现宽带增强的光提取与高相干单光子发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强光提取（light extraction）对宽场光致发光成像（wide-field photoluminescence imaging）与发射源预筛选至关重要，而无需依赖腔增强（Purcell enhancement）即可获得高相干单光子源，是构建确定性制备高质量纳米光子器件的前提。

Method: 采用低密度InAs/InP量子点生长在InGaAsP/InP DBR上形成平面结构，通过仿真评估光提取效率；在脉冲准共振激发（pulsed quasi-resonant excitation）下，以原始峰面积积分（raw peak-area integration）验证单光子纯度，并进行双光子干涉（two-photon interference）测量。

Result: 在40 nm光谱带内仿真向上光提取效率超过10%；提升的信噪比使量子点发射源达到约3 nm的统计质心定位精度（statistical centroid-localization precision）。1549.5 nm处的带电激子（charged exciton）单光子纯度高达94.7%；未加工平面结构中顺序发射光子在2 ns时间窗内原始双光子干涉可见度为51%，4 ns积分窗内为41%。

Conclusion: 该架构结合宽带光提取、高光子相干性与原生III-V晶圆上的发射源预筛选（emitter preselection），为基于标记的确定性制备（marker-based deterministic fabrication）高质量单片纳米光子器件铺平了道路。

Abstract: We demonstrate a monolithic telecom C-band single-photon platform based on low-density InAs/InP quantum dots grown on an InGaAsP/InP distributed Bragg reflector, with simulated upward light-extraction efficiency exceeding 10% within the 40 nm spectral band. Enhanced light extraction is crucial for wide-field photoluminescence imaging, enabling approximately 3 nm statistical centroid-localization precision of QD emitters due to improved signal-to-noise ratio. The identified charged exciton emitting at 1549.5 nm exhibits single-photon emission purity up to 94.7%, validated by a raw peak-area integration under pulsed quasi-resonant excitation. Under these conditions, photons emitted sequentially from the unprocessed planar structure reveal a raw two-photon interference visibility of 51% of the photon wave packet within the 2 ns time window and 41% over a 4 ns integration window, all without Purcell enhancement typical for cavity-embedded emitters. Combining broadband extraction, high photon coherence, and emitter preselection on a native III-V wafer, this architecture paves the way for marker-based deterministic fabrication of high-quality monolithic nanophotonic devices.

</details>


### [165] [Spin-Dependent Transport in One-Dimensional Mercury- and Silver-Mediated DNA Nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31508)
*Arpan De,William Livernois,Simon Vecchioni,Yoel P. Ohayon,M. P. Anantram*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作表明，周期性重金属配位使DNA由局域态转变为以金属为中心的色散导带，带宽可达约1 eV（第二近邻跃迁起关键作用），且自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）主导自旋相关输运；配位金属的选择（Hg2+ vs Ag+）决定自旋翻转电导的相对强弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属-DNA纳米线（metallo-DNA nanowires）可作为分子尺度电子学的可编程支架，但其重金属配位如何重塑DNA的电子结构、以及相对论效应在准一维输运中的作用尚缺乏系统认识，尤其缺少对配位金属种类调控自旋输运的明确判据。

Method: 基于理论计算与模型分析，构建周期性重金属配位的DNA纳米线，考察层间耦合（含第二近邻跃迁，second nearest-neighbor hopping）对导带色散与带宽的影响；并引入自旋轨道耦合，比较配体杂化（ligand hybridization）与有效SO劈裂、以及自旋翻转（spin-flip）与自旋守恒电导，对Hg2+与Ag+两种配位体系进行对照。

Result: Hg2+配位纳米线在LUMO流形（LUMO manifold）内表现出与自旋守恒电导相当的自旋翻转电导，该高效自旋转换在较长分子长度下仍保持稳健，并伴随一组高度离域、节点结构振荡的空态，可在低偏压输运测量中触及；Ag+配位纳米线则呈弱自旋翻转散射，比自旋保持输运小一个数量级以上。有效SO劈裂与次近邻跃迁强度相当，导致显著自旋混合。

Conclusion: 相对论效应与量子限域（quantum confinement）在生物模板化的准一维分子导体中交汇；配位金属的选择是调控金属-金属相互作用、电子离域化与自旋相关输运的决定性参数，为金属-DNA纳米线的自旋电子学设计提供依据。

Abstract: Metallo-DNA nanowires serve as a versatile, programmable scaffold for molecular-scale electronics. In this work, we demonstrate that periodic heavy-metal coordination fundamentally reshapes the electronic landscape of DNA, generating dispersive, metal-centered conduction bands rather than isolated localized states. Driven by enhanced inter-layer coupling, where second nearest-neighbor hopping plays a crucial role: the conduction bandwidth can approach 1 eV, more than double that of canonical DNA. These broad bands suggest enhanced robustness of transport against structural fluctuations. As a result of relativistic effects, in these quasi-one-dimensional materials, spin-orbit (SO) coupling enhances band dispersion and governs spin-dependent transport. We predict that although ligand hybridization reduces atomic SO splitting, the effective SO splitting in these nanowires is comparable to the next-nearest-neighbor hopping strength, which results in considerable spin mixing. We find that Hg2+-coordinated nanowire exhibit pronounced spin-flip conductance comparable to spin-conserving conductance across the LUMO manifold. This efficient spin conversion remains robust over extended molecular lengths and is accompanied by a set of highly delocalized unoccupied states with oscillating nodal structures, readily accessible under low-bias transport measurements. In contrast, the Ag+-coordinated nanowires exhibit weak spin-flip scattering that is more than an order of magnitude smaller than spin-preserving transport. This duality establishes the choice of coordination metal as a decisive parameter to govern metal-metal interaction, electron delocalization, and spin-dependent transport. Overall, our findings demonstrate how relativistic effects and quantum confinement intersect in these bio-templated, quasi-one-dimensional molecular conductors.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Alexander Torsion and Floer Comparisons in the BV-BFV Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30192)
*Nima Moshayedi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文比较相对阿贝尔BF挠率（relative Abelian BF torsion）与结Floer同调（knot Floer homology）的Alexander分次Euler示性数，并通过子午圈环带边界条件将归一化相对挠率与Alexander多项式等同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立BF型拓扑量子场论挠率不变量与结Floer同调Alexander分次信息之间的对应关系，并厘清标量比较与范畴层面等价可能存在的障碍。

Method: 采用子午圈环带（meridional annulus）边界条件识别归一化相对挠率；在尖锐亏格界（genus bound）下用单一分圆挠率值恢复Alexander多项式系数；考察连通和粘合（connected-sum gluing）与分支覆盖公式；计算界面收缩（interface contractions）与标量比较障碍，包括零次环面代数两扇区约化的配对缺陷（pairing defect）；构造六生成元dg消解（dg resolution）以得到保持导出配对的完美模范畴等价，并给出经典一维BV-BFV实现。

Result: 归一化相对挠率在子午圈环带边界条件下等于Alexander多项式；单一精确分圆挠率值可在尖锐亏格界内恢复其全部系数，并与连通和粘合及分支覆盖公式相容。存在阻碍标量比较的界面收缩与配对缺陷；六生成元dg消解给出保持导出配对的完美模范畴等价。在固定非共振多项式量子BFV扇区、特征零且$\hbar$取逆的条件下，态与Weyl可观测量均约化为真空场，且Hochschild不变量（Hochschild invariant）阻碍其与零次环面代数的Morita等价。

Conclusion: 所得结果并未建立完整的几何BV-BFV/Floer等价，仅在特定边界条件、扇区与范畴层面给出局部对应及明确障碍。

Abstract: We compare relative Abelian BF torsion with the Alexander-graded Euler characteristic of knot Floer homology. A meridional annulus boundary condition identifies the normalized relative torsion with the Alexander polynomial. One exact prime-cyclotomic torsion value recovers all its coefficients under a sharp genus bound, compatibly with connected-sum gluing and branched-cover formulas. We compute interface contractions and obstructions to scalar comparisons, including the pairing defect of two-sector reduction for the degree-zero torus algebra. A six-generator dg resolution yields an equivalence of perfect-module categories preserving derived pairings and admits a classical one-dimensional BV-BFV realization. In the fixed nonresonant polynomial quantum BFV sector, over characteristic zero with $\hbar$ inverted, both states and Weyl observables reduce to a vacuum field; a Hochschild invariant obstructs a Morita equivalence with the degree-zero torus algebra. These results do not establish a full geometric BV-BFV/Floer equivalence.

</details>


### [167] [On Borodin-Gorin presentation of Heckman-Opdam $\mathfrak{gl}_n$ hypergeometric function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30304)
*L. Cherepanov,S. Khoroshkin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明Heckman-Opdam $\mathfrak{gl}_n$超几何函数的Borodin-Gorin积分表示与arXiv:2508.18864中研究的两种积分表示等价，并讨论相关Baxter算子及Ruijsenaars双曲模型的极限过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机，仅指出需建立Borodin-Gorin表示与已有两种积分表示之间的关系。

Method: 采用交织积分（interlacing integrals）形式的Borodin-Gorin表示，并将其与arXiv:2508.18864中的两种积分表示进行等价性证明；同时引入相关Baxter算子（Baxter operators），考虑源自Ruijsenaars双曲模型（Ruijsenaars hyperbolic model）的极限过程。

Result: Borodin-Gorin对Heckman-Opdam $\mathfrak{gl}_n$超几何函数（hypergeometric function）的交织积分表示被证明与arXiv:2508.18864中的两种积分表示等价。

Conclusion: 三种积分表示的统一等价性得到确立，且相关Baxter算子与Ruijsenaars双曲模型的极限过程构成该框架的组成部分。

Abstract: We prove that Borodin-Gorin presentation for Heckman-Opdam $\mathfrak{gl}_n$ hypergeometric function by interlacing integrals is equivalent to two other integral presentations studied in arXiv:2508.18864. We also consider related Baxter operators and the corresponding limiting procedure from the Ruijsenaars hyperbolic model.

</details>


### [168] [Rational limit of Rains integrals of type $A_n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30317)
*L. Cherepanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了 $A_n$ 型椭圆 Rains 恒等式（elliptic Rains identity）的有理类比（rational analogue），导出其退化/约化形式，并说明其与文献中若干已知积分的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆 Rains 恒等式（elliptic Rains identity, arXiv:0309252）在 $A_n$ 型情形已有结果，但其中有理（rational）极限下的对应版本尚待建立；本文旨在补足这一空白并厘清其与既有积分结果的关系。

Method: 采用对椭圆 Rains 恒等式取有理极限（rational limit）的方式推导其有理类比，并对所得恒等式进一步作约化（reduction）处理。

Result: 得到了 $A_n$ 型有理 Rains 恒等式，获得其若干约化形式，并建立了这些结果与文献中知名积分（well-known integrals）之间的对应联系。

Conclusion: 有理类比及其约化统一地把椭圆 Rains 恒等式与文献中的经典积分结果联系起来，为相关恒等式的层级（elliptic–trigonometric–rational）理解提供了补充。

Abstract: We deduce the rational analogue of the elliptic Rains identity of type $A_n$ arXiv:0309252. We also derive reductions of the rational analogue and present the connection of them with well-known integrals in literature.

</details>


### [169] [Spin-Clifford Bundles and Spin Connections on the Hyperbolic Clifford Algebra - Part I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30542)
*E. Notte-Cuello,J. M. Hoff da Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立与双曲 Clifford 代数（hyperbolic Clifford algebra）$\mathbb{R}^{H}=\mathcal{C}\ell(H_V)$ 相关的旋量场（spinor field）理论，涵盖代数结构、双曲旋量结构（hyperbolic spin structure）的存在性以及双曲旋量联络（hyperbolic spin connection）的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋量理论多基于 Lorentzian 情形，其本原幂等元（primitive idempotent）在复化（complexification）后不再保持本原性；本文旨在为双曲空间 $H_V=V\oplus V^{*}$ 上的 Clifford 代数发展一套平行的旋量场框架。

Method: 代数上，显式构造实本原幂等元并刻画 Witten 超场（Witten superfields）理想、手征分解（chirality decomposition）与双曲旋量群（hyperbolic spin group）；几何上，在每个可定向流形上构造典范双曲旋量结构并研究相应旋量丛与理想旋量子丛，同时将任意线性联络各向同性延拓（isotropic extension）至 $TM\oplus T^*M$ 以定义双曲旋量联络。

Result: 所构造的本原幂等元在复化后仍保持本原性，区别于 Lorentzian 情形；每个可定向流形上均存在典范双曲旋量结构，除可定向性外无额外拓扑障碍（topological obstruction）；各向同性延拓的线性联络恒与双曲度量相容，且所导出双曲旋量联络的良定义性、相容性、曲率（curvature）与显式形式均得到确立。

Conclusion: 该工作为双曲 Clifford 代数上的 Dirac 算子（Dirac operator）提供了几何框架，具体 Dirac 算子的构造将在后续文章中给出。

Abstract: We develop the theory of spinor fields associated with the hyperbolic Clifford algebra $\mathbb{R}^{H}=\mathcal{C}\ell(H_V)$, which is associated with hyperbolic space $H_V=V\oplus V^{*}$. Algebraically, we exhibit an explicit real primitive idempotent which, unlike the Lorentzian case, remains primitive after complexification and generates the ideal of Witten superfields. We also describe the chirality decomposition and the hyperbolic spin group. Geometrically, we construct a canonical hyperbolic spin structure on every oriented manifold, showing that no topological obstructions arise beyond orientability and we study the associated spinor bundles, spin-Clifford bundles and ideal spinor subbundles. Finally, we introduce the isotropic extension of an arbitrary linear connection to $TM\oplus T^*M$, which is always compatible with the hyperbolic metric, and the associated hyperbolic spin connection, whose well-definedness, compatibilities, curvature and explicit form are established, providing the geometric framework for Dirac operators on the hyperbolic Clifford algebra, which we will present in a forthcoming article.

</details>


### [170] [First-order differential constraints for the nonlinear wave equation:wave fronts, admissible nonlinearities and exact solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30629)
*Oleg Kaptsov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文用线性确定方程（linear determining equations）方法在三空间维构造非线性波方程的精确解，所得解一般不具备李点对称性（Lie point symmetries），并给出正弦-戈尔登墙、涡旋构型、运动点源场及立方方程有限能量解等具体结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性波方程的精确解通常依赖对称性约化，而对称性假设限制了可解类型；本文旨在突破对称性限制，构造一般不具对称性的精确解。

Method: 采用线性确定方程方法：先求一阶约束函数作为线性确定方程的解，令其为零得到微分约束（differential constraints）并积分，再将所得拟设（ansatz）代入原方程；李点对称作为特例被涵盖。

Result: 确定方程显示允许的非线性（nonlinearity）与约束几何相关：在特征超平面上任意、在光锥（light cones）上为三次、对一对约束则在光锥上为指数型、在线源的柱面前沿为五次型。解包括平滑有界的正弦-戈尔登墙（sine-Gordon walls）、形状沿前沿任意变化的涡旋构型（vortex configurations）、沿任意轨迹运动的点源场（含立方情形的辐射功率非线性拉莫尔公式（Larmor formula）类比），以及具有任意数目环面节点曲面的立方方程光滑有限能量解。

Conclusion: 线性确定方程方法可系统生成非线性波方程的无对称性精确解，并将可容许非线性类型与约束的几何结构相联系，兼具有多种物理相关构型的构造能力。

Abstract: We construct exact solutions of the nonlinear wave equation in three space dimensions by the method of linear determining equations: first-order constraint functions are found as solutions of the linear determining equation, the differential constraints obtained by setting them equal to zero are integrated, and the resulting ansatz is substituted into the equation. Lie point symmetries are a special case, and the solutions obtained generically have no symmetry. The determining equations tie the admissible nonlinearity to the geometry of the constraint: arbitrary on characteristic hyperplanes, cubic on light cones, and, for a pair of constraints, exponential on light cones and quintic on the cylindrical fronts of a line source. Among the solutions are smooth bounded sine-Gordon walls and vortex configurations whose shape varies arbitrarily along a front, the fields of point sources moving on arbitrary trajectories for the cubic and Liouville equations, with an exact nonlinear analogue of the Larmor formula for the power radiated in the cubic case, and smooth finite-energy solutions of the cubic equation with any prescribed number of toroidal nodal surfaces.

</details>


### [171] [A sharp gradient-corrected Lieb--Thirring inequality in one dimension with applications to density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30887)
*Thiago Carvalho Corso,Louis Garrigue*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一维带半经典常数与梯度修正的尖锐 Lieb–Thirring 不等式，并刻画其优化元构成二维流形；进而将其应用于密度泛函理论中动能泛函的 Thomas–Fermi–von Weizsäcker（TFW）近似误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为密度泛函理论（density functional theory）中动能的 TFW 近似提供非渐近、显式的误差刻画，并厘清精确性成立的密度条件。摘要指出周期情形下含宇称依赖壳层修正的不等式此前未见，构成新的工具。

Method: 方法分两步：先证明一维尖锐 Lieb–Thirring 不等式（含半经典常数与梯度修正），周期情形额外含宇称依赖的壳层修正（parity dependent shell-correction），并刻画优化元；再将优化密度集合识别为二维流形（称为 Poincaré density manifold），用以控制 TFW 近似的全局与局部误差。

Result: 所得误差界显式且非渐近，由归一化密度到 Poincaré 流形的距离乘以粒子数控制，从而表明 TFW 近似在该流形上精确。周期情形下，Kohn–Sham 势与逆 TFW 势在自然对偶 Sobolev 空间中的距离亦由显式密度依赖常数乘以该流形距离控制；对固定密度，高密度极限 N→∞ 时收敛速率为 N^{-2} 且被证明最优。

Conclusion: 结论是所得不等式为 TFW 近似提供了可量化的密度依赖误差理论，并给出 Kohn–Sham 势收敛的最优速率 N^{-2}；精确性由密度位于 Poincaré 密度流形上刻画。

Abstract: We first prove a sharp Lieb-Thirring inequality with semi-classical constant and gradient correction in one dimension. In the periodic setting, the corresponding inequality also includes a parity dependent shell-correction term, which appears to be new. Moreover, we also characterize the optimizers of these inequalities. In particular, the optimizing density profiles form a two-dimensional manifold, which we call here the Poincaré density manifold.
  We then apply these results to study the Thomas--Fermi--von Weizsäcker (TFW) approximation of the kinetic energy in Density Functional Theory. More precisely, we first obtain an explicit and non-asymptotic bound on the global and local error made by the TFW approximation. These bounds are controlled by the distance of the normalized density profile from the Poincaré density manifold times the number of particles. In particular, these bounds show that the TFW approximation is exact on the Poincaré manifold. We then show that, in the periodic setting, the distance between the Kohn-Sham potential and the inverse TFW potential in the natural dual Sobolev space can also be controlled by explicit density dependent constants times the distance to the Poincaré manifold. As a corollary, we prove that for a fixed density profile, the Kohn-Sham potential converges towards the inverse Thomas--Fermi potential in the high-density limit $N \rightarrow \infty$ with asymptotic rate of order $N^{-2}$. This rate is shown to be optimal.

</details>


### [172] [Dirac Structures and Reduction of Optimal Control Problems with Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31033)
*A. Ibort,M. Leok,R. Salmoni,C. Sardón*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文以Dirac结构（Dirac structures）为工具研究最优控制问题（optimal control problems）的对称约化（symmetry reduction），将Pontryagin最大值原理（Pontryagin Maximum Principle）的一阶条件表述为Pontryagin丛上的隐式Dirac系统，并证明Lagrange-Pontryagin变分原理与Dirac结构的约化给出相同方程，可等同于Atiyah algebroid对偶上的Hamilton方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优控制理论中，对称约化与约束算法通常分别处理，缺乏统一的几何框架；论文旨在用Dirac几何统一处理Pontryagin条件的约化、奇异性（非正则）情形与异常极值曲线（abnormal extremals）的刻画。

Method: 在Pontryagin丛上构造隐式Dirac系统表示一阶条件；对Lie群不变问题同时约化Lagrange-Pontryagin变分原理与底层Dirac结构；在去正则性条件下将Gotay-Nester-Hinds约束算法改写为Dirac几何形式；对控制线性系统（control-linear systems）分析水平方向与主联络下异常极值的叶层结构、曲率配对退化及无度量指标形式。

Result: 证明约化后的变分原理与Dirac结构给出相同约化方程，并对应Atiyah algebroid对偶上的Hamilton方程；给出重构（reconstruction）、主联络独立性及临界性与最优性关系的结论；证明对称约化与Dirac形式的GNH约束算法可交换；对控制线性系统，异常极值位于基余态（base costate）为零的叶上，首个次级约束为曲率配对的退化，并给出严格异常性（strict abnormality）的约化判据与无度量指标形式。

Conclusion: 以转子航天器（平坦联络）、Heisenberg群（曲率处处非零）与Martinet问题（曲率在超曲面上消失）三个例子验证框架，表明Dirac几何可为含对称性的最优控制约化与异常极值分析提供统一表述。

Abstract: We study the symmetry reduction of optimal control problems using Dirac structures. The first-order conditions of the Pontryagin Maximum Principle are written as an implicit Dirac system on the Pontryagin bundle. For a problem invariant under a Lie group, we reduce both the Lagrange-Pontryagin variational principle and the underlying Dirac structure, and prove that they yield the same reduced equations, which we identify with the Hamiltonian equations on the dual of the Atiyah algebroid. Reconstruction, independence of the principal connection, and the relation between criticality and optimality complete the picture. Dropping regularity, we give the Gotay-Nester-Hinds constraint algorithm a Dirac-geometric form and prove that symmetry reduction commutes with it. For control-linear systems whose admissible directions are horizontal for a principal connection, abnormal extremals lie on the leaf where the base costate vanishes, and the first secondary constraint is the degeneracy of the curvature pairing. We also give a reduced criterion for strict abnormality and a metric-free index form. Examples: a spacecraft with rotors (flat connection), the Heisenberg group (nowhere-zero curvature) and the Martinet problem (curvature vanishing on a hypersurface).

</details>


### [173] [Hadamard states for linearized gravity on asymptotically flat spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31214)
*Christian Gérard,Mikhail Molodyk,Michał Wrochna*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在四维渐近平坦时空（asymptotically flat spacetimes）上研究线性化爱因斯坦方程（linearized Einstein equations）的量子化，证明了在调和规范（harmonic gauge）下、对空间无穷远处呈锥形邻域内渐近于闵可夫斯基度规（Minkowski metric）的任意爱因斯坦度规，均存在完全规范不变的 Hadamard 态（Hadamard states）。


<details>
