  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论在弯曲时空中的构造需要具有良好紫外性质的态，而规范理论中还需兼顾规范不变性；线性化引力情形下，在渐近平坦背景上同时满足 Hadamard 条件与完全规范不变性并非显然，构成该研究的出发点。

Method: 在线性化爱因斯坦方程的框架下，于调和规范中处理规范自由度，并针对空间无穷远处具锥形邻域（conic neighborhood）、渐近趋于闵可夫斯基度规的爱因斯坦度规背景，构造相应的量子态并论证其规范不变性。

Result: 证明了对上述一类渐近平坦爱因斯坦度规，完全规范不变的 Hadamard 态确实存在，从而在调和规范下同时实现了 Hadamard 性质与规范不变性。

Conclusion: 结果表明线性化量子引力的态构造可在渐近平坦背景下以规范不变方式实现，为后续在弯曲时空量子场论框架内讨论引力扰动提供了数学上严格的出发点。

Abstract: We consider the quantization of linearized Einstein equations on 4-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. We prove the existence of fully gauge invariant Hadamard states in the harmonic gauge for any Einstein metric asymptotic to the Minkowski metric in a conic neighborhood of spatial infinity.

</details>


### [174] [Symmetric and antisymmetric cumulant hierarchies and Wick polynomials of operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31374)
*Jani Lukkarinen,Sakari Pirnes*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将累积量（cumulants）与 Wick 多项式（Wick polynomials）推广至非对易随机变量情形，构建对称与反对称版本，并应用于有限格点上有两体相互作用的玻色子/费米子气体，导出截断关联函数（truncated correlation functions）的演化层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动理学理论（kinetic theory）的数学基础研究近年取得进展，其核心工具是对矩、累积量（cumulants）及相关关联误差所构成演化层级（hierarchies）的精细分析；但既有工具依赖随机变量的可交换性，无法直接处理 Fock 空间（Fock space）上产生与湮灭算子（creation and annihilation operators）的期望值。

Method: 将累积量与 Wick 多项式（Wick polynomials）两类基本工具推广到随机变量不必对易的设定，分别构造对称（symmetric）与反对称（antisymmetric）累积量及 Wick 多项式，以处理玻色型与费米型 Fock 空间上的算子期望。

Result: 作为应用，针对在有限格点（finite lattice）上跃迁并经两体势（two-body potential）相互作用的玻色子或费米子气体，导出这类累积量（此语境下通常称为截断关联函数）所满足的演化方程层级。

Conclusion: 若对所导出的层级解具有充分控制，则可估计以有限格点动理学方程近似量子系统状态时所产生的误差项大小；该近似与标准的玻色型及费米型 Boltzmann–Nordheim 方程密切相关。

Abstract: There has been much progress in understanding mathematical foundations of kinetic theory in recent years, in particular, for the original model of a rarefied gas of classical particles with repulsive interactions, as well as for weakly nonlinear wave evolutions related to wave turbulence. These results are mainly based on refined analysis of evolution hierarchies of moments of the relevant random variables, or of related quantities such as of cumulants or of correlation errors. In this work, we extend two of such basic tools, cumulants and Wick polynomials, to a setting where the random variables no longer need to commute, with the goal of handling expectations of creation and annihilation operators on a Fock space, both in the bosonic and fermionic case. We call these constructions symmetric and antisymmetric cumulants and Wick polynomials, respectively. As an application, we derive a hierarchy of evolution equations satisfied by such cumulants, usually called truncated correlation functions in this context, for a gas of bosons or fermions hopping on a finite lattice and interacting via a two-body potential. With sufficient control over solutions to these hierarchies, one could control the magnitude of the error terms related to an approximation of the state of the quantum system by a finite lattice kinetic equation studied in our earlier work, closely connected to standard bosonic and fermionic Boltzmann--Nordheim equations.

</details>


### [175] [An elliptic generalization of the $D^{(2)}_{2}$ Spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31381)
*Yi Qiao,Pei Sun,Xiaotian Xu,Junpeng Cao,Guang-Liang Li,Wen-Li Yang,Kangjie Shi,Yupeng Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了闭合 D^{(2)}_2 量子自旋链的一个可积椭圆推广（integrable elliptic generalization），并基于 R-矩阵与转移矩阵的因子化（factorization）给出该模型的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于对已知的闭合 D^{(2)}_2 量子自旋链（quantum spin chain）进行椭圆型推广，以获得更一般且保持可积性（integrability）的模型。

Method: 构造相应的 R-矩阵（R-matrix），并在三角极限（trigonometric limit）下证明其通过相似变换（similarity transformation）退化到 D^{(2)}_2 模型的 R-矩阵；再利用 R-矩阵与转移矩阵（transfer matrix）的因子化性质保证模型可积性，进而求解。

Result: 模型求得精确解（exact solution），该解可用齐次 T-Q 关系（homogeneous T-Q relation）表示。

Conclusion: 所得椭圆推广在三角极限下与 D^{(2)}_2 模型相容，且其因子化结构确保可积性，精确解由齐次 T-Q 关系刻画。

Abstract: An integrable elliptic generalization of the closed $D^{(2)}_2$ quantum spin chain is constructed. Taking the trigonometric limit, we have shown that the corresponding R-matrix reduces to that of the $D^{(2)}_2$ one, up to a similarity transformation. Basing on the very fact of the factorization for the R-matrix and the corresponding transfer matrix which indeed ensures the integrability of the model, we give the exact solution of the model which can be expressed in terms of a homogeneous T-Q relation.

</details>


### [176] [Some minimal characteristics and flat trajectories of a system in the N-body problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31582)
*Igor Pavlov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 针对N体问题（N-body problem），将若干空间非线性规划（nonlinear programming）问题应用于相空间上系统主要特征的条件下极小值与局部极值求解；证明当角动量非零时，这些极值只能在平的（一般为无限）空间轨迹上取得，并给出使轨迹为平坦有限（"圆"或椭圆）轨迹的运动积分取值条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对经典N体系统（system of N particles with interaction）的相空间，研究系统主要特征的条件下极小值与局部极值的空间分布问题，即用非线性规划方法刻画极值所在轨迹的几何性质及其与运动积分的关系。

Method: 构造相空间上关于系统主要特征的条件极小值与局部极值的非线性规划（nonlinear programming）模型，并据此分析极值轨迹的平坦性（flatness），进而推导限制角动量（angular momentum）与总能量（total energy）等运动积分（integrals of motion）取值的条件。

Result: 证明在系统角动量非零的条件下，系统特征的条件下极小值与局部极值只能在空间中的平坦轨迹（flat trajectories，一般为非有限）上取得；并给出使这些轨迹成为平坦有限（"圆形"或椭圆）轨迹的运动积分取值条件，同时确定这些平坦轨迹上系统关键特征的取值。

Conclusion: 极值问题的解与轨迹的平坦性及运动积分取值密切相关：仅在角动量非零且积分取值满足特定限制时，系统才在时间演化中保持平坦结构并对应有限轨迹。附录进一步给出更一般系统模型下的若干相关结果。

Abstract: For the classical model of a system of N particles with interaction (the N-body problem) we consider some spatial problems of nonlinear programming for finding conditional minima and local extrema for the main characteristics of the system on its phase space. We prove that these conditional minima and local extrema of the system characteristics can only be achieved on flat, in general, not necessarily finite, trajectories of the system in space, (under the condition that the system angular momentum is non-zero). And then we find the basic conditions limiting the values of the integrals of motion: angular momentum and total energy of the system under which these trajectories are flat finite ("circular" or elliptical) trajectories on which the flat structure of the system is preserved in time under the certain restrictions on the values of the system's integrals of motion. And we determine also the values of the system's key characteristics on these flat trajectories. Next, in the Appendix, we consider some additional related results for a more general model of the system.

</details>


<div id='math.SG'></div>

# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [The symplectic leaves of the affine Poisson $C^\infty$-scheme associated with a singular subalgebroid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30395)
*Ruben Louis*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 奇异子代数胚（singular subalgebroid），特别是奇异叶状结构（singular foliation），决定了一个通用的泊松 $C^\infty$-概形（Poisson $C^\infty$-scheme）$\mathrm{Spec}(\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G}))$。该工作的核心结果是：该概形的底空间可分解为辛流形（symplectic manifolds）的离散并，并且 Helffer–Nourrigat 锥可延拓至奇异子代数胚并成为其嵌入的泊松 $C^\infty$-子概形（embedded Poisson $C^\infty$-subscheme）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李代数胚（Lie algebroid）对偶上的线性泊松结构是经典构造，但对于奇异子代数胚与奇异叶状结构，缺少相应的通用泊松对象。该文旨在为此类奇异情形建立类比构造，并刻画其底空间的几何结构。

Method: 对每个奇异子代数胚 $\mathscr{G}$ 关联通用泊松 $C^\infty$-概形 $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G}))$，作为李代数胚对偶上线性泊松结构的类比；并在此基础上将 Helffer–Nourrigat 锥延拓至奇异子代数胚的框架。

Result: 证明了 $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G}))$ 的底空间 $X_{\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G})}$ 可分解为若干辛流形的离散并；同时证明延拓后的 Helffer–Nourrigat 锥是 $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G}))$ 的嵌入泊松 $C^\infty$-子概形。

Conclusion: 奇异子代数胚可赋予统一的泊松 $C^\infty$-概形描述，其底空间具有辛流形分层式的分解结构，且 Helffer–Nourrigat 锥在该框架中自然嵌入，表明该构造能统一处理奇异叶状结构的泊松几何与其锥结构。

Abstract: Every singular subalgebroid, and in particular every singular foliation $\mathscr{G}$, determines a universal Poisson $C^\infty$-scheme $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G}))$, which is the analogue of the linear Poisson structure on the dual of a Lie algebroid. We show that the underlying space $X_{\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G})}$ of $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G}))$ decomposes as a disjoint union of symplectic manifolds. Furthermore, we extend the Helffer-Nourrigat cone to a singular subalgebroid and show that it is an embedded Poisson $C^\infty$-subscheme of $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathcal{F}(\mathscr{G}))$.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Mutual transformations of arbitrary ternary qubit trees by Clifford gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16693)
*Alexander Yu. Vlasov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 具有相同节点数的三元量子比特树（ternary qubit trees）可通过一组自然定义的克利福德门（Clifford gates）序列相互变换，或变换为对应 Jordan-Wigner 变换（Jordan-Wigner transform）的一维链标准表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出所要证明的命题：同类节点数的三元量子比特树在克利福德门作用下等价，并可归约为 Jordan-Wigner 对应的一维链表示。

Method: 采用自然定义的克利福德门序列（sequence of Clifford gates）作为变换工具，作用于节点数相同的三元量子比特树，构造树间互变及其到一维链标准形的映射。

Result: 证明了同节点数的三元量子比特树在克利福德门序列下可相互转化，亦可转化为与 Jordan-Wigner 变换对应的一维链标准表示，从而表明这些树结构在该变换类下属于同一等价类。

Conclusion: 三元量子比特树在克利福德门意义下具有统一的可约化性，Jordan-Wigner 一维链可作为其标准代表形式。

Abstract: It is shown that ternary qubit trees with the same number of nodes can be transformed by the naturally defined sequence of Clifford gates into each other or into standard representation as one-dimensional chain corresponding to Jordan-Wigner transform.

</details>


### [179] [Size-independent Logarithmic Sobolev Inequalities for Quantum Gibbs Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31571)
*Hongrui Chen,Zhiyan Ding,Jianfeng Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种证明量子 Gibbs 采样器（quantum Gibbs sampler）满足 L² 与修正对数 Sobolev 不等式（modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality）且常数与系统尺寸无关的新方法，适用于满足 KMS 细致平衡的 Lindblad 生成元，并应用于一维量子自旋链与有限程高维高温系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子 Gibbs 采样器的收敛性分析需要与系统尺寸无关的 L² 及修正对数 Sobolev 不等式常数，而此类常数的证明在量子多体设定下较为困难，故需建立普适的证明框架。

Method: 沿依赖温度的参数路径，将 L² 对数 Sobolev 不等式从无限温乘积态传输到目标 Gibbs 态，同时保持夹心 Rényi-2 散度（sandwiched Rényi-2 divergence）的尺寸无关衰减率；再利用张量幂论证（tensor-power argument）结合量子渐近等分性质（quantum asymptotic equipartition property）将衰减界转移至相对熵（relative entropy）。

Result: 得到尺寸无关的 L² 对数 Sobolev 不等式与修正对数 Sobolev 不等式；应用于任意有限温度下的一维量子自旋链（quantum spin chain），以及任意固定维度、足够高温下的有限程量子自旋系统（finite-range quantum spin system）。

Conclusion: 该方法为证明量子 Gibbs 采样器的尺寸无关对数 Sobolev 不等式提供了一般性框架，并覆盖一维全温区与高维高温两类典型情形。

Abstract: We propose a new method for proving $L^2$ and modified logarithmic Sobolev inequalities with constants independent of system size for quantum Gibbs samplers whose Lindblad generators satisfy Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) detailed balance. We first establish the $L^2$ logarithmic Sobolev inequality by transporting it along a temperature-dependent path from the infinite-temperature product state to the target Gibbs state while maintaining a size-independent decay rate for the sandwiched Rényi-2 divergence. A tensor-power argument, combined with the quantum asymptotic equipartition property, then transfers this decay bound to relative entropy, yielding the modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality. This approach provides a general framework for proving size-independent logarithmic Sobolev inequalities for quantum Gibbs samplers. We apply it to two regimes: one-dimensional quantum spin chains at every finite temperature, and finite-range quantum spin systems at sufficiently high temperature in any fixed dimension.

</details>


### [180] [Quantum Constrained Hamiltonian Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31170)
*Yoshihiko Abe,Ryo Nagai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出量子约束哈密顿下降（QCHD），一种在光滑等式约束下进行优化的连续时间量子框架，通过狄拉克约束动力学构造内在量子哈密顿量，实现无需惩罚项的精确约束保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无约束情形下的量子哈密顿下降（QHD）通过模拟粒子在代价函数势场中的薛定谔动力学实现最小化，但实际优化问题常含等式约束，需要一种无需惩罚项即可精确保持约束的量子动力学方法。

Method: 对带约束的经典动力学应用狄拉克（Dirac）形式化理论，构造内在量子哈密顿量，其动能项采用约束流形上的拉普拉斯–贝尔特拉米算子（Laplace–Beltrami operator）；并分析其与添加强惩罚项（penalty term）的QHD之间的关系。

Result: 对于平方距离惩罚，有效切向动力学获得外曲率势（extrinsic-curvature potential），并在非平凡法向旋转扇区中出现与法丛联络（normal-bundle connection）的耦合；这些依赖嵌入的项在强惩罚极限下仍然存在，使动力学一般不同于QCHD。圆形、椭圆形及两个同时约束的数值比较显示，在所用初始态与有限惩罚强度下，QCHD优化更快且在全球极小值附近更集中。

Conclusion: QCHD提供了一种无惩罚项、精确保持约束的量子优化框架，其动力学与强惩罚QHD存在本质差异，并在数值实验中表现出更快收敛与更优的极小值聚集性。

Abstract: We propose Quantum Constrained Hamiltonian Descent (QCHD), a continuous-time quantum framework for optimization under smooth equality constraints. The unconstrained counterpart, Quantum Hamiltonian Descent (QHD), minimizes a cost function by simulating the Schrödinger dynamics of a particle in the potential given by the cost function. Applying Dirac's formalism to the classical dynamics with constraints, we construct an intrinsic quantum Hamiltonian with a Laplace--Beltrami operator on the constraint manifold in a kinetic term. Its evolution preserves the constraints exactly without penalty terms and is expected to solve quantum-dynamically the constrained optimization problem. We analyze its relation to Quantum Hamiltonian Descent supplemented by a strong penalty term. For a squared-distance penalty, the effective tangential dynamics acquires an extrinsic-curvature potential and, in nontrivial normal-rotation sectors, a coupling to the normal-bundle connection. These embedding-dependent terms remain in the strong-penalty limit, generally yielding quantum dynamics different from QCHD. Numerical comparisons for circular, elliptical, and two simultaneous constraints show faster optimization and improved concentration near global minima for the initial states and finite penalty strengths considered.

</details>


### [181] [Scalable Neural Wave Functions via Information Mixing and Hierarchical Network Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30257)
*Faith Oyedemi,Stefanie Czischek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出将多层感知机混合器（MLP mixer）用作量子多体系统的波函数拟设（wave function ansatz），通过轻量级全局与局部信息混合策略，兼顾长程关联（long-range correlations）捕捉能力与低计算成本，并在二维量子多体系统基态表示任务上验证其竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值模拟量子多体系统受希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）随系统规模指数增长的制约；现有生成式神经网络波函数中，循环神经网络（RNN）计算高效但难以捕捉长程关联，Transformer 虽擅长长程关联却计算开销大、仅适用于小系统，故需一种兼顾二者的架构。

Method: 将 MLP mixer 模型作为波函数拟设，借助计算轻量的混合策略在系统内进行全局与局部信息混合；在多种物相（phases of matter）的二维量子多体系统基态表示任务上进行基准测试，并与单站点（single-site）和分块（patched）输入定义下的 RNN 及 Transformer 模型对比，同时考察不同物理系统规模下表示精度与计算效率的变化。

Result: MLP mixer 架构的性能与当前最先进（state-of-the-art）网络模型具有竞争力；将其与 Transformer 模型进行分层组合（hierarchical combination）后，在大尺度量子系统上优于其他先进架构。

Conclusion: MLP mixer 为量子多体波函数表示提供了兼顾精度与效率的方案，其与 Transformer 的分层组合为大规模量子物相（quantum phases of matter）研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Numerically simulating quantum many-body systems is one of the central challenges in modern physics, as the exponential growth of the Hilbert space with system size renders large-scale exact solutions intractable. Over the past decade, generative artificial neural networks have emerged as powerful wave function ansätze that have powered advances in finding ground state representations, reconstructing quantum states from data, and modeling dynamics in quantum many-body systems. While recurrent neural networks (RNNs) are computationally efficient, the recurrent nature can limit their capacity to capture long-range correlations. Transformer models with their underlying self-attention mechanism excel at capturing these long-range correlations, but incur increased computational requirements, restricting applications to small physical systems. In this work, we address these limitations by exploring the multi-layer perceptron (MLP) mixer model as a wave function ansatz. By mixing information globally and locally through the system via a computationally lightweight mixing strategy, this architecture promises to combine the ability to capture long-range correlations with low computational costs. We benchmark the model on finding ground state representations of two-dimensional quantum many-body systems in various phases of matter and investigate the behavior of representational accuracy and computational efficiency for different physical system sizes. By comparing its performance to RNNs and transformer models with single-site and patched input definitions, we demonstrate that the MLP mixer architecture performs competitively with state-of-the-art network models. We further show that a hierarchical combination of this model with the transformer model outperforms other state-of-the-art architectures for large-scale quantum systems, opening new avenues for investigating quantum phases of matter.

</details>


### [182] [Delta-Doped GeV$^-$ in diamond with isotope resolvable optical spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30375)
*Ian N. Hammock,Kinfung Ngan,Antoni W. Wellisz,Keira Linder,Midrel Wilfried Ngandeu Ngambou,Jiefei Zhang,Xinghan Guo,Benjamin Pingault,Shuo Sun,David D. Awschalom,F. Joseph Heremans,Alexander A. High,Nazar Delegan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过微波等离子体化学气相沉积中GeH₄的定时注入，将锗空位色心（GeV⁻）局域化于小于10 nm的薄层中，并实现了低损伤、无应变环境，进一步经电子辐照与退火获得0.12 GeV⁻/μm²的面密度，实现了 isotopic cluster 的谱分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IV族色心（Group IV color centers）是量子网络（quantum networking）的有前景平台，但传统离子注入（ion implantation）方式导致色心深度分布宽泛及损伤诱导的应变不均匀（strain inhomogeneity），因此确定性定位（deterministic placement）与损伤抑制对该平台改进至关重要。

Method: 采用微波等离子体化学气相沉积（microwave plasma chemical vapor deposition）中定时注入GeH₄实现GeV⁻的delta掺杂定位，再结合电子辐照（electron irradiation）与退火（annealing）生成色心，并测量自旋T₂*与谱线性质。

Result: 在小于10 nm层内实现17 ppm的Ge浓度和无应变GeV⁻环境，面密度达0.12 GeV⁻/μm²；色心生成损伤极小，抑制了谱不均匀性，可分辨isotopic clusters，最佳cluster的不均匀分布为2.14 GHz、平均线宽47±11 MHz；估计空位到GeV⁻的产率≥90%，支持非热最近邻形成模型（athermal nearest-neighbor formation model）；自旋T₂*下界为730 ns，受顺磁缺陷（paramagnetic defect）限制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了delta掺杂体系中GeV⁻的形成动力学（formation dynamics），确立了非热最近邻形成模型，并为量子网络应用中IV族色心的确定性、低损伤制备提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Group IV color centers in diamond -- silicon, germanium, tin, and lead-vacancy centers -- are a promising platform for quantum networking. Typical Group-IV creation utilizes ion implantation during diamond growth that leaves the color centers with a broad depth distribution and damage-induced strain inhomogeneity. Deterministic placement and damage reduction in Group IV generation are essential for improving this platform. In this letter, we report on the localization of germanium-vacancy (GeV$^-$) to a $<$ 10 nm layer via the timed injection of GeH$_4$ during microwave plasma chemical vapor deposition. In this layer we have realized 17 ppm of Ge and a strain-free environment for the GeV$^-$. Subsequent electron irradiation and annealing have generated a sheet density of 0.12 GeV$^-$/$μ$m$^2$. The minimal damage from color center generation mitigates spectral inhomogeneity and allows spectral resolution of isotopic clusters, with the best cluster having an inhomogeneous distribution of 2.14 GHz and an average linewidth of $47 \pm 11$ MHz. Our study gives insight into formation dynamics of GeV$^-$ in delta-doped systems and estimates our vacancy to GeV$^-$ yield to be $\geq$ 90%, establishing an athermal nearest-neighbor formation model. We further characterized the spin $T_2^*$ and set a lower bound of 730 ns, limited by a paramagnetic defect.

</details>


### [183] [Memory--Transport Competition in Physical Two-Bath Quantum Reset Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30457)
*Jishad Kumar,Achilleas Lazarides,Tapio Ala-Nissila*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作指出，一个看似自然、能精确替换热浴（bath）占据数并保留系统-浴相干性的协议，并不必然对应物理的费米子态；作者改为构造双库（two-reservoir）协议，在二次型动力学（quadratic dynamics）下将频闪问题化为精确矩阵不动点方程，获得无需弱耦合或马尔可夫近似（Markov approximation）的相干操控与输运结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干性保持（coherence preservation）与环境更新（environmental renewal）是控制量子存储与输运的两种竞争性途径，但二者的物理可实现性及其对粒子输送与能量交换的约束关系尚不清晰，需要一个微观且物理允许的基准模型。

Method: 构造含两个独立、保持粒子数（number-conserving）的热衰减器（thermal attenuators）的双库协议，使其完全正性（complete positivity）与费米子可容许性（fermionic admissibility）由构造保证；对二次型动力学，将频闪问题转化为精确的矩阵不动点方程，不做弱耦合或马尔可夫近似。

Result: 物理衰减必然同时改变热浴占据数与交叉关联（cross correlations），相干控制并非独立的硬重置旋钮；相干保留提升短周期主导传输速率（含完全擦除极限），且不存在一般的端点归零定理。精确的稳态连续性定理表明，只要任一热浴完全未刷新，穿透电流（through-current）必然消失，但完全擦除时无类似抑制结论。偏置结构热浴的有限链精确计算显示相干、电流与带符号能量转移（signed energy-transfer）具有不同工作点，并出现分辨出的高保留边界层（boundary layer）与显著的有限尺寸敏感性。

Conclusion: 该模型为热浴相干性、粒子输运与能量交换之间的相互作用提供了一个微观、费米子可容许的基准（benchmark），并揭示相干保持与热浴更新之间存在不可忽略的物理耦合约束。

Abstract: Coherence preservation and environmental renewal offer competing ways of controlling quantum memory and transport. We show that a seemingly natural protocol which exactly replaces the bath occupations while preserving arbitrary system--bath coherences need not correspond to a physical fermionic state. We instead construct a two-reservoir protocol with independent, number-conserving thermal attenuators, for which complete positivity and fermionic admissibility hold by construction. Physical attenuation necessarily modifies bath populations as well as cross correlations; the coherence control is not an independent hard-reset knob. Quadratic dynamics reduces the stroboscopic problem to an exact matrix fixed-point equation without a weak-coupling or Markov approximation. Analytically, coherence retention enhances the leading short-cycle transfer rate, including the complete-erasure limit; no general endpoint-vanishing theorem exists. An exact stationary continuity theorem forces the through-current to vanish whenever either reservoir is left entirely unrefreshed, although no analogous theorem suppresses it at complete erasure. For biased structured reservoirs, exact finite-chain calculations display distinct coherence-, current-, and signed energy-transfer operating points; a resolved high-retention boundary layer exposes strong finite-size sensitivity of the coherence peak. The model provides a microscopic, fermion-admissible benchmark for the interplay of reservoir coherence, particle transport, and energetic exchange.

</details>


### [184] [Probing quasiperiodic spectral accessibility with a finite-rate quantum Otto cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30555)
*Shujie Cheng,Lucas C. Céleri,Norton G. de Almeida,Gao Xianlong,G. D. de Moraes Neto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 有限速率量子奥托循环（quantum Otto cycle）可作为热力学探针，揭示准周期系统（quasiperiodic systems）中哪些谱区在驱动过程中变得动力学可达且热力学相关；功涨落的统计不由静态准周期相区单独决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期系统兼具差异显著的谱与局域化（localisation）机制以及密集的动力学时间尺度层级，现有工作缺乏对其谱可达性的涨落分辨热力学刻画，需探索驱动过程与静态谱结构的关联。

Method: 以广义 Aubry-André-Harper 链为对象，研究有限速率量子奥托循环，分析扩展、近临界与交叉协议下的功涨落统计，并引入热加权谱敏感度（thermally weighted spectral susceptibility）与有限热接触下的有限尺寸分析。

Result: 扩展、近临界与交叉协议之间功涨落不存在普适排序；统计由动力学耦合、热占据与能量显著的跃迁路径共同决定。平均功可转正而布居转移仍强非绝热，因转移被重分配至能量代价更低的通道；此外存在有限速率窗口使正功循环占多数而平均功仍为负，该行为在有限尺寸与有限热接触下保持。

Conclusion: 量子奥托循环提供涨落分辨的准周期谱可达性热力学探针，其效应源自动力学活跃谱区的分布式层级而非单一最小能隙瓶颈，可与直接谱学与本征态分辨诊断互补。

Abstract: Quasiperiodic systems combine sharply different spectral and localisation regimes with a dense hierarchy of dynamical time scales. We show that a finite-rate quantum Otto cycle can use this structure as a thermodynamic probe, revealing which parts of the spectrum become dynamically accessible and thermodynamically relevant during a driven transformation. For a generalised Aubry-André-Harper chain, the resulting response is not determined by the static quasiperiodic regime alone: extended, near-critical, and crossing protocols do not exhibit universal ordering of work fluctuations. Instead, the statistics reflect transition pathways that are simultaneously dynamically coupled, thermally occupied, and energetically significant. The same spectral accessibility separates thermodynamic recovery from microscopic adiabatic recovery. Positive mean work can reappear while the population transfer dynamics remain strongly nonadiabatic because finite-rate population transfer is redistributed toward energetically less costly channels rather than suppressed. The full work distribution reveals a further separation, with a finite rate window in which positive-work cycles form a majority although the mean work remains negative. This behaviour persists in finite-size analysis. A thermally weighted spectral susceptibility connects these effects to a distributed hierarchy of dynamically active spectral regions rather than to a single minimum-gap bottleneck. Finite thermal contacts preserve this picture while introducing a second time scale and intercycle memory. Quantum Otto cycles can therefore provide a fluctuation-resolved thermodynamic probe of quasiperiodic spectral accessibility, complementary to direct spectroscopic and eigenstate-resolved diagnostics.

</details>


### [185] [Time-reversal-based quantum metrology in a central-spin architecture: from SATIN to butterfly echo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30686)
*Aditya Kumar,Sayan Choudhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究中心自旋模型（central spin model）下基于时间反演的量子增强计量（quantum-enhanced metrology），证明SATIN回波协议可达Fisher信息 $N^2/e$，并可通过蝶形回波（butterfly echo）实现与轴无关的海森标度（Heisenberg scaling）传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用星图拓扑（star-graph topology）的中心自旋架构实现量子增强的时间反演计量，并解决编码轴未知时的传感问题，同时寻找可调控最优协议时间尺度的参数。

Method: 在单个大小为 $S_{\rm cen}$ 的辅助自旋（ancilla spin）介导 $N$ 个浴自旋（bath spins）的模型中实施SATIN（Signal Amplification Through Time-reversal Interaction）回波协议；并通过在每步演化间共轭随机集体浴旋转（random collective bath rotation），实现蝶形回波协议（butterfly echo protocol）。

Result: 采用集体 $\langle J_y\rangle$ 读出时，SATIN回波给出Fisher信息 $N^2/e$，距海森极限（Heisenberg limit）4.34 dB；蝶形回波在任意未知编码轴下、以少量与 $N$ 无关的随机步数达到Fisher信息 $N^2/4$（低于海森极限6 dB），其平台增益（plateau gain）追踪Haar极限。

Conclusion: 中心自旋大小 $S_{\rm cen}$ 可作为调节旋钮，在不改变计量上限或达到各向同性（isotropy）所需线路深度（circuit depth）的前提下缩短最优协议时间尺度；该方案适用于腔QED与NMR量子处理器等中心自旋架构自然出现的平台。

Abstract: We investigate quantum-enhanced time-reversal-based metrology using the central spin model, in which a single ancilla spin of magnitude $S_{\rm cen}$ mediates interactions between $N$ bath spins through a star-graph topology. We demonstrate that the SATIN (Signal Amplification Through Time-reversal Interaction) echo protocol can be implemented in this setup to achieve a Fisher information of $N^2/e$ with the collective $\langle J_y\rangle$ readout, corresponding to a $4.34\,\mathrm{dB}$ offset from the Heisenberg limit. Furthermore, we establish that this platform provides a pathway to perform the butterfly echo protocol by conjugating each evolution step with a random collective bath rotation, thereby achieving axis-agnostic Heisenberg-scaled sensing. Crucially, with a small $N-$independent number of random steps, the butterfly echo reaches a Fisher information of $N^2/4$ ($6\,\mathrm{dB}$ below the Heisenberg limit) for any unknown encoding axis, with the plateau gain tracking the Haar limit. Finally, we demonstrate that the central-spin magnitude $S_{\rm cen}$ provides a powerful knob to reduce the optimal protocol timescale without altering the metrological ceiling or the circuit depth required to reach isotropy. The proposed scheme is relevant to cavity QED and NMR quantum processors, where the central spin architecture arises naturally.

</details>


### [186] [Scale-free localization in pseudo-Hermitian quantum walk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31067)
*Haiting Zhang,Lei Xiao,Cui-Xian Guo,Quan Lin,Peng Xue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在一维量子行走（quantum walks, QWs）中引入单个非厄米缺陷（non-Hermitian defect），可诱导出无标度局域化（scale-free localization），并伴随赝厄米（pseudo-Hermitian, PH）相变与第二相变，二维情形亦成立，显示该现象的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标度局域化是非厄米系统（non-Hermitian systems）中的独特现象，其局域长度随系统尺寸线性标度；本文旨在探究单个非厄米缺陷是否足以驱动该现象，并借此深化对缺陷物理与新型局域态（localized states, LSs）的理解。

Method: 以一维量子行走（quantum walks, QWs）为平台，引入单个非厄米缺陷，考察非厄米性增强过程中的谱与局域性质演化；并利用局域化性质（localization properties）确定相变临界点，随后推广至二维量子行走。

Result: 随非厄米性增强，系统先发生赝厄米（PH）相变，PH对称性破缺伴随无标度局域化的出现；非厄米性进一步增大时发生第二次相变，标志为两对束缚态（bound states）的出现；两类相变的临界点均可由局域化性质识别。二维量子行走中单个非厄米缺陷同样诱导无标度局域化。

Conclusion: 单个非厄米缺陷即可普适地诱导无标度局域化，为缺陷物理研究及非厄米量子系统中新型局域态的探索提供了有益视角。

Abstract: Scale-free localization is a distinctive phenomenon in non-Hermitian systems, where specific eigenstates, known as scale-free localized states (LSs), possess localization lengths that scale linearly with the system size. Here, we show that a single non-Hermitian defect in one-dimensional quantum walks (QWs) induces scale-free localization. Driven by non-Hermiticity, the QW system undergoes a pseudo-Hermitian (PH) phase transition and the PH symmetry breaking is accompanied by the emergence of scale-free localization. As non-Hermiticity is further increased, the system undergoes a second phase transition, marked by the emergence of two pairs of bound states. Crucially, we identify the critical points of both transitions using localization properties. Furthermore, we extend our investigation to two-dimensional QWs, where a single non-Hermitian defect also induces scale-free localization, thereby demonstrating the universality of this phenomenon. Our work provides a valuable perspective for further exploring defect physics and a type of novel LS in non-Hermitian quantum systems.

</details>


### [187] [Fluctuation-free Quantum Battery Charging Enabled by Bell-like Entangled and Separable Charger States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31242)
*Longyan Gong,Jing Zhang,Yongtao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究级联碰撞框架下三量子比特量子电池（quantum battery）的充电一致性问题，表明通过对充电器（charger）施加自适应测量（adaptive measurement），可在特定纠缠态与可分态下实现零涨落的均匀充电。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠（entanglement）在量子电池充电中的作用尚不清晰，且量子内禀随机性（intrinsic quantum randomness）使不同试验间的充电一致性面临根本挑战，故需探究二者关系并寻求均匀充电方案。

Method: 构建三量子比特系统于级联碰撞框架（cascaded collision frame）中，两比特作充电器、第三比特作电池；每次碰撞后对充电器施加自适应测量，得到四个期望储能相同的条件测量后电池态，并对任意固定充电器态推导储能的递推关系解析式。

Result: 储能涨落（stored-energy fluctuations）是否出现取决于动力学是否依赖电池相位（battery phase）γ_n；对两类类贝尔纠缠态族及其计算基态（类贝尔可分态），动力学与 γ_n 无关且涨落为零，而其他纠缠态与可分态则出现涨落。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了通过工程设计充电器态来构建均匀充电量子电池架构的范式，可在量子随机性存在下抑制充电涨落。

Abstract: The implication of entanglement in quantum battery charging remains poorly understood. In addition, intrinsic quantum randomness poses a fundamental challenge to charging uniformity across distinct trials. To address these issues, we study a three-qubit system within a cascaded collision frame, with two qubits acting as the charger and the third serving as the battery. After each collision, an adaptive measurement is performed on the charger, yielding four conditional post-measurement battery states with identical expected stored energy. For arbitrary fixed charger states, we derive analytical expressions for the recurrence relation of stored energy. We find that stored-energy fluctuations depend on whether the dynamics relies on the battery phase \(γ_n\). For two Bell-like families of entangled states, together with their computational basis states termed Bell-like separable states, the dynamics is \(γ_n\)-independent and fluctuations vanish, whereas for other entangled and separable states, fluctuations emerge. These insights establish a paradigm for engineering charger states to design uniform-charging quantum-battery architectures despite inherent quantum randomness.

</details>


### [188] [Quantum Fokker-Planck solvers as trainable generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31330)
*Tariq Mahassen,Marcin Jastrzebski,Oleksandr Kyriienko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于随机微分方程（stochastic differential equations）量子求解器的生成模型，通过参数化朗之万过程（Langevin process）的势函数而非变分量子线路来实现生成学习，并给出电路构造与资源分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子生成模型多依赖含参数化旋转的层状变分量子线路（layered variational quantum circuits），该文希望探索一条不同路径，将量子生成学习重新表述为量子模型发现（quantum model discovery），并为高维强关联科学数据的扩展奠定基础。

Method: 参数化朗之万过程的势函数；将相应的 Fokker-Planck（FP）方程厄米化（Hermitianized），利用量子奇异值变换（quantum singular value transformation）实现余弦算符的线性组合进行求解；通过编码平稳密度的平方根，使模型可由计算基测量采样，并由块编码（block-encoded）对角势的系数高效控制。

Result: 模型被训练以匹配 FP 方程族所描述的经验分布，实现了多变量概率分布的参数恢复（parameter recovery）；模型在大规模下仍可训练，且对分布内目标（in-distribution targets）具有样本高效（sample-efficient）与凸优化特性，该性质源于物理启发的归纳偏置（physics-informed inductive bias）且与问题维度无关。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了算法主要组件的电路构造与高效块编码，并分析了资源需求；将量子生成学习框定为量子模型发现，为把该框架推广至高维、强关联科学数据提供了基础。

Abstract: We develop and analyze generative models based on quantum solvers of stochastic differential equations. Unlike the common approach based on layered variational quantum circuits with parameterized rotations, we parameterize the potential of a Langevin process. The associated Fokker-Planck (FP) equations are Hermitianized and solved using a linear combination of cosine operators implemented through quantum singular value transformation. By encoding the square root of the stationary density, the model enables sampling through computational-basis measurements and is efficiently controlled by coefficients of the block-encoded diagonal potential. We train the proposed model to match empirical distributions within the family described by FP equations, demonstrating parameter recovery for multivariate probability distributions. Crucially, the model remains trainable at scale. Sample-efficient and convex optimization for in-distribution targets arises from a physics-informed inductive bias and holds regardless of the dimensionality of the problem. We provide circuit constructions and efficient block encodings of the main components of the algorithm and analyze resource requirements. Our approach frames quantum generative learning as quantum model discovery and provides a foundation for extending this framework to high-dimensional, strongly correlated scientific data.

</details>


### [189] [Mixed State Deep Thermalization under Generic Quantum Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31580)
*Xie-Hang Yu,Pavel Kos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究含噪量子动力学中的混合态深度热化（mixed-state deep thermalization），证明晚期混合态投影系综（projected ensemble）完全由误差信道的归一化Choi态决定且具有普适性，与底层幺正动力学的微观细节无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态深度热化预言投影系综趋向包含超越约化密度矩阵信息的普适分布，但现实量子系统中噪声不可避免、投影态通常为混合态，混合态深度热化是否存在类似的普适极限分布此前尚不清楚。

Method: 针对含局域量子信道（即误差）的量子动力学，解析地在对偶幺正电路（dual-unitary circuits）、全局Haar随机动力学及大局部维度下的局域Haar随机电路中建立结论，并在量子比特电路与踢Ising动力学（kicked Ising dynamics）中进行数值验证；另在一个可解设定下分析收敛速度。

Result: 晚期混合态投影系综由误差信道的归一化Choi态完全决定并呈普适性，不依赖底层幺正动力学的微观细节；在可解设定中进一步发现向极限系综的指数级快速收敛。

Conclusion: 研究将深度热化推广至含噪量子动力学，并建立了量子误差与普适随机混合态系综之间的直接对应关系。

Abstract: Pure-state deep thermalization predicts that, after sufficient scrambling, projected ensembles approach universal distributions that contain information beyond the reduced density matrix. In realistic quantum systems, however, noise is unavoidable and the projected states are generally mixed. Whether mixed-state deep thermalization admits an analogous universal limiting distribution has remained unclear. Here we answer this question for quantum dynamics containing local quantum channels, i.e., errors. We show that the late-time mixed-state projected ensemble is completely determined by the normalized Choi state of the error channel and is universal, independent of the microscopic details of the underlying unitary dynamics. We establish this result analytically for dual-unitary circuits, global Haar-random dynamics, and local Haar-random circuits at large local dimension, and verify it numerically in qubit circuits and kicked Ising dynamics. In a solvable setting, we further find exponentially fast convergence to the limiting ensemble. Our results extend deep thermalization to noisy quantum dynamics and establish a direct correspondence between quantum errors and universal random mixed-state ensembles.

</details>


### [190] [Screening strongly correlated materials in solid oxide cells with quantum simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31591)
*Ouns El Harzli,Jelle Bor,Linde Wester,Leonid Zhukov,Hanl Park,Jean-François Bobier,Matt Langione,Antonino Marco Saitta,Jonathan Owens,Richard Hart,Yudong Cao,François Jamet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个面向工业材料科学的端到端（end-to-end）框架，以固体氧化物燃料电池/电解池（SOFCs/SOECs）强关联氧电极材料的筛选为示范应用，贯通从界面微观可观测量、嵌入方法计算、量子杂质求解器到宏观工程指标与商业价值的完整链条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联氧电极材料的筛选缺乏将微观电子结构与宏观器件性能及经济价值连通的系统性评估手段；同时，随相互作用活性空间（active space）增大，经典求解器面临计算瓶颈，而量子哈密顿量模拟（Hamiltonian simulation）对高效表示的离散模型具有多项式标度（polynomial scaling），为量子优势（quantum advantage）提供潜在机会。

Method: 以氧空位形成能（oxygen-vacancy formation energies）、空位迁移势垒（vacancy migration barriers）与电子电导率（electronic conductivity）等界面可观测量表述材料问题；在DFT初步筛选的候选清单上，采用基于图解GW的嵌入方法（GW+DMFT或自能嵌入理论SEET）获取这些量，并由量子杂质求解器（quantum impurity solvers）提供有限温度杂质格林函数。

Result: 提出了评估量子优势所需的基准（benchmarks），包括空位与迁移能量学的收敛性验证以及与最佳可用经典求解器的对比；给出示意性逻辑量子比特（logical qubit）数量，并识别热态制备（thermal state preparation）、模拟、测量与重复求解等完整资源评估所需的成本项。同时通过混合离子-电子导电氧电极的连续介质模型（continuum model），将微观可观测量与宏观工程指标关联。

Conclusion: 进一步基于公开技术目标与电解槽部署数据（electrolyser deployment data），估算了在SOFC与SOEC部署情景下改进电极材料的潜在全球商业价值（business value），表明该框架可作为量子计算在工业材料科学中应用可行性的系统评估范式。

Abstract: We develop an end-to-end framework for assessing a quantum-computing application in industrial materials science: the screening of strongly correlated oxygen-electrode materials for SOFCs and SOECs. We formulate the materials problem in terms of interface observables including oxygen-vacancy formation energies, vacancy migration barriers, and electronic conductivity. We describe how these observables can be obtained using a diagrammatic GW-based embedding, either GW+DMFT or self-energy embedding theory (SEET), applied to a DFT-screened shortlist. The atomistic electrode/electrolyte interface defines the electronic structure problem from which the correlated impurity models are constructed. Quantum impurity solvers would provide the finite-temperature impurity Green's functions required by the embedding. The computational challenges that classical solvers may encounter as the interacting active space grows, together with the polynomial scaling of quantum Hamiltonian simulation for efficiently represented discretized models, open an opportunity for quantum advantage. We identify the benchmarks needed to assess this opportunity through convergence of vacancy and migration energetics and comparison with the best applicable classical solvers. We provide illustrative logical qubit counts and identify the thermal state preparation, simulation, measurement, and repeated-solve costs needed for a complete resource assessment. We connect microscopic observables to macroscopic engineering metrics using a continuum model for mixed ionic--electronic conducting oxygen electrodes that couples oxygen surface exchange, solid-state oxygen transport, electrode microstructure, and finite electrode thickness. Finally, we estimate the potential worldwide business value of improved electrode materials under SOFC and SOEC deployment scenarios informed by public technical targets and electrolyser deployment data.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Constraints on interacting dark energy with a constant equation of state in light of DESI DR2: The role of the interaction term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30784)
*Zhao-Yu Li,Tian-Nuo Li,Guo-Hong Du,Sheng-Han Zhou,Yun-He Li,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 利用Planck/ACT/SPT CMB、DESI DR2 BAO及多组Ia型超新星数据检验四类常数状态方程（EoS）的相互作用暗能量（IDE）模型，发现含Q∝ρ_de的模型更受偏好，其中Q=βH_0ρ_de模型对非零相互作用与w≠-1的偏离分别达3.2σ和3.0σ，且贝叶斯证据优于ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI的BAO分析暗示暗能量状态方程（EoS）随红移演化并穿越幻觉分界（phantom divide）w=-1；暗能量与暗物质之间的相互作用同样可在有效EoS中产生表观的穿越行为，因此需检验相互作用暗能量（IDE）模型能否解释当前观测偏好。

Method: 在常数EoS参数w下构建IDE模型，考虑四种典型相互作用项Q形式，联合使用Planck、ACT、SPT的CMB观测、DESI DR2 BAO测量，以及PantheonPlus、DES-Dovekie、DESY5的Ia型超新星数据进行分析，并采用贝叶斯证据（Bayesian evidence）进行模型比较。

Result: Q∝ρ_de类IDE模型普遍对非零暗区相互作用表现出更强偏好；在CMB+DESI+DES-Dovekie数据下，Q=βH_0ρ_de模型给出β=-2.52^{+0.80}_{-0.54}、w=-1.510^{+0.170}_{-0.120}，分别对应β=0与w=-1的3.2σ和3.0σ偏离，且其有效EoS呈现类似w_0w_aCDM模型的红移演化并穿越幻觉分界。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯证据表明该IDE模型优于ΛCDM及其余三种IDE模型；分析有助于判别哪类相互作用形式更能解释当前宇宙学观测。

Abstract: Recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) analyses suggest a preference for an evolving dark energy equation of state (EoS) that crosses the phantom divide $w=-1$. Interactions between dark energy and dark matter can also produce an apparent phantom-divide crossing in the effective dark energy EoS. In this work, we investigate interacting dark energy (IDE) models with a constant EoS parameter $w$, using cosmic microwave background (CMB) observations from Planck, ACT, and SPT, DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and type Ia supernova data from PantheonPlus, DES-Dovekie, and DESY5. We consider four typical forms of the interaction term $Q$. Our results show that the IDE models with $Q\proptoρ_{\rm de}$ generally exhibit a stronger preference for nonzero dark-sector interactions. In particular, for the CMB+DESI+DES-Dovekie data, the IDE model with $Q=βH_0ρ_{\rm de}$ yields $β=-2.52^{+0.80}_{-0.54}$ and $w=-1.510^{+0.170}_{-0.120}$, corresponding to deviations from $β=0$ and $w=-1$ at the $3.2σ$ and $3.0σ$ levels, respectively. Interestingly, the effective EoS of the IDE model with $Q=βH_0ρ_{\rm de}$ exhibits a redshift evolution resembling that of the $w_0w_a$CDM model, crossing the phantom divide. Bayesian evidence further indicates that this IDE model is favored over $Λ$CDM and the other three IDE models. Our analysis helps identify which interaction forms better account for current cosmological observations.

</details>


### [192] [Assessing the consistency of the CPL dark-energy model with cosmological data: Simulation-calibrated neural posterior disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31044)
*Tian-Yang Sun,Tian-Nuo Li,Guo-Hong Du,He Wang,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经后验估计（neural posterior estimation）与流匹配后验估计（flow-matching posterior estimation）后验差异的模型检验（model check）方法，并用独立模拟校准得到全局 p 值；应用于压缩 CMB+DESI+DESY5 数据得 p_global=0.524，表明观测与 CPL 参数化一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 DESI 观测暗示在 Chevallier-Polarski-Linder（CPL）参数化下偏好穿越幽灵分界（phantom divide）的动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy），因此需要评估 CPL 参数化与当前宇宙学观测的一致性，并发展高效的暗能量模型充分性检验工具。

Method: 构建基于两种估计器（神经后验估计与流匹配后验估计）在同一 CPL 模拟上训练后后验分歧的模型检验；用独立模拟校准该分歧以获得全局 p 值（global p-value），并通过受控模拟评估校准 p 值的可靠性与检测灵敏度对偏离模型形式的依赖。

Result: 受控模拟验证了校准 p 值的可靠性；应用于压缩 CMB+DESI+DESY5 数据向量得到 p_global=0.524，当前观测与 CPL 模型一致；方法的中位评估时间仅 0.53 s。

Conclusion: 该框架可高效检验暗能量模型，为探究宇宙加速膨胀（cosmic acceleration）的物理起源提供更可靠的基础。

Abstract: Recent DESI observations hint at a preference for dynamical dark energy with an equation of state that crosses the phantom divide within the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) parameterization. In this work, we assess the consistency of the CPL parameterization with current cosmological observations. We develop a model check based on the disagreement between posteriors inferred by neural posterior estimation and flow-matching posterior estimation. Both estimators are trained on the same CPL simulations, and their posterior disagreement is calibrated using independent simulations to obtain a global $p$-value. Controlled simulations assess the reliability of the calibrated \(p\)-values and show how detection sensitivity depends on the form of departure from the assumed model. Applying the test to the compressed CMB+DESI+DESY5 data vector yields $p_{\rm global}=0.524$, indicating that the current observations are consistent with the CPL model. The method has a median evaluation time of only $0.53\,{\rm s}$, enabling efficient assessments of cosmological model adequacy. This framework enables efficient tests of dark-energy models, providing a firmer basis for investigating the physical origin of cosmic acceleration.

</details>


### [193] [Cosmological Inference with Gravitational-Wave Dark Sirens and 21-cm Intensity Mapping: Forecasts for LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA with MeerKAT and SKAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31081)
*Dounia Nanadoumgar-Lacroze,Konstantin Leyde,Steven Cunnington,Tessa Baker,Ian Harrison,Ulyana Dupletsa,Simone Mastrogiovanni,Riccardo Murgia,Tommaso Ronconi,Matteo Schulz,Marta Spinelli*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于标准汽笛（standard siren）框架，联合约150个模拟引力波（gravitational waves, GW）探测与21厘米强度映射（intensity mapping, IM）模拟数据，研究两者协同对哈勃常数（Hubble constant, H0）的约束能力；结果显示引力波参数测量不确定度是主导限制因素，纳入HI信息可使H0不确定度相对谱汽笛（spectral siren）结果降低53%–61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波提供直接的光度距离测量，中性氢（neutral hydrogen, HI）强度映射在红移空间追踪物质分布，二者具有互补性，有望用于精确测量哈勃常数；本文旨在评估观测限制对联合约束的实际影响。

Method: 采用标准汽笛框架开展联合宇宙学与种群分析（joint cosmological and population analysis），使用LVK O5灵敏度下一年观测预期的约150个信噪比（SNR）大于12的模拟GW探测，并结合MeerKLASS与SKA-Mid的模拟HI强度图，系统考察GW与IM观测效应（波束卷积、仪器噪声、前景清除、部分天区覆盖等）对H0约束的影响。

Result: GW参数测量不确定度是宇宙学约束的主导限制；若忽略该不确定度，HI信息的加入可实现亚百分比的H0约束。考虑HI观测效应后约束变弱，但仍优于谱汽笛结果：MeerKLASS降低H0不确定度61%，SKA-Mid降低53%。波束卷积对约束削弱最严重；仪器噪声可导致后验多峰性（multimodality）并可能引入偏差；结果对前景欠清除与过清除敏感，部分天区覆盖影响相对较小。最现实情景下H0精度估计分别为10%（MeerKLASS）与15%（SKA-Mid）。

Conclusion: GW与HI强度映射的联合分析在现实观测限制下仍能显著改善H0约束，验证了协同观测的价值；但由于结果基于单一实现（single realisation），需考虑实现间变异性（realisation-to-realisation variability），且测量精度仍受GW参数不确定度与IM观测效应制约。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) and 21-cm intensity mapping (IM) are emerging as complementary cosmological probes: GWs provide direct luminosity-distance measurements, while neutral-hydrogen (HI) IM traces the matter distribution in redshift space. Their synergy offers a promising avenue for precise measurements of the Hubble constant. Within a standard-siren framework, we perform a joint cosmological and population analysis using $\mathcal{O}(150)$ mock GW detections with SNR > 12, as projected for one year of observations at LVK O5 sensitivity. We combine these with mock HI intensity maps from MeerKLASS and SKA-Mid, and study how observational limitations in both the GW and IM data affect the $H_0$ constraints. We find that uncertainty in GW parameter measurements is a dominant limitation on the cosmological constraints. Neglecting this uncertainty, the inclusion of HI information enables sub-percent constraints on $H_0$. We also find that observational effects in the HI surveys weaken the constraints, although they remain tighter than those obtained without HI information (spectral sirens). Despite accounting for HI IM observational limitations, including HI information reduces the $H_0$ uncertainty relative to the spectral-siren results by 61% for MeerKLASS and 53% for SKA-Mid. Because our results are based on a single realisation, they should be interpreted in light of possible realisation-to-realisation variability. Beam convolution most severely weakens the constraints. Instrumental noise can induce multimodality in the posterior, potentially biasing the inferred parameters. The results are sensitive to foreground under-cleaning and over-cleaning. Partial sky coverage has a relatively small impact. In the most realistic scenario, which accounts for both GW and HI IM limitations, we obtain $H_0$ precision estimates of 10% and 15% for MeerKLASS and SKA-Mid, respectively.

</details>


### [194] [Gravitational Wave Diffractive Images Reveal the Structure of Astrophysical Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31441)
*Han Gil Choi,Sunghoon Jung,Volodymyr Takhistov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本工作在线性阶引力透镜势下建立引力波衍射与透镜投影密度傅里叶变换的对应关系，并提出用质量形状因子刻画延展透镜的衍射贡献，进而量化下一代探测器对延展暗物质、晕着装原初黑洞及超辐射云的可观测分辨能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电粒子在原子核上的散射表明散射图样可编码延展物体结构；引力波衍射（gravitational wave diffraction）则有望成为探测不可见质量分布的类似手段，因此需要理论框架将延展透镜的结构信息与引力波波形联系起来。

Method: 在线性阶透镜势（lens potential）下推导中心衍射像贡献与投影透镜密度傅里叶变换（Fourier transform）的关系；对有限质量透镜，该贡献表示为点质量玻恩振幅（Born amplitude）乘以归一化质量形状因子（mass form factor），并采用完整数值波动光学（wave optics）计算评估可观测性。

Result: 合并致密双星引力波频率演化会使信号在空间尺度上采样该形状因子，并通过与主像的干涉留下透镜结构印记。计算表明，五年下一代探测器观测可将延展透镜与点质量区分，探测暗物质占比低至 O(10^-5)；可区分晕着装原初黑洞与裸露黑洞，探测原初黑洞暗物质占比低至 O(10^-6)；在足够近的源-透镜构型下，标量超辐射云（superradiance cloud）对约 10^-17–10^-12 eV 的玻色子质量产生可区分衍射特征。

Conclusion: 结果为刻画延展暗物质及致密天体天体物理环境提供了基于引力波衍射的框架，并可借助质量形状因子的干涉信号实现结构分辨。

Abstract: Scattering patterns encode the structure of extended objects, as demonstrated by charged particle scattering on atomic nuclei. Gravitational wave (GW) diffraction offers an analogous probe of otherwise invisible mass distributions. At linear order in the lens potential we derive expressions relating the central diffractive image contribution to a gravitationally lensed GW waveform to the Fourier transform of the projected lens density. For finite mass lenses this contribution takes the form of a point mass Born amplitude multiplied by a normalized mass form factor, analogous to that in nuclear scattering. As the frequency of GWs from a merging compact binary source evolves the signal samples this form factor across spatial scales, imprinting lens structure through interference with the primary image. Using full numerical wave optics calculations we quantify the observational reach for extended dark matter objects, primordial black holes halo-dressed by dark matter halos and superradiance clouds of ultralight bosons around black holes. For the considered source populations, five year observations with next generation detectors can distinguish extended lenses from point masses, probing dark matter fractions down to $\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$, and halo-dressed primordial black holes from matched bare black holes, probing primordial black hole dark matter fractions down to $\mathcal{O}(10^{-6})$. For sufficiently close source-lens configurations, scalar superradiance clouds can produce distinguishable diffraction signatures for boson masses of approximately $10^{-17}-10^{-12}~\mathrm{eV}$. These results provide a framework for characterizing extended dark matter and compact object astrophysical environments.

</details>


### [195] [ULTRAMAN: A Proposed ultrasound detector for macroscopic dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30507)
*Oem Trivedi,Robert J. Scherrer,Alfredo Gurrola*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: ULTRAMAN 提出一种被动式超声阵列实验，利用宏观暗物质（macroscopic dark matter, macro）高速穿越靶介质时产生的相干声学扰动来重建其超音速轨迹，从而在陆地实现事件级（event-level）的宏观暗物质搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗物质探测器多依赖闪烁（scintillation）、电离（ionization）等能量沉积信号，缺乏针对宏观暗物质整体穿越轨迹的直接探测手段；本文旨在利用声学响应开辟互补的探测通道。

Method: 采用多单元同步超声阵列（multielement ultrasound arrays）被动监听靶介质，将 macro 的停止本领（stopping power）与相互作用截面同产生的声压相联系，并以预期事件率约束 macro 的质量与丰度；通过多通道相关信号重建单个高超音速穿越轨迹。

Result: 真实候选事件表现为与单一高超音速穿越一致的多通道相关径迹（correlated multichannel track），而非孤立的射频尖峰（radio frequency spike）；macro、核微子（nuclearite）、轴子夸克团（axion quark nugget）与 primordial black hole（原初黑洞）等基准模型在声学可观测量空间中占据不同区域。

Conclusion: 现有超声技术为宏观暗物质的陆地事件级搜寻提供了现实可行路径，零观测结果（null observations）可转化为对其参数空间的互补约束。

Abstract: We propose ULTRAMAN (ULTRAsound Macro ANalyzer), a passive ultrasound array experiment designed to search for macroscopic dark matter (macros) through coherent acoustic disturbances generated as compact objects traverse a controlled target medium. Unlike existing dark matter detectors that primarily rely on scintillation, ionization, or other signatures of energy deposition, ULTRAMAN exploits the acoustic response of matter to reconstruct individual highly supersonic trajectories using synchronized multielement ultrasound arrays. The framework connects macro stopping power and interaction cross section to the resulting acoustic pressure, while the expected event rate probes the macro mass and abundance. A genuine candidate would appear not as an isolated radio frequency spike, but as a correlated multichannel track consistent with a single highly supersonic passage through the target. Representative macro, nuclearite, axion quark nugget, and primordial black hole benchmarks are shown to occupy distinct regions of the resulting acoustic observable space. Existing ultrasound technology offers a realistic route to an event-level terrestrial search for macroscopic dark matter, with null observations translating into complementary constraints on its parameter space.

</details>

# physics.data-an [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Track finding and particle identification performance with particle physics foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31180)
*Shuhang Li,Haiwang Yu,Gabor Galgoczi,Yeonju Go,Yi Huang,David Park,Michael Peters,Christof Roland,Jin Huang,Yihui Ren,Joseph D. Osborn*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该摘要报告了将基础模型（Foundation Model）应用于高能核与粒子物理稀疏数据处理，并在时间投影室（Time Projection Chamber）空间点数据集上验证其在带电粒子径迹重建等下游任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高能核与粒子物理实验产生大量稀疏数据，需在物理分析前大幅缩减数据规模；近期出现评估基础模型作为数据分析工具的尝试，因其可从多模态训练数据中学习高维数据集的特征，故有必要检验其在复杂模式识别任务中的有效性。

Method: 基于基础模型构建重建方法，并在由时间投影室（Time Projection Chamber）空间点（spacepoints）组成的数据集上，针对多个下游任务（downstream tasks）评估其性能，任务包括磁场中带电粒子的重建。

Result: 基础模型驱动的数据分析性能显著优于传统方法（traditional methods），且能够通过适配学习与初始训练目标未必相关的额外任务。

Conclusion: 基础模型可作为高能物理稀疏数据高效分析与重建的有效工具，并在多任务适配上具备灵活性；摘要未提供具体量化指标。

Abstract: Modern high energy nuclear and particle physics experiments produce large amounts of sparse data that must be reduced significantly in size before performing physics analysis. Recently, there has been an effort to evaluate Foundation Models as a potential tool for performing data analysis. These models have been shown to learn features in high dimensional datasets based on the multi-modal data they are trained on. To show their effectiveness at performing complex pattern recognition tasks, such as reconstructing charged particles in a magnetic field, we evaluated the performance of a Foundation Model based reconstruction on several downstream tasks with a dataset composed of spacepoints in a Time Projection Chamber. We show that the performance of the Foundation Model based data analysis significantly improves upon that of traditional methods, and furthermore can be adapted to learn additional tasks that are not necessarily related to the initial training objective.

</details>

### [197] [The Critical 2D KPZ Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31614)
*Quentin Berger,Francesco Caravenna,Jeremy Clark,Alexander Dunlap,Yu Gu,Ziyang Liu,Shuta Nakajima,Rongfeng Sun,Li-Cheng Tsai,Zuodi Xie,Nikos Zygouras*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文构造了临界二维 Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）方程的一个非平凡解，称其为临界二维 KPZ 过程（Critical 2D KPZ Process），它是临界二维高斯有向聚合物中心化对数配分函数的分布极限，并通过重整化 Cole-Hopf 变换成为临界二维随机热流（Critical 2D Stochastic Heat Flow）的可测泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维临界 KPZ 方程本身定义不良（ill-defined），因此需要给出一个有严格意义的解对象，以刻画临界二维高斯有向聚合物的涨落极限。

Method: 以临界二维高斯有向聚合物的中心化对数配分函数作为离散化 KPZ 解的极限取分布律；并将平滑化 SHF 取中心化对数后撤除平滑，通过重整化 Cole-Hopf 变换（renormalised Cole-Hopf transformation）建立与 SHF 的可测联系。证明方法综合领域内新旧成果并以新方式组合，且大量借助生成式 AI（generative AI）协助。

Result: 所构造过程为非高斯（non-Gaussian），其协方差（covariance）在对角线附近呈现对数-对数发散（log-log blow-up）；在收缩的空间尺度上平均时则涌现高斯涨落（Gaussian fluctuations）。

Conclusion: 该工作为定义不良的临界二维 KPZ 方程给出了严格的过程型解，并将聚合物、SHF 与 KPZ 三类对象统一起来；作者将其定位为汇集十余年集体努力的“社区项目”（community project），并强调生成式 AI 在证明与写作中的实质参与。

Abstract: This work constructs a non-trivial solution to the ill-defined two-dimensional Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) equation at criticality: a distribution-valued stochastic process on the plane, which we call the Critical 2D KPZ Process. It arises as the limit in law of centred log partition functions of two-dimensional Gaussian directed polymers at criticality, viewed as discretised KPZ solutions. It is also a measurable function of the Critical 2D Stochastic Heat Flow (SHF) via a renormalised Cole-Hopf transformation, obtained as the limit of the centred logarithm of the spatially mollified SHF as the smoothing is removed. The Critical 2D KPZ process is non-Gaussian and its covariance displays a log-log blow-up near the diagonal. Gaussian fluctuations emerge when averaging on shrinking spatial scales.
  The results presented here were obtained through extensive interactions with generative AI (ChatGPT 5.5, 5.6 and 6); see Appendix C for more details. The proof builds on key recent and older advances in our field, combined in novel ways with substantial AI input. We view this manuscript as a "community project" that represents more than a decade's collective effort. We have worked on making the presentation as accessible as possible, highlighting the main ideas and their relations to the literature.

</details>


### [198] [On the direct SLE-martingale approach to the integrability of Conformal Loop Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31648)
*Jason Miller,Wendelin Werner*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文讨论共形环圈系综（Conformal Loop Ensembles, CLE）的可积性结果（integrability results）的推导，即涉及少量标记点事件的显式概率公式；借助人工智能（AI）发掘SLE/CLE鞅方法（martingale approach）所给偏微分方程（PDE）的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLE被认为是统计物理（Statistical Physics）中离散临界平面格点模型的（猜想）标度极限，其可积性结果的已有推导多依赖刘维尔量子引力（Liouville quantum gravity）、共形场论（conformal field theory）或库仑气体方法（Coulomb gas methods），作者希望提供更短更直接的替代路径。

Method: 基于原始的SLE/CLE鞅方法所导出的偏微分方程，讨论其显式解，并指出这类解的发掘因AI而成为可能；以此直接途径重新推导既有公式并导出新公式。

Result: 给出若干近期由刘维尔量子引力、共形场论或库仑气体方法得到的公式的简短直接的新证明，并能推导出一些新公式；文中以临界渗流（critical percolation）的骨干指数（backbone exponent）与环域穿越概率（annulus crossing probabilities）为例作了简要说明。

Conclusion: 该直接方法似乎适用于与共形场论相关的二维系统的一大类可观测量（observables），具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: This paper is about the derivation of "integrability results" (i.e., for example, explicit formulas for the probability of events involving a few marked points) for Conformal Loop Ensembles, that are the (conjectured) scaling limits of discrete crititical planar lattice models from Statistical Physics. We discuss the explicit solutions to the PDEs provided by the original SLE/CLE martingale approach that AI now makes it possible to uncover. This provides short and direct new proofs of a number of formulas that have recently been derived based on Liouville quantum gravity, conformal field theory or Coulomb gas methods, and also makes it possible to derive some new ones. We briefly illustrate this with the examples of the backbone exponent and annulus crossing probabilities for critical percolation, but this direct approach seems to apply to a wide class of observables of two-dimensional systems related to conformal field theories.

</details>


### [199] [Atomic limits of empirical measures of interacting particle systems with self-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31319)
*Mario Ayala,Nicolas Dirr,Johannes Zimmer*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\\mathbb{Z}^d$ 上保守粒子系统（独立随机游走、对称排斥过程、对称包含过程）统一自对偶族在固定粒子数极限下的经验测度行为，证明其收敛到由 $k$ 个独立扩散构成的原子经验测度，即 Dean-Kawasaki 鞅问题在质量量子化 $1/k$ 下的唯一原子解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注密度或平均场层面的宏观极限，而固定粒子数 $k$ 下相互作用系统（排斥与包含过程）是否与独立游走者有相同的原子极限尚缺乏统一刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用扩散时空尺度（diffusive space-time scaling）参数 $n$，结合自对偶性（self-duality）、基于相容性（consistency）的两粒子归约，以及对称随机游走有限扰动下的格林函数估计（Green-function estimates）完成证明。

Result: 当 $n\\to\\infty$ 时，归一化经验测度在 $D([0,T],\\mathcal{M}_1(\\mathbb{R}^d))$ 中收敛到 $k$ 个独立扩散支撑的原子经验测度，协方差由微观跳跃核（jump kernel）决定；排斥与包含过程在固定 $k$ 下与独立游走者具有相同原子极限。

Conclusion: 相互作用仅通过微观近碰撞（near-collisions）起作用，其重标度占据时间（rescaled occupation time）趋于零；该极限等价于质量量子化 $1/k$ 的 Dean-Kawasaki 鞅问题的唯一原子解。

Abstract: We study the fixed-particle limit of empirical measures for a unified self-dual family of conservative particle systems on $\mathbb{Z}^d$: independent random walkers, the symmetric exclusion process, and the symmetric inclusion process. For a fixed number of particles $k$, under diffusive space-time scaling parametrised by $n$, we prove that as $n\to\infty$ the normalised empirical measure converges in $D([0,T],\mathcal{M}_1(\mathbb{R}^d))$ to an atomic empirical measure carried by $k$ independent diffusions, with constant covariance determined by the microscopic jump kernel.
  Equivalently, the limit is the unique atomic solution of the Dean-Kawasaki martingale problem with mass quantisation $1/k$. In particular, exclusion and inclusion have the same fixed-$k$ atomic limit as independent walkers: the interaction contributes only through microscopic near-collisions, whose rescaled occupation time vanishes. The proof combines self-duality, consistency-based reduction to two particles, and Green-function estimates for finite perturbations of symmetric random walks.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Plabic promotions beyond cluster: quantum cluster structures and the dual canonical basis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31655)
*Jian-Rong Li,Michael Oren Perlstein,Ran J. Tessler*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文将 plabic tangle（带状缠结）诱导的簇代数提升（promotion）研究拓展至量子（quantum）层面，提出量子簇拟同态（quantum cluster quasi-homomorphism）与簇-Lusztig 拟同态（cluster-Lusztig quasi-homomorphism）两个概念，并借此否证了 Lam 关于 amplituhedron 胞腔面的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Tangles）猜想：主可解缠结（dominant solvable tangle）诱导的代数提升是簇拟同态（cluster quasi-homomorphism）。本文旨在从量子化与对偶典范基（dual canonical basis）保持性两个方向扩展该簇视角，并考察不可解核（non solvable core）情形下的提升。

Method: 首先启动量子提升的研究，猜想所有由主可解缠结定义的提升均可自然提升为量子簇拟同态，并对文献中已被证明为经典簇拟同态的全部情形给出证明；其次定义簇-Lusztig 拟同态，要求其将 Lusztig 对偶典范基元素映为对偶典范基元素至多差一个冻结变量（frozen variables）上的 Laurent 单项式，且在每个内容分支（content component）上一致选取清除（clearing）与归一化（normalization）。随后讨论交数（intersection number）大于一的不可解核所诱导的提升。

Result: 证明了经典与量子 m-元星形提升（m-unary star promotion）均属于簇-Lusztig 拟同态这一类别，并猜想所有来自主可解缠结的提升具有同一性质。利用交数大于一的不可解核提升研究 amplituhedron 胞腔的面（facets of amplituhedron cells），证明其中某些面不落在对偶典范基元素的零点轨迹（zero loci）上，从而否证 T. Lam 的猜想（LamGP）。

Conclusion: Plabic tangle 提升的簇框架可自然量子化，并与对偶典范基保持性相容；不可解核情形揭示了 amplituhedron 胞腔面与对偶典范基零点轨迹之间的偏离，表明 Lam 的猜想不成立。

Abstract: Plabic tangles define geometric promotion maps between configurations and, after choosing a lift, between Grassmannians. The main conjecture in \cite{Tangles} is that when the tangle is dominant and solvable the induced algebraic promotion is cluster quasi-homomorphism. This work extends the cluster perspective in two directions. First, we initiate the study of quantum promotions. We conjecture that all promotions defined from dominant solvable tangles are naturally lifted to a \emph{quantum cluster quasi homomorphism}, and prove for all cases in the literature that the were shown to be (classical) cluster quasi-homomorphisms. Secondly, we define the notion of cluster-Lusztig quasi-homomorphisms, which are cluster quasi homomorphisms that take Lustig's dual canonical basis elements to dual canonical basis element, up to a Laurent monomial in frozen variables, with clearing and normalization chosen uniformly on each content component. We prove the unexpected result that the classical and quantum $m$-unary star promotions fall into this category, and conjecture that all promotions coming from dominant solvable tangles share the same property. We then consider promotions via non solvable cores of intersection number higher than one. We use it to study facets of amplituhedron cells, and to show that some of them do not lie in zero loci of dual canonical basis elements, disproving a conjecture by T. Lam \cite{LamGP}.

</details>
